## A

- <a id="abandoned-hypothesis"></a>**abandoned hypothesis**: an explanation of an incident that you tested and dropped ([47 §2](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#2-four-front-end-stories-a-performance-win-a-migration-a-production-incident-and-a-disagreement-over-architecture))
- <a id="abort"></a>**abort**: `Navigation.abort()`, which cancels a running navigation with code `Aborted` until activation starts ([24 §7](modules/24-routing.md#7-the-navigation-lifecycle-events-cancellation-and-the-transition-pipeline))
- <a id="abort-a-request"></a>**abort (a request)**: stop it on the client; `HttpClient`'s backends do it in their teardown (`aborter.abort()`, `xhr.abort()`) ([23 §2](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#2-choosing-a-flattening-operator-races-lost-writes-cancellation-and-recursion))
- <a id="abortcontroller"></a>**AbortController**: the object that carries a cancel signal to an operation ([22 §1](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#1-push-collections-observables-promises-iterators-and-laziness)) · also in [43 §4](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#4-the-angular-sse-client-eventsource-or-a-fetch-stream-as-an-observable-and-when-httpclient-fits)
- <a id="abortcontroller--abortsignal"></a>**AbortController / AbortSignal**: the controller that aborts, and the signal it hands to APIs that should stop, with an abort `reason` ([04 §7](modules/04-js-async-event-loop.md#7-cancellation-with-abortcontroller)).
- <a id="abortsignal"></a>**AbortSignal**: the platform's cancellation token; the fetch backend passes one to `fetch` and aborts it when you unsubscribe ([27 §1](modules/27-http-client.md#1-httpclient-versus-fetch-what-the-framework-adds-over-the-platform))
- <a id="abstractcontrol"></a>**AbstractControl**: the base class of every node of a form model; `value`, `status`, `setValue`, `patchValue`, `reset` and `get` live here ([25 §2](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#2-the-reactive-model-formcontrol-formgroup-formarray-and-formrecord))
- <a id="accept-language"></a>**Accept-Language**: the request header listing the browser's preferred languages, used to pick a locale for `/` ([34 §7](modules/34-i18n.md#7-compile-time-localization-one-build-per-locale-subpath-lang-and-dir))
- <a id="access-token"></a>**access token**: the credential an API accepts on each request, usually sent as `Authorization: Bearer <token>` ([33 §8](modules/33-security.md#8-where-the-token-lives-memory-web-storage-httponly-cookies-and-a-backend-for-frontend)) · also in [42 §2](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#2-oauth-20-and-openid-connect-for-a-single-page-app-authorization-code-with-pkce-and-why-the-implicit-flow-is-gone)
- <a id="accessibility"></a>**accessibility**: the property that people with different abilities and in different situations can perceive, operate and understand a page ([11 §1](modules/11-accessibility.md#1-why-accessibility-and-what-the-standards-require)).
- <a id="accessibility-tree"></a>**accessibility tree**: the browser's tree of objects with a name, description, role and state, built from the DOM and exposed to assistive technology ([11 §2](modules/11-accessibility.md#2-semantic-html-landmarks-and-headings)). · also in [30 §6](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#6-visual-regression-and-accessibility-checks-in-end-to-end-tests)
- <a id="accessible-name"></a>**accessible name**: the text assistive technology announces for a control; a query by it tests the page the way a user meets it ([29 §6](modules/29-testing-angular.md#6-component-tests-dom-queries-componentfixture-signal-inputs-with-setinput-and-bindings-outputs-and-onpush)) · also in [30 §4](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#4-locators-page-objects-and-fixtures)
- <a id="accessible-name--accessible-description"></a>**accessible name / accessible description**: the short text (name) and optional longer text (description) the accessibility tree holds for a node ([11 §6](modules/11-accessibility.md#6-accessible-names-descriptions-and-forms)).
- <a id="accessor-field"></a>**accessor field**: a class field declared with `accessor`, which creates a getter/setter pair that a decorator can intercept ([07 §7](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#7-decorators-tc39-standard-versus-experimentaldecorators-and-what-angular-does-with-them)).
- <a id="accname"></a>**AccName**: the Accessible Name and Description Computation, the W3C algorithm that picks the name from `aria-labelledby`, `aria-label`, native labels, content or `title` ([11 §6](modules/11-accessibility.md#6-accessible-names-descriptions-and-forms)).
- <a id="accumulator"></a>**accumulator**: the running state that `scan`, `mergeScan` and `switchScan` carry from one value to the next ([23 §2](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#2-choosing-a-flattening-operator-races-lost-writes-cancellation-and-recursion))
- <a id="accumulator-and-seed"></a>**accumulator and seed**: the running result `scan` and `reduce` carry, and the optional initial value of it ([22 §6](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#6-the-operator-catalogue-creation-transformation-filtering-utility-conditional-and-aggregation))
- <a id="action"></a>**action**: a plain object whose string `type` says what happened, written `[Source] Event`, plus optional payload ([28 §4](modules/28-state-management.md#4-ngrx-store-actions-reducers-createactiongroup-createfeature-and-runtime-checks))
- <a id="action-creator"></a>**action creator**: a function that builds an action object ([28 §4](modules/28-state-management.md#4-ngrx-store-actions-reducers-createactiongroup-createfeature-and-runtime-checks))
- <a id="action-group"></a>**action group**: a `createActionGroup` call that creates one action creator per event of one source, with camelCase names and `[Source] Event` types ([28 §4](modules/28-state-management.md#4-ngrx-store-actions-reducers-createactiongroup-createfeature-and-runtime-checks))
- <a id="action-method"></a>**action method**: a store method named after intent (`search`) that is the only writer of the store's state ([28 §2](modules/28-state-management.md#2-the-service-with-signals-store-state-shape-derived-selectors-methods-and-async-loads))
- <a id="actionability"></a>**actionability**: the set of checks an element must pass before Playwright performs an action on it (exactly one match, visible, stable, receives events, enabled) ([30 §3](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#3-auto-waiting-actionability-checks-web-first-assertions-and-cypress-retry-ability))
- <a id="actions-and-oftype"></a>**Actions** and **ofType**: the injectable stream of actions after their reducer ran, and the operator that narrows it to given action creators ([28 §6](modules/28-state-management.md#6-effects-functional-effects-flattening-error-handling-and-dispatch-false))
- <a id="actionssubject-reducermanager-and-state"></a>**ActionsSubject**, **ReducerManager** and **State**: the `BehaviorSubject` of dispatched actions, the holder of the combined reducer, and the class that applies the reducer to each action with `scan` ([28 §12](modules/28-state-management.md#12-under-the-hood-how-ngrx-store-reduces-actions-and-how-signalstore-keeps-state-in-signals))
- <a id="activatedroute-and-snapshot"></a>**ActivatedRoute and snapshot**: the object exposing a route's params, query, fragment and data as observables, and its snapshot, which does not follow later changes ([24 §2](modules/24-routing.md#2-route-parameters-path-matrix-query-and-fragment-inheritance-and-component-input-binding))
- <a id="activation"></a>**activation**: the step that creates or reuses the components of the new router state and ends in `NavigationEnd` ([24 §7](modules/24-routing.md#7-the-navigation-lifecycle-events-cancellation-and-the-transition-pipeline))
- <a id="active-descendant"></a>**active descendant**: the popup option named in `aria-activedescendant` while DOM focus stays on the combobox input ([46 §2](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#2-autocomplete-and-typeahead-search-a-request-policy-a-prefix-cache-ranking-on-the-server-and-an-accessible-combobox))
- <a id="active-descendant-aria-activedescendant"></a>**active descendant (aria-activedescendant)**: an ARIA attribute on a focused container that names the id of the option treated as current, so focus stays on the container ([36 Q36.23](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#q36-23))
- <a id="active-item"></a>**active item**: the list item a key manager currently points at ([36 §9](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#9-cdk-accessibility-focustrap-focusmonitor-liveannouncer-and-the-key-managers))
- <a id="active-support"></a>**active support**: the first 12 months of a major, with regularly scheduled updates and patches ([39 §3](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#3-how-angular-ships-semantic-versioning-the-release-cadence-support-windows-and-the-deprecation-policy))
- <a id="adapter"></a>**adapter**: a class that gives a foreign API the shape a token promises, so consumers keep one interface ([16 §9](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#9-di-patterns-testing-and-the-spring-container)). · also in [38 §3](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#3-facades-and-the-data-access-layer)
- <a id="adapter-data-access"></a>**adapter (data access)**: a class that gives one data source the shape a port promises, such as `HttpOrdersApi` ([38 §3](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#3-facades-and-the-data-access-layer))
- <a id="adapter-reporter"></a>**adapter (reporter)**: the code behind the reporter port that sends a report to a vendor SDK or to your own backend ([37 §11](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#11-logging-and-error-tracking-a-reporter-port-context-privacy-source-maps-and-sampling))
- <a id="add_header-level"></a>**add_header level**: one configuration level (`http`, `server`, `location`) that inherits the parent's `add_header` fields only if it declares none ([44 §3](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#3-nginx-for-a-single-page-app-location-matching-the-try_files-fallback-caching-headers-for-hashed-assets-and-gzip-or-brotli))
- <a id="add_header_inherit"></a>**add_header_inherit**: an Nginx directive (1.29.3+) whose `merge` value appends the parent level's `add_header` fields to the current level's ([44 §3](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#3-nginx-for-a-single-page-app-location-matching-the-try_files-fallback-caching-headers-for-hashed-assets-and-gzip-or-brotli))
- <a id="advisory"></a>**advisory**: a published report that a package version is vulnerable or malicious, which `npm audit` matches against your dependencies ([33 §11](modules/33-security.md#11-dependency-and-supply-chain-security-lockfiles-npm-audit-install-scripts-and-subresource-integrity))
- <a id="affected-project"></a>**affected project**: a touched project or any project that reaches one through imports ([38 §7](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#7-nx-monorepos-the-project-graph-tags-nxenforce-module-boundaries-affected-commands-and-caching))
- <a id="afterrendereffect"></a>**afterRenderEffect**: an effect for DOM work after rendering, split into `earlyRead`, `write`, `mixedReadWrite` and `read` phases; browser only ([17 §4](modules/17-signals.md#4-effects)).
- <a id="agent"></a>**agent**: ECMA-262's term for one thread of execution with its realms, such as a Node process's main thread and its `vm` contexts ([05 Ex 05.2](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#ex05-2)).
- <a id="aggregated-endpoint"></a>**aggregated endpoint**: an endpoint that returns several resources in one response ([45 §5](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#5-state-and-api-design-where-each-piece-of-state-lives-and-the-api-the-screens-need))
- <a id="aggregateerror"></a>**AggregateError**: an error that carries several failures in its `errors` array ([05 §6](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#6-errors-subclasses-causes-aggregation-and-global-handlers)).
- <a id="aggregation"></a>**aggregation**: merging several service calls into one answer, so the browser pays one round trip ([44 §1](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#1-where-the-api-calls-go-direct-calls-a-same-origin-reverse-proxy-an-api-gateway-or-a-backend-for-frontend))
- <a id="aggregation-window"></a>**aggregation window**: the span whose events become one value per widget ([46 §6](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#6-a-real-time-operations-dashboard-aggregation-per-widget-staleness-hidden-tabs-and-screens-left-open-for-days))
- <a id="algorithm-allow-list"></a>**algorithm allow-list**: the signature algorithms (`alg` values) a token verifier accepts; any other, including `none`, is refused ([42 §5](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#5-spring-security-as-a-resource-server-validating-jwts-and-turning-scopes-into-authorities))
- <a id="alias"></a>**alias**: a second token that answers with the instance of another token, made by `useExisting` ([16 §2](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#2-providers-and-tokens-useclass-usevalue-usefactory-and-useexisting)).
- <a id="alias-as"></a>**alias (as)**: a name for the value of an `@if` or `@else if` condition, visible only in the branch it came from ([18 §1](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#1-built-in-blocks-versus-directives-and-the-if-family-with-as))
- <a id="alias-input-option"></a>**alias (input option)**: a public binding name different from the field name ([19 §1](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#1-inputs-outputs-and-two-way-binding-the-parent-and-child-contract))
- <a id="alias-input-or-output"></a>**alias (input or output)**: the public name an input or output has in templates, set with `alias`, which can differ from the class member ([14 §1](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#1-directives-and-attribute-directives-host-inputs-elementref-and-renderer2)).
- <a id="alias-of-an-input"></a>**alias (of an input)**: a public binding name different from the property name; `SimpleChanges` still uses the property name ([20 §2](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#2-constructor-ngoninit-and-inputs-what-exists-when))
- <a id="allocation-timeline"></a>**allocation timeline**: a DevTools recording of where objects were allocated over a period instead of at one moment ([31 §8](modules/31-performance.md#8-memory-leaks-heap-snapshots-detached-nodes-and-the-devtools-workflow))
- <a id="allowlist"></a>**allowlist**: a list of what is permitted, with everything else dropped ([33 §3](modules/33-security.md#3-sanitization-by-security-context-html-style-url-and-resource-url))
- <a id="allviews"></a>**allViews**: the `ApplicationRef`'s list of attached views, each refreshed by `synchronizeOnce` and compared by the development check ([21 §9](modules/21-change-detection.md#9-under-the-hood-the-notification-the-dirty-flags-and-the-refresh))
- <a id="alpn"></a>**ALPN**: Application-Layer Protocol Negotiation, the TLS extension in which client and server agree on `h2` or `h3` ([09 §3](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#3-http11-http2-and-http3)).
- <a id="always"></a>**always**: the `add_header` parameter that adds the field whatever the status, error responses included ([44 §3](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#3-nginx-for-a-single-page-app-location-matching-the-try_files-fallback-caching-headers-for-hashed-assets-and-gzip-or-brotli))
- <a id="ambient-credential"></a>**ambient credential**: a credential the browser attaches by itself, such as a cookie, HTTP authentication or a client certificate; an `Authorization` header set by script is not ambient ([33 §9](modules/33-security.md#9-csrf-in-single-page-apps-when-cookies-make-you-vulnerable-samesite-and-angulars-xsrf-support)) · also in [42 §1](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#1-sessions-versus-tokens-what-the-server-keeps-what-each-request-carries-revocation-and-scaling)
- <a id="ambient-module"></a>**ambient module**: `declare module 'name' { … }`, types for a module specifier without an implementation in the program ([06 §8](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#8-declaration-files-types-and-augmentation)).
- <a id="amd--umd"></a>**AMD / UMD**: the asynchronous browser module format of RequireJS, and a wrapper that runs one file as AMD, CommonJS or a global ([05 §2](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#2-commonjs-interop-and-dynamic-import)).
- <a id="ancestor-path"></a>**ancestor path**: the parents of a view up to the root; `markForCheck` flags it and a signal does not ([21 §7](modules/21-change-detection.md#7-changedetectorref-markforcheck-detectchanges-detach-and-reattach))
- <a id="anchor-post"></a>**anchor post**: the id of the post at the top of the viewport, used to restore position ([46 §3](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#3-an-infinite-scroll-feed-cursor-pages-deduplication-new-items-without-a-jump-and-a-bounded-window))
- <a id="anchor-scrolling"></a>**anchor scrolling**: scrolling to the element named by the URL fragment, enabled with `anchorScrolling: 'enabled'` ([24 §9](modules/24-routing.md#9-view-transitions-scroll-restoration-and-titles))
- <a id="angular-aria"></a>**Angular Aria**: `@angular/aria`, headless directives that implement WAI-ARIA patterns on the author's markup; stable since v22.0 ([36 §1](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#1-material-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-styled-components-behavior-primitives-and-headless-patterns))
- <a id="angular-devtools"></a>**Angular DevTools**: the Chrome and Firefox extension that inspects a running development build ([12 §5](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#5-the-dev-loop-ng-serve-hot-module-replacement-and-angular-devtools)).
- <a id="angular-devtools-profiler"></a>**Angular DevTools profiler**: the Angular DevTools tab that shows which components' change detection took the time; development builds only ([31 §1](modules/31-performance.md#1-measure-first-the-loop-lab-versus-field-and-what-each-tool-answers))
- <a id="angular-elements"></a>**Angular Elements**: the `@angular/elements` package, whose `createCustomElement(Component, { injector })` returns a custom element class for one component ([37 §1](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#1-angular-elements-turning-a-component-into-a-custom-element))
- <a id="angular-material"></a>**Angular Material**: `@angular/material`, styled Material Design components for Angular, built on the CDK ([36 §1](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#1-material-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-styled-components-behavior-primitives-and-headless-patterns))
- <a id="angular-package-format"></a>**Angular Package Format**: the folder layout of a built library: `package.json` with `exports`, FESM bundles and bundled typings ([38 §5](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#5-angular-cli-libraries-ng-generate-library-ng-packagr-and-consuming-the-build)) · also in [39 §5](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#5-the-version-matrix-part-2-v9-to-v15-from-ivy-to-standalone)
- <a id="angular-testing-library"></a>**Angular Testing Library**: `@testing-library/angular`, which renders a component through `TestBed` and queries the page like a user ([29 §11](modules/29-testing-angular.md#11-component-harnesses-and-angular-testing-library))
- <a id="angularappengine"></a>**AngularAppEngine**: the `@angular/ssr` engine that turns a web-standard `Request` into a `Response` (or `null`), so it runs on any runtime with the Fetch types; `AngularNodeAppEngine` wraps it for Node ([32 §9](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#9-deploying-ssr-node-and-other-runtimes-static-output-caching-rendered-pages-and-allowed-hosts))
- <a id="angularjs"></a>**AngularJS**: the 1.x framework, whose support officially ended in January 2022 ([39 §1](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#1-angularjs-scopes-watchers-and-the-digest-cycle))
- <a id="animatechild"></a>**animateChild**: a function that lets a parent transition run the triggers of its child elements ([35 §8](modules/35-animations.md#8-legacy-angularanimations-triggers-states-transitions-query-and-stagger))
- <a id="animation"></a>**animation**: a change of style values spread over time instead of applied at once ([35 §1](modules/35-animations.md#1-motion-in-a-ui-what-to-animate-the-browsers-three-animation-mechanisms-and-where-angular-fits))
- <a id="animation-driver"></a>**animation driver**: the engine's adapter to the platform: the web driver plays with `element.animate()`, the noop driver plays nothing ([35 §8](modules/35-animations.md#8-legacy-angularanimations-triggers-states-transitions-query-and-stagger))
- <a id="animation-map"></a>**animation map**: the per-view record (internal `LView[ANIMATIONS]`) of the enter and leave functions of each node, keyed by node index ([35 §12](modules/35-animations.md#12-under-the-hood-from-animateleave-to-a-removed-node))
- <a id="animation-object"></a>**Animation object**: the platform's handle on one animation, with a play state and timing, whatever mechanism created it ([35 §1](modules/35-animations.md#1-motion-in-a-ui-what-to-animate-the-browsers-three-animation-mechanisms-and-where-angular-fits))
- <a id="animation-queue"></a>**animation queue**: the per-injector set of animation functions (internal `ANIMATION_QUEUE`) that runs once after the next render ([35 §12](modules/35-animations.md#12-under-the-hood-from-animateleave-to-a-removed-node))
- <a id="animation-state"></a>**animation state**: a named value of a trigger, with styles given by `state('name', style({…}))`; `void` and `*` are built in ([35 §8](modules/35-animations.md#8-legacy-angularanimations-triggers-states-transitions-query-and-stagger))
- <a id="animationcallbackevent"></a>**AnimationCallbackEvent**: the object a function-form callback receives: `target`, the element, and `animationComplete` ([35 §6](modules/35-animations.md#6-function-callbacks-and-the-v22-leave-semantics-animationcallbackevent-animationcomplete-and-nested-leaves))
- <a id="animationcomplete"></a>**animationComplete**: the function a leave callback calls to let Angular remove the element; a no-op in an enter callback ([35 §6](modules/35-animations.md#6-function-callbacks-and-the-v22-leave-semantics-animationcallbackevent-animationcomplete-and-nested-leaves))
- <a id="animationevent"></a>**AnimationEvent**: the object a `(@name.start)` or `(@name.done)` callback receives, with `fromState`, `toState`, `totalTime` and `phaseName` ([35 §8](modules/35-animations.md#8-legacy-angularanimations-triggers-states-transitions-query-and-stagger))
- <a id="announcement"></a>**announcement**: text a screen reader speaks, usually through a live region ([45 §6](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#6-performance-and-accessibility-as-design-steps-budgets-a-loading-plan-long-lists-and-keyboard-and-pointer-paths))
- <a id="anonymoussubject"></a>**AnonymousSubject**: a `Subject` that forwards `next`, `error` and `complete` to a given observer and subscribers to a given source; `WebSocketSubject` extends it ([22 §4](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#4-subjects-subject-behaviorsubject-replaysubject-and-asyncsubject))
- <a id="anti-pattern"></a>**anti-pattern**: a habit that looks right and causes a defect ([15 §5](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#5-legacy-ngmodule-patterns-declarations-exports-forrootforchild-sharedmodule-and-coremodule)).
- <a id="any"></a>**any**: the type that turns checking off in both directions, so it spreads to everything it touches ([06 §5](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#5-any-unknown-never-void-and-object-versus-)).
- <a id="aot-ahead-of-time-compilation"></a>**AOT (ahead-of-time compilation)**: compiling templates and decorators at build time, so a module's scope is written into each declared component; the `ng test` build does it too ([15 §7](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#7-migrating-with-ng-generate-angularcorestandalone-three-passes-hybrid-apps-and-common-failures); owner [Q12.19](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#q12-19)).
- <a id="aot-ahead-of-time-compiled-output"></a>**AOT (ahead-of-time compiled) output**: the component code the compiler generates at build time ([39 §4](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#4-the-version-matrix-part-1-v2-to-v8))
- <a id="aot-ahead-of-time-compilation-1"></a>**AOT (ahead-of-time) compilation**: compiling templates and decorators into definitions during the build, so no compiler ships ([12 §7](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#7-aot-versus-jit-and-template-type-checking)).
- <a id="apg"></a>**APG**: the ARIA Authoring Practices Guide, W3C's reference for how widgets should behave ([11 §3](modules/11-accessibility.md#3-aria-roles-states-properties-and-its-rules)).
- <a id="api-gateway"></a>**API gateway**: one entry point for many services that applies cross-cutting concerns such as routing, TLS and rate limits ([44 §1](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#1-where-the-api-calls-go-direct-calls-a-same-origin-reverse-proxy-an-api-gateway-or-a-backend-for-frontend))
- <a id="api-version"></a>**API version**: the value a request carries (header, query parameter, path segment or media type parameter) that names the contract it expects ([41 §4](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#4-evolving-the-contract-compatible-changes-api-versioning-in-spring-framework-7-and-deprecation-headers))
- <a id="api_origin"></a>**API_ORIGIN**: the exercise's injection token for the one origin whose requests carry the bearer token; a relative URL counts as on it ([27 Exercise 27.4](modules/27-http-client.md#ex27-4))
- <a id="apierror"></a>**ApiError**: the exercise's discriminated union of failures (`invalid-response`, `not-found`, `offline`, `validation`, `unknown`) that a service returns in place of an `HttpErrorResponse` ([27 Exercise 27.1](modules/27-http-client.md#ex27-1))
- <a id="app-initializer"></a>**app initializer**: a function that the application awaits before it starts, registered with `provideAppInitializer` ([42 §7](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#7-the-angular-side-an-auth-state-at-startup-guards-as-ux-interceptors-and-angular-oauth2-oidc-versus-angular-auth-oidc-client))
- <a id="app-shell"></a>**app shell**: the HTML, JavaScript and CSS that render an app's basic UI, usually precached ([09 §9](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#9-service-workers-and-the-pwa-manifest)). · also in [37 §5](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#5-caching-with-asset-and-data-groups-navigation-requests-and-the-app-shell)
- <a id="app-version-service-worker"></a>**app version (service worker)**: one `ngsw.json` and the files it names, as installed by the worker ([37 §4](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#4-angularservice-worker-registration-ngsw-configjson-and-the-generated-ngswjson))
- <a id="app_id"></a>**APP_ID**: the injection token holding the application's id (`ng` unless set), used in generated attribute names and as the default CSS-variable namespace ([13 §8](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#8-styling-strategies-component-styles-global-styles-css-variables-and-tailwind)).
- <a id="appdata"></a>**appData**: free-form data from `ngsw-config.json` carried inside the generated manifest ([37 §4](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#4-angularservice-worker-registration-ngsw-configjson-and-the-generated-ngswjson))
- <a id="appearance-matbutton"></a>**appearance (matButton)**: the input of `MatButton` that picks its style: `text`, `filled`, `elevated`, `outlined` or `tonal` ([36 §2](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#2-material-components-in-a-v22-app-standalone-imports-form-fields-dialogs-direction-and-motion))
- <a id="application-builder"></a>**application builder**: the `application` builder, today `@angular/build:application`, that `ng new` has used since v17 ([39 §6](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#6-the-version-matrix-part-3-v16-to-v22-from-signals-to-onpush-by-default); owner [12 §3](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#3-building-the-application-builder-output-layout-and-budgets))
- <a id="application-destination-prefix"></a>**application destination prefix**: the prefix (`/app`) that routes a message to `@MessageMapping` methods, removed before matching ([43 §5](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#5-websockets-and-stomp-in-spring-the-handshake-frames-messagemapping-brokers-user-destinations-and-heart-beats))
- <a id="application-providers-applicationproviders"></a>**application providers (applicationProviders)**: the `bootstrapModule` option that adds root providers to an NgModule app without editing the module ([15 §3](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#3-bootstrapping-and-providers-without-modules-bootstrapapplication-provide-functions-and-importprovidersfrom)).
- <a id="applicationconfig"></a>**ApplicationConfig**: an object with one `providers` array for the root environment injector, by convention in `app.config.ts`; exported from `@angular/core` ([15 §3](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#3-bootstrapping-and-providers-without-modules-bootstrapapplication-provide-functions-and-importprovidersfrom)).
- <a id="applicationref"></a>**ApplicationRef**: the handle to a running application: its root components, injector and `destroy()` ([12 §6](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#6-bootstrapping-bootstrapapplication-the-platform-and-initializers)). · also in [15 §3](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#3-bootstrapping-and-providers-without-modules-bootstrapapplication-provide-functions-and-importprovidersfrom), [19 §7](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#7-dynamic-components-viewcontainerrefcreatecomponent-createcomponent-and-bindings)
- <a id="applywhen"></a>**applyWhen**: mounts a schema on a path only while a condition holds ([26 §5](modules/26-signal-forms.md#5-cross-field-and-conditional-logic-valueof-validatetree-applywhen-disabled-hidden-and-readonly)).
- <a id="arabic-indic-digits"></a>**Arabic-Indic digits**: the digit characters U+0660 to U+0669 (`٠` to `٩`), used by `Intl` for `ar-EG` where Angular's data prints Latin digits ([34 §3](modules/34-i18n.md#3-angulars-locale-data-and-formatting-functions-versus-intl))
- <a id="args"></a>**args**: Storybook's name for a component's inputs gathered in one object ([38 §10](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#10-storybook-for-component-development))
- <a id="argument-resolver"></a>**argument resolver**: the Spring MVC strategy that builds one controller-method parameter, such as `RequestResponseBodyMethodProcessor` for `@RequestBody` ([41 §10](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#10-under-the-hood-one-failed-request-through-springs-filters-and-handlers-and-angulars-backend))
- <a id="aria"></a>**ARIA**: Accessible Rich Internet Applications, the W3C vocabulary of roles, states and properties that annotates the accessibility tree and adds no behavior ([11 §3](modules/11-accessibility.md#3-aria-roles-states-properties-and-its-rules)). · also in [13 §1](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#1-component-anatomy-selector-template-styles-imports-and-the-v22-file-convention)
- <a id="aria-snapshot"></a>**ARIA snapshot**: a YAML description of the accessibility tree that a test compares with the page ([30 §6](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#6-visual-regression-and-accessibility-checks-in-end-to-end-tests))
- <a id="aria-activedescendant"></a>**aria-activedescendant**: a property on a focused container naming the descendant that assistive technology should treat as active ([11 §4](modules/11-accessibility.md#4-keyboard-focus-order-roving-tabindex-and-focus-traps)).
- <a id="aria-busy"></a>**aria-busy**: the ARIA state that marks an element as being updated, so assistive technology may wait; owner [11 §3](modules/11-accessibility.md#3-aria-roles-states-properties-and-its-rules) ([14 §3](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#3-host-directives-the-composition-api)).
- <a id="aria-invalid"></a>**aria-invalid**: the ARIA state marking that a field's value does not match the expected format ([11 §6](modules/11-accessibility.md#6-accessible-names-descriptions-and-forms)).
- <a id="aria-invalid-and-aria-describedby"></a>**aria-invalid and aria-describedby**: attributes a template binds from the control's state so the input is marked invalid and points at its message ([25 §8](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#8-error-display-strategies-and-accessible-errors))
- <a id="aria-sort"></a>**aria-sort**: the ARIA attribute on a column header that tells assistive technology the column's sort state ([46 §4](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#4-a-large-data-grid-server-side-filtering-sorting-and-pagination-the-query-in-the-url-and-keyset-cursors))
- <a id="arrow-function"></a>**arrow function**: a function with no `this`, `arguments` or `new` of its own; it uses the enclosing scope's `this` ([02 §4](modules/02-js-scope-closures-this.md#4-how-this-is-bound)).
- <a id="artifact-ci"></a>**artifact (CI)**: a named file set stored by the CI service and handed to a later stage ([38 §11](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#11-a-ci-pipeline-for-a-front-end-lint-test-build-end-to-end-and-deploy-previews))
- <a id="asobservable"></a>**asObservable**: returns an Observable view of a Subject that has no `next`, `error` or `complete` ([22 §4](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#4-subjects-subject-behaviorsubject-replaysubject-and-asyncsubject))
- <a id="assertininjectioncontext"></a>**assertInInjectionContext**: the helper a function that wraps `inject()` calls first, so a call outside a context throws `NG0203` naming that function ([16 §4](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#4-the-injection-context-inject-runininjectioncontext-and-injectasync)).
- <a id="assertion-lighthouse-ci"></a>**assertion (Lighthouse CI)**: a per-audit check with a level and a limit, such as `["error", {"minScore": 0.8}]` ([31 §2](modules/31-performance.md#2-lab-metrics-in-a-real-browser-performance-observers-devtools-protocol-counters-and-lighthouse-in-ci))
- <a id="assertion-function"></a>**assertion function**: a function typed `asserts value is T` that narrows everything after the call and throws otherwise ([06 §3](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#3-narrowing-and-control-flow-analysis)).
- <a id="asset"></a>**asset**: what a threat model protects: data, money, a session ([33 §1](modules/33-security.md#1-threat-model-of-an-angular-single-page-app-what-the-attacker-controls-what-runs-where-and-where-each-defense-lives))
- <a id="asset-group"></a>**asset group**: a set of the app's own files listed in `ngsw-config.json`, cached and versioned with the app ([37 §5](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#5-caching-with-asset-and-data-groups-navigation-requests-and-the-app-shell))
- <a id="assistive-technology-at"></a>**assistive technology (AT)**: software or hardware a person uses to reach a page, such as a screen reader, magnifier or switch ([11 §1](modules/11-accessibility.md#1-why-accessibility-and-what-the-standards-require)).
- <a id="assumed-numbers"></a>**assumed numbers**: figures a case picks and labels "assumed", so its choices can be judged against them ([46 §1](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#1-from-method-to-case-the-parts-every-case-study-carries-and-where-each-cases-difficulty-lies))
- <a id="async-function"></a>**async function**: a function that returns a promise and can pause at `await` without blocking the thread ([04 §5](modules/04-js-async-event-loop.md#5-async-and-await)).
- <a id="async-generator"></a>**async generator**: an `async function*` that can both `await` and `yield` ([04 §6](modules/04-js-async-event-loop.md#6-async-iteration)).
- <a id="async-iterator"></a>**async iterator**: an iterator whose `next()` returns a promise of `{ value, done }`, consumed with `for await` ([04 §6](modules/04-js-async-event-loop.md#6-async-iteration)).
- <a id="async-pipe"></a>**async pipe**: an impure pipe that subscribes, stores the latest value, marks the view, and disposes on source change or destroy ([14 §8](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#8-the-async-pipe-under-the-hood)). · also in [28 §3](modules/28-state-management.md#3-the-service-with-behaviorsubject-store-the-legacy-but-common-pattern-and-its-migration)
- <a id="async-request-processing"></a>**async request processing**: a Servlet request put in asynchronous mode so the Servlet thread can return while the response stays open for later writes (Spring MVC reference, Asynchronous Requests) ([43 §3](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#3-server-sent-events-in-spring-sseemitter-in-spring-mvc-and-a-flux-of-serversentevent-in-webflux))
- <a id="async-request-timeout"></a>**async request timeout**: how long an async request may stay open: the emitter's own value, else MVC's default (`spring.mvc.async.request-timeout`), else the container's ([43 §3](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#3-server-sent-events-in-spring-sseemitter-in-spring-mvc-and-a-flux-of-serversentevent-in-webflux))
- <a id="async-rule"></a>**async rule**: a validation whose verdict arrives later; it runs only when the sync rules pass and leaves the field pending while it loads ([26 §6](modules/26-signal-forms.md#6-async-validation-and-debounce-validateasync-validatehttp-pending-and-cancellation)).
- <a id="async-validator-asyncvalidatorfn"></a>**async validator (AsyncValidatorFn)**: a function returning a Promise or Observable of `ValidationErrors | null` that runs only after the sync validators pass and leaves the control `PENDING` until it emits ([25 §6](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#6-async-validators-pending-status-cancellation-and-debouncing))
- <a id="asyncsubject"></a>**AsyncSubject**: a `Subject` that emits only its last value, and only when it completes ([22 §4](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#4-subjects-subject-behaviorsubject-replaysubject-and-asyncsubject))
- <a id="attached--detached-view"></a>**attached / detached (view)**: a view is attached while the tree walk may visit it and detached after `detach()`, until `reattach()` ([21 §7](modules/21-change-detection.md#7-changedetectorref-markforcheck-detectchanges-detach-and-reattach))
- <a id="attack-surface"></a>**attack surface**: every input an attacker can reach ([33 §1](modules/33-security.md#1-threat-model-of-an-angular-single-page-app-what-the-attacker-controls-what-runs-where-and-where-each-defense-lives))
- <a id="attribute-versus-property"></a>**attribute (versus property)**: text in the markup, as opposed to a value on the element's JavaScript object ([37 §2](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#2-the-elements-public-contract-attributes-properties-events-projected-content-and-typings))
- <a id="attribute-directive"></a>**attribute directive**: a directive that changes how its element looks or behaves; the structural kind adds and removes elements ([14 §1](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#1-directives-and-attribute-directives-host-inputs-elementref-and-renderer2)).
- <a id="audience-aud"></a>**audience (aud)**: the claim naming the APIs a token is intended for ([42 §5](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#5-spring-security-as-a-resource-server-validating-jwts-and-turning-scopes-into-authorities))
- <a id="audit"></a>**audit**: start a timer on a value and emit the latest value when it ends ([22 §8](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#8-timing-operators-debouncetime-throttletime-audittime-and-sampletime))
- <a id="auth-interceptor"></a>**auth interceptor**: an interceptor that adds the credential to requests for the API's origin and reacts to a 401 ([27 §7](modules/27-http-client.md#7-authentication-attaching-tokens-refreshing-on-401-with-a-request-queue-and-avoiding-refresh-loops))
- <a id="auth-state"></a>**auth state**: what the page knows about the visitor: unknown, anonymous or signed in; session state loaded once at startup ([42 §7](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#7-the-angular-side-an-auth-state-at-startup-guards-as-ux-interceptors-and-angular-oauth2-oidc-versus-angular-auth-oidc-client))
- <a id="authentication"></a>**authentication**: establishing who the caller is; a request with no valid identity is answered `401` ([33 §10](modules/33-security.md#10-route-guards-are-ux-authorization-belongs-on-the-server)) · also in [42 §1](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#1-sessions-versus-tokens-what-the-server-keeps-what-each-request-carries-revocation-and-scaling)
- <a id="authentication-entry-point"></a>**authentication entry point**: what Spring Security runs to answer an unauthenticated request, such as a redirect to login or a 401 ([42 §3](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#3-where-the-oauth-client-runs-a-browser-only-client-a-token-mediating-backend-or-a-backend-for-frontend-with-spring))
- <a id="authority"></a>**authority**: a permission string Spring Security checks, such as `SCOPE_orders:read` or `ROLE_admin` ([42 §5](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#5-spring-security-as-a-resource-server-validating-jwts-and-turning-scopes-into-authorities))
- <a id="authorization"></a>**authorization**: deciding whether a known caller may do this; a refusal is answered `403`, and only the server can enforce it ([33 §10](modules/33-security.md#10-route-guards-are-ux-authorization-belongs-on-the-server)) · also in [42 §1](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#1-sessions-versus-tokens-what-the-server-keeps-what-each-request-carries-revocation-and-scaling)
- <a id="authorization-code"></a>**authorization code**: the short-lived value the IdP returns to the redirect URI and the app exchanges for tokens ([42 §2](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#2-oauth-20-and-openid-connect-for-a-single-page-app-authorization-code-with-pkce-and-why-the-implicit-flow-is-gone))
- <a id="authorization-server-idp"></a>**authorization server (IdP)**: the server that authenticates the user and issues tokens; the identity provider ([42 §2](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#2-oauth-20-and-openid-connect-for-a-single-page-app-authorization-code-with-pkce-and-why-the-implicit-flow-is-gone))
- <a id="authorized-client"></a>**authorized client**: Spring's record of a client registration, a user and the tokens issued to them ([42 §3](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#3-where-the-oauth-client-runs-a-browser-only-client-a-token-mediating-backend-or-a-backend-for-frontend-with-spring))
- <a id="auto-cleanup-withautocleanupinjectors"></a>**auto cleanup (withAutoCleanupInjectors)**: the router feature that destroys the injectors of routes that are no longer active after each `NavigationEnd` ([24 §4](modules/24-routing.md#4-lazy-loading-in-depth-loaders-canmatch-and-route-injectors))
- <a id="auto-detect"></a>**auto-detect**: the fixture renders by itself, like the app, when something notifies the scheduler; always on in a zoneless test ([29 §5](modules/29-testing-angular.md#5-zoneless-fixtures-whenstable-testbedtick-detectchanges-and-automatic-change-detection))
- <a id="auto-waiting"></a>**auto-waiting**: Playwright retrying the actionability checks until they pass or the action's timeout ends ([30 §3](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#3-auto-waiting-actionability-checks-web-first-assertions-and-cypress-retry-ability))
- <a id="autocsp"></a>**autoCsp**: the build option (`security.autoCsp`) that writes a hash-based script policy into `index.html` as a `<meta>` tag; client-rendered builds only ([33 §5](modules/33-security.md#5-content-security-policy-with-angular-nonces-csp_nonce-and-autocsp))
- <a id="autoprovided"></a>**autoProvided**: the `@Service` option; `false` leaves the class unregistered until a `providers` array lists it ([16 §3](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#3-providedin-service-scopes-and-tree-shaking)).
- <a id="axe"></a>**axe**: a rule engine that checks a rendered page against accessibility rules; `@axe-core/playwright` runs it in end-to-end tests ([30 §6](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#6-visual-regression-and-accessibility-checks-in-end-to-end-tests))

## B

- <a id="back-channel-logout"></a>**back-channel logout**: the identity provider telling each app, server to server, to end a user's sessions, with a logout token ([42 §8](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#8-logout-everywhere-and-session-timeout-ux))
- <a id="back-of-envelope-estimate"></a>**back-of-envelope estimate**: arithmetic good to an order of magnitude ([45 §3](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#3-requirements-first-functional-and-non-functional-requirements-constraints-and-back-of-envelope-numbers))
- <a id="backend-httpbackend"></a>**backend (HttpBackend)**: the implementation at the end of the interceptor chain that makes the real call; `FetchBackend` is the default in v22 and `HttpXhrBackend` uses `XMLHttpRequest` ([27 §1](modules/27-http-client.md#1-httpclient-versus-fetch-what-the-framework-adds-over-the-platform))
- <a id="backend-decorator"></a>**backend decorator**: in Exercise 44.2, a class that implements `HttpBackend`, wraps the real backend and is provided in its place, so it sees each request after every interceptor ([Exercise 44.2](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#ex44-2))
- <a id="backend-for-frontend-bff"></a>**backend-for-frontend (BFF)**: a server on the app's own site that keeps the tokens and gives the browser only a session cookie ([33 §8](modules/33-security.md#8-where-the-token-lives-memory-web-storage-httponly-cookies-and-a-backend-for-frontend)) · also in [42 §3](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#3-where-the-oauth-client-runs-a-browser-only-client-a-token-mediating-backend-or-a-backend-for-frontend-with-spring), [44 §1](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#1-where-the-api-calls-go-direct-calls-a-same-origin-reverse-proxy-an-api-gateway-or-a-backend-for-frontend)
- <a id="backoff--exponential-backoff"></a>**backoff / exponential backoff**: a wait before each retry; exponential backoff doubles it each time ([23 §4](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#4-retrying-and-repeating-retry-with-backoff-and-repeat))
- <a id="backpressure"></a>**backpressure**: the consumer telling the producer how much it can take; Reactor has it through `request(n)`, RxJS 7 has no such channel ([22 §3](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#3-cold-and-hot-unicast-and-multicast))
- <a id="backward-compatible-change"></a>**backward-compatible change**: a change after which every deployed client still works, such as an optional field or a new endpoint ([41 §4](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#4-evolving-the-contract-compatible-changes-api-versioning-in-spring-framework-7-and-deprecation-headers))
- <a id="bailout"></a>**bailout**: React skipping work: an `Object.is`-equal state update, or a component whose props and context did not change ([40 §3](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#3-reacts-model-re-rendering-reconciliation-memoization-and-the-react-compiler))
- <a id="banana-in-a-box"></a>**banana-in-a-box**: the nickname for `[()]`, parentheses inside brackets; `([x])` is the common mistake ([13 §5](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#5-event-binding-and-two-way-binding)).
- <a id="bare-specifier"></a>**bare specifier**: an import source that is a package name rather than a relative path or URL, which browsers cannot resolve without an import map ([05 §3](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#3-import-maps-importmeta-and-import-attributes)).
- <a id="barrel"></a>**barrel**: an index file that re-exports other files; one scratch build kept unused services re-exported through one ([16 §3](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#3-providedin-service-scopes-and-tree-shaking)). · also in [18 §7](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#7-what-lands-in-a-chunk-testing-deferred-blocks-and-the-ssr-pointer)
- <a id="barrel-file"></a>**barrel file**: an `index.ts` that only re-exports other modules ([31 §6](modules/31-performance.md#6-bundle-analysis-budgets-and-tree-shaking-pitfalls)) · also in [38 §1](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#1-folder-structures-that-scale-by-feature-domain-slices-and-core-shared-and-feature-boundaries)
- <a id="base-href"></a>**base href**: the URL prefix of the app, from `APP_BASE_HREF` or the document's `<base href>`, which `PathLocationStrategy` puts before every URL ([24 §10](modules/24-routing.md#10-the-router-and-the-platform-history-api-navigation-api-and-location-strategies)) · also in [34 §7](modules/34-i18n.md#7-compile-time-localization-one-build-per-locale-subpath-lang-and-dir)
- <a id="base-image"></a>**base image**: the image a Dockerfile starts from with `FROM` ([44 §2](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#2-a-docker-multi-stage-build-for-angular-build-and-runtime-stages-layer-caching-dockerignore-and-a-non-root-image))
- <a id="base64url"></a>**base64url**: base64 with a URL-safe alphabet and no padding; encoding, not encryption ([42 §1](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#1-sessions-versus-tokens-what-the-server-keeps-what-each-request-carries-revocation-and-scaling))
- <a id="baseline"></a>**Baseline**: MDN's cross-browser label; *newly available* once the latest stable version of every core browser supports a feature, *widely available* about 2.5 years later ([08 Contents](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#contents)). · also in [11 header](modules/11-accessibility.md), [35 §1](modules/35-animations.md#1-motion-in-a-ui-what-to-animate-the-browsers-three-animation-mechanisms-and-where-angular-fits), [42 §4](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#4-refresh-tokens-rotation-reuse-detection-silent-renew-and-refreshing-across-tabs), [44 §3](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#3-nginx-for-a-single-page-app-location-matching-the-try_files-fallback-caching-headers-for-hashed-assets-and-gzip-or-brotli)
- <a id="baseline-budget"></a>**baseline (budget)**: the reference size that percentage thresholds are relative to ([12 §3](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#3-building-the-application-builder-output-layout-and-budgets)).
- <a id="baseline-mdn"></a>**Baseline (MDN)**: MDN's label for browser platform features; newly available means the latest stable Chrome, Edge, Firefox and Safari support it ([34 §2](modules/34-i18n.md#2-the-platforms-intl-apis-dates-numbers-plurals-lists-collation-and-display-names))
- <a id="baseline-measurement"></a>**baseline (measurement)**: the measurement taken before a change, against which the result is stated ([47 §2](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#2-four-front-end-stories-a-performance-win-a-migration-a-production-incident-and-a-disagreement-over-architecture))
- <a id="baseline-visual"></a>**baseline (visual)**: the approved reference screenshot, stored per project and platform next to the spec ([30 §6](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#6-visual-regression-and-accessibility-checks-in-end-to-end-tests))
- <a id="baseline-version"></a>**baseline version**: Spring's `"1.2+"` mapping, which matches 1.2 and the supported versions above it ([41 §4](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#4-evolving-the-contract-compatible-changes-api-versioning-in-spring-framework-7-and-deprecation-headers))
- <a id="batching"></a>**batching**: several synchronous writes cause one effect run with the final values ([17 §4](modules/17-signals.md#4-effects)).
- <a id="batteries-included"></a>**batteries included**: what most applications need (router, forms, HTTP, i18n, build, testing) ships with the framework ([12 §1](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#1-what-a-framework-does-and-angulars-philosophy)).
- <a id="bcp-47-language-tag"></a>**BCP 47 language tag**: the IETF format for naming a language (RFC 5646), such as `fr`, `fr-CA` or `zh-Hant-TW`; case-insensitive, with a canonical form ([34 §1](modules/34-i18n.md#1-i18n-l10n-and-the-locale-language-tags-what-a-locale-decides-and-locale_id))
- <a id="beacon"></a>**beacon**: a small asynchronous HTTP POST (`navigator.sendBeacon`) the browser sends without delaying the page's exit ([44 §9](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#9-frontend-error-tracking-and-real-user-monitoring-releases-source-maps-beacons-and-sampling))
- <a id="bean-validation"></a>**Bean Validation**: Jakarta Validation, the Java standard for declaring constraints on an object's fields and checking them at run time ([41 §7](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#7-validation-on-both-layers-bean-validation-angular-validators-and-server-errors-on-form-fields))
- <a id="bearer-token"></a>**bearer token**: a credential sent as `Authorization: Bearer <token>`; whoever holds it is served ([27 §7](modules/27-http-client.md#7-authentication-attaching-tokens-refreshing-on-401-with-a-request-queue-and-avoiding-refresh-loops))
- <a id="behavior-primitive"></a>**behavior primitive**: a service, directive or function that gives UI a behavior (positioning, focus, dragging, keyboard handling) but no markup or styles of its own ([36 §1](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#1-material-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-styled-components-behavior-primitives-and-headless-patterns))
- <a id="behavior-preserving-migration"></a>**behavior-preserving migration**: a migration that rewrites your code so the program behaves the same under the new version, usually by writing an old default back ([39 §11](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#11-the-v22-upgrade-what-ng-update-changes-and-why-each-migration-keeps-the-old-behavior))
- <a id="behavioral-question"></a>**behavioral question**: a question that asks for a past situation, as evidence of how you work ([47 §1](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#1-the-behavioral-round-what-a-story-is-evidence-of-star-with-the-weight-on-action-and-an-explicit-result-and-a-story-bank))
- <a id="behaviorsubject"></a>**BehaviorSubject**: a `Subject` constructed with an initial value that gives every new subscriber the current value at once; `getValue()` reads it ([22 §4](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#4-subjects-subject-behaviorsubject-replaysubject-and-asyncsubject))
- <a id="behaviorsubject-store"></a>**BehaviorSubject store**: a service that keeps its state in a private `BehaviorSubject`, exposes a stream and `select`, and changes state with `next` of a new object ([28 §3](modules/28-state-management.md#3-the-service-with-behaviorsubject-store-the-legacy-but-common-pattern-and-its-migration))
- <a id="bem"></a>**BEM**: Block Element Modifier, a class naming convention such as `.card__title` ([10 §7](modules/10-css-essentials.md#7-native-nesting-versus-sass)).
- <a id="bfc-block-formatting-context"></a>**BFC (block formatting context)**: the region where block boxes stack vertically and margins can collapse; floats, `flow-root`, `overflow` other than `visible` and others create a new one ([10 §1](modules/10-css-essentials.md#1-the-box-model-and-formatting-contexts)).
- <a id="bfcache"></a>**bfcache**: the back/forward cache, which restores a whole page on Back; an `unload` listener makes a page ineligible ([44 §9](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#9-frontend-error-tracking-and-real-user-monitoring-releases-source-maps-beacons-and-sampling))
- <a id="bfcache-backforward-cache"></a>**bfcache (back/forward cache)**: the browser's stored snapshot of a whole page, restored at once on Back or Forward; `unload` handlers and `no-store` can cost it ([09 §2](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#2-fetch-xhr-and-the-request-lifecycle)).
- <a id="bidi-algorithm"></a>**bidi algorithm**: the browser's ordering of text runs of mixed direction, which also places neutral characters such as `!` and spaces ([34 §11](modules/34-i18n.md#11-right-to-left-layouts-dir-logical-css-properties-and-bidirectional-text))
- <a id="bigint"></a>**BigInt**: the arbitrary-precision integer type (`10n`); it does not mix with `number` in arithmetic ([01 §2](modules/01-js-values-types-coercion.md#2-numbers)).
- <a id="binding"></a>**binding**: the association between a name and its storage slot in an environment record ([02 §1](modules/02-js-scope-closures-this.md#1-lexical-scope-and-the-scope-chain)).
- <a id="binding-template"></a>**binding (template)**: a template expression tied to a value shown on screen ([20 §6](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#6-expressionchangedafterithasbeencheckederror-cause-and-fixes))
- <a id="binding-collision"></a>**binding collision**: the parent's template and the component's `host` binding the same property, attribute, class or style on one element; the winner depends on static versus dynamic and, for class and style, on the template ([13 §7](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#7-the-host-element-host-metadata-hostbinding-and-hostlistener)).
- <a id="binding-function"></a>**binding function**: `inputBinding`, `outputBinding` or `twoWayBinding`, passed in `bindings` when a component is created in code ([19 §7](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#7-dynamic-components-viewcontainerrefcreatecomponent-createcomponent-and-bindings))
- <a id="binding-target"></a>**binding target**: the thing a binding writes to: a DOM property, an HTML attribute, a class, a style declaration or a component input ([13 §4](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#4-property-attribute-class-and-style-bindings)).
- <a id="bindings"></a>**bindings**: an option that takes binding functions such as `inputBinding` and `outputBinding` to feed inputs and listen to outputs ([29 §6](modules/29-testing-angular.md#6-component-tests-dom-queries-componentfixture-signal-inputs-with-setinput-and-bindings-outputs-and-onpush))
- <a id="blame-free-account"></a>**blame-free account**: our term for describing old code through the constraints of its time, how you learned it and what you would do differently, without judging people ([47 §3](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#3-legacy-codebases-and-migrations-without-blame-the-constraints-of-the-time-how-you-learned-the-code-before-changing-it-and-words-that-explain-instead-of-accuse))
- <a id="blob-report"></a>**blob report**: Playwright's raw per-shard results plus attachments, which `merge-reports` joins into one report ([30 §8](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#8-ci-strategy-for-end-to-end-tests-workers-sharding-retries-policy-and-test-data))
- <a id="blocking-and-non-blocking-resource"></a>**blocking and non-blocking resource**: a blocking resource makes the navigation wait and binds its value; a `nonBlocking` one lets the route activate and binds the whole `Resource` ([24 §6](modules/24-routing.md#6-resolvers-and-route-data-resolvefn-failures-and-router-resources))
- <a id="blocking-library"></a>**blocking library**: a dependency that cannot be installed beside the next Angular major, because its peer range excludes it or it cannot run there ([39 §8](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#8-skipping-versions-safely-one-major-per-hop-node-and-typescript-ranges-and-libraries-that-block-a-hop))
- <a id="blue-green-deployment"></a>**blue-green deployment**: running two full environments and flipping all traffic from one to the other ([44 §8](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#8-feature-flags-with-openfeature-and-canary-releases-providers-evaluation-context-sticky-rollout-kill-switches-and-version-skew))
- <a id="bootstrap"></a>**bootstrap**: the startup sequence that creates the platform, the environment injector, runs initializers and renders the root component ([12 §6](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#6-bootstrapping-bootstrapapplication-the-platform-and-initializers)).
- <a id="bootstrap-listener"></a>**bootstrap listener**: a callback Angular runs after the root component is created; the router's one starts preloading and in-memory scrolling ([24 §8](modules/24-routing.md#8-preloading-strategies-and-route-reuse))
- <a id="bootstrapapplication"></a>**bootstrapApplication**: the function that starts a standalone app: it builds the root environment injector from an `ApplicationConfig` and renders the root component ([15 §3](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#3-bootstrapping-and-providers-without-modules-bootstrapapplication-provide-functions-and-importprovidersfrom)).
- <a id="bootstrapcontext"></a>**BootstrapContext**: the object `bootstrapApplication` takes on the server to carry a platform created for the request ([32 §2](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#2-adding-angularssr-what-ng-add-generates-the-build-output-and-the-server-entry))
- <a id="bootstrapmodule"></a>**bootstrapModule**: the `PlatformRef` method that starts an NgModule app from its root module, rendering the components in `bootstrap` ([15 §3](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#3-bootstrapping-and-providers-without-modules-bootstrapapplication-provide-functions-and-importprovidersfrom)).
- <a id="borda-count"></a>**Borda count**: a ranking method in which each criterion gives a candidate one point per candidate scored strictly lower and half a point per tie, multiplied by the weight, so only order counts ([40 Exercise 40.2](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#ex40-2))
- <a id="bottleneck"></a>**bottleneck**: the step that limits a metric ([47 §2](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#2-four-front-end-stories-a-performance-win-a-migration-a-production-incident-and-a-disagreement-over-architecture))
- <a id="bottom-up-migration"></a>**bottom-up migration**: moving to Signal Forms by placing `SignalFormControl` leaves inside an existing `FormGroup` ([26 §9](modules/26-signal-forms.md#9-interop-and-migration-compatform-signalformcontrol-and-what-changed-from-v21-to-v22)).
- <a id="bound-function"></a>**bound function**: the exotic function `bind` returns, with a fixed `this` and preset leading arguments ([02 §4](modules/02-js-scope-closures-this.md#4-how-this-is-bound)).
- <a id="boundary-folder"></a>**boundary (folder)**: the line a folder draws so that outside files use only what it exposes ([38 §1](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#1-folder-structures-that-scale-by-feature-domain-slices-and-core-shared-and-feature-boundaries))
- <a id="box-model"></a>**box model**: the nested content, padding, border and margin rectangles every element generates; `box-sizing` picks which of them `width` measures ([10 §1](modules/10-css-essentials.md#1-the-box-model-and-formatting-contexts)).
- <a id="brand-check"></a>**brand check**: a test of an object's internal slot rather than its prototype chain, such as `Error.isError` ([05 §6](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#6-errors-subclasses-causes-aggregation-and-global-handlers)).
- <a id="brand-override"></a>**brand override**: a component-token override on an ancestor that fixes one component's color in every color scheme, because the component reads the token before the system variable ([Exercise 36.1](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#ex36-1))
- <a id="branded-type"></a>**branded type**: a primitive intersected with a type-only tag (`string & { readonly [brand]: 'UserId' }`) so that same-shaped values are incompatible at compile time ([07 §6](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#6-branded-types-for-ids-and-money)).
- <a id="breach"></a>**BREACH**: an attack on HTTPS through HTTP compression; Spring Security defends its CSRF token by masking it ([42 §6](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#6-csrf-with-cookiecsrftokenrepository-and-angulars-xsrf-support-csrfspa-in-spring-security-7))
- <a id="breaking-change"></a>**breaking change**: a change that stops some deployed client working: removing or renaming, optional becoming required, a narrower type, a new enum value a closed enum rejects ([41 §4](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#4-evolving-the-contract-compatible-changes-api-versioning-in-spring-framework-7-and-deprecation-headers))
- <a id="breakpoint"></a>**breakpoint**: a viewport size at which the layout changes, written as a media query such as `(max-width: 599.98px)` ([36 §8](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#8-the-smaller-primitives-the-stepper-breakpointobserver-and-clipboard))
- <a id="breakpoint-image"></a>**breakpoint (image)**: a width in pixels, listed in `IMAGE_CONFIG`, for which the loader builds one `srcset` file ([31 §11](modules/31-performance.md#11-under-the-hood-the-cdks-rendered-range-ngoptimizedimages-srcset-and-the-builders-chunks))
- <a id="broadcastchannel"></a>**BroadcastChannel**: a browser API that sends messages between same-origin tabs and frames by channel name ([42 §8](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#8-logout-everywhere-and-session-timeout-ux))
- <a id="broker-relay"></a>**broker relay**: Spring's handler that forwards `/topic` and `/queue` messages to an external STOMP broker such as RabbitMQ, over one TCP connection per client session plus a system connection ([43 §9](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#9-scaling-out-and-under-the-hood-sticky-sessions-the-broker-relay-backpressure-and-one-message-traced-across-two-instances))
- <a id="brokeravailabilityevent"></a>**BrokerAvailabilityEvent**: Spring's application event saying the relay's system connection was lost or re-established ([43 §9](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#9-scaling-out-and-under-the-hood-sticky-sessions-the-broker-relay-backpressure-and-one-message-traced-across-two-instances))
- <a id="brotli"></a>**Brotli**: a compression format; the build's "Estimated transfer size" column is a Brotli estimate ([12 §3](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#3-building-the-application-builder-output-layout-and-budgets)). · also in [44 §3](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#3-nginx-for-a-single-page-app-location-matching-the-try_files-fallback-caching-headers-for-hashed-assets-and-gzip-or-brotli)
- <a id="browser-context"></a>**browser context**: an isolated browser profile with its own cookies and storage; Playwright gives each test a fresh one ([30 §1](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#1-what-belongs-at-the-end-to-end-level-journeys-cost-and-a-strategy-across-levels))
- <a id="browser-provider"></a>**browser provider**: a package that lets Vitest drive a real browser (browser mode) ([29 §2](modules/29-testing-angular.md#2-the-runner-vitest-through-angularbuildunit-test-jsdom-and-browser-mode))
- <a id="browser-based-oauth-client"></a>**browser-based OAuth client**: a single-page app that runs the whole OAuth flow and holds every token itself ([42 §3](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#3-where-the-oauth-client-runs-a-browser-only-client-a-token-mediating-backend-or-a-backend-for-frontend-with-spring))
- <a id="browserslist"></a>**browserslist**: the config that lists the browsers an app supports ([05 §8](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#8-modern-features-by-edition-es2015-to-es2026)).
- <a id="browserurl"></a>**browserUrl**: the navigation option, also a `RouterLink` input, that sets the address-bar URL apart from the routed URL ([24 §10](modules/24-routing.md#10-the-router-and-the-platform-history-api-navigation-api-and-location-strategies))
- <a id="bubbling"></a>**bubbling**: the order in which a DOM event reaches the clicked element first and then each ancestor ([14 Exercise 14.1](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#ex14-1)). · also in [37 §2](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#2-the-elements-public-contract-attributes-properties-events-projected-content-and-typings)
- <a id="bucket"></a>**bucket**: the slot a hash of the flag key and targeting key puts a user in; a rollout serves the new variant to the lowest slots ([44 §8](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#8-feature-flags-with-openfeature-and-canary-releases-providers-evaluation-context-sticky-rollout-kill-switches-and-version-skew))
- <a id="budget-build"></a>**budget (build)**: a size limit on part of the build output that warns or fails the build ([12 §3](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#3-building-the-application-builder-output-layout-and-budgets)). · also in [31 §6](modules/31-performance.md#6-bundle-analysis-budgets-and-tree-shaking-pitfalls)
- <a id="budget-bundle"></a>**budget (bundle)**: a bundle size limit in the build configuration ([39 §4](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#4-the-version-matrix-part-1-v2-to-v8))
- <a id="buffer-flattening"></a>**buffer (flattening)**: the queue of outer values waiting for a free inner slot in `mergeMap` with a limit and `concatMap` ([23 §1](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#1-higher-order-observables-switchmap-mergemap-concatmap-and-exhaustmap))
- <a id="buffer-replaysubject"></a>**buffer (ReplaySubject)**: the stored values a `ReplaySubject` replays to a late subscriber ([22 §4](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#4-subjects-subject-behaviorsubject-replaysubject-and-asyncsubject))
- <a id="buffer-virtual-scrolling"></a>**buffer (virtual scrolling)**: the extra distance rendered beyond the viewport on each side, with a minimum and a maximum ([31 §4](modules/31-performance.md#4-long-lists-track-virtual-scrolling-with-the-cdk-and-the-alternatives))
- <a id="buffered-entries"></a>**buffered entries**: entries recorded before an observer was created, delivered to it when it observes with `buffered: true` ([31 §2](modules/31-performance.md#2-lab-metrics-in-a-real-browser-performance-observers-devtools-protocol-counters-and-lighthouse-in-ci))
- <a id="build-context"></a>**build context**: the folder sent to the builder, which is all a `COPY` can read ([44 §2](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#2-a-docker-multi-stage-build-for-angular-build-and-runtime-stages-layer-caching-dockerignore-and-a-non-root-image))
- <a id="build-graph"></a>**build graph**: ng-packagr's internal map of nodes keyed by URL, where each entry point is a node and an edge means "imports" ([38 §13](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#13-under-the-hood-ng-packagrs-build-graph-and-how-nx-computes-affected-projects-and-task-hashes))
- <a id="build-stage"></a>**build stage**: the stage that installs dependencies and builds the app, and is not part of the final image ([44 §2](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#2-a-docker-multi-stage-build-for-angular-build-and-runtime-stages-layer-caching-dockerignore-and-a-non-root-image))
- <a id="builder"></a>**builder**: the function a target runs, named `<package>:<name>` ([12 §2](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#2-the-cli-and-the-workspace-angularjson-projects-schematics-and-ng-update)). · also in [29 §2](modules/29-testing-angular.md#2-the-runner-vitest-through-angularbuildunit-test-jsdom-and-browser-mode), [30 §2](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#2-playwright-and-cypress-architecture-ng-e2e-and-the-end-of-protractor)
- <a id="built-in-accessor"></a>**built-in accessor**: an accessor Angular ships and matches by selector on a native element, such as `NumberValueAccessor` or `CheckboxControlValueAccessor` ([25 §10](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#10-controlvalueaccessor-and-the-validator-interface-custom-controls-under-the-hood))
- <a id="built-in-directive"></a>**built-in directive**: an ordinary directive shipped in `@angular/common` (`NgClass`, `NgStyle`, the outlets, and the deprecated `NgIf`, `NgForOf`, `NgSwitch`); nothing in it is privileged ([14 §4](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#4-built-in-directives-ngclass-ngstyle-the-outlets-and-what-control-flow-replaced)).
- <a id="bundle"></a>**bundle**: a file the build emits by joining many modules ([12 §3](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#3-building-the-application-builder-output-layout-and-budgets)).
- <a id="bundle-chunk"></a>**bundle chunk**: one JavaScript file the builder emits; initial chunks load with the page, lazy chunks on a dynamic `import()` ([35 §9](modules/35-animations.md#9-migrating-from-angularanimations-to-native-css-and-animateenteranimateleave))
- <a id="bundled-typings"></a>**bundled typings**: one `.d.ts` per entry point that holds only its exported names ([38 §6](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#6-public-api-surfaces-public-apits-exports-secondary-entry-points-and-versioning))
- <a id="bundler"></a>**bundler**: a build tool (esbuild, webpack) that combines modules into a few files and drops unused code ([05 §1](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#1-es-modules-static-structure-linking-and-live-bindings)).
- <a id="burst"></a>**burst**: values that arrive close together, followed by a pause ([22 §8](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#8-timing-operators-debouncetime-throttletime-audittime-and-sampletime))
- <a id="bycss"></a>**By.css**: a predicate builder for `DebugElement.query` that matches nodes by CSS selector ([29 §6](modules/29-testing-angular.md#6-component-tests-dom-queries-componentfixture-signal-inputs-with-setinput-and-bindings-outputs-and-onpush))
- <a id="bypass"></a>**bypass**: a `DomSanitizer.bypassSecurityTrust*` call, which wraps a string in a safe value and so turns Angular's check off for it ([33 §4](modules/33-security.md#4-domsanitizer-and-bypasssecuritytrust-when-trusting-a-value-is-dangerous-and-the-safe-patterns))
- <a id="bytesinoutput"></a>**bytesInOutput**: the bytes one input module contributed to one output file in the metafile ([31 §6](modules/31-performance.md#6-bundle-analysis-budgets-and-tree-shaking-pitfalls))

## C

- <a id="c4-container"></a>**C4 container**: a runtime boundary around code that runs or data that is stored; not an Angular container component ([45 §2](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#2-communicating-under-time-pressure-a-time-plan-complete-trade-off-statements-and-diagrams-at-three-zoom-levels))
- <a id="cache-entry-in-flight-sharing"></a>**cache entry (in-flight sharing)**: the exercise's `Map` value that holds an Observable replayed to every caller of one key, so callers that overlap share one backend request ([27 Exercise 27.3](modules/27-http-client.md#ex27-3))
- <a id="cache-hit"></a>**cache hit**: a layer answers from a stored entry ([45 §7](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#7-rendering-strategy-and-caching-layers-a-rendering-choice-per-route-and-every-cache-from-the-cdn-to-the-component))
- <a id="cache-interceptor"></a>**cache interceptor**: an interceptor that answers repeated GETs from memory, keyed by URL, so the backend is skipped on a hit ([27 §6](modules/27-http-client.md#6-cancellation-and-caching-unsubscribing-aborts-cache-interceptors-sharereplay-and-the-ssr-transfer-cache))
- <a id="cache-invalidation"></a>**cache invalidation**: dropping cache entries that a write has made stale ([27 §6](modules/27-http-client.md#6-cancellation-and-caching-unsubscribing-aborts-cache-interceptors-sharereplay-and-the-ssr-transfer-cache))
- <a id="cache-key"></a>**cache key**: the identity under which an answer is stored ([45 §7](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#7-rendering-strategy-and-caching-layers-a-rendering-choice-per-route-and-every-cache-from-the-cdn-to-the-component))
- <a id="cache-key-ci"></a>**cache key (CI)**: the label a saved folder is stored under in CI, so the same key restores it ([38 §11](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#11-a-ci-pipeline-for-a-front-end-lint-test-build-end-to-end-and-deploy-previews))
- <a id="cache-miss"></a>**cache miss**: a layer has no usable entry and asks the next ([45 §7](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#7-rendering-strategy-and-caching-layers-a-rendering-choice-per-route-and-every-cache-from-the-cdn-to-the-component))
- <a id="cache-mount"></a>**cache mount**: a folder, such as npm's download cache, that a `RUN --mount=type=cache` step keeps between builds ([44 §2](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#2-a-docker-multi-stage-build-for-angular-build-and-runtime-stages-layer-caching-dockerignore-and-a-non-root-image))
- <a id="cache_clock"></a>**CACHE_CLOCK**: the exercise's injected function that returns the current time in milliseconds, so cache expiry needs no real time ([27 Exercise 27.3](modules/27-http-client.md#ex27-3))
- <a id="cache-control"></a>**Cache-Control**: the HTTP header telling caches whether and how long to store a response ([32 §9](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#9-deploying-ssr-node-and-other-runtimes-static-output-caching-rendered-pages-and-allowed-hosts); directives in [09 §4](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#4-http-caching))
- <a id="cache-then-network"></a>**cache-then-network**: showing a stored answer first and then the fresh one, in that order ([23 §8](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#8-angular-patterns-typeahead-polling-cache-then-network-and-racing-requests))
- <a id="caching-layer"></a>**caching layer**: a store that answers requests under a key ([45 §7](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#7-rendering-strategy-and-caching-layers-a-rendering-choice-per-route-and-every-cache-from-the-cdn-to-the-component))
- <a id="calendar-date"></a>**calendar date**: a day with no time and no zone, such as a birthday or Java's `LocalDate`; on the wire, `YYYY-MM-DD` ([41 §9](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#9-dates-time-zones-decimals-and-money-across-the-wire))
- <a id="call-site"></a>**call site**: the expression that calls a function; for ordinary functions it decides `this` ([02 §4](modules/02-js-scope-closures-this.md#4-how-this-is-bound)).
- <a id="call-stack"></a>**call stack**: the stack of running function calls; the loop moves on only when it is empty ([04 §1](modules/04-js-async-event-loop.md#1-the-event-loop-tasks-microtasks-and-rendering)).
- <a id="callsetdisabledstate"></a>**callSetDisabledState**: the `withConfig` option choosing whether `setDisabledState` is called on every attach (`'always'`, the default since v15) or only for a disabled control (`'whenDisabledForLegacyCode'`) ([25 §10](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#10-controlvalueaccessor-and-the-validator-interface-custom-controls-under-the-hood))
- <a id="canary-release"></a>**canary release**: sending a slice of traffic to a new version before the rest ([44 §8](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#8-feature-flags-with-openfeature-and-canary-releases-providers-evaluation-context-sticky-rollout-kill-switches-and-version-skew))
- <a id="cancellation-request"></a>**cancellation (request)**: stopping a request nobody wants any more; on the fetch backend, unsubscribing aborts it ([27 §6](modules/27-http-client.md#6-cancellation-and-caching-unsubscribing-aborts-cache-interceptors-sharereplay-and-the-ssr-transfer-cache))
- <a id="cancelled-testrequest"></a>**cancelled (TestRequest)**: `true` once the subscriber unsubscribed from the request ([27 §11](modules/27-http-client.md#11-testing-and-the-backend-seam-providehttpclienttesting-httptestingcontroller-and-what-they-replace))
- <a id="candidates-questions"></a>**candidate's questions**: our term for the questions you ask at the end of a round, to show what you care about and to collect evidence about the team ([47 §6](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#6-questions-to-ask-about-the-front-end-practice-versions-and-updates-testing-the-design-system-and-accessibility-performance-contracts-and-releases-and-how-to-read-the-answers))
- <a id="canload"></a>**canLoad**: the guard that ran before a `loadChildren` loader, deprecated in favor of `canMatch` ([24 §5](modules/24-routing.md#5-functional-guards-canmatch-canactivate-candeactivate-and-redirectcommand))
- <a id="canmatch-guard"></a>**canMatch guard**: a route guard that decides whether the router considers a route at all ([42 §7](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#7-the-angular-side-an-auth-state-at-startup-guards-as-ux-interceptors-and-angular-oauth2-oidc-versus-angular-auth-oidc-client))
- <a id="canonical-query"></a>**canonical query**: one written form of a grid query (filters sorted by name, a sort ending with the unique id, a page size, a cursor) that gives one URL and one cache key ([46 §4](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#4-a-large-data-grid-server-side-filtering-sorting-and-pagination-the-query-in-the-url-and-keyset-cursors))
- <a id="canonical-url"></a>**canonical URL**: a URL you build yourself from parsed, checked parts instead of passing the input through ([33 §4](modules/33-security.md#4-domsanitizer-and-bypasssecuritytrust-when-trusting-a-value-is-dangerous-and-the-safe-patterns))
