- <a id="capture--bubble-phase"></a>**capture / bubble phase**: the two directions of event propagation, down from the window to the target and back up ([08 §4](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#4-events-propagation-default-actions-passive-listeners-and-delegation)).
- <a id="capture-versus-replay"></a>**capture versus replay**: capture records an event before hydration; replay delivers it to the real listener after hydration ([32 §5](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#5-event-replay-keeping-the-clicks-made-before-the-code-arrives))
- <a id="cardinal-and-ordinal-plurals"></a>**cardinal and ordinal plurals**: cardinal rules count things ("1 file"), ordinal rules rank them ("1st"); `Intl.PluralRules` takes `type: 'ordinal'` for the second ([34 §2](modules/34-i18n.md#2-the-platforms-intl-apis-dates-numbers-plurals-lists-collation-and-display-names))
- <a id="cascade"></a>**cascade**: the algorithm that picks one winning declaration per property per element, by origin and importance, context, inline style, layer, specificity, then order ([10 §2](modules/10-css-essentials.md#2-the-cascade-origins-specificity-and-layers)).
- <a id="cascade-layer"></a>**cascade layer**: a named group of rules ordered within an origin by first declaration; unlayered rules beat layered ones for normal declarations ([10 §2](modules/10-css-essentials.md#2-the-cascade-origins-specificity-and-layers)).
- <a id="case-study"></a>**case study**: one product screen designed across the browser and the server ([46 §1](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#1-from-method-to-case-the-parts-every-case-study-carries-and-where-each-cases-difficulty-lies))
- <a id="case-template"></a>**case template**: this module's six parts every case carries, in order: numbers, diagram, contract, state, failure modes, kernel ([46 §1](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#1-from-method-to-case-the-parts-every-case-study-carries-and-where-each-cases-difficulty-lies))
- <a id="catch-up"></a>**catch-up**: the events a reconnecting client receives after its last event id, which may repeat some it already has ([46 §7](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#7-a-notifications-center-and-an-editing-indicator-delivery-unread-counts-across-tabs-and-presence-with-a-time-to-live))
- <a id="caught"></a>**caught**: the second argument of a `catchError` selector, the source with the same `catchError` applied; returning it subscribes again ([23 §3](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#3-errors-catcherror-placement-finalize-and-error-versus-complete))
- <a id="cdp-session"></a>**CDP session**: one attached connection through which a test sends protocol commands to a page ([31 §2](modules/31-performance.md#2-lab-metrics-in-a-real-browser-performance-observers-devtools-protocol-counters-and-lighthouse-in-ci))
- <a id="change-detection"></a>**change detection**: the walk over the component tree that checks the template bindings of each view it refreshes ([20 §1](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#1-the-lifecycle-map-phases-and-the-order-of-the-hooks)) · also in [21 §1](modules/21-change-detection.md#1-the-synchronization-problem-dirty-checking-and-fine-grained-reactivity)
- <a id="change-by-copy-array-methods"></a>**change-by-copy array methods**: `toSorted`, `toReversed`, `toSpliced` and `with` (ES2023), which return a new array instead of mutating ([03 §7](modules/03-js-objects-prototypes-classes.md#7-immutability-and-change-by-copy-array-methods)).
- <a id="channelinterceptor"></a>**ChannelInterceptor**: Spring Messaging's hook on a message channel; its `preSend` sees each inbound message and may change it (set the user) or throw to refuse it ([43 §7](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#7-authentication-on-the-handshake-cookies-tokens-in-the-connect-frame-origin-checks-and-csrf-in-spring-security))
- <a id="characterization-test"></a>**characterization test**: a test that pins what existing code does now, quirks included, so a refactor can prove it kept them (Wikipedia, read 2026-10-23) ([47 §3](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#3-legacy-codebases-and-migrations-without-blame-the-constraints-of-the-time-how-you-learned-the-code-before-changing-it-and-words-that-explain-instead-of-accuse))
- <a id="checkpoint"></a>**checkpoint**: the build, tests, render check and commit that follow each migration batch ([39 §10](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#10-a-migration-program-for-an-old-angular-codebase-order-dependencies-and-checkpoints))
- <a id="checkpoint-interview"></a>**checkpoint (interview)**: a pause where you ask the interviewer where to go deeper ([45 §2](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#2-communicating-under-time-pressure-a-time-plan-complete-trade-off-statements-and-diagrams-at-three-zoom-levels)) · also in [47 §4](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#4-live-coding-an-angular-task-clarify-say-the-plan-build-in-working-increments-narrate-and-recover-when-stuck)
- <a id="child-translation-service"></a>**child translation service**: an ngx-translate `TranslateService` from `provideChildTranslateService`, with its own loader and store, that asks its parent for keys it lacks ([34 §9](modules/34-i18n.md#9-runtime-translation-libraries-transloco-and-ngx-translate-lazy-scopes-and-the-trade-off-against-built-in-i18n))
- <a id="chips--partitioned"></a>**CHIPS / Partitioned**: Cookies Having Independent Partitioned State, a cookie attribute giving one cookie jar per top-level site ([09 §6](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#6-cookies)).
- <a id="chrome-devtools-protocol-cdp"></a>**Chrome DevTools Protocol (CDP)**: the command channel Chromium's DevTools use, also open to test tools ([31 §2](modules/31-performance.md#2-lab-metrics-in-a-real-browser-performance-observers-devtools-protocol-counters-and-lighthouse-in-ci))
- <a id="chrome-ux-report-crux"></a>**Chrome UX Report (CrUX)**: Google's public dataset of performance metrics from Chrome users ([31 §1](modules/31-performance.md#1-measure-first-the-loop-lab-versus-field-and-what-each-tool-answers))
- <a id="chunk"></a>**chunk**: a separate output file a bundler emits so that part of an app loads on demand ([05 Q05.08](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#q05-08)). · also in [12 §3](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#3-building-the-application-builder-output-layout-and-budgets), [15 §4](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#4-lazy-loading-without-modules-loadcomponent-and-loadchildren-with-route-arrays), [31 §5](modules/31-performance.md#5-loading-the-initial-bundle-lazy-routes-defer-and-preloading-as-one-plan)
- <a id="chunk-optimization"></a>**chunk optimization**: the Angular builder's second bundling pass over esbuild's output, which can merge lazy chunks and rewrites the metafile ([31 §11](modules/31-performance.md#11-under-the-hood-the-cdks-rendered-range-ngoptimizedimages-srcset-and-the-builders-chunks))
- <a id="chunked-upload"></a>**chunked upload**: sending a file as slices, each with its byte range, instead of one request ([41 §8](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#8-files-multipart-and-chunked-uploads-progress-and-downloads-with-content-disposition-and-streaming))
- <a id="circular-dependency"></a>**circular dependency**: two tokens that need each other, so neither can be built; it fails at run time as `NG0200` with a path ([16 §8](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#8-lifetime-and-dynamic-injectors-destroyref-environmentinjector-injectorcreate-forward-references-and-cycles)).
- <a id="claim"></a>**claim**: a statement about the caller inside a token, such as `sub` (who) or `exp` (when the token expires) ([42 §1](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#1-sessions-versus-tokens-what-the-server-keeps-what-each-request-carries-revocation-and-scaling))
- <a id="claim-a-node"></a>**claim (a node)**: during hydration, keeping an existing server node as if Angular had created it, found by position ([32 §4](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#4-hydration-reusing-the-servers-dom-mismatches-and-ngskiphydration))
- <a id="class-interceptor"></a>**class interceptor**: an `HttpInterceptor` class registered under `HTTP_INTERCEPTORS`, the legacy form that runs only with `withInterceptorsFromDi()` ([42 §7](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#7-the-angular-side-an-auth-state-at-startup-guards-as-ux-interceptors-and-angular-oauth2-oidc-versus-angular-auth-oidc-client))
- <a id="classlist"></a>**classList**: an element's live set of class names, which class bindings add to and remove from ([13 §4](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#4-property-attribute-class-and-style-bindings)).
- <a id="cldr"></a>**CLDR**: the project the typings name as the source of currency data; named only on that basis ([14 §6](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#6-built-in-pipes-and-locale-data)). · also in [34 §1](modules/34-i18n.md#1-i18n-l10n-and-the-locale-language-tags-what-a-locale-decides-and-locale_id)
- <a id="clean-close-wasclean"></a>**clean close (wasClean)**: a close after the closing handshake finished (RFC 6455 §7.1.4); RxJS completes the stream on it and errors otherwise ([43 §6](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#6-the-angular-websocket-client-rxjs-websocket-stomprx-stomp-and-reconnecting-without-losing-subscriptions))
- <a id="cleanup-hook"></a>**cleanup hook**: the function `@angular/core/testing` exports for the runner's `beforeEach` and `afterEach`; it resets the testing module ([29 §12](modules/29-testing-angular.md#12-under-the-hood-how-the-builder-boots-testbed-and-how-a-fixture-decides-it-is-stable))
- <a id="clear-site-data"></a>**Clear-Site-Data**: a response header that tells the browser to clear an origin's cookies, storage or cache ([42 §8](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#8-logout-everywhere-and-session-timeout-ux))
- <a id="clearance"></a>**clearance**: space `clear` adds above a box's top margin to push it below a float; it inhibits margin collapsing ([10 §1](modules/10-css-essentials.md#1-the-box-model-and-formatting-contexts)).
- <a id="cleartranslations"></a>**clearTranslations**: the `@angular/localize` function that removes `$localize.translate` and empties the map ([34 §8](modules/34-i18n.md#8-runtime-translation-with-angularlocalize-loadtranslations-before-bootstrap))
- <a id="cli-angular"></a>**CLI (Angular)**: the command-line interface, the `ng` command ([12 §1](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#1-what-a-framework-does-and-angulars-philosophy)).
- <a id="client"></a>**client**: the application that asks for access on a user's behalf, here the Angular app ([42 §2](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#2-oauth-20-and-openid-connect-for-a-single-page-app-authorization-code-with-pkce-and-why-the-implicit-flow-is-gone))
- <a id="client-openfeature"></a>**client (OpenFeature)**: the object the application asks for a typed flag value with a default ([44 §8](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#8-feature-flags-with-openfeature-and-canary-releases-providers-evaluation-context-sticky-rollout-kill-switches-and-version-skew))
- <a id="client-hijacking"></a>**client hijacking**: injected script using the open page to call the API as the user, without stealing a token ([42 §3](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#3-where-the-oauth-client-runs-a-browser-only-client-a-token-mediating-backend-or-a-backend-for-frontend-with-spring))
- <a id="client-state"></a>**client state**: state the client owns, such as UI, form, URL and session state; everything except the server cache ([28 §1](modules/28-state-management.md#1-kinds-of-state-server-cache-ui-form-url-and-session-state-and-where-each-belongs))
- <a id="client-side-gating"></a>**client-side gating**: hiding a route, button or field in the browser so the page offers less; it shapes the user experience and does not limit what a request can do ([33 §10](modules/33-security.md#10-route-guards-are-ux-authorization-belongs-on-the-server))
- <a id="client-side-model"></a>**client-side model**: how the browser keeps entities in memory ([45 §4](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#4-component-architecture-data-model-and-data-flow-from-screens-to-a-component-tree-and-a-client-side-model))
- <a id="clock-scheduler"></a>**clock (scheduler)**: the `now()` a scheduler supplies, which is what virtual time replaces in tests ([23 §9](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#9-schedulers-queue-asap-async-and-animation-frame-observeon-and-subscribeon))
- <a id="clock-skew"></a>**clock skew**: the tolerated difference between the issuer's and the verifier's clocks when checking `exp` and `nbf`; 60 seconds in Spring Security ([42 §5](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#5-spring-security-as-a-resource-server-validating-jwts-and-turning-scopes-into-authorities))
- <a id="close-code"></a>**close code**: the status number a WebSocket Close frame carries, 1000 for a normal closure (RFC 6455 §7.4.1) ([43 §5](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#5-websockets-and-stomp-in-spring-the-handshake-frames-messagemapping-brokers-user-destinations-and-heart-beats))
- <a id="closed"></a>**closed**: the flag of a subscription that has been unsubscribed or ended; `Subscription.closed` ([22 §2](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#2-anatomy-observable-observer-subscription-and-teardown))
- <a id="closure"></a>**closure**: a function together with the environment it was created in, which keeps that environment's variables reachable ([02 §3](modules/02-js-scope-closures-this.md#3-closures)).
- <a id="cms-content-management-system"></a>**CMS (content management system)**: a system that stores and edits content, such as markup, outside the application's code ([12 §7](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#7-aot-versus-jit-and-template-type-checking)).
- <a id="code-generator"></a>**code generator**: a tool that turns an OpenAPI description into TypeScript models plus request code ([41 §3](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#3-openapi-with-springdoc-and-generated-typescript-clients-openapi-generator-orval-and-ng-openapi-gen))
- <a id="code-splitting"></a>**code splitting**: dividing the output into chunks loaded on demand ([12 §3](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#3-building-the-application-builder-output-layout-and-budgets)).
- <a id="code_challenge"></a>**code_challenge**: the hash of the verifier sent with the authorization request ([42 §2](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#2-oauth-20-and-openid-connect-for-a-single-page-app-authorization-code-with-pkce-and-why-the-implicit-flow-is-gone))
- <a id="code_verifier"></a>**code_verifier**: the random secret the page keeps and sends only to the token endpoint ([42 §2](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#2-oauth-20-and-openid-connect-for-a-single-page-app-authorization-code-with-pkce-and-why-the-implicit-flow-is-gone))
- <a id="codemod"></a>**codemod**: a program that rewrites source code mechanically ([18 §8](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#8-migrating-with-ng-generate-angularcorecontrol-flow)) · also in [19 §6](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#6-decorator-based-inputs-outputs-and-queries-and-the-migration-schematics), [39 §10](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#10-a-migration-program-for-an-old-angular-codebase-order-dependencies-and-checkpoints)
- <a id="coercion"></a>**coercion**: an implicit type conversion performed by an operator or a built-in ([01 §5](modules/01-js-values-types-coercion.md#5-coercion)).
- <a id="cold-source"></a>**cold (source)**: a source whose producer is created by each subscription, so it is always unicast ([22 §3](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#3-cold-and-hot-unicast-and-multicast))
- <a id="collation"></a>**collation**: a locale's rules for sorting text ([34 §1](modules/34-i18n.md#1-i18n-l10n-and-the-locale-language-tags-what-a-locale-decides-and-locale_id))
- <a id="collection-schematics"></a>**collection (schematics)**: a package's set of schematics ([12 §2](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#2-the-cli-and-the-workspace-angularjson-projects-schematics-and-ng-update)).
- <a id="color-scheme"></a>**color-scheme**: the property that declares which light or dark schemes an element supports and so changes browser-drawn UI ([10 §6](modules/10-css-essentials.md#6-custom-properties-theming-and-color-scheme)).
- <a id="column-definition"></a>**column definition**: a `cdkColumnDef` or `matColumnDef` that names a table column and holds its header and cell templates ([36 §7](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#7-tables-and-long-lists-cdktable-datasource-mattable-and-virtual-scrolling))
- <a id="combinelatest"></a>**combineLatest**: emits an array of the latest value of each source on every emission, once every source has emitted ([22 §7](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#7-combining-streams-combinelatest-withlatestfrom-forkjoin-zip-merge-and-concat))
- <a id="comment-line"></a>**comment line**: an event-stream line starting with `:`, which the parser ignores ([43 §2](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#2-server-sent-events-on-the-wire-the-textevent-stream-format-and-how-eventsource-reconnects))
- <a id="comment-node"></a>**comment node**: a DOM node that holds text and renders nothing; `nativeElement` is one on `<ng-template>` and `<ng-container>` ([14 §1](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#1-directives-and-attribute-directives-host-inputs-elementref-and-renderer2)).
- <a id="commit"></a>**commit**: the phase that writes the differences between renders to the DOM ([40 §3](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#3-reacts-model-re-rendering-reconciliation-memoization-and-the-react-compiler))
- <a id="commit-history"></a>**commit history**: the sequence of commits, which should tell the story in steps ([47 §5](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#5-take-home-assignments-scope-and-time-box-what-the-repository-must-show-the-readme-and-the-review-call))
- <a id="commit-to-a-decision"></a>**commit to a decision**: to build the chosen option well after arguing against it ([47 §2](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#2-four-front-end-stories-a-performance-win-a-migration-a-production-incident-and-a-disagreement-over-architecture))
- <a id="commitstyles"></a>**commitStyles()**: the `Animation` method that writes the animation's current values into the element's inline style ([35 §4](modules/35-animations.md#4-the-web-animations-api-elementanimate-the-animation-object-and-getanimations))
- <a id="commonjs"></a>**CommonJS**: Node's original module system: `require` runs a file once and returns its `module.exports` value ([05 §2](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#2-commonjs-interop-and-dynamic-import)). · also in [31 §6](modules/31-performance.md#6-bundle-analysis-budgets-and-tree-shaking-pitfalls)
- <a id="commonmodule"></a>**CommonModule**: the NgModule that bundles `NgIf`, `NgForOf`, `NgSwitch` and the common pipes, no longer needed for the blocks ([18 §8](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#8-migrating-with-ng-generate-angularcorecontrol-flow))
- <a id="comparator"></a>**comparator**: a function of two entries that decides their order, the optional argument of `keyvalue` ([14 §6](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#6-built-in-pipes-and-locale-data)).
- <a id="compatform"></a>**compatForm**: the function in `@angular/forms/signals/compat` that builds a form from a signal model that may contain `FormControl` and `FormGroup` instances, exposing them as fields ([26 §9](modules/26-signal-forms.md#9-interop-and-migration-compatform-signalformcontrol-and-what-changed-from-v21-to-v22)).
- <a id="compatibility-matrix"></a>**compatibility matrix**: a table of which client versions work with which API releases, needed when the two layers live in separate repositories ([44 §7](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#7-cicd-for-both-layers-one-image-promoted-through-stages-a-monorepo-or-separate-repositories-and-versioning-the-contract))
- <a id="compatibility-table"></a>**compatibility table**: angular.dev's "Version compatibility" page, listing the Node.js, TypeScript and RxJS each Angular minor requires ([39 §8](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#8-skipping-versions-safely-one-major-per-hop-node-and-typescript-ranges-and-libraries-that-block-a-hop))
- <a id="compatvalidationerror"></a>**CompatValidationError**: the error class that wraps a reactive control's error key and context as a signal-forms error of that `kind` ([26 §9](modules/26-signal-forms.md#9-interop-and-migration-compatform-signalformcontrol-and-what-changed-from-v21-to-v22)).
- <a id="compilation-scope-template-scope"></a>**compilation scope (template scope)**: the selectors and pipe names a component's template may use ([15 §1](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#1-what-ngmodules-did-compilation-scope-injector-and-lazy-loading-unit)).
- <a id="compile-time-versus-run-time"></a>**compile time versus run time**: what a build tool computes once (Sass variables, loops, math) versus what the browser resolves while the page runs (`var()`, `@media`) ([10 §7](modules/10-css-essentials.md#7-native-nesting-versus-sass)).
- <a id="compiler-angular"></a>**compiler (Angular)**: turns templates and decorators into JavaScript at build time ([12 §1](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#1-what-a-framework-does-and-angulars-philosophy)).
- <a id="compiler-abi"></a>**compiler ABI**: the internal runtime API (`ɵɵ`-prefixed names) that the compiler's generated code calls ([39 §3](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#3-how-angular-ships-semantic-versioning-the-release-cadence-support-windows-and-the-deprecation-policy))
- <a id="compiler-marker-function"></a>**compiler marker function**: a call in source that the compiler rewrites and that does not exist at runtime, such as `$safeNavigationMigration(…)` ([39 §11](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#11-the-v22-upgrade-what-ng-update-changes-and-why-each-migration-keeps-the-old-behavior))
- <a id="compiler-first-framework"></a>**compiler-first framework**: a framework that moves much of its work to build time ([40 §1](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#1-library-framework-progressive-framework-what-each-project-decides-for-you))
- <a id="complete-result--truncated-result"></a>**complete result / truncated result**: a server answer holding every match, or only the top ones that fit the limit ([46 §2](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#2-autocomplete-and-typeahead-search-a-request-policy-a-prefix-cache-ranking-on-the-server-and-an-accessible-combobox))
- <a id="component"></a>**component**: an Angular class plus a template and optional styles, bound to every element that matches its selector ([13 §1](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#1-component-anatomy-selector-template-styles-imports-and-the-v22-file-convention)).
- <a id="component-boundary"></a>**component boundary**: the edge of a child component's own view, which nested leave animations do not cross ([35 §6](modules/35-animations.md#6-function-callbacks-and-the-v22-leave-semantics-animationcallbackevent-animationcomplete-and-nested-leaves))
- <a id="component-dev-kit-cdk"></a>**Component Dev Kit (CDK)**: `@angular/cdk`, the behavior layer of `angular/components`: overlays, portals, drag and drop, tables, accessibility helpers and test harnesses, with no look ([36 §1](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#1-material-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-styled-components-behavior-primitives-and-headless-patterns))
- <a id="component-diagram"></a>**component diagram**: the diagram level that opens one container into its parts ([45 §2](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#2-communicating-under-time-pressure-a-time-plan-complete-trade-off-statements-and-diagrams-at-three-zoom-levels))
- <a id="component-effect--root-effect"></a>**component effect / root effect**: an effect tied to a component's change detection, versus one created outside the component tree that runs before components are checked ([17 §4](modules/17-signals.md#4-effects)).
- <a id="component-harness"></a>**component harness**: a class, written once per component, that exposes intent methods and hides the component's DOM from tests ([29 §11](modules/29-testing-angular.md#11-component-harnesses-and-angular-testing-library))
- <a id="component-input-binding"></a>**component input binding**: `withComponentInputBinding`: the router sets the routed component's inputs from query, params, data and resolved data, and `undefined` for a missing key ([24 §2](modules/24-routing.md#2-route-parameters-path-matrix-query-and-fragment-inheritance-and-component-input-binding))
- <a id="component-library"></a>**component library**: a package of ready-made UI components; `angular/components` releases three together: Angular Material, the CDK and Angular Aria ([36 §1](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#1-material-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-styled-components-behavior-primitives-and-headless-patterns))
- <a id="component-mirror-componentmirror"></a>**component mirror (ComponentMirror)**: the read-only view of a component's compiled metadata (selector, inputs, outputs, `ng-content` selectors, standalone flag) that the public `reflectComponentType` returns ([12, Exercise 12.1](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#ex12-1)).
- <a id="component-override"></a>**component override**: a component's `*-overrides` mixin, such as `mat.button-overrides((filled-container-color: …))`, which redefines that component's tokens for a subtree ([36 §4](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#4-customizing-the-theme-system-and-component-token-overrides-and-theming-your-own-components))
- <a id="component-reuse"></a>**component reuse**: the router keeps the same component instance when the next route has the same route config, so only observables and inputs see new params ([24 §2](modules/24-routing.md#2-route-parameters-path-matrix-query-and-fragment-inheritance-and-component-input-binding))
- <a id="component-state-react"></a>**component state (React)**: data a component owns through a hook like `useState` ([40 §3](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#3-reacts-model-re-rendering-reconciliation-memoization-and-the-react-compiler))
- <a id="component-style"></a>**component style**: CSS declared in a component's `styles`, `styleUrl` or a `<style>` element in its template; by default it reaches only that component's template ([13 §8](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#8-styling-strategies-component-styles-global-styles-css-variables-and-tailwind)).
- <a id="component-test"></a>**component test**: a test that renders one component's template in `TestBed` on a DOM such as jsdom ([29 §1](modules/29-testing-angular.md#1-what-to-test-where-unit-integration-component-and-end-to-end-tests-the-pyramid-and-the-trophy))
- <a id="component-token"></a>**component token**: a Material component's own custom property, such as `--mat-button-filled-container-color`, which falls back to a system variable when unset ([36 §3](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#3-theming-with-material-3-mattheme-system-variables-and-light-and-dark-with-color-scheme))
- <a id="component-tree"></a>**component tree**: a diagram of which component renders which ([45 §4](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#4-component-architecture-data-model-and-data-flow-from-screens-to-a-component-tree-and-a-client-side-model))
- <a id="componentless-route"></a>**componentless route**: a route with no `component` (or `loadComponent`), which passes its params and data to children under both strategies ([24 §2](modules/24-routing.md#2-route-parameters-path-matrix-query-and-fragment-inheritance-and-component-input-binding))
- <a id="componentmirror"></a>**ComponentMirror**: the public description of a component that `reflectComponentType` returns: selector, inputs, outputs and slot selectors ([19 §8](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#8-under-the-hood-the-compiled-definition-projection-slots-and-query-refresh))
- <a id="componentportal"></a>**ComponentPortal**: a portal that names a component to create at run time and attach to an outlet ([36 Q36.09](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#q36-09))
- <a id="componentstore-and-updater"></a>**ComponentStore** and **updater**: the RxJS-based local store of `@ngrx/component-store`, a class you extend (`setState`, `select`, `effect`), and a pure `(state, value) => state` function exposed as a method ([28 §8](modules/28-state-management.md#8-componentstore-and-signalstore-signalstate-signalstore-withstate-withcomputed-withmethods-and-protected-state))
- <a id="composedpath"></a>**composedPath()**: the event's propagation path as an array, starting at the real target; nodes inside closed shadow roots are left out ([08 §4](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#4-events-propagation-default-actions-passive-listeners-and-delegation)).
- <a id="composition"></a>**composition**: building behavior by holding collaborators ("has a") instead of inheriting ("is a") ([03 §4](modules/03-js-objects-prototypes-classes.md#4-inheritance-composition-and-mixins)). · also in [14 §3](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#3-host-directives-the-composition-api)
- <a id="compositor-layer"></a>**compositor layer**: a part of the page painted separately so the compositor can move or fade it without layout or paint ([08 §2](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#2-layout-paint-and-composite-what-each-change-costs)).
- <a id="compositor-only-property"></a>**compositor-only property**: `transform` or `opacity` on an element with its own layer, whose change skips layout and paint ([08 §2](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#2-layout-paint-and-composite-what-each-change-costs)). · also in [35 §11](modules/35-animations.md#11-performance-and-reduced-motion-compositor-properties-measuring-jank-and-prefers-reduced-motion)
- <a id="computation-cache"></a>**computation cache**: stored task results that Nx replays when the task's inputs hash the same ([38 §7](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#7-nx-monorepos-the-project-graph-tags-nxenforce-module-boundaries-affected-commands-and-caching))
- <a id="computed"></a>**computed**: a signal derived from others; `toSignal` returns one over its internal state signal ([23 §7](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#7-rxjs-and-signals-decision-rules-tosignal-toobservable-and-rxresource))
- <a id="computed-id"></a>**computed ID**: the ID a message gets when it has no `@@id`: a hash of its text and meaning, so any edit to either changes it ([34 §6](modules/34-i18n.md#6-extraction-and-translation-files-ng-extract-i18n-formats-and-message-ids))
- <a id="computed-signal"></a>**computed signal**: a read-only signal derived from others, lazy and memoized, with dependencies rebuilt on every run ([17 §3](modules/17-signals.md#3-computed-signals)).
- <a id="computed-value"></a>**computed value**: the specified value resolved (relative lengths made absolute), and the value transferred in inheritance ([10 §2](modules/10-css-essentials.md#2-the-cascade-origins-specificity-and-layers)).
- <a id="computed-value-time"></a>**computed-value time**: the step after the cascade where `var()` is substituted; a declaration that fails there is "invalid at computed-value time" and computes as `unset` ([10 §6](modules/10-css-essentials.md#6-custom-properties-theming-and-color-scheme)).
- <a id="concurrency-flattening"></a>**concurrency (flattening)**: the most inner Observables subscribed at once; `mergeMap` defaults to `Infinity`, `concatMap` is 1 ([23 §1](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#1-higher-order-observables-switchmap-mergemap-concatmap-and-exhaustmap))
- <a id="concurrency-merge"></a>**concurrency (merge)**: the most sources `merge` subscribes to at a time; later sources wait for a slot ([22 §7](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#7-combining-streams-combinelatest-withlatestfrom-forkjoin-zip-merge-and-concat))
- <a id="conditional-request"></a>**conditional request**: a request that carries a precondition, such as `If-Match` with the `ETag` the client read, so the server can refuse it with 412 (`ETag`: [09 §4](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#4-http-caching); [41 §5](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#5-error-contracts-with-problemdetail-rfc-9457-and-mapping-them-in-angular))
- <a id="conditional-step"></a>**conditional step**: a step that has a visibility rule ([46 §5](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#5-a-multi-step-form-wizard-with-draft-saving-a-step-model-per-step-validation-and-drafts-that-survive-and-migrate))
- <a id="conditional-type"></a>**conditional type**: `T extends U ? X : Y`, a type chosen by an assignability check ([07 §3](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#3-conditional-types-distributivity-infer-and-recursive-types)).
- <a id="conditional-write"></a>**conditional write**: a write that carries a precondition, such as `If-Match` with the entity tag the client read ([45 §8](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#8-under-the-hood-failure-modes-observability-offline-levels-and-consistency-choices))
- <a id="confidence"></a>**confidence**: how strongly a passing test predicts working software ([29 §1](modules/29-testing-angular.md#1-what-to-test-where-unit-integration-component-and-end-to-end-tests-the-pyramid-and-the-trophy))
- <a id="confidential-client"></a>**confidential client**: a client that can keep a secret, such as a Spring server ([42 §2](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#2-oauth-20-and-openid-connect-for-a-single-page-app-authorization-code-with-pkce-and-why-the-implicit-flow-is-gone))
- <a id="config-template"></a>**config template**: a file with `${NAME}` placeholders that a start-up step fills from the environment to produce the real file, such as `config.json` ([44 §5](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#5-runtime-configuration-without-rebuilding-configjson-environment-substitution-at-container-start-and-provideappinitializer))
- <a id="config-driven-form"></a>**config-driven form**: a dynamic form whose controls and fields are built from data, such as a server's JSON config ([25 §9](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#9-dynamic-forms-formarray-patterns-and-config-driven-forms))
- <a id="configuration-build"></a>**configuration (build)**: a named set of options that overrides a target's base options, selected with `-c` ([12 §2](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#2-the-cli-and-the-workspace-angularjson-projects-schematics-and-ng-update)).
- <a id="configuration-object"></a>**configuration object**: a token holding typed settings, with a factory default when one is safe ([16 §9](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#9-di-patterns-testing-and-the-spring-container)).
- <a id="conflation"></a>**conflation**: merging a burst of updates into one write per window, so the array copy and the signal write run once per window ([43 §8](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#8-from-streams-to-signals-one-shared-connection-tosignal-and-rxresource-bursts-and-connection-state))
- <a id="conformance-level"></a>**conformance level**: A, AA or AAA, each including the levels below it ([11 §1](modules/11-accessibility.md#1-why-accessibility-and-what-the-standards-require)).
- <a id="confounder"></a>**confounder**: another change that could explain the same result ([47 §7](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#7-under-the-hood-how-one-story-is-drilled-into-numbers-you-can-defend-and-telling-a-design-or-a-migration-as-your-own-project))
- <a id="connect"></a>**connect**: an operator that subscribes the source once into a Subject and lets a selector build several branches over it inside one subscription ([23 §5](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#5-multicasting-in-depth-share-configuration-sharereplay-and-connect))
- <a id="connected-drop-lists"></a>**connected drop lists**: drop lists that accept each other's items, through a `cdkDropListGroup` around them or `cdkDropListConnectedTo` naming the others ([Exercise 36.3](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#ex36-3))
- <a id="connected-lists"></a>**connected lists**: drop lists that can exchange items, joined with `cdkDropListConnectedTo` or by sitting in one drop list group ([36 §6](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#6-drag-and-drop-sorting-transferring-between-lists-and-the-keyboard-gap))
- <a id="connection-service"></a>**connection service**: the one service that owns the socket and shares it, as one Observable and a state signal, with every reader ([43 §8](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#8-from-streams-to-signals-one-shared-connection-tosignal-and-rxresource-bursts-and-connection-state))
- <a id="connection-state-signal"></a>**connection-state signal**: a signal holding `connecting`, `open`, `reconnecting` or `closed`, for a banner that says whether the data is live ([43 §8](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#8-from-streams-to-signals-one-shared-connection-tosignal-and-rxresource-bursts-and-connection-state))
- <a id="connector"></a>**connector**: the `share`/`connect` option that creates the Subject between the source and the subscribers (`shareReplay` uses a `ReplaySubject`) ([23 §5](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#5-multicasting-in-depth-share-configuration-sharereplay-and-connect))
- <a id="const-assertion-as-const"></a>**const assertion (as const)**: makes a literal expression keep its literal types and become deeply `readonly` ([06 §6](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#6-enums-literal-unions-as-const-and-satisfies)).
- <a id="const-type-parameter"></a>**const type parameter**: a type parameter declared `<const T>`, inferred as if the argument were written `as const` ([07 §1](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#1-generics-constraints-defaults-inference-and-const-type-parameters)).
- <a id="constraint"></a>**constraint**: an `extends` bound on a type parameter that a type argument must satisfy (`T extends { length: number }`) ([07 §1](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#1-generics-constraints-defaults-inference-and-const-type-parameters)).
- <a id="constraint-design"></a>**constraint (design)**: a limit fixed from outside the design, such as team, existing API or deadline ([45 §3](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#3-requirements-first-functional-and-non-functional-requirements-constraints-and-back-of-envelope-numbers))
- <a id="constraint-annotation"></a>**constraint annotation**: a Bean Validation annotation, such as `@NotBlank`, `@Size` or `@Email`, that states one rule on a field or parameter ([41 §7](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#7-validation-on-both-layers-bean-validation-angular-validators-and-server-errors-on-form-fields))
- <a id="constraint-of-the-time"></a>**constraint of the time**: a version, tool, deadline or team size that shaped old code ([47 §3](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#3-legacy-codebases-and-migrations-without-blame-the-constraints-of-the-time-how-you-learned-the-code-before-changing-it-and-words-that-explain-instead-of-accuse))
- <a id="constraint-validation"></a>**constraint validation**: the platform's built-in form checking (`required`, `validity`, `setCustomValidity`, the `invalid` event) ([11 §6](modules/11-accessibility.md#6-accessible-names-descriptions-and-forms)).
- <a id="constructor-of-a-component"></a>**constructor (of a component)**: plain class construction; the injection context is open and inputs still hold their defaults ([20 §2](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#2-constructor-ngoninit-and-inputs-what-exists-when))
- <a id="consts"></a>**consts**: the attribute arrays a component definition's elements refer to by index ([12 §8](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it)).
- <a id="consts-function"></a>**consts function**: the function in a component's compiled definition that builds its template constants, `$localize` messages included; the runtime calls it once per component type ([34 §12](modules/34-i18n.md#12-under-the-hood-from-an-i18n-attribute-to-translated-dom))
- <a id="consumer-driven-contract"></a>**consumer-driven contract**: a contract the consumer's tests write down (requests made, responses needed) and the provider verifies in its own build ([30 §5](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#5-api-mocking-at-the-network-layer-and-where-contract-tests-take-over))
- <a id="consumerobserver-internal"></a>**ConsumerObserver (internal)**: the wrapper inside `SafeSubscriber` that catches a consumer handler's throw and reports it later ([22 §9](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#9-under-the-hood-subscribe-the-subscriber-chain-and-the-subjects-observer-list))
- <a id="container"></a>**container**: a running instance of an image ([44 §2](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#2-a-docker-multi-stage-build-for-angular-build-and-runtime-stages-layer-caching-dockerignore-and-a-non-root-image))
- <a id="container-component"></a>**container (component)**: a component that reads state and passes it down; a presentational component takes inputs and emits outputs ([45 §4](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#4-component-architecture-data-model-and-data-flow-from-screens-to-a-component-tree-and-a-client-side-model))
- <a id="container-component-1"></a>**container component**: a component that injects the facade, passes its signals down and turns outputs into facade calls ([38 §2](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#2-smart-container-and-presentational-components))
- <a id="container-query"></a>**container query**: an `@container` condition on the size of the nearest ancestor with `container-type`, so a component responds to its own space ([10 §5](modules/10-css-essentials.md#5-responsive-css-media-queries-container-queries-and-fluid-type)).
- <a id="containing-block"></a>**containing block**: the rectangle a box's percentage sizes and offsets resolve against, for ordinary boxes the content edge of the nearest block-container ancestor ([10 §1](modules/10-css-essentials.md#1-the-box-model-and-formatting-contexts)).
- <a id="content-projected"></a>**content (projected)**: the markup a parent writes between a component's tags and the component shows through `<ng-content>`; owner module 19 ([16 §6](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#6-providers-versus-viewproviders-and-per-instance-state)).
- <a id="content-projection"></a>**content projection**: the markup a caller writes between a component's tags, shown where the component places `<ng-content>` ([19 §3](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#3-content-projection-ng-content-slots-ngprojectas-fallback-content-and-the-lifecycle-trap))
- <a id="content-query"></a>**content query**: a query over the content the caller projected between the component's tags ([19 §5](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#5-queries-view-and-content-queries-with-signal-queries))
- <a id="content-disposition"></a>**Content-Disposition**: the header that tells the recipient to show a body inline or save it as an attachment, and under which file name (RFC 6266) ([41 §8](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#8-files-multipart-and-chunked-uploads-progress-and-downloads-with-content-disposition-and-streaming))
- <a id="content-range"></a>**Content-Range**: the header that names the byte range of a body and the complete length, such as `bytes 0-4/12` (RFC 9110 §14.4) ([41 §8](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#8-files-multipart-and-chunked-uploads-progress-and-downloads-with-content-disposition-and-streaming))
- <a id="content-visibility"></a>**content-visibility**: the CSS property that lets the browser skip rendering work for off-screen elements while keeping their nodes ([31 §4](modules/31-performance.md#4-long-lists-track-virtual-scrolling-with-the-cdk-and-the-alternatives))
- <a id="context-of-a-template"></a>**context (of a template)**: the object a renderer supplies to a template; `$implicit` feeds a bare `let-x` and other properties feed `let-x="name"` ([19 §4](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#4-template-driven-apis-ng-template-ngtemplateoutlet-and-typed-context))
- <a id="context-react"></a>**context (React)**: a value a provider hands to the descendants that read it, without props ([40 §3](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#3-reacts-model-re-rendering-reconciliation-memoization-and-the-react-compiler))
- <a id="context-manager"></a>**context manager**: the OpenTelemetry component that holds the active span for code running now ([44 §10](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#10-under-the-hood-opentelemetry-for-the-web-traceparent-and-one-click-traced-to-a-spring-boot-span-and-log-line))
- <a id="context-variable"></a>**context variable**: a name such as `$index` or `$first` that a `@for` row provides, aliased with `let` ([18 §2](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#2-for-track-context-variables-empty-and-what-angular-can-iterate))
- <a id="continuous-delivery"></a>**continuous delivery**: keeping every change that passes the pipeline ready to deploy, by extending the pipeline's stages to the deployment itself ([44 §7](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#7-cicd-for-both-layers-one-image-promoted-through-stages-a-monorepo-or-separate-repositories-and-versioning-the-contract))
- <a id="continuous-integration-ci"></a>**continuous integration (CI)**: a server that runs the same checks on every change ([38 §11](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#11-a-ci-pipeline-for-a-front-end-lint-test-build-end-to-end-and-deploy-previews))
- <a id="contract"></a>**contract**: the JSON shape both sides agree on, not either side's class ([41 §2](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#2-dto-contracts-what-crosses-the-wire-absent-versus-null-and-checking-responses-at-the-boundary))
- <a id="contract-of-a-case"></a>**contract (of a case)**: the calls and payloads between browser and server ([46 §1](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#1-from-method-to-case-the-parts-every-case-study-carries-and-where-each-cases-difficulty-lies))
- <a id="contract-of-a-component"></a>**contract (of a component)**: the inputs and outputs a component declares, which the compiler checks every parent against ([19 §1](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#1-inputs-outputs-and-two-way-binding-the-parent-and-child-contract))
- <a id="contract-gate"></a>**contract gate**: the pipeline step that diffs the committed API description against the previous one and fails the pull request on a breaking change ([44 §7](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#7-cicd-for-both-layers-one-image-promoted-through-stages-a-monorepo-or-separate-repositories-and-versioning-the-contract))
- <a id="contract-test"></a>**contract test**: a test that checks both consumer and provider against one shared description of their messages ([30 §5](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#5-api-mocking-at-the-network-layer-and-where-contract-tests-take-over))
- <a id="control-flow-block"></a>**control flow block**: template syntax (`@if`, `@for`, `@switch`, `@defer`) that the compiler understands, in place of a directive ([18 §1](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#1-built-in-blocks-versus-directives-and-the-if-family-with-as))
- <a id="control-flow-analysis"></a>**control-flow analysis**: following branches, joins and assignments to compute each variable's type at each point in the code ([06 §3](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#3-narrowing-and-control-flow-analysis)).
- <a id="controlcontainer"></a>**ControlContainer**: the abstract parent token that `NgForm` and `NgModelGroup` provide and `NgModel` injects with `@Host()` ([25 §4](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#4-template-driven-forms-in-depth-ngmodel-ngform-and-asynchronous-registration))
- <a id="controlvalueaccessor-cva"></a>**ControlValueAccessor (CVA)**: the adapter between a control and a view: the form directive calls `writeValue` (model to view) and `setDisabledState`, and gives it the `registerOnChange` and `registerOnTouched` callbacks (view to model) ([25 §10](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#10-controlvalueaccessor-and-the-validator-interface-custom-controls-under-the-hood))
- <a id="conventional-commits"></a>**conventional commits**: commit messages shaped `<type>[optional scope]: <description>` ([38 §9](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#9-linting-and-formatting-angular-eslint-prettier-import-ordering-and-commit-hooks))
- <a id="conversion-at-the-edge"></a>**conversion at the edge**: turning a stream into state once, where it enters the signal graph (`toSignal`, `rxResource`), and back into a stream only when time or cancellation is needed ([23 §7](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#7-rxjs-and-signals-decision-rules-tosignal-toobservable-and-rxresource))
- <a id="cookie-to-header-token"></a>**cookie-to-header token**: a defense in which the server sets a script-readable cookie and the client copies it into a header the server compares ([27 §8](modules/27-http-client.md#8-xsrf-protection-how-httpclient-reads-the-cookie-and-sets-the-header-and-when-it-does-not))
- <a id="cookiecsrftokenrepository"></a>**CookieCsrfTokenRepository**: Spring Security's store for the expected CSRF token in an `XSRF-TOKEN` cookie, read back from `X-XSRF-TOKEN` ([42 §6](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#6-csrf-with-cookiecsrftokenrepository-and-angulars-xsrf-support-csrfspa-in-spring-security-7))
- <a id="core-folder"></a>**core (folder)**: app-wide singletons and setup, such as providers, interceptors and the layout shell, configured once ([38 §1](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#1-folder-structures-that-scale-by-feature-domain-slices-and-core-shared-and-feature-boundaries))
- <a id="core-web-vitals-lcp-inp-cls"></a>**Core Web Vitals (LCP, INP, CLS)**: loading, responsiveness and visual stability metrics, each judged at the 75th percentile of real page loads ([08 §6](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#6-core-web-vitals-lcp-inp-and-cls)).
- <a id="coremodule"></a>**CoreModule**: an NgModule holding app-wide singletons, imported only by the root module and guarded against a second import ([15 §5](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#5-legacy-ngmodule-patterns-declarations-exports-forrootforchild-sharedmodule-and-coremodule)).
- <a id="correlation-id"></a>**correlation ID**: an ID such as `X-Request-ID` that names one request across logs, not the tree of spans a trace ID joins ([Q44.19](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#q44-19))
- <a id="cors"></a>**CORS**: the browser's cross-origin permission check, which module scripts always go through (taught in module 09) ([05 §1](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#1-es-modules-static-structure-linking-and-live-bindings)). · also in [09 §5](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#5-the-same-origin-policy-and-cors)
- <a id="cors-safelisted-header"></a>**CORS-safelisted header**: one of the seven response headers (such as `Content-Type`) that cross-origin script may read without being listed ([41 §1](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#1-cors-on-both-sides-springs-corsconfigurationsource-spring-security-preflight-caching-credentials-and-the-dev-proxy))
- <a id="corsconfigurationsource"></a>**CorsConfigurationSource**: Spring's object that maps URL patterns to `CorsConfiguration` rules; Spring MVC and Spring Security read the same one ([41 §1](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#1-cors-on-both-sides-springs-corsconfigurationsource-spring-security-preflight-caching-credentials-and-the-dev-proxy))
- <a id="counter-widget-kind"></a>**counter (widget kind)**: a widget kind whose window value is the sum of its events ([46 §6](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#6-a-real-time-operations-dashboard-aggregation-per-widget-staleness-hidden-tabs-and-screens-left-open-for-days))
- <a id="counting-normalizer"></a>**counting normalizer**: a normalizer that adds one to a counter per call, so a test counts work without timers ([14 Exercise 14.4](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#ex14-4)).
- <a id="counting-source"></a>**counting source**: a test source that records how many times it was subscribed to and unsubscribed from, so a test counts work without timers ([14 Exercise 14.5](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#ex14-5)).
- <a id="covariance--contravariance"></a>**covariance / contravariance**: a generic type is covariant in `T` when `F<Dog>` fits where `F<Animal>` is expected (outputs), contravariant when the reverse holds (inputs) ([07 §5](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#5-variance-parameter-bivariance-and-strictfunctiontypes)).
- <a id="coverage"></a>**coverage**: a report of which source lines the tests executed ([29 §2](modules/29-testing-angular.md#2-the-runner-vitest-through-angularbuildunit-test-jsdom-and-browser-mode))
- <a id="crawler"></a>**crawler**: a program that fetches pages to index them ([32 §1](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#1-why-render-on-the-server-csr-ssr-ssg-and-hybrid-rendering-and-what-each-costs))
- <a id="create-pass-i18n"></a>**create pass (i18n)**: the first run of a view's template, which builds its nodes and, for a type's first view, parses each message into opcodes ([34 §12](modules/34-i18n.md#12-under-the-hood-from-an-i18n-attribute-to-translated-dom))
- <a id="create-pass-and-update-pass"></a>**create pass and update pass**: the template function's first run, which builds a view's nodes, and later runs, which compare bindings and write the changed ones ([12 §8](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it)).
- <a id="createenvironmentinjector"></a>**createEnvironmentInjector**: the function that builds an environment injector at run time from providers and a required parent; `destroy()` ends it ([16 §8](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#8-lifetime-and-dynamic-injectors-destroyref-environmentinjector-injectorcreate-forward-references-and-cycles)).
- <a id="creation-function"></a>**creation function**: a function that returns an Observable, such as `of` or `interval` ([22 §5](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#5-pipeable-operators-and-writing-your-own))
- <a id="credentialed-request"></a>**credentialed request**: a cross-origin request sent with cookies or HTTP authentication (`credentials: 'include'`), for which the response needs an explicit origin and `Access-Control-Allow-Credentials: true` ([09 §5](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#5-the-same-origin-policy-and-cors)).
- <a id="critical-css"></a>**critical CSS**: the CSS the first paint needs, kept small so the rest can load without blocking rendering ([08 §1](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#1-the-critical-rendering-path-from-bytes-to-pixels)).
- <a id="critical-path"></a>**critical path**: the work the browser must finish before the first view appears ([31 §5](modules/31-performance.md#5-loading-the-initial-bundle-lazy-routes-defer-and-preloading-as-one-plan))
- <a id="critical-rendering-path"></a>**critical rendering path**: the steps from HTML, CSS and scripts to the first pixels: DOM, CSSOM, render tree, layout, paint, composite ([08 §1](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#1-the-critical-rendering-path-from-bytes-to-pixels)).
- <a id="cross-cutting-concern"></a>**cross-cutting concern**: work every request needs whatever the service, such as routing, TLS, rate limiting or request IDs ([44 §1](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#1-where-the-api-calls-go-direct-calls-a-same-origin-reverse-proxy-an-api-gateway-or-a-backend-for-frontend))
- <a id="cross-field-validator"></a>**cross-field validator**: a validator placed on a parent group that reads several children and puts its error on the group, not on a child ([25 §5](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#5-validators-built-in-custom-and-cross-field))
- <a id="cross-realm"></a>**cross-realm**: created by another global environment (an iframe, a worker, a Node `vm` context), so its prototypes differ from yours ([05 §6](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#6-errors-subclasses-causes-aggregation-and-global-handlers)).
- <a id="cross-site-websocket-hijacking"></a>**cross-site WebSocket hijacking**: another site's page opening a WebSocket to your server that carries the user's cookies, possible because browsers apply no same-origin policy to WebSocket ([43 §7](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#7-authentication-on-the-handshake-cookies-tokens-in-the-connect-frame-origin-checks-and-csrf-in-spring-security))
- <a id="cross-tab-sync"></a>**cross-tab sync**: telling the app's other tabs about a change, for example with `BroadcastChannel` ([45 §8](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#8-under-the-hood-failure-modes-observability-offline-levels-and-consistency-choices))
- <a id="csp"></a>**CSP**: Content Security Policy, a response header (or restricted `<meta>`) that tells the browser which scripts, styles and connections the page may use ([09 §8](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#8-content-security-policy-and-trusted-types)).
- <a id="csp_nonce"></a>**CSP_NONCE**: the `@angular/core` injection token for the nonce Angular puts on the `<style>` elements it adds; by default it reads `ngCspNonce` ([33 §5](modules/33-security.md#5-content-security-policy-with-angular-nonces-csp_nonce-and-autocsp))
- <a id="csr-client-side-rendering"></a>**CSR (client-side rendering)**: the server sends a nearly empty shell and the browser builds the page by running JavaScript ([31 §10](modules/31-performance.md#10-rendering-strategy-as-a-performance-decision-csr-ssr-and-ssg)) · also in [32 §1](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#1-why-render-on-the-server-csr-ssr-ssg-and-hybrid-rendering-and-what-each-costs)
- <a id="csrf"></a>**CSRF**: cross-site request forgery, making a victim's browser send an authenticated request; `SameSite` is defense in depth, the SPA treatment is in 33 and 42 ([09 §6](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#6-cookies)).
- <a id="csrf-cross-site-request-forgery"></a>**CSRF (cross-site request forgery)**: another site making the user's browser send a state-changing request to your site, with your cookies attached ([33 §9](modules/33-security.md#9-csrf-in-single-page-apps-when-cookies-make-you-vulnerable-samesite-and-angulars-xsrf-support))
- <a id="csrf-token"></a>**CSRF token**: a secret the server expects back in a header or form field, which a forging site cannot read ([42 §6](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#6-csrf-with-cookiecsrftokenrepository-and-angulars-xsrf-support-csrfspa-in-spring-security-7))
- <a id="csrf-token-on-connect"></a>**CSRF token on CONNECT**: the token Spring Security requires in a `CONNECT` frame header by default, compared with the one copied from the handshake request; it must be the masked value ([43 §7](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#7-authentication-on-the-handshake-cookies-tokens-in-the-connect-frame-origin-checks-and-csrf-in-spring-security))
- <a id="csrfspa"></a>**csrf.spa()**: Spring Security 7's CSRF setting for single-page apps: a script-readable cookie and a resolver that accepts the raw header value ([42 §6](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#6-csrf-with-cookiecsrftokenrepository-and-angulars-xsrf-support-csrfspa-in-spring-security-7))
- <a id="css-custom-property"></a>**CSS custom property**: a `--name` value that inherits down the DOM and is read with `var()`, so it crosses component boundaries ([13 §8](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#8-styling-strategies-component-styles-global-styles-css-variables-and-tailwind); owner [10 §6](modules/10-css-essentials.md#6-custom-properties-theming-and-color-scheme)).
- <a id="css-keyframe-animation"></a>**CSS keyframe animation**: a named `@keyframes` sequence the browser runs while the `animation` property applies to an element ([35 §1](modules/35-animations.md#1-motion-in-a-ui-what-to-animate-the-browsers-three-animation-mechanisms-and-where-angular-fits))
- <a id="css-transition"></a>**CSS transition**: the browser animating a property from its old computed value to its new one when the value changes ([35 §1](modules/35-animations.md#1-motion-in-a-ui-what-to-animate-the-browsers-three-animation-mechanisms-and-where-angular-fits))
- <a id="cssanimation"></a>**CSSAnimation**: the `Animation` subclass the browser creates for each running CSS animation; its `animationName` names the `@keyframes` rule ([35 §3](modules/35-animations.md#3-css-keyframe-animations-keyframes-fill-modes-iteration-and-component-scoped-names))
- <a id="cssom"></a>**CSSOM**: the CSS Object Model, the parsed style rules the browser combines with the DOM to build the render tree ([08 §1](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#1-the-critical-rendering-path-from-bytes-to-pixels)).
- <a id="csstransition"></a>**CSSTransition**: the `Animation` subclass the browser creates for each transitioning property; its `transitionProperty` names it ([35 §2](modules/35-animations.md#2-css-transitions-triggers-interruption-events-and-entry-with-starting-style))
- <a id="currentnavigation"></a>**currentNavigation**: the `Router` signal holding the running `Navigation`, or `null` when idle ([24 §7](modules/24-routing.md#7-the-navigation-lifecycle-events-cancellation-and-the-transition-pipeline))
- <a id="currentobservers-internal"></a>**currentObservers (internal)**: the copy of a Subject's observer list that `next` iterates ([22 §4](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#4-subjects-subject-behaviorsubject-replaysubject-and-asyncsubject))
- <a id="currentroles"></a>**CurrentRoles**: the exercise's small injectable that holds a signal of the user's roles, a stand-in for a permission backend ([14 Exercise 14.2](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#ex14-2)).
- <a id="currying"></a>**currying**: turning an n-argument function into a chain of one-argument calls ([02 §6](modules/02-js-scope-closures-this.md#6-higher-order-functions-currying-partial-application-debounce-and-throttle)).
- <a id="cursor"></a>**cursor**: a marker for the position after the last item of a page ([45 §5](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#5-state-and-api-design-where-each-piece-of-state-lives-and-the-api-the-screens-need))
- <a id="cursor-keyset-pagination"></a>**cursor (keyset) pagination**: asking for the items after a position taken from the last item seen; stable under inserts, but no page jumps and usually no total ([41 §6](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#6-pagination-sorting-and-filtering-offset-versus-cursor-and-how-spring-data-serializes-a-page))
- <a id="cursor-page"></a>**cursor page**: a page that continues after the last item seen, so an insert cannot shift it ([46 §3](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#3-an-infinite-scroll-feed-cursor-pages-deduplication-new-items-without-a-jump-and-a-bounded-window))
- <a id="custom-change-detection"></a>**custom change detection**: a component's own comparison in `ngDoCheck`, run when its declaring view is checked ([20 §3](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#3-which-hooks-still-matter-with-signals))
- <a id="custom-control"></a>**custom control**: a component with one required `model()` (`value` or `checked`) that `[formField]` binds directly ([26 §8](modules/26-signal-forms.md#8-custom-controls-formvaluecontrol-formcheckboxcontrol-and-how-formfield-picks-a-control)).
- <a id="custom-element"></a>**custom element**: a class registered with `customElements.define` for a hyphenated tag name; elements already in the page are upgraded ([08 §5](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#5-web-components-custom-elements-shadow-dom-and-templates)). · also in [37 §1](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#1-angular-elements-turning-a-component-into-a-custom-element)
- <a id="custom-feature-signalstorefeature-and-type"></a>**custom feature**, **signalStoreFeature** and **type<>()**: a reusable bundle of store features, the function that builds it, and the compile-time declaration of members the host store must have ([28 §9](modules/28-state-management.md#9-signalstore-in-depth-rxmethod-withentities-custom-features-and-the-events-plugin))
- <a id="custom-id"></a>**custom ID**: an ID written after `@@` in a message's metadata that replaces the computed ID ([34 §4](modules/34-i18n.md#4-marking-text-for-translation-i18n-attributes-metadata-ids-and-localize-in-code))
- <a id="custom-property"></a>**custom property**: a `--name` value declared on an element, inherited by descendants and read with `var()` ([10 §6](modules/10-css-essentials.md#6-custom-properties-theming-and-color-scheme)).
- <a id="custom_elements_schema"></a>**CUSTOM_ELEMENTS_SCHEMA**: a compiler setting letting a template use dash-named non-Angular elements and properties ([37 §2](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#2-the-elements-public-contract-attributes-properties-events-projected-content-and-typings))
- <a id="customevent"></a>**CustomEvent**: the browser's event class for application events, with its payload in `detail` ([37 §2](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#2-the-elements-public-contract-attributes-properties-events-projected-content-and-typings))
- <a id="cut-over"></a>**cut-over**: the moment all traffic moves from the old system to the new one ([39 §9](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#9-from-angularjs-to-angular-ngupgrade-hybrids-the-strangler-fig-and-rewrites))
- <a id="cypress"></a>**Cypress**: an end-to-end test runner whose test code runs inside the browser, beside the app ([30 §2](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#2-playwright-and-cypress-architecture-ng-e2e-and-the-end-of-protractor))

## D

- <a id="data-buffer"></a>**data buffer**: the text an event's `data` lines build, each value followed by an LF; the final LF is removed at dispatch ([43 §2](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#2-server-sent-events-on-the-wire-the-textevent-stream-format-and-how-eventsource-reconnects))
- <a id="data-grid"></a>**data grid**: the APG's grid pattern used for tabular data, with one tab stop and arrow-key movement inside it ([46 §4](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#4-a-large-data-grid-server-side-filtering-sorting-and-pagination-the-query-in-the-url-and-keyset-cursors))
- <a id="data-group"></a>**data group**: a set of API URLs the worker caches with a `performance` or `freshness` strategy, outside the app's versions ([37 §5](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#5-caching-with-asset-and-data-groups-navigation-requests-and-the-app-shell))
- <a id="data-property--accessor-property"></a>**data property / accessor property**: a property that stores a value, versus one that runs a getter and setter ([03 §1](modules/03-js-objects-prototypes-classes.md#1-properties-and-descriptors)).
- <a id="data-access-layer"></a>**data-access layer**: the part of a feature that knows where data comes from: HTTP services, the store and DTO mapping ([38 §3](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#3-facades-and-the-data-access-layer))
- <a id="data-fetching-library"></a>**data-fetching library**: a library that loads and caches server data for components, such as `@tanstack/react-query` ([40 §6](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#6-routing-forms-data-fetching-tooling-and-testing-first-party-or-ecosystem))
- <a id="datasource-cdk"></a>**DataSource (CDK)**: an object whose `connect` returns an `Observable` of rows for a table and whose `disconnect` cleans up when the table switches source or is destroyed ([36 §7](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#7-tables-and-long-lists-cdktable-datasource-mattable-and-virtual-scrolling))
- <a id="de-duplication-host-directives"></a>**de-duplication (host directives)**: keeping one instance of a directive reached more than once; a template match wins and repeated host-directive matches merge [Changed in v22.0] ([14 §3](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#3-host-directives-the-composition-api)).
- <a id="de-duplication-reports"></a>**de-duplication (reports)**: sending the same error once inside a time window ([37 §11](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#11-logging-and-error-tracking-a-reporter-port-context-privacy-source-maps-and-sampling))
- <a id="de-duplication-window"></a>**de-duplication window**: the time during which a repeat of the same error from the same widget counts as the one already reported ([37 Exercise 37.3](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#ex37-3))
- <a id="dead-code-elimination"></a>**dead-code elimination**: the minifier deleting code that can never run ([12 §4](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#4-environments-and-configuration-build-time-versus-run-time)).
- <a id="debounce"></a>**debounce**: run only after calls stop for a given wait ([02 §6](modules/02-js-scope-closures-this.md#6-higher-order-functions-currying-partial-application-debounce-and-throttle)). · also in [22 §8](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#8-timing-operators-debouncetime-throttletime-audittime-and-sampletime)
- <a id="debounce-inside-a-validator"></a>**debounce inside a validator**: a `timer(ms)` before the request in an async validator, so a run cancelled by the next value never reaches the server ([25 §6](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#6-async-validators-pending-status-cancellation-and-debouncing))
- <a id="debounce-rule"></a>**debounce() rule**: delays model writes from the UI by milliseconds, until blur or by a custom `Debouncer`; touching the field flushes it ([26 §6](modules/26-signal-forms.md#6-async-validation-and-debounce-validateasync-validatehttp-pending-and-cancellation)).
- <a id="debouncer"></a>**Debouncer**: a function `(context, abortSignal)` returning a promise to hold a UI write, or nothing to apply it at once ([26 §6](modules/26-signal-forms.md#6-async-validation-and-debounce-validateasync-validatehttp-pending-and-cancellation)).
- <a id="debug-id"></a>**debug ID**: an ID inserted into a minified file and its source map so a tracker can match them without a public URL ([44 §9](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#9-frontend-error-tracking-and-real-user-monitoring-releases-source-maps-beacons-and-sampling))
- <a id="debugelement"></a>**DebugElement**: Angular's wrapper around a rendered node, with `query`, `queryAll`, `listeners` and `triggerEventHandler` ([29 §6](modules/29-testing-angular.md#6-component-tests-dom-queries-componentfixture-signal-inputs-with-setinput-and-bindings-outputs-and-onpush))
- <a id="decimal-string"></a>**decimal string**: an amount written as text, such as `"19.90"`, so that no JSON number turns it into a double ([41 §9](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#9-dates-time-zones-decimals-and-money-across-the-wire))
- <a id="decision-framework-and-traceability"></a>**decision framework** and **traceability**: a fixed list of questions that picks a state tool from facts about the state, and a record of which change produced which state ([28 §11](modules/28-state-management.md#11-alternatives-and-the-decision-framework-ngxs-elf-tanstack-query-and-when-a-store-library-is-justified))
- <a id="decision-record"></a>**decision record**: a written record of a choice's gates, weights, sourced scores and deciding assumption ([40 §8](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#8-choosing-a-framework-balanced-trade-offs-and-how-to-answer-why-angular))
- <a id="declarable"></a>**declarable**: a component, directive or pipe, the kinds of class an NgModule can declare ([15 §1](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#1-what-ngmodules-did-compilation-scope-injector-and-lazy-loading-unit)).
- <a id="declaration"></a>**declaration**: one `property: value` pair ([10 §2](modules/10-css-essentials.md#2-the-cascade-origins-specificity-and-layers)).
- <a id="declaration-file-dts"></a>**declaration file (.d.ts)**: types for code whose implementation lives elsewhere; contains no run-time code ([06 §8](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#8-declaration-files-types-and-augmentation)).
- <a id="declaration-view-of-a-block"></a>**declaration view (of a block)**: the view whose template declared a block; the block reads `@let` values from it ([18 §4](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#4-let-inside-blocks-scope-reactivity-and-when-it-helps))
- <a id="declarations"></a>**declarations**: the NgModule metadata listing the declarables that belong to the module ([15 §1](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#1-what-ngmodules-did-compilation-scope-injector-and-lazy-loading-unit)).
- <a id="declarative-shadow-dom"></a>**declarative shadow DOM**: a `<template shadowrootmode>` that the HTML parser turns into a shadow root, for server-rendered components ([08 §5](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#5-web-components-custom-elements-shadow-dom-and-templates)).
- <a id="declaring-template"></a>**declaring template**: the template in which a piece of content is written, which creates its nodes and owns them for change detection ([19 §3](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#3-content-projection-ng-content-slots-ngprojectas-fallback-content-and-the-lifecycle-trap))
- <a id="declaring-view"></a>**declaring view**: the template that contains a component's tag; a component's hooks run when that view is checked, not its own ([20 §1](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#1-the-lifecycle-map-phases-and-the-order-of-the-hooks))
- <a id="decls-and-vars"></a>**decls and vars**: in a component definition, the number of slots the create pass fills and the number of binding slots after them ([12 §8](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it)).
- <a id="decorator-angular"></a>**decorator (Angular)**: a function applied to a class member with `@` that registers it as an input, output or query ([19 §6](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#6-decorator-based-inputs-outputs-and-queries-and-the-migration-schematics))
- <a id="decorator-storybook"></a>**decorator (Storybook)**: a wrapper around a rendered story, not the TypeScript or Angular decorator syntax ([38 §10](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#10-storybook-for-component-development))
- <a id="deduplication"></a>**deduplication**: keeping one item per id when pages and live pushes overlap ([46 §3](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#3-an-infinite-scroll-feed-cursor-pages-deduplication-new-items-without-a-jump-and-a-bounded-window))
- <a id="deep-dive"></a>**deep dive**: one step explored far past its planned share ([45 §2](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#2-communicating-under-time-pressure-a-time-plan-complete-trade-off-statements-and-diagrams-at-three-zoom-levels))
- <a id="deep-link-and-server-fallback"></a>**deep link and server fallback**: a URL that names a path inside the app, and the server rule that answers unknown paths with `index.html` so a reload reaches the Router ([24 §10](modules/24-routing.md#10-the-router-and-the-platform-history-api-navigation-api-and-location-strategies))
- <a id="deep-signal"></a>**deep signal**: the signal `withState` makes for a nested object; it also exposes one signal per property ([28 §8](modules/28-state-management.md#8-componentstore-and-signalstore-signalstate-signalstore-withstate-withcomputed-withmethods-and-protected-state))
- <a id="deep-test"></a>**deep test**: a component test that renders the component with its real children ([29 §1](modules/29-testing-angular.md#1-what-to-test-where-unit-integration-component-and-end-to-end-tests-the-pyramid-and-the-trophy))
- <a id="default-for-new-projects"></a>**default (for new projects)**: what `ng new` or the compiler does when you set nothing; existing projects keep their behavior until `ng update` or you change it ([39 §6](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#6-the-version-matrix-part-3-v16-to-v22-from-signals-to-onpush-by-default))
- <a id="default-action"></a>**default action**: what the browser does after dispatch (follow a link, submit, scroll), cancelled by `preventDefault()` on a cancelable event ([08 §4](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#4-events-propagation-default-actions-passive-listeners-and-delegation)).
- <a id="default-export-lazy-loading"></a>**default export (lazy loading)**: an `export default` that the router unwraps, so a loader can return `import('./file')` without `.then` ([15 §4](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#4-lazy-loading-without-modules-loadcomponent-and-loadchildren-with-route-arrays)).
- <a id="defaultvalue-httpresource-option"></a>**defaultValue (httpResource option)**: the value a resource reports while it has none, for example while loading ([27 §10](modules/27-http-client.md#10-httpresource-signal-driven-requests-and-when-to-prefer-it-over-httpclient))
- <a id="defense-in-depth"></a>**defense in depth**: layering independent controls so that one failure is not a breach ([33 §1](modules/33-security.md#1-threat-model-of-an-angular-single-page-app-what-the-attacker-controls-what-runs-where-and-where-each-defense-lives))
- <a id="defensible-number"></a>**defensible number**: a number with a source (a dashboard, a report, a test), a baseline and a kind (field or lab) ([47 §7](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#7-under-the-hood-how-one-story-is-drilled-into-numbers-you-can-defend-and-telling-a-design-or-a-migration-as-your-own-project))
- <a id="defer"></a>**defer**: a creation function that runs a factory at each `subscribe` and subscribes to what it returns ([22 §3](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#3-cold-and-hot-unicast-and-multicast))
- <a id="deferblockbehavior"></a>**DeferBlockBehavior**: the `TestBed` option that makes `@defer` blocks play through like a browser (`Playthrough`, the runtime default) or wait for the test (`Manual`) ([18 §7](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#7-what-lands-in-a-chunk-testing-deferred-blocks-and-the-ssr-pointer))
- <a id="deferblockfixture"></a>**DeferBlockFixture**: the test handle for one `@defer` block, from `getDeferBlocks()`; `render(state)` moves it to a state ([18 §7](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#7-what-lands-in-a-chunk-testing-deferred-blocks-and-the-ssr-pointer))
- <a id="deferrable-view"></a>**deferrable view**: a template part wrapped in `@defer`, whose code loads later and which moves between placeholder, loading, complete and error ([18 §5](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#5-defer-blocks-and-states-placeholder-loading-error-minimum-and-after))
- <a id="deferred-token"></a>**deferred token**: a CSRF token that Spring Security loads only when a request needs it ([42 §6](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#6-csrf-with-cookiecsrftokenrepository-and-angulars-xsrf-support-csrfspa-in-spring-security-7))
- <a id="define-build-option"></a>**define (build option)**: replaces a global identifier with a constant value at build time ([12 §4](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#4-environments-and-configuration-build-time-versus-run-time)).
- <a id="define-semantics-class-fields"></a>**define semantics (class fields)**: class fields are created with `defineProperty`, so they do not call inherited setters ([03 §3](modules/03-js-objects-prototypes-classes.md#3-classes-the-sugar-and-what-is-not-sugar)).
- <a id="definitelytyped--types"></a>**DefinitelyTyped / @types**: the community repository and npm scope of declaration files for packages that ship none ([06 §8](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#8-declaration-files-types-and-augmentation)).
- <a id="definition-ɵcmp-ɵdir-ɵpipe-ɵprov-ɵfac"></a>**definition (ɵcmp, ɵdir, ɵpipe, ɵprov, ɵfac)**: the static property the compiler adds to a component, directive, pipe or injectable class, or its factory ([12 §8](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it)).
- <a id="definition-of-done"></a>**definition of done**: what the reviewer will accept as finished ([47 §5](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#5-take-home-assignments-scope-and-time-box-what-the-repository-must-show-the-readme-and-the-review-call))
- <a id="degraded-behavior"></a>**degraded behavior**: what a screen does while a dependency fails ([45 §8](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#8-under-the-hood-failure-modes-observability-offline-levels-and-consistency-choices))
