- <a id="dehydrated-block"></a>**dehydrated block**: a server-rendered `@defer` block whose code has not loaded in the browser: server DOM, no listeners ([32 §6](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#6-incremental-hydration-defer-blocks-with-hydrate-triggers))
- <a id="dehydrated-block-registry"></a>**dehydrated block registry**: the internal map (`DEHYDRATED_BLOCK_REGISTRY`) from a server-rendered `@defer` block's id to its view, where the browser keeps blocks that still wait for a hydrate trigger ([32 Q32.27](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#q32-27))
- <a id="deliberate-change"></a>**deliberate change**: a change to a pinned quirk made on purpose, as its own visible step ([47 §3](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#3-legacy-codebases-and-migrations-without-blame-the-constraints-of-the-time-how-you-learned-the-code-before-changing-it-and-words-that-explain-instead-of-accuse))
- <a id="delta"></a>**delta**: one change after a snapshot, sent over the live connection with a sequence number ([43 §8](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#8-from-streams-to-signals-one-shared-connection-tosignal-and-rxresource-bursts-and-connection-state))
- <a id="density-material"></a>**density (Material)**: a spacing scale for components, 0 by default and down to -5 per the theming guide ([36 §3](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#3-theming-with-material-3-mattheme-system-variables-and-light-and-dark-with-color-scheme))
- <a id="density-descriptor"></a>**density descriptor**: a `srcset` suffix such as `2x`, the file's multiple of the screen's pixel density ([31 §7](modules/31-performance.md#7-images-ngoptimizedimage-with-priority-fill-loaders-and-placeholders))
- <a id="deny-list"></a>**deny list**: stored ids of tokens the server refuses even though they have not expired ([42 §1](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#1-sessions-versus-tokens-what-the-server-keeps-what-each-request-carries-revocation-and-scaling))
- <a id="dependency"></a>**dependency**: anything a class requires but does not create ([16 §1](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#1-why-dependency-injection-containers-constructor-injection-and-inject)).
- <a id="dependency-constraint"></a>**dependency constraint**: a rule that says which tags a tagged project may depend on ([38 §7](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#7-nx-monorepos-the-project-graph-tags-nxenforce-module-boundaries-affected-commands-and-caching))
- <a id="dependency-cycle"></a>**dependency cycle**: nodes that depend on each other (A needs B, B needs A), so no topological order exists ([38 §13](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#13-under-the-hood-ng-packagrs-build-graph-and-how-nx-computes-affected-projects-and-task-hashes))
- <a id="dependency-edge"></a>**dependency edge**: a rule that one migration must finish before another starts, because the later one needs its result ([39 §10](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#10-a-migration-program-for-an-old-angular-codebase-order-dependencies-and-checkpoints))
- <a id="dependency-injection-di"></a>**dependency injection (DI)**: the mechanism that constructs a class and supplies its dependencies, calling the compiled factory `ɵfac` ([12 §8](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it)). · also in [16 §1](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#1-why-dependency-injection-containers-constructor-injection-and-inject)
- <a id="dependency-rule"></a>**dependency rule**: a one-way direction between library types and scopes that imports must follow ([38 §4](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#4-layers-library-types-and-dependency-rules))
- <a id="dependency-tracking"></a>**dependency tracking**: a `computed` recording which signals it read so it re-runs only when one changed; owner [17](modules/17-signals.md) ([14 §7](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#7-pipe-computed-or-method-call-in-a-template)).
- <a id="deploy-preview"></a>**deploy preview**: a build of one change published at its own address for review ([38 §11](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#11-a-ci-pipeline-for-a-front-end-lint-test-build-end-to-end-and-deploy-previews))
- <a id="deprecation"></a>**deprecation**: marking an export with `@deprecated` for one major before it is removed ([38 §6](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#6-public-api-surfaces-public-apits-exports-secondary-entry-points-and-versioning))
- <a id="deprecation-header"></a>**deprecation header**: the `Deprecation` response header (RFC 9745), whose value is a Date such as `@1688169599` ([41 §4](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#4-evolving-the-contract-compatible-changes-api-versioning-in-spring-framework-7-and-deprecation-headers))
- <a id="deprecation-period"></a>**deprecation period**: the time a deprecated API stays present, at least until the next major release, about 12 months ([39 §3](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#3-how-angular-ships-semantic-versioning-the-release-cadence-support-windows-and-the-deprecation-policy))
- <a id="deprecation-policy"></a>**deprecation policy**: a project's statement of how long a deprecated feature keeps working before removal; Angular's is owner [39 §3](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#3-how-angular-ships-semantic-versioning-the-release-cadence-support-windows-and-the-deprecation-policy), Vue's "removed in the next major release" ([40 §7](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#7-ecosystem-size-release-policies-and-breaking-changes))
- <a id="depsfn"></a>**DepsFn**: the arrow function the compiler passes to `ɵɵdefer`; it returns a dynamic `import()` for a deferrable dependency and the class itself for an eager one ([18 §7](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#7-what-lands-in-a-chunk-testing-deferred-blocks-and-the-ssr-pointer))
- <a id="derived-state"></a>**derived state**: a value computed from other state, such as a total from a list ([14 §7](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#7-pipe-computed-or-method-call-in-a-template)). · also in [20 §3](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#3-which-hooks-still-matter-with-signals), [28 §1](modules/28-state-management.md#1-kinds-of-state-server-cache-ui-form-url-and-session-state-and-where-each-belongs), [31 §3](modules/31-performance.md#3-runtime-cost-of-a-change-onpush-zoneless-signals-and-per-check-template-work)
- <a id="descendants-query-option"></a>**descendants (query option)**: whether a content query looks beyond the host's direct children; `contentChildren` defaults to no, `contentChild` to yes ([19 §5](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#5-queries-view-and-content-queries-with-signal-queries))
- <a id="description"></a>**description**: the part of a message's metadata, after `|`, that tells the translator the context; it does not affect the ID ([34 §4](modules/34-i18n.md#4-marking-text-for-translation-i18n-attributes-metadata-ids-and-localize-in-code))
- <a id="design-system"></a>**design system**: the design tokens, components, usage rules and tests that several products share ([36 §12](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#12-a-design-system-on-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-or-a-wrapper-around-material)) · also in [36 §12](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#12-a-design-system-on-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-or-a-wrapper-around-material)
- <a id="design-token"></a>**design token**: a named design decision (colour, spacing) stored once as a custom property ([10 §6](modules/10-css-essentials.md#6-custom-properties-theming-and-color-scheme)). · also in [13 §8](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#8-styling-strategies-component-styles-global-styles-css-variables-and-tailwind), [36 §3](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#3-theming-with-material-3-mattheme-system-variables-and-light-and-dark-with-color-scheme), [38 §12](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#12-a-complete-production-folder-tree-folder-by-folder)
- <a id="destination"></a>**destination**: the observer or subscriber a subscriber forwards notifications to ([22 §9](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#9-under-the-hood-subscribe-the-subscriber-chain-and-the-subjects-observer-list))
- <a id="destination-stomp"></a>**destination (STOMP)**: the header that says where a `SEND` goes or what a `SUBSCRIBE` listens to, such as `/app/room.1` or `/topic/room.1` ([43 §5](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#5-websockets-and-stomp-in-spring-the-handshake-frames-messagemapping-brokers-user-destinations-and-heart-beats))
- <a id="destroy-delay"></a>**destroy delay**: the short wait, 10 ms in the default strategy, between an element's disconnection and its component's destruction ([37 §3](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#3-elements-at-run-time-lifecycle-change-detection-without-zonejs-styles-and-several-apps-on-one-page))
- <a id="destroyref"></a>**DestroyRef**: the v16.0 handle whose `onDestroy(callback)` registers cleanup for the scope it was injected in (a component, or an injector) and returns an unregister function ([16 §8](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#8-lifetime-and-dynamic-injectors-destroyref-environmentinjector-injectorcreate-forward-references-and-cycles)).
- <a id="destructive-hydration"></a>**destructive hydration**: discarding the server's DOM and rendering the page again in the browser, Angular's behavior before non-destructive hydration (16.0) ([32 §4](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#4-hydration-reusing-the-servers-dom-mismatches-and-ngskiphydration))
- <a id="desugaring"></a>**desugaring**: rewriting convenience syntax into the simpler constructs it stands for, as `[(x)]` becomes `[x]` and `(xChange)` ([13 §5](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#5-event-binding-and-two-way-binding)).
- <a id="detach"></a>**detach**: the runtime step that takes a view's nodes out of the DOM, which a leave animation postpones ([35 §12](modules/35-animations.md#12-under-the-hood-from-animateleave-to-a-removed-node))
- <a id="detached-dom-node"></a>**detached DOM node**: an element removed from the page that JavaScript still references, so it and its subtree stay in memory ([05 §4](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#4-garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks)).
- <a id="detached-node"></a>**detached node**: a DOM element removed from the document but still referenced, so it is not collected ([31 §8](modules/31-performance.md#8-memory-leaks-heap-snapshots-detached-nodes-and-the-devtools-workflow))
- <a id="detached-route-and-detachedroutehandle"></a>**detached route and DetachedRouteHandle**: a leaving route kept alive but out of the page and stored by a strategy as an opaque `DetachedRouteHandle`, which holds its component until the router reattaches it or `destroyDetachedRouteHandle` destroys it ([24 §8](modules/24-routing.md#8-preloading-strategies-and-route-reuse))
- <a id="detached-view"></a>**detached view**: a view taken out of its container without being destroyed, kept by key so a later row can re-attach it ([18 §9](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#9-under-the-hood-what-the-blocks-compile-to-how-for-reconciles-and-how-defer-moves-between-states))
- <a id="dev-server"></a>**dev server**: the `ng serve` process that rebuilds in memory on save and pushes changes to the page ([12 §5](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#5-the-dev-loop-ng-serve-hot-module-replacement-and-angular-devtools)).
- <a id="dev-server-proxy"></a>**dev-server proxy**: `ng serve` forwarding matching paths (such as `/api/**`) to the API, so the browser sees one origin in development ([41 §1](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#1-cors-on-both-sides-springs-corsconfigurationsource-spring-security-preflight-caching-credentials-and-the-dev-proxy))
- <a id="developer-preview"></a>**developer preview**: an Angular API label for APIs that "can change at any time, even in new patch versions" ([12 §1](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#1-what-a-framework-does-and-angulars-philosophy), [37 §9](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#9-error-boundaries-boundary-error-reset-onviewerror-and-onerror)) · also in [39 §3](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#3-how-angular-ships-semantic-versioning-the-release-cadence-support-windows-and-the-deprecation-policy)
- <a id="development-mode"></a>**development mode**: the state in which `ngDevMode` is on and `isDevMode()` returns true ([12 §5](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#5-the-dev-loop-ng-serve-hot-module-replacement-and-angular-devtools)). · also in [20 §6](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#6-expressionchangedafterithasbeencheckederror-cause-and-fixes)
- <a id="di-dependency-injection"></a>**DI (dependency injection)**: Angular's way of giving a class the objects it asks for; owner [16](modules/16-dependency-injection.md) ([13 §1](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#1-component-anatomy-selector-template-styles-imports-and-the-v22-file-convention)).
- <a id="dialog-ref"></a>**dialog ref**: the `MatDialogRef` that `MatDialog.open` returns: a handle to close the dialog or await its result with `afterClosed()` ([36 §2](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#2-material-components-in-a-v22-app-standalone-imports-form-fields-dialogs-direction-and-motion))
- <a id="diamond-problem"></a>**diamond problem**: two paths reaching one shared part, so which copy applies is unclear in multiple inheritance ([14 §3](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#3-host-directives-the-composition-api)).
- <a id="differential-loading"></a>**differential loading**: building two bundles, one for modern browsers and one for older ones ([39 §4](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#4-the-version-matrix-part-1-v2-to-v8))
- <a id="digest"></a>**digest**: the AngularJS loop that compares every watcher and repeats until a full pass changes nothing ([39 §1](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#1-angularjs-scopes-watchers-and-the-digest-cycle))
- <a id="digit-pattern"></a>**digit pattern**: the `min-int.min-frac-max-frac` string (`3.1-1`) that `number`, `percent` and `currency` take to set integer and fraction digits ([14 Q14.22](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#q14-22)).
- <a id="dir-attribute"></a>**dir attribute**: the text direction of a page, `ltr` or `rtl`, set on `<html>` from the locale data by a localized build ([34 §7](modules/34-i18n.md#7-compile-time-localization-one-build-per-locale-subpath-lang-and-dir))
- <a id="direct-call"></a>**direct call**: the browser calling a service's own origin, with no server in between, so it is cross-origin unless the host matches ([44 §1](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#1-where-the-api-calls-go-direct-calls-a-same-origin-reverse-proxy-an-api-gateway-or-a-backend-for-frontend))
- <a id="direction-animation"></a>**direction (animation)**: whether the iterations of a CSS animation run forward, backward or alternate, set by `animation-direction` ([35 §3](modules/35-animations.md#3-css-keyframe-animations-keyframes-fill-modes-iteration-and-component-scoped-names))
- <a id="directionality"></a>**Directionality**: the CDK service that reports the reading direction, `ltr` or `rtl`, read from the page or from the nearest `dir` directive ([36 §2](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#2-material-components-in-a-v22-app-standalone-imports-form-fields-dialogs-direction-and-motion))
- <a id="directive"></a>**directive**: behavior attached to an existing element by a selector, with no view of its own; a component is a directive that owns a view ([14 §1](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#1-directives-and-attribute-directives-host-inputs-elementref-and-renderer2)).
- <a id="directivefixture"></a>**DirectiveFixture**: the fixture `TestBed.createDirective` returns; it adds `directiveInstance` and `onDestroy` to the usual fixture members ([29 §8](modules/29-testing-angular.md#8-testing-services-pipes-and-directives-including-testbedcreatedirective))
- <a id="dirty-view"></a>**dirty (view)**: flagged for a check by a mark such as a new input value, an event, a signal change or `markForCheck` ([21 §2](modules/21-change-detection.md#2-onpush-and-eager-strategies-and-what-marks-a-view-dirty))
- <a id="dirty-checking"></a>**dirty checking**: change detection by walking the component tree and comparing every template binding with its previous value ([17 §1](modules/17-signals.md#1-why-signals-exist)). · also in [21 §1](modules/21-change-detection.md#1-the-synchronization-problem-dirty-checking-and-fine-grained-reactivity), [39 §1](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#1-angularjs-scopes-watchers-and-the-digest-cycle)
- <a id="dirty-counter"></a>**dirty counter**: the signal a query signal reads and the runtime bumps when the query's results may have changed ([19 §8](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#8-under-the-hood-the-compiled-definition-projection-slots-and-query-refresh))
- <a id="dirty-flag-of-the-application"></a>**dirty flag (of the application)**: a bit on `ApplicationRef` meaning a pass is owed; any dirty pass also sets the after-render flag, so the render queue runs even when no view was refreshed ([20 §7](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#7-under-the-hood-hook-lists-the-view-refresh-and-the-render-hook-queue))
- <a id="disabled-control"></a>**disabled control**: a control with status `DISABLED`, which leaves its parent's `value` and validation but stays in `getRawValue()` ([25 §2](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#2-the-reactive-model-formcontrol-formgroup-formarray-and-formrecord))
- <a id="disabled-hidden-and-readonly"></a>**disabled, hidden and readonly**: rules that take `{ when }`; a field in any of those states is skipped by validation ([26 §5](modules/26-signal-forms.md#5-cross-field-and-conditional-logic-valueof-validatetree-applywhen-disabled-hidden-and-readonly)).
- <a id="disabledreasons"></a>**disabledReasons**: the list of reasons a field is disabled, one entry per disabling rule ([26 §5](modules/26-signal-forms.md#5-cross-field-and-conditional-logic-valueof-validatetree-applywhen-disabled-hidden-and-readonly)).
- <a id="discrete-property"></a>**discrete property**: a property whose values have no in-between steps, such as `display`; it transitions only with `transition-behavior: allow-discrete` ([35 §2](modules/35-animations.md#2-css-transitions-triggers-interruption-events-and-entry-with-starting-style))
- <a id="discriminant"></a>**discriminant**: that shared literal-typed property, such as `kind` ([06 §4](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#4-unions-intersections-and-discriminated-unions)).
- <a id="discriminated-union"></a>**discriminated union**: a union whose members share a property with distinct literal types, so checking it narrows to one member ([06 §4](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#4-unions-intersections-and-discriminated-unions)).
- <a id="dispatch-sse"></a>**dispatch (SSE)**: what a blank line does: the event built so far fires, unless its data buffer is empty ([43 §2](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#2-server-sent-events-on-the-wire-the-textevent-stream-format-and-how-eventsource-reconnects))
- <a id="dispatch-false"></a>**dispatch: false**: the `createEffect` option for an effect that emits no action ([28 §6](modules/28-state-management.md#6-effects-functional-effects-flattening-error-handling-and-dispatch-false))
- <a id="dispatcher"></a>**dispatcher**: the part of the runner that hands test groups to free worker processes and replaces a worker after a failure ([30 §9](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#9-under-the-hood-how-playwrights-runner-groups-shards-retries-and-isolates-tests))
- <a id="dispatcher-stack"></a>**dispatcher stack**: the array of attached overlays that the CDK's keyboard and outside-click dispatchers walk from the most recently attached ([36 §13](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#13-under-the-hood-from-createoverlayref-to-a-popover-in-the-top-layer-and-from-mattheme-to-a-computed-color))
- <a id="dispatcher-stack-overlays"></a>**dispatcher stack (overlays)**: the array of attached overlays that the CDK's keyboard and outside-click dispatchers walk from the newest ([36 Q36.25](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#q36-25))
- <a id="dispatcherservlet"></a>**DispatcherServlet**: Spring MVC's servlet, which finds the handler, invokes it and asks the exception resolvers when it throws ([41 §10](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#10-under-the-hood-one-failed-request-through-springs-filters-and-handlers-and-angulars-backend))
- <a id="disposable"></a>**disposable**: an object with a `[Symbol.dispose]()` (or `[Symbol.asyncDispose]()`) method that `using` calls at block exit ([05 §7](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#7-explicit-resource-management-using-and-disposablestack)).
- <a id="disposablestack"></a>**DisposableStack**: a run-time collection of cleanups (`use`, `adopt`, `defer`) disposed in reverse order; `move()` transfers ownership ([05 §7](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#7-explicit-resource-management-using-and-disposablestack)).
- <a id="distinctuntilchanged"></a>**distinctUntilChanged**: an operator that drops a value equal to the previous one; the default comparison is `===`, so objects and arrays compare by reference ([28 §3](modules/28-state-management.md#3-the-service-with-behaviorsubject-store-the-legacy-but-common-pattern-and-its-migration))
- <a id="distributed-trace"></a>**distributed trace**: the tree of spans that share one trace ID across browser, proxy and server ([44 §10](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#10-under-the-hood-opentelemetry-for-the-web-traceparent-and-one-click-traced-to-a-spring-boot-span-and-log-line))
- <a id="distributive-conditional-type"></a>**distributive conditional type**: a conditional type over a naked type parameter, applied to each member of a union separately ([07 §3](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#3-conditional-types-distributivity-infer-and-recursive-types)).
- <a id="distributive-omit"></a>**distributive Omit**: `T extends unknown ? Omit<T, K> : never`, which removes keys from each union member instead of collapsing the union to its common keys ([07 §4](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#4-utility-types-built-in-and-by-hand)).
- <a id="document-root-loader"></a>**document root loader**: a harness loader rooted at `document.body`, for content a component renders outside its own element, such as overlays ([36 §10](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#10-component-harnesses-for-material-the-cdk-and-your-own-components))
- <a id="documentfragment"></a>**DocumentFragment**: a parentless node container; appending it moves its children into the tree and leaves it empty ([08 §3](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#3-dom-apis-and-observers)).
- <a id="dom-clobbering"></a>**DOM clobbering**: markup whose `name` or `id` shadows a DOM property (an `<input name="nextSibling">` inside a `form`), which Angular's HTML sanitizer detects and refuses ([33 §12](modules/33-security.md#12-under-the-hood-from-a-template-binding-to-a-sanitized-dom-write))
- <a id="dom-emulation"></a>**DOM emulation**: the server's `document` and elements, built by the domino library in `@angular/platform-server`, with no layout or styles ([32 §7](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#7-platform-safe-code-browser-apis-afternextrender-document-request-and-response_init))
- <a id="dom-property"></a>**DOM property**: a field on an element's JavaScript object, such as `title` or `value`; `[title]` writes it ([13 §4](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#4-property-attribute-class-and-style-bindings)).
- <a id="dom-based-xss"></a>**DOM-based XSS**: cross-site scripting where the page's own script moves attacker data into the DOM and the response does not change ([33 §2](modules/33-security.md#2-xss-and-angulars-template-model-escaping-by-default-trusted-templates-and-the-paths-around-them))
- <a id="domain-error"></a>**domain error**: an application-level failure kind (offline, timeout, not found, validation, server) that hides HTTP details from the UI ([27 §5](modules/27-http-client.md#5-errors-httperrorresponse-an-error-mapping-layer-and-retry-with-backoff-and-jitter))
- <a id="domain-slice"></a>**domain slice**: a larger feature area kept as a folder with its own sub-features ([38 §1](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#1-folder-structures-that-scale-by-feature-domain-slices-and-core-shared-and-feature-boundaries))
- <a id="domportal"></a>**DomPortal**: a portal for an existing DOM element, which the outlet moves into itself (leaving a comment anchor) and puts back on detach ([36 Q36.09](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#q36-09))
- <a id="double-submit"></a>**double submit**: a second send of the same intent (two clicks, two tabs), which must stay one intent with one key ([46 §8](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#8-under-the-hood-a-money-transfer-with-idempotency-keys-optimistic-ui-that-never-claims-success-and-the-unknown-outcome))
- <a id="downgrade-ngupgrade"></a>**downgrade (ngUpgrade)**: wrap an Angular component or service so AngularJS can use it ([39 §9](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#9-from-angularjs-to-angular-ngupgrade-hybrids-the-strangler-fig-and-rewrites))
- <a id="downleveling"></a>**downleveling**: compiling newer syntax into older JavaScript (here, `using` into TypeScript's helper calls) so it runs on engines without it ([05 §7](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#7-explicit-resource-management-using-and-disposablestack)).
- <a id="download-count"></a>**download count**: npm's tally of tarball downloads of a package in a period, including build servers and mirrors; not users ([40 §7](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#7-ecosystem-size-release-policies-and-breaking-changes))
- <a id="download-progress-event"></a>**download progress event**: `HttpClient`'s event for a response body in progress; its `partialText` is the cumulative text so far ([43 §4](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#4-the-angular-sse-client-eventsource-or-a-fetch-stream-as-an-observable-and-when-httpclient-fits))
- <a id="dpop"></a>**DPoP**: OAuth 2.0 Demonstrating Proof of Possession (RFC 9449), one way to sender-constrain tokens ([42 §4](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#4-refresh-tokens-rotation-reuse-detection-silent-renew-and-refreshing-across-tabs))
- <a id="draft"></a>**draft**: a wizard's saved answers, kept with a schema version so the form can be resumed ([46 §5](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#5-a-multi-step-form-wizard-with-draft-saving-a-step-model-per-step-validation-and-drafts-that-survive-and-migrate))
- <a id="drag-handle"></a>**drag handle**: a `cdkDragHandle` element that limits where a drag may start ([36 §6](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#6-drag-and-drop-sorting-transferring-between-lists-and-the-keyboard-gap))
- <a id="drag-item"></a>**drag item**: a `cdkDrag` element inside a drop list that the user can pick up ([36 §6](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#6-drag-and-drop-sorting-transferring-between-lists-and-the-keyboard-gap))
- <a id="drag-placeholder"></a>**drag placeholder**: the copy of a dragged item left in the list to mark the gap where it will land ([36 §6](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#6-drag-and-drop-sorting-transferring-between-lists-and-the-keyboard-gap))
- <a id="drag-preview"></a>**drag preview**: the copy of a dragged item that follows the pointer; the CDK appends it to `body` as a manual popover ([36 §6](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#6-drag-and-drop-sorting-transferring-between-lists-and-the-keyboard-gap))
- <a id="drain"></a>**drain**: letting the requests already in flight finish after new connections stop being accepted ([44 §6](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#6-serving-ssr-apps-in-containers-the-node-server-as-pid-1-graceful-shutdown-health-probes-and-what-nginx-or-a-cdn-still-serves))
- <a id="drift-check"></a>**drift check**: a build step that compares the client's schemas with the API description and fails on disagreement ([41 §3](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#3-openapi-with-springdoc-and-generated-typescript-clients-openapi-generator-orval-and-ng-openapi-gen))
- <a id="driver"></a>**driver**: the object a test drives a component through, such as a harness or the rendered page ([29 §11](modules/29-testing-angular.md#11-component-harnesses-and-angular-testing-library))
- <a id="driver-angular-service-worker"></a>**driver (Angular service worker)**: the internal main class of `ngsw-worker.js` that assigns versions, checks for updates and holds the worker's state ([37 §13](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#13-under-the-hood-createcustomelements-strategy-and-the-service-workers-driver))
- <a id="drop-list"></a>**drop list**: the `cdkDropList` container that holds drag items, reports each drop and can exchange items with connected lists ([36 §6](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#6-drag-and-drop-sorting-transferring-between-lists-and-the-keyboard-gap))
- <a id="drop-list-group"></a>**drop list group**: the `cdkDropListGroup` directive that connects every drop list inside it ([36 §6](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#6-drag-and-drop-sorting-transferring-between-lists-and-the-keyboard-gap))
- <a id="dto"></a>**DTO**: data transfer object, the shape the server sends ([38 §3](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#3-facades-and-the-data-access-layer))
- <a id="dto-data-transfer-object"></a>**DTO (data transfer object)**: plain fields with no methods, sent across a boundary and turned back into an instance by a factory ([03 Q03.16](modules/03-js-objects-prototypes-classes.md#q03-16)). · also in [07 §4](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#4-utility-types-built-in-and-by-hand), [41 §2](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#2-dto-contracts-what-crosses-the-wire-absent-versus-null-and-checking-responses-at-the-boundary)
- <a id="dummy"></a>**dummy**: a test double passed around but never used, usually to fill a parameter list ([29 §7](modules/29-testing-angular.md#7-test-doubles-fakes-stubs-spies-and-mocks-vifn-provider-overrides-and-over-mocking))
- <a id="duplex-half"></a>**duplex: 'half'**: the `fetch` option required to send a stream as the request body ([09 §2](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#2-fetch-xhr-and-the-request-lifecycle)).
- <a id="dynamic-component"></a>**dynamic component**: a component whose class is chosen at run time, rendered through `NgComponentOutlet` ([14 §4](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#4-built-in-directives-ngclass-ngstyle-the-outlets-and-what-control-flow-replaced)). · also in [19 §7](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#7-dynamic-components-viewcontainerrefcreatecomponent-createcomponent-and-bindings)
- <a id="dynamic-dependencies"></a>**dynamic dependencies**: a computed or effect depends only on the signals its last run actually read ([17 §3](modules/17-signals.md#3-computed-signals)).
- <a id="dynamic-form"></a>**dynamic form**: a form whose shape is known only at run time, such as repeatable rows ([25 §9](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#9-dynamic-forms-formarray-patterns-and-config-driven-forms))
- <a id="dynamic-context-paradigm"></a>**dynamic-context paradigm**: server-side evaluation on behalf of many users, with a context per request ([44 §8](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#8-feature-flags-with-openfeature-and-canary-releases-providers-evaluation-context-sticky-rollout-kill-switches-and-version-skew))

## E

- <a id="eager-request"></a>**eager request**: a request sent as soon as its inputs exist, with no subscriber; `httpResource` is eager and `HttpClient` is lazy ([27 §10](modules/27-http-client.md#10-httpresource-signal-driven-requests-and-when-to-prefer-it-over-httpclient))
- <a id="easing"></a>**easing**: the timing function that shapes how fast a transition or animation progresses over its duration ([35 §2](modules/35-animations.md#2-css-transitions-triggers-interruption-events-and-entry-with-starting-style))
- <a id="ecmascript-edition"></a>**ECMAScript edition**: the yearly snapshot of the specification (ES2026 = 17th edition), cut in March from the Stage 4 proposals ([05 §8](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#8-modern-features-by-edition-es2015-to-es2026)).
- <a id="edge"></a>**edge**: the entry point of a system, where requests first arrive ([44 §1](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#1-where-the-api-calls-go-direct-calls-a-same-origin-reverse-proxy-an-api-gateway-or-a-backend-for-frontend))
- <a id="edge-case"></a>**edge case**: an input at the border of what the code handles, such as an empty result or a page past the end ([47 §4](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#4-live-coding-an-angular-task-clarify-say-the-plan-build-in-working-increments-narrate-and-recover-when-stuck))
- <a id="edge-function"></a>**edge function**: code a CDN runs on each response before it reaches the visitor, for example to write a fresh nonce into cached HTML ([Q33.15](modules/33-security.md#q33-15))
- <a id="edge-runtime"></a>**edge runtime**: a JavaScript runtime at a CDN's locations, without Node's modules; Angular targets it with `ssr.platform: 'neutral'` ([32 §9](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#9-deploying-ssr-node-and-other-runtimes-static-output-caching-rendered-pages-and-allowed-hosts))
- <a id="editable-combobox"></a>**editable combobox**: a text input with a popup of suggestions, where DOM focus stays in the input and `aria-activedescendant` names the current option ([Exercise 36.2](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#ex36-2))
- <a id="editing-indicator"></a>**editing indicator**: the display of who is editing, a hint and not a lock ([46 §7](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#7-a-notifications-center-and-an-editing-indicator-delivery-unread-counts-across-tabs-and-presence-with-a-time-to-live))
- <a id="effect"></a>**effect**: a function Angular re-runs when a signal it read changes; owner [17 §4](modules/17-signals.md#4-effects) ([14 §2](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#2-structural-directives-ng-template-templateref-viewcontainerref-and-the-microsyntax)). · also in [17 §4](modules/17-signals.md#4-effects)
- <a id="effect-ngrx"></a>**effect (NgRx)**: a long-lived stream over `Actions` that runs side effects and usually answers with new actions ([28 §6](modules/28-state-management.md#6-effects-functional-effects-flattening-error-handling-and-dispatch-false))
- <a id="effect-runner"></a>**effect runner**: the part that performs the effects a pure reducer returns (send, save, post) and feeds their answers back as events ([45 Exercise 45.2](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#ex45-2))
- <a id="effects-error-handler"></a>**effects error handler**: the function NgRx wraps around each effect; the default reports the error to `ErrorHandler` and resubscribes, up to ten times ([28 §6](modules/28-state-management.md#6-effects-functional-effects-flattening-error-handling-and-dispatch-false))
- <a id="egress-allowlist"></a>**egress allowlist**: the destinations a server is allowed to call, checked in code and by network rules; the defense against SSRF that Angular does not provide ([33 §7](modules/33-security.md#7-server-rendered-pages-escaping-in-the-server-html-the-state-script-and-ssrf))
- <a id="element-injector"></a>**element injector**: the injector that belongs to one element, empty unless its component or directive lists `providers`; searched first ([16 §5](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#5-the-injector-tree-platform-root-route-and-element-injectors-and-the-modifiers)).
- <a id="elementref"></a>**ElementRef**: a wrapper whose `nativeElement` holds the host's DOM node; its typings carry an XSS `@security` note ([14 §1](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#1-directives-and-attribute-directives-host-inputs-elementref-and-renderer2)).
- <a id="elf-store"></a>**Elf store**: the store `createStore` returns in `@ngneat/elf`, a `BehaviorSubject` with `update(...reducers)` ([28 §11](modules/28-state-management.md#11-alternatives-and-the-decision-framework-ngxs-elf-tanstack-query-and-when-a-store-library-is-justified))
- <a id="embed-allowlist"></a>**embed allowlist**: the hosts and ID shapes an app accepts for embedded content ([33 §4](modules/33-security.md#4-domsanitizer-and-bypasssecuritytrust-when-trusting-a-value-is-dangerous-and-the-safe-patterns))
- <a id="embedded-view"></a>**embedded view**: the template fragment an `@if`, `@for` block or `<ng-template>` creates, with its own scope ([13 §6](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#6-template-reference-variables-and-let)). · also in [14 §2](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#2-structural-directives-ng-template-templateref-viewcontainerref-and-the-microsyntax), [18 §1](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#1-built-in-blocks-versus-directives-and-the-if-family-with-as), [35 §6](modules/35-animations.md#6-function-callbacks-and-the-v22-leave-semantics-animationcallbackevent-animationcomplete-and-nested-leaves)
- <a id="emit"></a>**emit**: the JavaScript (and `.d.ts`) files the compiler writes; types are absent from it ([06 §1](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#1-what-typescript-is-erased-types-tsc-and-transpile-only-tools)).
- <a id="emitevent-and-onlyself"></a>**emitEvent and onlySelf**: the two options of the model-changing methods: `emitEvent: false` emits nothing, `onlySelf: true` leaves the parent untouched ([25 §7](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#7-status-updateon-valuechanges-and-the-unified-events-stream))
- <a id="emitter-registry"></a>**emitter registry**: the map of live emitters that sending code walks, and that callbacks must remove emitters from ([43 §3](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#3-server-sent-events-in-spring-sseemitter-in-spring-mvc-and-a-flux-of-serversentevent-in-webflux))
- <a id="empty-state"></a>**empty state**: the template branch for a resolved resource whose value is an empty list, distinct from idle, loading and error ([27 Exercise 27.6](modules/27-http-client.md#ex27-6))
- <a id="emptyerror"></a>**EmptyError**: the error `first`, `last`, `single` and `throwIfEmpty` send when the source completes without a value ([22 §6](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#6-the-operator-catalogue-creation-transformation-filtering-utility-conditional-and-aggregation))
- <a id="emulated-encapsulation"></a>**emulated encapsulation**: Angular's default mode: every selector is rewritten to also need an attribute that only this component's elements carry ([13 §9](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#9-view-encapsulation-emulated-shadowdom-and-none-and-what-the-compiler-does-to-your-css)). · also in [35 §3](modules/35-animations.md#3-css-keyframe-animations-keyframes-fill-modes-iteration-and-component-scoped-names)
- <a id="en-301-549"></a>**EN 301 549**: a harmonised European standard of accessibility requirements for ICT products and services; V3.2.1 (2021-03) maps WCAG 2.1 AA for Directive 2016/2102, V4.1.1 (2026-09) WCAG 2.2 and adds Directive 2019/882 ([11 §1](modules/11-accessibility.md#1-why-accessibility-and-what-the-standards-require)).
- <a id="encapsulation-context"></a>**encapsulation context**: a tree whose styles are scoped, such as a shadow tree; between contexts the outer one wins for normal rules and the inner one for important rules ([10 §2](modules/10-css-essentials.md#2-the-cascade-origins-specificity-and-layers)).
- <a id="end-of-life"></a>**end of life**: the date a version stops receiving new features, updates and fixes, such as Vue 2 on December 31st, 2023 ([40 §7](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#7-ecosystem-size-release-policies-and-breaking-changes))
- <a id="end-session-endpoint"></a>**end-session endpoint**: the identity provider's logout URL, published as `end_session_endpoint` in its discovery metadata ([42 §8](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#8-logout-everywhere-and-session-timeout-ux))
- <a id="end-to-end-e2e-test"></a>**end-to-end (e2e) test**: a test that drives the built app in a real browser against a running server and checks a user journey ([30 §1](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#1-what-belongs-at-the-end-to-end-level-journeys-cost-and-a-strategy-across-levels))
- <a id="end-to-end-test"></a>**end-to-end test**: a test that drives the built app in a real browser against a running server; module 30 owns it ([29 §1](modules/29-testing-angular.md#1-what-to-test-where-unit-integration-component-and-end-to-end-tests-the-pyramid-and-the-trophy))
- <a id="enter-animation"></a>**enter animation**: an animation that runs when an element is inserted; `animate.enter` adds its classes and removes them when it ends ([35 §1](modules/35-animations.md#1-motion-in-a-ui-what-to-animate-the-browsers-three-animation-mechanisms-and-where-angular-fits))
- <a id="enter-classes"></a>**enter classes**: the CSS classes `animate.enter` adds when an element is inserted and removes when its longest animation ends ([35 §5](modules/35-animations.md#5-animateenter-and-animateleave-with-css-classes-syntax-bindings-and-when-the-element-leaves))
- <a id="entity"></a>**entity**: a thing with an id, such as a board, column, card, comment or user ([45 §4](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#4-component-architecture-data-model-and-data-flow-from-screens-to-a-component-tree-and-a-client-side-model))
- <a id="entity-persistence"></a>**entity (persistence)**: the class mapped to a database table, such as a JPA entity; returning it exposes every column ([41 §2](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#2-dto-contracts-what-crosses-the-wire-absent-versus-null-and-checking-responses-at-the-boundary))
- <a id="entity-adapter"></a>**entity adapter**: the pure functions from `createEntityAdapter` (`addMany`, `updateOne`, `getSelectors()`) over `EntityState`; `selectId` reads an id and `sortComparer` orders `ids` ([28 §7](modules/28-state-management.md#7-ngrx-entity-router-store-and-store-devtools))
- <a id="entity-and-normalized-state"></a>**entity** and **normalized state**: a record with a unique id, and a collection stored once as a list of ids plus a lookup by id; `EntityState<T>` is `{ ids, entities }` ([28 §7](modules/28-state-management.md#7-ngrx-entity-router-store-and-store-devtools))
- <a id="entry-point"></a>**entry point**: a folder of a library with its own public surface and bundle ([38 §5](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#5-angular-cli-libraries-ng-generate-library-ng-packagr-and-consuming-the-build))
- <a id="entry-point-secondary-entry-point"></a>**entry point (secondary entry point)**: an import path of a package, such as `@angular/cdk/dialog`, listed in its `exports` map and imported on its own ([36 §1](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#1-material-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-styled-components-behavior-primitives-and-headless-patterns))
- <a id="entrypoint-script"></a>**entrypoint script**: a shell file the container runs before its main process; in the nginx image, an executable `*.sh` file in `/docker-entrypoint.d` ([44 §5](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#5-runtime-configuration-without-rebuilding-configjson-environment-substitution-at-container-start-and-provideappinitializer))
- <a id="environment-file"></a>**environment file**: a TypeScript module of per-build constants under `src/environments/`, swapped by file replacements ([12 §4](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#4-environments-and-configuration-build-time-versus-run-time)).
- <a id="environment-initializer"></a>**environment initializer**: a function run when an environment injector is created (defined in [16 §7](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#7-injectiontoken-multi-providers-environment-providers-and-initializers), used in [37 §8](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#8-errorhandler-and-global-listeners-what-reaches-the-handler-and-what-does-not))
- <a id="environment-injector"></a>**environment injector**: the injector of the application scope, passable to `NgComponentOutlet`; owner [16](modules/16-dependency-injection.md) ([14 §4](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#4-built-in-directives-ngclass-ngstyle-the-outlets-and-what-control-flow-replaced)). · also in [16 §5](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#5-the-injector-tree-platform-root-route-and-element-injectors-and-the-modifiers), [37 §1](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#1-angular-elements-turning-a-component-into-a-custom-element)
- <a id="environment-injector-root"></a>**environment injector (root)**: the application-wide injector created from `ApplicationConfig.providers`; "the root injector" ([12 §6](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#6-bootstrapping-bootstrapapplication-the-platform-and-initializers); owned by module 16).
- <a id="environment-record"></a>**environment record**: the specification's storage for the bindings of one scope (a call, a block, a module) ([02 §1](modules/02-js-scope-closures-this.md#1-lexical-scope-and-the-scope-chain)).
- <a id="environmentproviders"></a>**EnvironmentProviders**: the branded wrapper type for providers only an environment injector may hold; components refuse it ([16 §7](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#7-injectiontoken-multi-providers-environment-providers-and-initializers)).
- <a id="envsubst"></a>**envsubst**: GNU gettext's program that replaces `$NAME` and `${NAME}` in text with environment values, only for the listed names when a list is given ([44 §5](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#5-runtime-configuration-without-rebuilding-configjson-environment-substitution-at-container-start-and-provideappinitializer))
- <a id="ephemeral-state"></a>**ephemeral state**: state that dies with the interaction that made it ([45 §5](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#5-state-and-api-design-where-each-piece-of-state-lives-and-the-api-the-screens-need))
- <a id="ephemeron"></a>**ephemeron**: a key/value pair whose value is kept only while the key is reachable from elsewhere, which is how a `WeakMap` entry behaves ([05 §5](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#5-weak-references-weakmap-weakset-weakref-and-finalizationregistry)).
- <a id="equal-jitter"></a>**equal jitter**: a retry delay of half the plain backoff plus a random share of the other half, so it stays between half and all of the backoff ([27 Exercise 27.2](modules/27-http-client.md#ex27-2))
- <a id="equality-cutoff"></a>**equality cutoff**: a recomputation that yields an equal value keeps the old version, so nothing downstream recomputes ([17 §3](modules/17-signals.md#3-computed-signals), [§9](modules/17-signals.md#9-under-the-hood-the-reactive-graph)).
- <a id="erasable-syntax"></a>**erasable syntax**: TypeScript syntax that can be deleted without changing behavior; `enum`, parameter properties and namespaces with values are not erasable ([06 §1](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#1-what-typescript-is-erased-types-tsc-and-transpile-only-tools)).
- <a id="ergonomic-brand-check"></a>**ergonomic brand check**: `#field in obj`, which tests whether an object has a class's private element ([03 §3](modules/03-js-objects-prototypes-classes.md#3-classes-the-sugar-and-what-is-not-sugar)).
- <a id="error-boundary"></a>**error boundary**: a template region, written with `@boundary`, whose content is replaced by a fallback when it throws while created or checked ([37 §9](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#9-error-boundaries-boundary-error-reset-onviewerror-and-onerror))
- <a id="error-cause"></a>**error cause**: the `cause` option of `Error` that links a wrapping error to the one that triggered it ([05 §6](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#6-errors-subclasses-causes-aggregation-and-global-handlers)).
- <a id="error-display-policy"></a>**error display policy**: the rule that decides when a field's error shows, here `invalid` and (`touched` or `submitted`) ([25 §8](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#8-error-display-strategies-and-accessible-errors))
- <a id="error-factory"></a>**error factory**: the function passed to `throwError(() => err)`, called at each subscription to build the error ([23 §3](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#3-errors-catcherror-placement-finalize-and-error-versus-complete))
- <a id="error-map-validationerrors"></a>**error map (ValidationErrors)**: the object a validator returns, keyed by rule name, which the control merges with the other validators' maps into `errors` ([25 §5](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#5-validators-built-in-custom-and-cross-field))
- <a id="error-mapping-layer"></a>**error mapping layer**: one function at the edge that turns an `HttpErrorResponse` into a small union of domain errors the UI renders ([27 §5](modules/27-http-client.md#5-errors-httperrorresponse-an-error-mapping-layer-and-retry-with-backoff-and-jitter)) · also in [27 §5](modules/27-http-client.md#5-errors-httperrorresponse-an-error-mapping-layer-and-retry-with-backoff-and-jitter)
- <a id="error-message-map"></a>**error message map**: one function from error keys and their payload, such as `requiredLength`, to the text every field shows ([25 §8](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#8-error-display-strategies-and-accessible-errors))
- <a id="error-rate"></a>**error rate**: errors per 1,000 page views, compared per release instead of counting errors ([44 §9](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#9-frontend-error-tracking-and-real-user-monitoring-releases-source-maps-beacons-and-sampling))
- <a id="error-summary"></a>**error summary**: a block at the top of a form listing every error with a link to its field ([11 §6](modules/11-accessibility.md#6-accessible-names-descriptions-and-forms)).
- <a id="errordetails"></a>**ErrorDetails**: the second argument of `onViewError` and `onError`: the component whose view threw and, for a boundary, its host component and `reset` ([37 §9](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#9-error-boundaries-boundary-error-reset-onviewerror-and-onerror))
- <a id="errorhandler"></a>**ErrorHandler**: the application's central error sink; owner [37](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md) ([14 §8](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#8-the-async-pipe-under-the-hood)). · also in [18 §5](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#5-defer-blocks-and-states-placeholder-loading-error-minimum-and-after), [37 §8](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#8-errorhandler-and-global-listeners-what-reaches-the-handler-and-what-does-not)
- <a id="errorstatematcher"></a>**ErrorStateMatcher**: the Material service whose `isErrorState` decides when a form field shows its error; by default the control is invalid and either touched or in a submitted form ([36 §2](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#2-material-components-in-a-v22-app-standalone-imports-form-fields-dialogs-direction-and-motion))
- <a id="errorsummary"></a>**errorSummary**: the field state signal listing the errors of the field and all its descendants, while `errors()` holds only the field's own ([26 §2](modules/26-signal-forms.md#2-form-the-field-tree-and-field-state)).
- <a id="es-module"></a>**ES module**: a file whose `import` and `export` declarations the engine links before running it ([39 §2](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#2-why-angular-was-rewritten-and-what-carried-over); owner [05 §1](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#1-es-modules-static-structure-linking-and-live-bindings))
- <a id="esbuild"></a>**esbuild**: a fast bundler that compiles each file on its own and only strips TypeScript types ([06 header](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md)). · also in [12 §3](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#3-building-the-application-builder-output-layout-and-budgets)
- <a id="escape-hatch"></a>**escape hatch**: a construct that bypasses a check, such as `$any(x)`, which switches type checking off for one template expression ([13 §3](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#3-safe-navigation-the-non-null-assertion-and-any)).
- <a id="estimated-total"></a>**estimated total**: a row count the server marks as approximate (`exact: false`) so a slow exact count is not waited for ([46 §4](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#4-a-large-data-grid-server-side-filtering-sorting-and-pagination-the-query-in-the-url-and-keyset-cursors))
- <a id="etag"></a>**ETag**: an opaque, quoted version tag for a representation, strong by default or weak with `W/` ([09 §4](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#4-http-caching)).
- <a id="european-accessibility-act-eaa"></a>**European Accessibility Act (EAA)**: Directive (EU) 2019/882, accessibility requirements for certain products and services, applied from 28 June 2025 ([11 §1](modules/11-accessibility.md#1-why-accessibility-and-what-the-standards-require)).
- <a id="evaluation-context"></a>**evaluation context**: the data a flag evaluation may use, such as the targeting key and attributes of the user ([44 §8](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#8-feature-flags-with-openfeature-and-canary-releases-providers-evaluation-context-sticky-rollout-kill-switches-and-version-skew))
- <a id="event-versus-state"></a>**event (versus state)**: something that happens at a time, with a when, a how often, a way to cancel and a way to combine, which is what an Observable describes ([23 §7](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#7-rxjs-and-signals-decision-rules-tosignal-toobservable-and-rxresource))
- <a id="event-binding"></a>**event binding**: `(event)="statement"`, a subscription to a DOM event or a component output that runs the statement each time it fires ([13 §5](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#5-event-binding-and-two-way-binding)).
- <a id="event-bus"></a>**event bus**: a `Subject` that any component can emit on and subscribe to; it has no current value ([19 §2](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#2-sharing-state-beyond-the-tree-services-with-signals-and-di-scoped-state))
- <a id="event-coalescing"></a>**event coalescing**: `provideZoneChangeDetection({ eventCoalescing: true })`, which merges the ticks of the listeners of one event into one ([21 §3](modules/21-change-detection.md#3-zonejs-and-ngzone-how-changes-used-to-be-detected-and-why-zone-left-the-default))
- <a id="event-contract"></a>**event contract**: the small inline script that listens from the start of `<body>` and queues the events for replay ([32 §5](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#5-event-replay-keeping-the-clicks-made-before-the-code-arrives))
- <a id="event-delegation"></a>**event delegation**: one listener on a container that handles events from its descendants by inspecting `event.target` ([08 §4](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#4-events-propagation-default-actions-passive-listeners-and-delegation)).
- <a id="event-log-replay-buffer"></a>**event log (replay buffer)**: a bounded list of recent events with ids, from which a reconnecting client's missed events are replayed, or a reload is requested when they are gone ([43 §3](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#3-server-sent-events-in-spring-sseemitter-in-spring-mvc-and-a-flux-of-serversentevent-in-webflux))
- <a id="event-loop"></a>**event loop**: the host's loop that runs one task, drains the microtask queue, and then may render ([04 §1](modules/04-js-async-event-loop.md#1-the-event-loop-tasks-microtasks-and-rendering)).
- <a id="event-loop-phases-node"></a>**event loop phases (Node)**: libuv's repeating cycle of pending callbacks, idle/prepare, poll, check, close callbacks and timers (timers run after poll since libuv 1.45 / Node 20) ([04 §2](modules/04-js-async-event-loop.md#2-nodes-event-loop-and-why-it-matters-for-ssr)).
- <a id="event-replay"></a>**event replay**: recording user events on server-rendered HTML before hydration and delivering them to the real listeners afterwards ([32 §5](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#5-event-replay-keeping-the-clicks-made-before-the-code-arrives))
- <a id="event-stream"></a>**event stream**: the `text/event-stream` body of an SSE response, UTF-8 text in `name: value` lines grouped into events by blank lines (WHATWG HTML §9.2.5) ([43 §2](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#2-server-sent-events-on-the-wire-the-textevent-stream-format-and-how-eventsource-reconnects))
- <a id="event-type"></a>**event type**: the name an `event` field gives an event, `message` when none is given ([43 §2](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#2-server-sent-events-on-the-wire-the-textevent-stream-format-and-how-eventsource-reconnects))
- <a id="eventemitter"></a>**EventEmitter**: the RxJS `Subject` subclass that decorator outputs use ([19 §1](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#1-inputs-outputs-and-two-way-binding-the-parent-and-child-contract))
- <a id="events-and-controlevent"></a>**events and ControlEvent**: the unified stream of typed events (`ValueChangeEvent`, `StatusChangeEvent`, `PristineChangeEvent`, `TouchedChangeEvent`, `FormSubmittedEvent`, `FormResetEvent`), each with a `source`, since v18.0 ([25 §7](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#7-status-updateon-valuechanges-and-the-unified-events-stream))
- <a id="events-plugin-event-eventgroup-and-dispatcher"></a>**Events plugin**, **event**, **eventGroup** and **dispatcher**: the Flux-style layer of `@ngrx/signals/events`, a `{ type, payload }` object, the creator of a family of events typed `[Source] name`, and the bus that delivers them ([28 §9](modules/28-state-management.md#9-signalstore-in-depth-rxmethod-withentities-custom-features-and-the-events-plugin))
- <a id="exact-match"></a>**exact match**: an ICU plural case written `=n` that tests the value itself and wins over any plural category ([34 §5](modules/34-i18n.md#5-icu-expressions-plural-select-and-nesting))
- <a id="exact-match-nginx"></a>**exact match (Nginx)**: a `location =` block, used only for exactly that URI; finding one ends the search ([44 §3](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#3-nginx-for-a-single-page-app-location-matching-the-try_files-fallback-caching-headers-for-hashed-assets-and-gzip-or-brotli))
- <a id="exception-resolver"></a>**exception resolver**: a Spring MVC `HandlerExceptionResolver`, which turns an exception from mapping or a handler into a response ([41 §10](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#10-under-the-hood-one-failed-request-through-springs-filters-and-handlers-and-angulars-backend))
- <a id="excess-property-check"></a>**excess property check**: the extra check that rejects unknown properties in a fresh object literal ([06 §2](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#2-structural-typing-assignability-and-readonly)).
- <a id="exec-form"></a>**exec form**: a Dockerfile `CMD` or `ENTRYPOINT` written as a JSON array, run directly without a shell ([44 §6](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#6-serving-ssr-apps-in-containers-the-node-server-as-pid-1-graceful-shutdown-health-probes-and-what-nginx-or-a-cdn-still-serves))
- <a id="exfiltration"></a>**exfiltration**: copying a secret out of the page to a place the attacker controls ([33 §8](modules/33-security.md#8-where-the-token-lives-memory-web-storage-httponly-cookies-and-a-backend-for-frontend))
- <a id="exhaustive-check"></a>**exhaustive check**: `provideCheckNoChangesConfig({ exhaustive: true })`, which re-checks every view as if it were `Eager`, so it also reports plain-field writes hidden by `OnPush`; its `interval` repeats the check on a timer ([21 §8](modules/21-change-detection.md#8-debugging-the-profiler-providechecknochangesconfig-and-stability))
- <a id="exhaustiveness-of-a-switch"></a>**exhaustiveness (of a @switch)**: every member of the switched union has a `@case`, proved at compile time by `@default never;` ([18 §3](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#3-switch-and-exhaustive-checking-with-default-never))
- <a id="exhaustiveness-check"></a>**exhaustiveness check**: making the leftover type in a `default` branch reach `never`, so a missing case is a compile error ([06 §4](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#4-unions-intersections-and-discriminated-unions)).
- <a id="expand-and-contract"></a>**expand and contract**: shipping a breaking change in three steps: an API that accepts old and new, then the client, then the removal of the old form after the support window ([44 §7](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#7-cicd-for-both-layers-one-image-promoted-through-stages-a-monorepo-or-separate-repositories-and-versioning-the-contract))
- <a id="expected-error"></a>**expected error**: a failure the screen that made the call can name and recover from, such as a 404 for an optional resource ([37 §10](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#10-error-ux-expected-and-unexpected-errors-http-failures-offline-state-and-route-errors))
- <a id="expectnone"></a>**expectNone**: the controller method that throws when any open request matches ([27 §11](modules/27-http-client.md#11-testing-and-the-backend-seam-providehttpclienttesting-httptestingcontroller-and-what-they-replace))
- <a id="expectone"></a>**expectOne**: the controller method that returns the one open request matching a URL, object or predicate and throws on none or many ([27 §11](modules/27-http-client.md#11-testing-and-the-backend-seam-providehttpclienttesting-httptestingcontroller-and-what-they-replace))
- <a id="experimental-api-status"></a>**experimental (API status)**: an API that might not become stable, outside the versioning and deprecation policies ([39 §3](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#3-how-angular-ships-semantic-versioning-the-release-cadence-support-windows-and-the-deprecation-policy))
- <a id="exportas"></a>**exportAs**: the name under which a directive makes its instance available to a reference, as in `#t="labTally"` ([13 §6](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#6-template-reference-variables-and-let)).
- <a id="exports-ngmodule"></a>**exports (NgModule)**: the declarables (or modules) an NgModule makes usable in the templates of modules that import it ([15 §1](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#1-what-ngmodules-did-compilation-scope-injector-and-lazy-loading-unit)).
- <a id="exports-map"></a>**exports map**: the generated `package.json` field that lists the only subpaths a consumer can import ([38 §6](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#6-public-api-surfaces-public-apits-exports-secondary-entry-points-and-versioning))
- <a id="exposed-header"></a>**exposed header**: a response header listed in `Access-Control-Expose-Headers`, so cross-origin script may read it beyond the CORS-safelisted ones ([41 §1](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#1-cors-on-both-sides-springs-corsconfigurationsource-spring-security-preflight-caching-credentials-and-the-dev-proxy))
- <a id="exposed-module"></a>**exposed module**: a file a remote publishes under a key such as `./Component` ([38 §8](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#8-micro-frontends-module-federation-native-federation-and-when-they-are-justified))
- <a id="exposure-host-directive-inputs-and-outputs"></a>**exposure (host directive inputs and outputs)**: listing a host directive's input or output in `inputs`/`outputs` so templates can bind it, optionally under an alias ([14 §3](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#3-host-directives-the-composition-api)).
- <a id="express-middleware"></a>**Express middleware**: a function `(req, res, next)` that answers a request or calls `next()` to pass it on ([32 §2](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#2-adding-angularssr-what-ng-add-generates-the-build-output-and-the-server-entry))
- <a id="extended-diagnostic"></a>**extended diagnostic**: a compiler check beyond the type check, such as `NG8011`, reported as a warning by default ([12](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#q12-21)) ([19 §3](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#3-content-projection-ng-content-slots-ngprojectas-fallback-content-and-the-lifecycle-trap))
- <a id="extended-diagnostics"></a>**extended diagnostics**: optional compiler checks for likely template mistakes (codes `NG81xx`, plus `NG8011`), warnings by default and configurable per check ([12 §7](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#7-aot-versus-jit-and-template-type-checking)).
- <a id="extensible"></a>**extensible**: whether new properties can be added to an object; `preventExtensions`, `seal` and `freeze` turn it off ([03 §1](modules/03-js-objects-prototypes-classes.md#1-properties-and-descriptors)).
- <a id="extension-member"></a>**extension member**: a member a problem type adds beyond the standard five, such as an `errors` list; clients ignore the ones they do not recognize ([41 §5](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#5-error-contracts-with-problemdetail-rfc-9457-and-mapping-them-in-angular))
- <a id="external-store-react"></a>**external store (React)**: state held outside React, read in a component with `useSyncExternalStore` ([40 §8](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#8-choosing-a-framework-balanced-trade-offs-and-how-to-answer-why-angular))
- <a id="external-style"></a>**external style**: a `<link>` or `@import` reference in a component; Angular does not scope it ([13 §8](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#8-styling-strategies-component-styles-global-styles-css-variables-and-tailwind)).
- <a id="extract-function"></a>**extract function**: a refactoring step that moves a block of code into a named function without changing what it returns ([Exercise 47.1](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#ex47-1))
- <a id="extraction"></a>**extraction**: the build step (`ng extract-i18n`) that collects every message from the compiled app into one source file ([34 §6](modules/34-i18n.md#6-extraction-and-translation-files-ng-extract-i18n-formats-and-message-ids))
- <a id="extractvalue"></a>**extractValue**: a compat function that unwraps a field tree, controls included, into its raw value, optionally filtered by dirty, touched or enabled ([26 §9](modules/26-signal-forms.md#9-interop-and-migration-compatform-signalformcontrol-and-what-changed-from-v21-to-v22)).

## F

- <a id="facade"></a>**facade**: one class in front of several services, so consumers learn a single method ([16 §9](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#9-di-patterns-testing-and-the-spring-container)). · also in [41 §3](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#3-openapi-with-springdoc-and-generated-typescript-clients-openapi-generator-orval-and-ng-openapi-gen)
- <a id="facade-feature"></a>**facade (feature)**: one class in front of a feature's store and API that exposes read-only signals and intent methods ([38 §3](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#3-facades-and-the-data-access-layer))
- <a id="factory-function"></a>**factory function**: a function that returns the value for a token; Angular calls it on first lookup ([16 §2](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#2-providers-and-tokens-useclass-usevalue-usefactory-and-useexisting)).
- <a id="fail-fast"></a>**fail fast**: stopping at the first sign of misconfiguration, so the orchestrator sees the failure, instead of running on with a bad value ([44 §5](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#5-runtime-configuration-without-rebuilding-configjson-environment-substitution-at-container-start-and-provideappinitializer))
- <a id="failure-mode"></a>**failure mode**: a way a design breaks ([45 §1](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#1-the-front-end-system-design-interview-what-it-evaluates-its-formats-and-how-it-differs-from-a-backend-design-round))
- <a id="fake"></a>**fake**: a test double with a working implementation that takes a shortcut, such as an in-memory store ([29 §7](modules/29-testing-angular.md#7-test-doubles-fakes-stubs-spies-and-mocks-vifn-provider-overrides-and-over-mocking))
- <a id="fake-clock-vitest"></a>**fake clock (Vitest)**: `vi.useFakeTimers` replaces the named timer functions and, when `Date` is listed, the clock too, so code under test sees time pass only when the test runs the timers ([36 Q36.18](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#q36-18))
- <a id="fake-timers"></a>**fake timers**: a test clock that replaces the timer functions and is advanced by hand, `vi.useFakeTimers()` ([22 §8](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#8-timing-operators-debouncetime-throttletime-audittime-and-sampletime)) · also in [29 §10](modules/29-testing-angular.md#10-async-tests-vitest-fake-timers-versus-fakeasync-and-waitforasync)
- <a id="fallback-error-boundary"></a>**fallback (error boundary)**: the `@error` block a boundary renders in place of content that threw ([37 §9](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#9-error-boundaries-boundary-error-reset-onviewerror-and-onerror))
- <a id="fallback--rethrow-catcherror"></a>**fallback / rethrow (catchError)**: the replacement a `catchError` selector returns: a substitute value or stream (fallback), or a new error (rethrow) ([23 §3](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#3-errors-catcherror-placement-finalize-and-error-versus-complete))
- <a id="fallback-chain-custom-properties"></a>**fallback chain (custom properties)**: nested `var()` calls whose second argument applies when the first custom property is not set, such as a component token falling back to a system variable ([36 §13](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#13-under-the-hood-from-createoverlayref-to-a-popover-in-the-top-layer-and-from-mattheme-to-a-computed-color))
- <a id="fallback-content"></a>**fallback content**: the content inside an `<ng-content>` tag, shown when no node matched the slot ([19 §3](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#3-content-projection-ng-content-slots-ngprojectas-fallback-content-and-the-lifecycle-trap))
- <a id="fallback-language"></a>**fallback language**: the language a runtime library consults when the active one lacks a key ([34 §9](modules/34-i18n.md#9-runtime-translation-libraries-transloco-and-ngx-translate-lazy-scopes-and-the-trade-off-against-built-in-i18n))
- <a id="fallback-position"></a>**fallback position**: a later entry in a connected strategy's position list, used when an earlier one does not fit the viewport ([36 §5](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#5-portals-and-overlays-where-floating-ui-renders-how-it-is-positioned-and-how-it-closes))
- <a id="fallback-text"></a>**fallback text**: the text the server HTML shows where a browser-only value will go, replaced after hydration by `afterNextRender` ([32 Exercise 32.2](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#ex32-2))
- <a id="fallback-timer"></a>**fallback timer**: the `setTimeout` of the longest animation's duration plus 50 ms that ends a leave when no end event arrives ([35 §12](modules/35-animations.md#12-under-the-hood-from-animateleave-to-a-removed-node))
- <a id="false-negative"></a>**false negative**: a real defect a tool does not report ([11 §7](modules/11-accessibility.md#7-testing-accessibility)).
- <a id="fan-out"></a>**fan-out**: copying one message to every subscriber of a destination; a simple broker does it only inside its own JVM ([43 §9](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#9-scaling-out-and-under-the-hood-sticky-sessions-the-broker-relay-backpressure-and-one-message-traced-across-two-instances))
- <a id="feature"></a>**feature**: one named slice of the store's state tree; `createFeature` returns its name, reducer and one selector per state key ([28 §4](modules/28-state-management.md#4-ngrx-store-actions-reducers-createactiongroup-createfeature-and-runtime-checks))
- <a id="feature-area"></a>**feature area**: a part of the app named for what the user does, such as orders or catalog, that owns its own folder ([38 §1](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#1-folder-structures-that-scale-by-feature-domain-slices-and-core-shared-and-feature-boundaries))
- <a id="feature-boundary"></a>**feature boundary**: the limit around one feature's files ([45 §4](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#4-component-architecture-data-model-and-data-flow-from-screens-to-a-component-tree-and-a-client-side-model))
- <a id="feature-flag"></a>**feature flag**: a token whose value, or whose factory's choice, selects an implementation ([16 §9](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#9-di-patterns-testing-and-the-spring-container)). · also in [44 §8](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#8-feature-flags-with-openfeature-and-canary-releases-providers-evaluation-context-sticky-rollout-kill-switches-and-version-skew)
- <a id="feature-module"></a>**feature module**: an NgModule that groups one feature ([15 §1](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#1-what-ngmodules-did-compilation-scope-injector-and-lazy-loading-unit)).
- <a id="feature-store"></a>**feature store**: one service that owns a typed state in a private signal, exposes read-only selectors and changes the state only through methods ([28 §2](modules/28-state-management.md#2-the-service-with-signals-store-state-shape-derived-selectors-methods-and-async-loads))
- <a id="fesm"></a>**FESM**: a flat ES module, one file holding all of an entry point's code ([38 §5](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#5-angular-cli-libraries-ng-generate-library-ng-packagr-and-consuming-the-build))
- <a id="fetch-metadata"></a>**Fetch Metadata**: request headers the browser sets (`Sec-Fetch-Site`, `Sec-Fetch-Mode`, `Sec-Fetch-Dest`) that tell the server where a request came from ([33 §9](modules/33-security.md#9-csrf-in-single-page-apps-when-cookies-make-you-vulnerable-samesite-and-angulars-xsrf-support))
- <a id="fetchpriority"></a>**fetchpriority**: the HTML attribute hinting how urgently the browser should fetch an image ([31 §7](modules/31-performance.md#7-images-ngoptimizedimage-with-priority-fill-loaders-and-placeholders))
- <a id="field"></a>**field**: a node of the field tree, a function you call to get its `FieldState`; its properties are the child fields ([26 §2](modules/26-signal-forms.md#2-form-the-field-tree-and-field-state)).
- <a id="field-event-stream"></a>**field (event stream)**: one `name: value` line of an event stream; `data`, `event`, `id` and `retry` are known, others are ignored ([43 §2](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#2-server-sent-events-on-the-wire-the-textevent-stream-format-and-how-eventsource-reconnects))
- <a id="field-data"></a>**field data**: performance metrics collected from real users' devices and networks ([31 §1](modules/31-performance.md#1-measure-first-the-loop-lab-versus-field-and-what-each-tool-answers)) · also in [08 §6](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#6-core-web-vitals-lcp-inp-and-cls)
- <a id="field-data--lab-data"></a>**field data / lab data**: measurements from real users (CrUX, RUM) versus from one controlled load (Lighthouse, DevTools) ([08 §6](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#6-core-web-vitals-lcp-inp-and-cls)).
- <a id="field-initializer"></a>**field initializer**: the expression after `=` in a class body, run while the object is constructed, so `inject()` works there ([16 §4](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#4-the-injection-context-inject-runininjectioncontext-and-injectasync)). · also in [20 §2](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#2-constructor-ngoninit-and-inputs-what-exists-when)
- <a id="field-registry"></a>**field registry**: a strategy per field type, a lookup from a field's `type` to the template or component that renders it ([25 §9](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#9-dynamic-forms-formarray-patterns-and-config-driven-forms))
- <a id="field-state"></a>**field state**: the signals a field exposes next to its value: errors, touched, dirty, pending and the rest ([26 §1](modules/26-signal-forms.md#1-why-a-third-forms-api-model-first-signals-end-to-end))
- <a id="field-tree"></a>**field tree**: the object `form()` returns, shaped like the model, whose nodes expose each value and its field state as signals ([26 §1](modules/26-signal-forms.md#1-why-a-third-forms-api-model-first-signals-end-to-end))
- <a id="field-level-merge"></a>**field-level merge**: a write policy that accepts concurrent edits to different fields and refuses edits to a field changed since the read ([45 §8](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#8-under-the-hood-failure-modes-observability-offline-levels-and-consistency-choices))
- <a id="fieldcontext"></a>**FieldContext**: the object a rule receives, with `value`, `valueOf(path)`, `stateOf(path)` and `fieldTree`, so a rule can read other fields ([26 §5](modules/26-signal-forms.md#5-cross-field-and-conditional-logic-valueof-validatetree-applywhen-disabled-hidden-and-readonly)).
- <a id="fieldstate"></a>**FieldState**: the object a field returns: `value` (writable) and the signals for errors, validity, touched, dirty and the rest, with `markAsTouched()`, `markAsDirty()`, `reset()` and `getError()` ([26 §2](modules/26-signal-forms.md#2-form-the-field-tree-and-field-state)).
- <a id="file-replacement"></a>**file replacement**: a configuration entry that makes the build load one source file in place of another ([12 §4](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#4-environments-and-configuration-build-time-versus-run-time)).
- <a id="filename"></a>**filename***: the `Content-Disposition` parameter that carries the file name as charset'language'percent-encoded text, for non-Latin names (RFC 5987) ([41 §8](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#8-files-multipart-and-chunked-uploads-progress-and-downloads-with-content-disposition-and-streaming))
- <a id="fill-mode"></a>**fill mode**: `NgOptimizedImage`'s option where the image takes its parent's box and carries no `width` or `height` ([31 §7](modules/31-performance.md#7-images-ngoptimizedimage-with-priority-fill-loaders-and-placeholders))
- <a id="fill-mode-animation"></a>**fill mode (animation)**: what a CSS animation applies outside its active interval: `backwards` the first keyframe during the delay, `forwards` the last keyframe after the end, `both`, or `none` (the default) ([35 §3](modules/35-animations.md#3-css-keyframe-animations-keyframes-fill-modes-iteration-and-component-scoped-names))
- <a id="filter-chain"></a>**filter chain**: the servlet filters a request passes before the servlet, each able to answer and stop it, such as Spring Security's ([41 §10](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#10-under-the-hood-one-failed-request-through-springs-filters-and-handlers-and-angulars-backend))
- <a id="filter-order"></a>**filter order**: each Spring Security filter's fixed place in the chain, set by the internal `FilterOrderRegistration` ([42 §9](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#9-under-the-hood-one-bff-login-and-the-first-api-call-through-spring-securitys-filters))
- <a id="finalize"></a>**finalize**: an operator whose callback runs once when the subscription ends by complete, error or unsubscribe, after the source teardown ([23 §3](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#3-errors-catcherror-placement-finalize-and-error-versus-complete))
- <a id="finalizer"></a>**finalizer**: a teardown stored in a subscription's internal `_finalizers` list and run on `unsubscribe` ([22 §9](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#9-under-the-hood-subscribe-the-subscriber-chain-and-the-subjects-observer-list))
- <a id="fine-grained-reactivity"></a>**fine-grained reactivity**: refreshing only the views that read the changed value ([17 §1](modules/17-signals.md#1-why-signals-exist)). · also in [40 §4](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#4-signals-beyond-angular-vue-refs-solid-svelte-runes-preact-and-qwik)
- <a id="finished-promise"></a>**finished promise**: the promise of an `Animation` that resolves when it finishes and rejects with an `AbortError` when it is cancelled ([35 §4](modules/35-animations.md#4-the-web-animations-api-elementanimate-the-animation-object-and-getanimations))
- <a id="first-party-package"></a>**first-party package**: a package the project's own team maintains ([40 §1](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#1-library-framework-progressive-framework-what-each-project-decides-for-you))
- <a id="first-screen-read"></a>**first-screen read**: the data the first view needs to render ([45 §5](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#5-state-and-api-design-where-each-piece-of-state-lives-and-the-api-the-screens-need))
