- <a id="fixture"></a>**fixture**: the test's handle on one component rendered by `TestBed`, with its element, instance and change-detection controls ([29 §5](modules/29-testing-angular.md#5-zoneless-fixtures-whenstable-testbedtick-detectchanges-and-automatic-change-detection))
- <a id="fixture-playwright"></a>**fixture (Playwright)**: a value `test.extend` builds for a test, with setup before `use` and teardown after; unrelated to Angular's `ComponentFixture` ([30 §4](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#4-locators-page-objects-and-fixtures))
- <a id="flaky-budget"></a>**flaky budget**: a cap on flaky outcomes past which fixing them comes before new work ([30 §8](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#8-ci-strategy-for-end-to-end-tests-workers-sharding-retries-policy-and-test-data))
- <a id="flaky-test"></a>**flaky test**: a test that gives both a passing and a failing result on the same code ([30 §7](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#7-flaky-tests-root-causes-diagnosis-and-fixes))
- <a id="flame-chart"></a>**flame chart**: a recording drawn with time across and the call stack downward ([31 §1](modules/31-performance.md#1-measure-first-the-loop-lab-versus-field-and-what-each-tool-answers))
- <a id="flame-graph"></a>**flame graph**: the DevTools Profiler view where each tile is an element, the x-axis is time and the y-axis the element hierarchy ([21 §8](modules/21-change-detection.md#8-debugging-the-profiler-providechecknochangesconfig-and-stability))
- <a id="flat-config"></a>**flat config**: ESLint's `eslint.config.js`, an array of file patterns and rules ([38 §9](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#9-linting-and-formatting-angular-eslint-prettier-import-ordering-and-commit-hooks))
- <a id="flattening-operator"></a>**flattening operator**: an operator that maps each outer value to an inner Observable, subscribes to it and forwards its values into one stream (`switchMap`, `mergeMap`, `concatMap`, `exhaustMap`) ([23 §1](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#1-higher-order-observables-switchmap-mergemap-concatmap-and-exhaustmap))
- <a id="flex-container--flex-item"></a>**flex container / flex item**: an element with `display: flex` and its direct children, laid out along one axis ([10 §3](modules/10-css-essentials.md#3-flexbox-grid-and-subgrid)).
- <a id="flip"></a>**FLIP**: a pattern, not an Angular feature: First (read positions), Last (let the change happen), Invert (offset each element back with a `transform`), Play (animate the offset to none) ([35 §7](modules/35-animations.md#7-animating-lists-for-enter-leave-reorder-and-stagger))
- <a id="fluid-typography"></a>**fluid typography**: a font size that scales with the viewport between a minimum and a maximum, written with `clamp()` ([10 §5](modules/10-css-essentials.md#5-responsive-css-media-queries-container-queries-and-fluid-type)).
- <a id="flush"></a>**flush**: running the queued virtual actions in frame order, which `run` does after the callback ([23 §10](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#10-testing-with-testscheduler-marble-syntax-and-virtual-time-under-the-hood))
- <a id="flush-http-testing"></a>**flush (HTTP testing)**: the `TestRequest` method that answers with a body and status, as a response for 2xx and an `HttpErrorResponse` otherwise ([27 §11](modules/27-http-client.md#11-testing-and-the-backend-seam-providehttpclienttesting-httptestingcontroller-and-what-they-replace))
- <a id="flux"></a>**Flux**: Project Reactor's asynchronous sequence of 0 to N items ([22 §3](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#3-cold-and-hot-unicast-and-multicast))
- <a id="focus--focusable-element"></a>**focus / focusable element**: the element that receives keyboard input, and an element that can take it ([11 §4](modules/11-accessibility.md#4-keyboard-focus-order-roving-tabindex-and-focus-traps)).
- <a id="focus-indicator"></a>**focus indicator**: the visible mark of keyboard focus, styled with `:focus` or `:focus-visible` ([11 §4](modules/11-accessibility.md#4-keyboard-focus-order-roving-tabindex-and-focus-traps)).
- <a id="focus-mode"></a>**focus mode**: the Angular Aria input that puts DOM focus on the active item (`roving`) or keeps it on the container, which names the active item in `aria-activedescendant` (`activedescendant`) ([36 §11](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#11-angular-aria-headless-signal-driven-aria-patterns))
- <a id="focus-origin"></a>**focus origin**: how an element received focus, one of `keyboard`, `mouse`, `touch` or `program` (code), which `FocusMonitor` reports ([36 §9](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#9-cdk-accessibility-focustrap-focusmonitor-liveannouncer-and-the-key-managers))
- <a id="focus-restoration"></a>**focus restoration**: returning focus to a sensible element after an action ([45 §6](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#6-performance-and-accessibility-as-design-steps-budgets-a-loading-plan-long-lists-and-keyboard-and-pointer-paths))
- <a id="focus-trap"></a>**focus trap**: a region that sends Tab back inside it, which the CDK builds from two invisible anchor elements around the region ([36 §9](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#9-cdk-accessibility-focustrap-focusmonitor-liveannouncer-and-the-key-managers))
- <a id="focus-trap--modal-dialog"></a>**focus trap / modal dialog**: code that keeps Tab inside a region, and a dialog that makes the rest of the page inert while open ([11 §4](modules/11-accessibility.md#4-keyboard-focus-order-roving-tabindex-and-focus-traps)).
- <a id="follow-up-ladder"></a>**follow-up ladder**: our term for six follow-up questions that drill into one story: what you did, how you know, what it cost, what else could explain it, what you would change, what happened after ([47 §7](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#7-under-the-hood-how-one-story-is-drilled-into-numbers-you-can-defend-and-telling-a-design-or-a-migration-as-your-own-project))
- <a id="follow-up-schematic"></a>**follow-up schematic**: a migration run after the three passes to finish the job, such as `route-lazy-loading`, `cleanup-unused-imports` or `common-to-standalone` ([15 §7](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#7-migrating-with-ng-generate-angularcorestandalone-three-passes-hybrid-apps-and-common-failures)).
- <a id="forced-synchronous-layout"></a>**forced synchronous layout**: a geometry read (`offsetHeight`, `getBoundingClientRect()`) after a style write, which makes the browser run layout immediately inside the current task ([08 §2](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#2-layout-paint-and-composite-what-each-change-costs)).
- <a id="forchild"></a>**forChild**: by convention, a static NgModule method that returns the module without the root-only providers, for feature and lazy modules ([15 §5](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#5-legacy-ngmodule-patterns-declarations-exports-forrootforchild-sharedmodule-and-coremodule)).
- <a id="forkjoin"></a>**forkJoin**: emits the last value of each source once all have completed, and nothing if one completes empty ([22 §7](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#7-combining-streams-combinelatest-withlatestfrom-forkjoin-zip-merge-and-concat))
- <a id="form-model"></a>**form model**: the tree of `FormControl`, `FormGroup` and related objects that holds a form's values and states ([25 §1](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#1-two-forms-apis-template-driven-and-reactive-and-the-control-directive-and-accessor-triangle))
- <a id="form-state"></a>**form state**: a draft with validity, owned by the forms API ([28 §1](modules/28-state-management.md#1-kinds-of-state-server-cache-ui-form-url-and-session-state-and-where-each-belongs))
- <a id="form_field"></a>**FORM_FIELD**: the injection token the `FormField` directive provides ([26 §8](modules/26-signal-forms.md#8-custom-controls-formvaluecontrol-formcheckboxcontrol-and-how-formfield-picks-a-control)).
- <a id="form"></a>**form()**: the function that wraps a writable model signal and returns its field tree; it needs an injection context or the `injector` option ([26 §2](modules/26-signal-forms.md#2-form-the-field-tree-and-field-state)).
- <a id="formarrayname-and-formarray"></a>**formArrayName and [formArray]**: two directives: the first links a nested `FormArray` by name, the second binds a root `FormArray` to an element ([25 §9](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#9-dynamic-forms-formarray-patterns-and-config-driven-forms))
- <a id="formatter"></a>**formatter**: a tool that rewrites layout only, such as line breaks and quotes ([38 §9](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#9-linting-and-formatting-angular-eslint-prettier-import-ordering-and-commit-hooks))
- <a id="formatter-intl"></a>**formatter (Intl)**: an object built once from a locale and options that formats, selects or compares values, such as `Intl.DateTimeFormat` or `Intl.Collator` ([34 §2](modules/34-i18n.md#2-the-platforms-intl-apis-dates-numbers-plurals-lists-collation-and-display-names))
- <a id="formatting-context"></a>**formatting context**: a region of the page with one set of layout rules (block, inline, flex, grid) ([10 §1](modules/10-css-essentials.md#1-the-box-model-and-formatting-contexts)).
- <a id="formatting-function"></a>**formatting function**: `formatDate`, `formatNumber`, `formatCurrency` and `formatPercent` from `@angular/common`, which the built-in pipes call ([34 §3](modules/34-i18n.md#3-angulars-locale-data-and-formatting-functions-versus-intl))
- <a id="formatting-pipe"></a>**formatting pipe**: a pipe (`date`, `currency`, `number`, `percent`, `i18nPlural`) that turns a value into text using locale data ([14 §6](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#6-built-in-pipes-and-locale-data)).
- <a id="formbuilder-and-nonnullableformbuilder"></a>**FormBuilder and NonNullableFormBuilder**: shorthand that builds the model classes from plain values, with nullable controls for `FormBuilder` and non-nullable ones for `NonNullableFormBuilder` ([25 §3](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#3-typed-forms-formbuilder-and-nonnullableformbuilder))
- <a id="formcheckboxcontrol"></a>**FormCheckboxControl**: the contract for a boolean control: a required `checked` model and a `value` that must stay undefined ([26 §8](modules/26-signal-forms.md#8-custom-controls-formvaluecontrol-formcheckboxcontrol-and-how-formfield-picks-a-control)).
- <a id="formfield"></a>**FormField**: the directive with selector `[formField]` that binds one field to one native or custom control, writing both ways and reporting blur as touched ([26 §3](modules/26-signal-forms.md#3-binding-with-formfield-native-inputs-attributes-classes-and-rendering)).
- <a id="formgroup-and-formrecord"></a>**FormGroup and FormRecord**: a control holding named children, of a fixed shape for `FormGroup` and of one type under run-time keys for `FormRecord`, which extends it ([25 §2](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#2-the-reactive-model-formcontrol-formgroup-formarray-and-formrecord))
- <a id="formroot"></a>**FormRoot**: the `form[formRoot]` directive that sets `novalidate`, prevents the native submit and calls `submit()` when the form defines `submission` options ([26 §7](modules/26-signal-forms.md#7-submission-submit-formroot-submitting-and-server-errors)).
- <a id="formuicontrol"></a>**FormUiControl**: the base interface whose optional inputs and `touch` output `[formField]` binds when a control declares them ([26 §8](modules/26-signal-forms.md#8-custom-controls-formvaluecontrol-formcheckboxcontrol-and-how-formfield-picks-a-control)).
- <a id="formvaluecontrol"></a>**FormValueControl**: the contract for a control that edits a value: a required `value` model and a `checked` that must stay undefined ([26 §8](modules/26-signal-forms.md#8-custom-controls-formvaluecontrol-formcheckboxcontrol-and-how-formfield-picks-a-control)).
- <a id="forroot"></a>**forRoot**: by convention, a static NgModule method that returns the module plus its root-only providers (configuration, singletons) ([15 §5](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#5-legacy-ngmodule-patterns-declarations-exports-forrootforchild-sharedmodule-and-coremodule)).
- <a id="forward-reference"></a>**forward reference**: `forwardRef(() => X)`, a wrapper that delays reading a class declared later; it does not break a circular dependency ([16 §8](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#8-lifetime-and-dynamic-injectors-destroyref-environmentinjector-injectorcreate-forward-references-and-cycles)).
- <a id="fr"></a>**fr**: the grid unit for a fraction of the leftover space ([10 §3](modules/10-css-essentials.md#3-flexbox-grid-and-subgrid)).
- <a id="fragile-base-class"></a>**fragile base class**: the problem that changes to a base class silently break subclasses that depend on its internals ([03 §4](modules/03-js-objects-prototypes-classes.md#4-inheritance-composition-and-mixins)).
- <a id="fragment"></a>**fragment**: the part after `#`, shared by the whole URL ([24 §2](modules/24-routing.md#2-route-parameters-path-matrix-query-and-fragment-inheritance-and-component-input-binding))
- <a id="frame"></a>**frame**: one tick of virtual time in a marble diagram; frame 0 is the first character ([22 §7](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#7-combining-streams-combinelatest-withlatestfrom-forkjoin-zip-merge-and-concat))
- <a id="frame-websocket"></a>**frame (WebSocket)**: the unit of data on a WebSocket connection, sent by either side after the handshake ([43 §5](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#5-websockets-and-stomp-in-spring-the-handshake-frames-messagemapping-brokers-user-destinations-and-heart-beats))
- <a id="framework"></a>**framework**: code that calls yours; it owns the entry point, creates your objects and decides when they run ([12 §1](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#1-what-a-framework-does-and-angulars-philosophy)). · also in [12 §1](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#1-what-a-framework-does-and-angulars-philosophy)
- <a id="fresh--stale"></a>**fresh / stale**: a stored response is fresh while its age is within its freshness lifetime, and stale after; a stale one must be revalidated or may be reused only where allowed ([09 §4](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#4-http-caching)).
- <a id="full-duplex"></a>**full duplex**: both sides of a connection may send at any time ([43 §1](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#1-choosing-a-transport-polling-long-polling-server-sent-events-websockets-and-web-push-when-no-tab-is-open))
- <a id="function-form-animateenteranimateleave"></a>**function form (animate.enter/animate.leave)**: the event-binding syntax `(animate.leave)="fn($event)"` that calls a method with an `AnimationCallbackEvent` instead of adding classes ([35 §6](modules/35-animations.md#6-function-callbacks-and-the-v22-leave-semantics-animationcallbackevent-animationcomplete-and-nested-leaves))
- <a id="functional-effect"></a>**functional effect**: an effect written as a constant with `createEffect(fn, { functional: true })` and registered with `provideEffects({ … })` ([28 §6](modules/28-state-management.md#6-effects-functional-effects-flattening-error-handling-and-dispatch-false))
- <a id="functional-requirement"></a>**functional requirement**: a statement of what the product does ([45 §1](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#1-the-front-end-system-design-interview-what-it-evaluates-its-formats-and-how-it-differs-from-a-backend-design-round))

## G

- <a id="gap"></a>**gap**: a delta whose sequence number skips the next expected one, meaning a change was lost, so the store reloads the snapshot ([43 §8](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#8-from-streams-to-signals-one-shared-connection-tosignal-and-rxresource-bursts-and-connection-state))
- <a id="gate"></a>**gate**: the rule that a later stage starts only if the earlier one passed ([38 §11](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#11-a-ci-pipeline-for-a-front-end-lint-test-build-end-to-end-and-deploy-previews))
- <a id="gauge-widget-kind"></a>**gauge (widget kind)**: a widget kind whose window value is the last event ([46 §6](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#6-a-real-time-operations-dashboard-aggregation-per-widget-staleness-hidden-tabs-and-screens-left-open-for-days))
- <a id="gc-root"></a>**GC root**: a starting point the garbage collector always keeps, such as `window`; anything reachable from it stays in memory ([31 §8](modules/31-performance.md#8-memory-leaks-heap-snapshots-detached-nodes-and-the-devtools-workflow))
- <a id="gc-roots"></a>**GC roots**: where reachability starts: the global object, the call stacks, and host handles such as active timers and listeners ([05 §4](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#4-garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks)).
- <a id="generated-client"></a>**generated client**: the models and request functions or services a code generator emits; kept behind a data-access facade ([41 §3](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#3-openapi-with-springdoc-and-generated-typescript-clients-openapi-generator-orval-and-ng-openapi-gen))
- <a id="generational-hypothesis"></a>**generational hypothesis**: "most objects die young", the basis for collecting a small young generation often and the old generation rarely ([05 §4](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#4-garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks)).
- <a id="generator"></a>**generator**: a function declared with `function*` that can pause at `yield` and returns an iterator over its yields ([03 §8](modules/03-js-objects-prototypes-classes.md#8-iteration-protocols-and-generators)).
- <a id="generic-component"></a>**generic component**: a component class with a type parameter that the template type-checker infers from the host's bindings under `strictTemplates` ([07 §9](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#9-typing-patterns-in-angular-code)).
- <a id="getprerenderparams"></a>**getPrerenderParams**: the function on a parameterized `Prerender` server route that returns the parameter values to build at build time ([32 §3](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#3-server-routes-and-rendermode-per-route-rendering-getprerenderparams-fallbacks-headers-and-status))
- <a id="getrawvalue"></a>**getRawValue()**: a form group's full value including disabled controls, unlike `value`, which is `Partial` ([07 §9](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#9-typing-patterns-in-angular-code)).
- <a id="getvalue"></a>**getValue()**: the synchronous read of a `BehaviorSubject`'s current value; it notifies no view ([28 §3](modules/28-state-management.md#3-the-service-with-behaviorsubject-store-the-legacy-but-common-pattern-and-its-migration))
- <a id="glitch"></a>**glitch**: a derived value computed from an inconsistent mix of new and old inputs; Angular's graph is glitch-free ([17 §9](modules/17-signals.md#9-under-the-hood-the-reactive-graph)).
- <a id="global-augmentation"></a>**global augmentation**: `declare global { … }` inside a module, adding to global types such as `Window` ([06 §8](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#8-declaration-files-types-and-augmentation)).
- <a id="global-event-target"></a>**global event target**: a `window:`, `document:` or `body:` prefix that makes a `host` listener attach to that object instead of the element ([13 §5](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#5-event-binding-and-two-way-binding)).
- <a id="global-input"></a>**global input**: a file outside every project, such as the root `package.json`, whose change touches every project ([Exercise 38.4](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#ex38-4))
- <a id="global-mode--targeted-mode"></a>**global mode / targeted mode**: the two modes of the walk: global refreshes every `Eager` (`CheckAlways`) view and every flagged view, targeted only views flagged `RefreshView` or whose signal reader is dirty ([21 §4](modules/21-change-detection.md#4-the-tree-walk-global-and-targeted-refreshes))
- <a id="global-style"></a>**global style**: a stylesheet listed in `angular.json` `styles`; its rules are not rewritten and reach every element ([13 §8](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#8-styling-strategies-component-styles-global-styles-css-variables-and-tailwind)).
- <a id="global-symbol-registry"></a>**global symbol registry**: the process-wide table behind `Symbol.for(key)`, which returns the same symbol for the same key ([01 §4](modules/01-js-values-types-coercion.md#4-symbols)).
- <a id="global-variant"></a>**global variant**: a locale file under `@angular/common/locales/global/` that assigns its data to `ng.common.locales` on the global object instead of exporting it ([34 §3](modules/34-i18n.md#3-angulars-locale-data-and-formatting-functions-versus-intl))
- <a id="gold-plating"></a>**gold-plating**: adding features or polish nobody asked for ([47 §5](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#5-take-home-assignments-scope-and-time-box-what-the-repository-must-show-the-readme-and-the-review-call))
- <a id="grace-period"></a>**grace period**: the wait after the last reader leaves during which a returning reader cancels the close, so a route change keeps the socket ([43 §8](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#8-from-streams-to-signals-one-shared-connection-tosignal-and-rxresource-bursts-and-connection-state))
- <a id="grace-period-share"></a>**grace period (share)**: a reset notifier such as `resetOnRefCountZero: () => timer(n)` that delays the reset, so a subscriber arriving within the wait reuses the run ([23 §5](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#5-multicasting-in-depth-share-configuration-sharereplay-and-connect))
- <a id="grace-period-shutdown"></a>**grace period (shutdown)**: how long `docker stop` (10 s by default for Linux containers) or an orchestrator waits after the stop signal before sending SIGKILL ([44 §6](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#6-serving-ssr-apps-in-containers-the-node-server-as-pid-1-graceful-shutdown-health-probes-and-what-nginx-or-a-cdn-still-serves))
- <a id="graceful-shutdown"></a>**graceful shutdown**: stopping in order: mark not ready, let routing stop, stop accepting, finish in-flight requests, exit before the deadline ([44 §6](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#6-serving-ssr-apps-in-containers-the-node-server-as-pid-1-graceful-shutdown-health-probes-and-what-nginx-or-a-cdn-still-serves))
- <a id="granularity"></a>**granularity**: the unit a write re-runs: a view, a component or one DOM expression ([40 §4](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#4-signals-beyond-angular-vue-refs-solid-svelte-runes-preact-and-qwik))
- <a id="grapheme-cluster"></a>**grapheme cluster**: what a reader sees as one character, possibly several code points; split with `Intl.Segmenter` ([01 §3](modules/01-js-values-types-coercion.md#3-strings-and-unicode)).
- <a id="guaranteed-invalid-value"></a>**guaranteed-invalid value**: the initial value of every custom property; using it in `var()` without a fallback invalidates the declaration ([10 §6](modules/10-css-essentials.md#6-custom-properties-theming-and-color-scheme)).
- <a id="guard"></a>**guard**: a function the router calls at one step of a navigation (`canMatch`, `canActivate`, `canActivateChild`, `canDeactivate`) to decide whether the navigation goes on ([24 §5](modules/24-routing.md#5-functional-guards-canmatch-canactivate-candeactivate-and-redirectcommand))
- <a id="guard-factory"></a>**guard factory**: a function that returns a guard and closes over its arguments, such as `requireRole('admin')` ([24 §5](modules/24-routing.md#5-functional-guards-canmatch-canactivate-candeactivate-and-redirectcommand))
- <a id="guardresult"></a>**GuardResult**: `boolean | UrlTree | RedirectCommand`, what a guard returns: `false` cancels, the other two redirect ([24 §5](modules/24-routing.md#5-functional-guards-canmatch-canactivate-candeactivate-and-redirectcommand))
- <a id="gzip_static"></a>**gzip_static**: an Nginx module, not built by default, that sends `file.gz` instead of `file` when it exists and the client accepts gzip ([44 §3](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#3-nginx-for-a-single-page-app-location-matching-the-try_files-fallback-caching-headers-for-hashed-assets-and-gzip-or-brotli))
- <a id="gzip_types"></a>**gzip_types**: the MIME types Nginx gzips in addition to `text/html`, its default ([44 §3](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#3-nginx-for-a-single-page-app-location-matching-the-try_files-fallback-caching-headers-for-hashed-assets-and-gzip-or-brotli))

## H

- <a id="handler-mapping"></a>**handler mapping**: the Spring MVC strategy that picks the controller method for a request, such as `RequestMappingHandlerMapping` ([41 §10](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#10-under-the-hood-one-failed-request-through-springs-filters-and-handlers-and-angulars-backend))
- <a id="handshake-principal"></a>**handshake principal**: the user authenticated on the HTTP upgrade request, which Spring keeps for the WebSocket session and stamps on every STOMP message ([43 §7](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#7-authentication-on-the-handshake-cookies-tokens-in-the-connect-frame-origin-checks-and-csrf-in-spring-security))
- <a id="har-http-archive"></a>**HAR (HTTP Archive)**: a JSON record of requests and responses that `routeFromHAR` can replay ([30 §5](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#5-api-mocking-at-the-network-layer-and-where-contract-tests-take-over))
- <a id="harmonised-standard"></a>**harmonised standard**: a European standard adopted on a Commission request for Union harmonisation legislation (Directive (EU) 2019/882 Article 3, Regulation (EU) No 1025/2012); citation in the Official Journal gives a presumption of conformity ([11 §1](modules/11-accessibility.md#1-why-accessibility-and-what-the-standards-require)).
- <a id="harness-environment"></a>**harness environment**: the object that connects component harnesses to a test runner; `TestbedHarnessEnvironment` is the TestBed one and `SeleniumWebDriverHarnessEnvironment` the Selenium one ([36 §10](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#10-component-harnesses-for-material-the-cdk-and-your-own-components))
- <a id="harness-loader"></a>**harness loader**: the object that finds harnesses under a root element; `TestbedHarnessEnvironment.loader(fixture)` creates one ([29 §11](modules/29-testing-angular.md#11-component-harnesses-and-angular-testing-library)) · also in [36 §10](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#10-component-harnesses-for-material-the-cdk-and-your-own-components)
- <a id="harness-predicate"></a>**harness predicate**: a `HarnessPredicate` that narrows a harness query by options such as a label ([29 §11](modules/29-testing-angular.md#11-component-harnesses-and-angular-testing-library)) · also in [36 §10](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#10-component-harnesses-for-material-the-cdk-and-your-own-components)
- <a id="hash-based-policy"></a>**hash-based policy**: a CSP that trusts inline scripts by the SHA-256 of their text, so a cached page stays valid ([33 §5](modules/33-security.md#5-content-security-policy-with-angular-nonces-csp_nonce-and-autocsp))
- <a id="head-sampling"></a>**head sampling**: deciding whether a trace is recorded when it starts, before anything about its outcome is known ([44 §10](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#10-under-the-hood-opentelemetry-for-the-web-traceparent-and-one-click-traced-to-a-spring-boot-span-and-log-line))
- <a id="head-of-line-hol-blocking"></a>**head-of-line (HOL) blocking**: one stalled item delaying everything queued behind it: requests on an HTTP/1.1 connection, or every stream on one TCP connection ([09 §3](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#3-http11-http2-and-http3)).
- <a id="header-row"></a>**header row**: the table row, set by `cdkHeaderRowDef`, that lists the columns whose header templates the table shows ([36 §7](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#7-tables-and-long-lists-cdktable-datasource-mattable-and-virtual-scrolling))
- <a id="heading-level"></a>**heading level**: the number 1 to 6 of an `h1` to `h6`, which gives the page its outline ([11 §2](modules/11-accessibility.md#2-semantic-html-landmarks-and-headings)).
- <a id="headless-component"></a>**headless component**: a component or directive that owns behavior and ARIA attributes but renders no styles, so the author supplies markup and look ([36 §1](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#1-material-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-styled-components-behavior-primitives-and-headless-patterns)) · also in [36 Q36.22](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#q36-22)
- <a id="headless-directive"></a>**headless directive**: a directive that adds behavior and ARIA state to an element the author wrote and brings no styles, such as Angular Aria's `ngListbox` ([36 §11](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#11-angular-aria-headless-signal-driven-aria-patterns))
- <a id="healthcheck"></a>**HEALTHCHECK**: the Dockerfile instruction that runs a command periodically and marks the container unhealthy after `--retries` consecutive failures ([44 §6](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#6-serving-ssr-apps-in-containers-the-node-server-as-pid-1-graceful-shutdown-health-probes-and-what-nginx-or-a-cdn-still-serves))
- <a id="heap-snapshot"></a>**heap snapshot**: a DevTools capture of every object and its retainers, compared across snapshots to find what grows ([05 §4](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#4-garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks)). · also in [31 §8](modules/31-performance.md#8-memory-leaks-heap-snapshots-detached-nodes-and-the-devtools-workflow)
- <a id="heart-beat-stomp"></a>**heart-beat (STOMP)**: an EOL sent when no frame is due; `heart-beat:<can send>,<wants>` on `CONNECT` and `CONNECTED` sets each direction to the max of the two, or none when either is 0 ([43 §5](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#5-websockets-and-stomp-in-spring-the-handshake-frames-messagemapping-brokers-user-destinations-and-heart-beats))
- <a id="heartbeat"></a>**heartbeat**: a message sent only to keep an idle connection alive or detect a dead one; for SSE, a comment line every 15 seconds or so ([43 §2](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#2-server-sent-events-on-the-wire-the-textevent-stream-format-and-how-eventsource-reconnects))
- <a id="heartbeat-presence"></a>**heartbeat (presence)**: a small message an active editor sends so its entry is renewed; not a STOMP heart-beat, which shows a connection is alive ([46 §7](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#7-a-notifications-center-and-an-editing-indicator-delivery-unread-counts-across-tabs-and-presence-with-a-time-to-live))
- <a id="heartbeat-comment"></a>**heartbeat comment**: a comment-only event sent periodically so that the write fails when the client has gone ([43 §3](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#3-server-sent-events-in-spring-sseemitter-in-spring-mvc-and-a-flux-of-serversentevent-in-webflux))
- <a id="hidden-source-map"></a>**hidden source map**: a map the build writes for error reporting tools, set with `sourceMap.hidden` ([37 §11](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#11-logging-and-error-tracking-a-reporter-port-context-privacy-source-maps-and-sampling))
- <a id="hidden-tab"></a>**hidden tab**: a page whose document is not visible, such as a background tab, where timers are throttled and frames stop ([46 §6](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#6-a-real-time-operations-dashboard-aggregation-per-widget-staleness-hidden-tabs-and-screens-left-open-for-days))
- <a id="higher-order-function"></a>**higher-order function**: a function that takes or returns a function ([02 §6](modules/02-js-scope-closures-this.md#6-higher-order-functions-currying-partial-application-debounce-and-throttle)).
- <a id="higher-order-mapping"></a>**higher-order mapping**: an operator that takes a function returning an Observable, such as `switchMap` (module 23) ([22 §6](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#6-the-operator-catalogue-creation-transformation-filtering-utility-conditional-and-aggregation))
- <a id="higher-order-observable"></a>**higher-order Observable**: an Observable whose values are Observables ([23 §1](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#1-higher-order-observables-switchmap-mergemap-concatmap-and-exhaustmap))
- <a id="hoisting"></a>**hoisting**: declarations are set up when their scope is entered, before any of its code runs ([02 §2](modules/02-js-scope-closures-this.md#2-hoisting-and-the-temporal-dead-zone)).
- <a id="home-object"></a>**home object**: the object a method is defined on, fixed at definition time, from which `super` lookups start ([03 §3](modules/03-js-objects-prototypes-classes.md#3-classes-the-sugar-and-what-is-not-sugar)).
- <a id="homomorphic-mapped-type"></a>**homomorphic mapped type**: a mapped type over `keyof T`, which keeps each property's `readonly` and `?` modifiers ([07 §2](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#2-keyof-indexed-access-mapped-and-template-literal-types)).
- <a id="hop"></a>**hop**: one `ng update` from one major to the next; a version can be skipped in the calendar but not in the update ([39 §8](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#8-skipping-versions-safely-one-major-per-hop-node-and-typescript-ranges-and-libraries-that-block-a-hop); owner of the mechanics [12 §2](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#2-the-cli-and-the-workspace-angularjson-projects-schematics-and-ng-update))
- <a id="hop-network"></a>**hop (network)**: one more network step between the browser and the service that answers ([44 §1](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#1-where-the-api-calls-go-direct-calls-a-same-origin-reverse-proxy-an-api-gateway-or-a-backend-for-frontend))
- <a id="hop-by-hop-header"></a>**hop-by-hop header**: a header meant for one connection only, so a proxy does not pass it on; `Upgrade` and `Connection` are examples ([44 §4](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#4-nginx-as-the-reverse-proxy-proxy_pass-forwarded-headers-into-spring-boot-and-sse-or-websocket-connections-through-the-proxy))
- <a id="host"></a>**host**: the environment embedding the JavaScript engine (a browser, Node.js) that provides the event loop, timers and I/O ([04 §1](modules/04-js-async-event-loop.md#1-the-event-loop-tasks-microtasks-and-rendering)).
- <a id="host-shell"></a>**host (shell)**: the app that loads micro-frontends at run time ([38 §8](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#8-micro-frontends-module-federation-native-federation-and-when-they-are-justified))
- <a id="host-binding"></a>**host binding**: a binding declared in a component's `host` metadata (or with `@HostBinding`) that writes to the component's own element, not to its template ([13 §7](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#7-the-host-element-host-metadata-hostbinding-and-hostlistener)). · also in [14 §1](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#1-directives-and-attribute-directives-host-inputs-elementref-and-renderer2)
- <a id="host-component"></a>**host component**: a small test-only component whose template contains the component under test, used when the template contract (projection, bindings in markup) is what the test checks ([29 §6](modules/29-testing-angular.md#6-component-tests-dom-queries-componentfixture-signal-inputs-with-setinput-and-bindings-outputs-and-onpush))
- <a id="host-directive"></a>**host directive**: a directive that a class applies to its own host element through `hostDirectives`; its inputs and outputs are hidden unless listed ([14 §3](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#3-host-directives-the-composition-api)).
- <a id="host-element"></a>**host element**: the DOM element a component renders into, such as `<lab-root>` for the root component ([12 §6](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#6-bootstrapping-bootstrapapplication-the-platform-and-initializers)). · also in [13 §1](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#1-component-anatomy-selector-template-styles-imports-and-the-v22-file-convention), [29 §8](modules/29-testing-angular.md#8-testing-services-pipes-and-directives-including-testbedcreatedirective)
- <a id="host-listener"></a>**host listener**: an event listener declared in `host` metadata (or with `@HostListener`) on the component's own element or on a global target ([13 §7](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#7-the-host-element-host-metadata-hostbinding-and-hostlistener)).
- <a id="host-selector"></a>**host selector**: the static CSS selector (`hostSelector`) of the component a harness wraps ([36 §10](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#10-component-harnesses-for-material-the-cdk-and-your-own-components))
- <a id="host-validation"></a>**host validation**: the engine's check of `Host` (and trusted proxy host headers) against `allowedHosts`, answering 400 for an unknown host since v22 ([32 §9](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#9-deploying-ssr-node-and-other-runtimes-static-output-caching-rendered-pages-and-allowed-hosts))
- <a id="host-view"></a>**host view**: the view that wraps a created component, which `ComponentRef.hostView` returns ([19 §7](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#7-dynamic-components-viewcontainerrefcreatecomponent-createcomponent-and-bindings))
- <a id="host_tag_name"></a>**HOST_TAG_NAME**: a token that injects the host element's tag name ([16 §6](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#6-providers-versus-viewproviders-and-per-instance-state)).
- <a id="host-only-cookie"></a>**host-only cookie**: a cookie set without `Domain`, returned only to the exact host that set it ([09 §6](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#6-cookies)).
- <a id="hostattributetoken"></a>**HostAttributeToken**: an injection token that reads a static attribute of the host element with `inject()`; it throws when the attribute is missing unless the injection is optional ([13 §7](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#7-the-host-element-host-metadata-hostbinding-and-hostlistener)). · also in [16 §6](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#6-providers-versus-viewproviders-and-per-instance-state)
- <a id="hot-source"></a>**hot (source)**: a source whose producer exists outside the subscriptions, so it is almost always multicast ([22 §3](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#3-cold-and-hot-unicast-and-multicast))
- <a id="hot-module-replacement-hmr"></a>**hot module replacement (HMR)**: replacing only the changed stylesheet or component template in the running page ([12 §5](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#5-the-dev-loop-ng-serve-hot-module-replacement-and-angular-devtools)).
- <a id="hpack"></a>**HPACK**: HTTP/2's stateful header (field) compression ([09 §3](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#3-http11-http2-and-http3)).
- <a id="html-attribute"></a>**HTML attribute**: the named text in an element's markup; `[attr.title]` writes it with `setAttribute` and removes it on `null` ([13 §4](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#4-property-attribute-class-and-style-bindings)).
- <a id="http-feature-httpfeature"></a>**HTTP feature (HttpFeature)**: an argument of `provideHttpClient` such as `withInterceptors` or `withXhr` that contributes extra providers ([27 §2](modules/27-http-client.md#2-setting-up-httpclient-providehttpclient-its-features-and-the-fetch-backend-default))
- <a id="http-transfer-cache"></a>**HTTP transfer cache**: the `HttpClient` feature that stores the `GET` and `HEAD` responses of the server render in the state script and answers the same requests in the browser until the app is stable ([32 §8](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#8-data-across-the-boundary-the-http-transfer-cache-transferstate-and-resource-ids))
- <a id="http_interceptors"></a>**HTTP_INTERCEPTORS**: the multi-provider token under which class interceptors are registered ([27 §4](modules/27-http-client.md#4-interceptors-the-functional-chain-order-and-next-and-class-interceptors-through-withinterceptorsfromdi))
- <a id="httpclient"></a>**HttpClient**: Angular's HTTP service (`@angular/common/http`): a typed request goes through interceptors to a backend and returns a cold Observable of events ([27 §1](modules/27-http-client.md#1-httpclient-versus-fetch-what-the-framework-adds-over-the-platform))
- <a id="httpclientmodule"></a>**HttpClientModule**: the deprecated NgModule form of the setup; its providers are `provideHttpClient(withInterceptorsFromDi(), withXhr())` ([27 §2](modules/27-http-client.md#2-setting-up-httpclient-providehttpclient-its-features-and-the-fetch-backend-default))
- <a id="httpcontext"></a>**HttpContext**: a per-request, Map-backed bag of metadata that interceptors read and that is not sent to the server ([27 §3](modules/27-http-client.md#3-making-requests-typed-responses-observe-responsetype-immutable-params-and-headers-and-httpcontext))
- <a id="httpcontexttoken"></a>**HttpContextToken**: the typed key of an `HttpContext` entry, with a default-value factory ([27 §3](modules/27-http-client.md#3-making-requests-typed-responses-observe-responsetype-immutable-params-and-headers-and-httpcontext))
- <a id="httperrorresponse"></a>**HttpErrorResponse**: the error `HttpClient` emits for a non-2xx status or a failed call ([27 §1](modules/27-http-client.md#1-httpclient-versus-fetch-what-the-framework-adds-over-the-platform)) · also in [27 §5](modules/27-http-client.md#5-errors-httperrorresponse-an-error-mapping-layer-and-retry-with-backoff-and-jitter)
- <a id="httpeventtype"></a>**HttpEventType**: the enum that types each event of an `observe: 'events'` stream, from `Sent` to `Response` ([27 §3](modules/27-http-client.md#3-making-requests-typed-responses-observe-responsetype-immutable-params-and-headers-and-httpcontext))
- <a id="httphandlerfn-next"></a>**HttpHandlerFn (next)**: the function an interceptor calls to run the rest of the chain; not calling it short-circuits the request ([27 §4](modules/27-http-client.md#4-interceptors-the-functional-chain-order-and-next-and-class-interceptors-through-withinterceptorsfromdi))
- <a id="httpheaders"></a>**HttpHeaders**: the immutable header collection; lookups are case-insensitive and changes return a new instance ([27 §3](modules/27-http-client.md#3-making-requests-typed-responses-observe-responsetype-immutable-params-and-headers-and-httpcontext))
- <a id="httpinterceptor"></a>**HttpInterceptor**: the legacy class form of an interceptor, registered under `HTTP_INTERCEPTORS` and run only with `withInterceptorsFromDi()` ([27 §4](modules/27-http-client.md#4-interceptors-the-functional-chain-order-and-next-and-class-interceptors-through-withinterceptorsfromdi))
- <a id="httpinterceptorfn"></a>**HttpInterceptorFn**: the function type `(req, next) => Observable<HttpEvent>` of a functional interceptor, listed with `withInterceptors` ([27 §4](modules/27-http-client.md#4-interceptors-the-functional-chain-order-and-next-and-class-interceptors-through-withinterceptorsfromdi))
- <a id="httpparams"></a>**HttpParams**: the immutable URL query-parameter collection; `set`, `append` and `delete` return a new instance ([27 §3](modules/27-http-client.md#3-making-requests-typed-responses-observe-responsetype-immutable-params-and-headers-and-httpcontext))
- <a id="httprequest"></a>**HttpRequest**: the immutable object a call builds from the method, URL, body and request options; `clone` returns a changed copy ([27 §3](modules/27-http-client.md#3-making-requests-typed-responses-observe-responsetype-immutable-params-and-headers-and-httpcontext))
- <a id="httpresource"></a>**httpResource**: the signal-driven read built on `HttpClient`, stable since v22.0 ([27 §1](modules/27-http-client.md#1-httpclient-versus-fetch-what-the-framework-adds-over-the-platform)) · also in [27 §10](modules/27-http-client.md#10-httpresource-signal-driven-requests-and-when-to-prefer-it-over-httpclient)
- <a id="httpresourcetext"></a>**httpResource.text**: the `httpResource` variant that reads the body as a string; `blob` and `arrayBuffer` read the other body types ([27 §10](modules/27-http-client.md#10-httpresource-signal-driven-requests-and-when-to-prefer-it-over-httpclient))
- <a id="httpresourceref"></a>**HttpResourceRef**: the resource type that `httpResource` returns; it adds the `headers`, `statusCode` and `progress` signals to a writable resource ([27 §10](modules/27-http-client.md#10-httpresource-signal-driven-requests-and-when-to-prefer-it-over-httpclient))
- <a id="httpresourcerequest"></a>**HttpResourceRequest**: the request object a resource's request function may return instead of a URL; it mirrors the `HttpClient` request options ([27 §10](modules/27-http-client.md#10-httpresource-signal-driven-requests-and-when-to-prefer-it-over-httpclient))
- <a id="httpresponse"></a>**HttpResponse**: the full response event with status, headers and body, emitted by `observe: 'response'` ([27 §3](modules/27-http-client.md#3-making-requests-typed-responses-observe-responsetype-immutable-params-and-headers-and-httpcontext))
- <a id="httptestingcontroller"></a>**HttpTestingController**: the test-side handle on the in-memory test backend; the `cancelled` flag of each `TestRequest` it returns reports that the request's subscription was torn down ([23 §2](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#2-choosing-a-flattening-operator-races-lost-writes-cancellation-and-recursion)) · also in [27 §11](modules/27-http-client.md#11-testing-and-the-backend-seam-providehttpclienttesting-httptestingcontroller-and-what-they-replace)
- <a id="httpuploadprogressevent-and-httpdownloadprogressevent"></a>**HttpUploadProgressEvent and HttpDownloadProgressEvent**: the event types of request-body and response-body progress, each with `loaded` and an optional `total` ([27 §9](modules/27-http-client.md#9-progress-events-reportuploadprogress-reportdownloadprogress-and-why-upload-progress-needs-xhr))
- <a id="httpxsrftokenextractor"></a>**HttpXsrfTokenExtractor**: the public abstract class that supplies the XSRF token; its default reads the cookie ([27 §8](modules/27-http-client.md#8-xsrf-protection-how-httpclient-reads-the-cookie-and-sets-the-header-and-when-it-does-not))
- <a id="hybrid-app"></a>**hybrid app**: an app that mixes NgModules and standalone components, as every migration does for a while ([15 §6](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#6-interop-ngmodule-libraries-in-standalone-apps-and-standalone-pieces-in-ngmodule-apps)).
- <a id="hybrid-app-zone"></a>**hybrid app (zone)**: an application that still provides `provideZoneChangeDetection` beside the notification scheduler ([21 §6](modules/21-change-detection.md#6-migrating-a-zone-based-app-to-zoneless))
- <a id="hybrid-application"></a>**hybrid application**: a page in which AngularJS and Angular both run, each owning its own DOM elements ([39 §9](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#9-from-angularjs-to-angular-ngupgrade-hybrids-the-strangler-fig-and-rewrites))
- <a id="hybrid-rendering"></a>**hybrid rendering**: mixing CSR, SSR and SSG in one app, route by route ([32 §1](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#1-why-render-on-the-server-csr-ssr-ssg-and-hybrid-rendering-and-what-each-costs))
- <a id="hybrid-template"></a>**hybrid template**: a template that mixes directives and blocks, as after a migration that keeps a referenced `ng-template` ([18 §8](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#8-migrating-with-ng-generate-angularcorecontrol-flow))
- <a id="hydrate-trigger"></a>**hydrate trigger**: a `hydrate on …`, `hydrate when` or `hydrate never` condition on a `@defer` block that makes the server render its content and decides when the browser hydrates it ([32 §6](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#6-incremental-hydration-defer-blocks-with-hydrate-triggers))
- <a id="hydration"></a>**hydration**: the browser reusing the DOM the server rendered instead of building it again; owned by module 32 ([18 §7](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#7-what-lands-in-a-chunk-testing-deferred-blocks-and-the-ssr-pointer)) · also in [31 §10](modules/31-performance.md#10-rendering-strategy-as-a-performance-decision-csr-ssr-and-ssg), [32 §1](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#1-why-render-on-the-server-csr-ssr-ssg-and-hybrid-rendering-and-what-each-costs)
- <a id="hydration-annotation-ngh"></a>**hydration annotation (ngh)**: the `ngh` attribute on a component host pointing at an `__nghData__` entry in the state script that records what the view rendered ([32 §4](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#4-hydration-reusing-the-servers-dom-mismatches-and-ngskiphydration))
- <a id="hydration-boundary"></a>**hydration boundary**: the edge of a `@defer` block with hydrate triggers, where hydration of the rest of the page stops ([32 §6](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#6-incremental-hydration-defer-blocks-with-hydrate-triggers))
- <a id="hydration-mismatch"></a>**hydration mismatch**: a DOM that differs from what the template expects at hydration, raised as an NG05xx error ([32 §4](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#4-hydration-reusing-the-servers-dom-mismatches-and-ngskiphydration))

## I

- <a id="i18n-hydration"></a>**i18n hydration**: reusing the server-rendered nodes of an `i18n` block in the browser instead of creating them; enabled by `withI18nSupport()` ([34 §12](modules/34-i18n.md#12-under-the-hood-from-an-i18n-attribute-to-translated-dom))
- <a id="iana-time-zone"></a>**IANA time zone**: a region name from the IANA time zone database, such as `Europe/Madrid`, whose offset changes with daylight saving ([41 §9](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#9-dates-time-zones-decimals-and-money-across-the-wire))
- <a id="icu-library"></a>**ICU (library)**: International Components for Unicode, the C/C++ and Java libraries that JavaScript engines such as Node's build `Intl` on; not ICU message syntax ([34 §1](modules/34-i18n.md#1-i18n-l10n-and-the-locale-language-tags-what-a-locale-decides-and-locale_id))
- <a id="icu-case-swap"></a>**ICU case swap**: replacing the nodes of an ICU's active case with those of the case a new value selects ([34 §12](modules/34-i18n.md#12-under-the-hood-from-an-i18n-attribute-to-translated-dom))
- <a id="icu-message-syntax"></a>**ICU message syntax**: the `{variable, plural|select, case {…} …}` notation inside a message, where the translation supplies the branches; not the ICU library ([34 §5](modules/34-i18n.md#5-icu-expressions-plural-select-and-nesting))
- <a id="id-token"></a>**ID token**: a JWT with claims about the login, meant for the client, not the API ([42 §2](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#2-oauth-20-and-openid-connect-for-a-single-page-app-authorization-code-with-pkce-and-why-the-implicit-flow-is-gone))
- <a id="idempotency-fingerprint"></a>**idempotency fingerprint**: a value the resource derives from the request payload to tell a retry from a different request with the same key (draft-08 §2.4) ([46 §8](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#8-under-the-hood-a-money-transfer-with-idempotency-keys-optimistic-ui-that-never-claims-success-and-the-unknown-outcome))
- <a id="idempotency-key"></a>**idempotency key**: a unique token a client sends with a `POST` so the server recognizes a repeat; an API convention, not defined by RFC 9110 ([09 §1](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#1-http-semantics-methods-status-codes-and-headers)). · also in [27 §5](modules/27-http-client.md#5-errors-httperrorresponse-an-error-mapping-layer-and-retry-with-backoff-and-jitter), [46 §8](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#8-under-the-hood-a-money-transfer-with-idempotency-keys-optimistic-ui-that-never-claims-success-and-the-unknown-outcome)
- <a id="idempotent-request"></a>**idempotent (request)**: repeating it changes nothing more than doing it once ([23 §2](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#2-choosing-a-flattening-operator-races-lost-writes-cancellation-and-recursion))
- <a id="idempotent-unsubscribe"></a>**idempotent (unsubscribe)**: a second call has no further effect ([22 Exercise 22.1](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#ex22-1))
- <a id="idempotent-method"></a>**idempotent method**: a method whose intended effect repeated N times equals one request (`PUT`, `DELETE`, the safe methods) ([09 §1](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#1-http-semantics-methods-status-codes-and-headers)).
- <a id="idle-bucket"></a>**idle bucket**: the group of `@defer` idle callbacks that share one timeout and one idle request in the `IdleScheduler` ([18 §9](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#9-under-the-hood-what-the-blocks-compile-to-how-for-reconciles-and-how-defer-moves-between-states))
- <a id="idle-timeout"></a>**idle timeout**: the time without requests after which a server ends a session ([42 §8](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#8-logout-everywhere-and-session-timeout-ux))
- <a id="idleservice"></a>**IdleService**: the interface behind the `idle` trigger, replaceable with `provideIdleServiceWith` ([18 §6](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#6-triggers-and-prefetching))
- <a id="ieee-754-double"></a>**IEEE-754 double**: the 64-bit binary floating-point format of every JavaScript `number`, which cannot represent most decimal fractions exactly ([01 §2](modules/01-js-values-types-coercion.md#2-numbers)).
- <a id="ignorevalidators"></a>**ignoreValidators**: the `submit()` option that decides which validator states block the action: `'pending'` (default), `'none'` or `'all'` ([26 §7](modules/26-signal-forms.md#7-submission-submit-formroot-submitting-and-server-errors)).
- <a id="iife"></a>**IIFE**: an immediately invoked function expression, the pre-ES2015 way to create a private scope ([02 §1](modules/02-js-scope-closures-this.md#1-lexical-scope-and-the-scope-chain)).
- <a id="image"></a>**image**: a read-only stack of layers, built from a base image, that a container runs ([44 §2](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#2-a-docker-multi-stage-build-for-angular-build-and-runtime-stages-layer-caching-dockerignore-and-a-non-root-image))
- <a id="image-cdn"></a>**image CDN**: a service that serves resized images at URLs a loader builds ([31 §7](modules/31-performance.md#7-images-ngoptimizedimage-with-priority-fill-loaders-and-placeholders))
- <a id="image-digest"></a>**image digest**: the SHA-256 of a container image's content; it names that exact image and cannot be moved to another ([44 §7](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#7-cicd-for-both-layers-one-image-promoted-through-stages-a-monorepo-or-separate-repositories-and-versioning-the-contract))
- <a id="image-loader"></a>**image loader**: a function that builds an image URL for a requested width ([31 §7](modules/31-performance.md#7-images-ngoptimizedimage-with-priority-fill-loaders-and-placeholders))
- <a id="image-tag"></a>**image tag**: a label on a container image that the registry can move to another image ([44 §7](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#7-cicd-for-both-layers-one-image-promoted-through-stages-a-monorepo-or-separate-repositories-and-versioning-the-contract))
- <a id="implicit-flow"></a>**implicit flow**: the retired flow that returned the access token in the redirect URL ([42 §2](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#2-oauth-20-and-openid-connect-for-a-single-page-app-authorization-code-with-pkce-and-why-the-implicit-flow-is-gone))
- <a id="implicit-grid"></a>**implicit grid**: the extra tracks a grid creates when items are placed outside the explicit tracks ([10 §3](modules/10-css-essentials.md#3-flexbox-grid-and-subgrid)).
- <a id="import-attributes"></a>**import attributes**: the `with { type: 'json' }` clause that tells the host what kind of module it must load ([05 §3](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#3-import-maps-importmeta-and-import-attributes)).
- <a id="import-map"></a>**import map**: a `<script type="importmap">` JSON table that maps specifiers to URLs before the browser fetches modules ([05 §3](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#3-import-maps-importmeta-and-import-attributes)).
- <a id="importmetaurl"></a>**import.meta.url**: the URL of the current module, which the builder reads in `new Worker(new URL('./x.worker', import.meta.url))` ([31 §9](modules/31-performance.md#9-off-the-main-thread-web-workers-in-angular-and-runoutsideangular-in-zone-apps))
- <a id="importer-chain"></a>**importer chain**: the path of imports from an entry to a module, showing who asked for its code ([31 §6](modules/31-performance.md#6-bundle-analysis-budgets-and-tree-shaking-pitfalls))
- <a id="importprovidersfrom"></a>**importProvidersFrom**: a function that collects the providers of NgModules (and their imported modules and `ModuleWithProviders`) into `EnvironmentProviders` for an environment injector ([15 §3](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#3-bootstrapping-and-providers-without-modules-bootstrapapplication-provide-functions-and-importprovidersfrom)).
- <a id="imports-component"></a>**imports (component)**: the metadata listing a standalone component's template dependencies: standalone components, directives, pipes and NgModules (whose exports enter the scope) ([15 §2](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#2-standalone-components-and-the-imports-array)).
- <a id="impure-pipe"></a>**impure pipe**: a pipe declared `pure: false`, called on every check of its view ([14 §5](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#5-pipes-pure-and-impure-custom-pipes-injection-and-imports)).
- <a id="in-flight-sharing"></a>**in-flight sharing**: giving concurrent callers of the same request one backend request instead of one each ([27 §6](modules/27-http-client.md#6-cancellation-and-caching-unsubscribing-aborts-cache-interceptors-sharereplay-and-the-ssr-transfer-cache))
- <a id="in-memory-scrolling-withinmemoryscrolling"></a>**in-memory scrolling (withInMemoryScrolling)**: the router feature that scrolls after a navigation to the stored position, the top or the fragment's anchor; off by default ([24 §9](modules/24-routing.md#9-view-transitions-scroll-restoration-and-titles))
- <a id="in-place-mutation"></a>**in-place mutation**: editing an object's properties without replacing it, which no signal or input reference announces ([20 §3](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#3-which-hooks-still-matter-with-signals))
- <a id="increment"></a>**increment**: one running behavior added to a working version ([47 §4](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#4-live-coding-an-angular-task-clarify-say-the-plan-build-in-working-increments-narrate-and-recover-when-stuck))
- <a id="incremental-hydration"></a>**incremental hydration**: hydrating a server-rendered page block by block, each `@defer` block with a hydrate trigger only when its trigger fires ([32 §6](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#6-incremental-hydration-defer-blocks-with-hydrate-triggers))
- <a id="indexed-access-type"></a>**indexed access type**: `T[K]`, the type of property `K` of `T`; a union of keys gives the union of their types ([07 §2](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#2-keyof-indexed-access-mapped-and-template-literal-types)).
- <a id="inert"></a>**inert**: the global attribute that makes an element and its descendants unfocusable, unclickable and absent from the accessibility tree ([11 §3](modules/11-accessibility.md#3-aria-roles-states-properties-and-its-rules)).
- <a id="inert-document"></a>**inert document**: a separate in-memory DOM (from `DOMParser`) where parsed scripts and event handlers do not run, and where Angular's HTML sanitizer inspects markup (images may still load) ([33 §12](modules/33-security.md#12-under-the-hood-from-a-template-binding-to-a-sanitized-dom-write))
- <a id="infer"></a>**infer**: a declaration inside a conditional type's pattern that captures part of the matched type for the true branch ([07 §3](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#3-conditional-types-distributivity-infer-and-recursive-types)).
- <a id="inference-site"></a>**inference site**: a position from which TypeScript collects candidates for a type parameter: arguments, callback returns and the expected result type ([07 §1](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#1-generics-constraints-defaults-inference-and-const-type-parameters)).
- <a id="infinite-scroll-feed"></a>**infinite-scroll feed**: a list that loads the next page as the reader nears its end ([46 §3](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#3-an-infinite-scroll-feed-cursor-pages-deduplication-new-items-without-a-jump-and-a-bounded-window))
- <a id="inheritance"></a>**inheritance**: passing a property's computed value from parent to child for inherited properties such as `color` ([10 §2](modules/10-css-essentials.md#2-the-cascade-origins-specificity-and-layers)).
- <a id="init-phase"></a>**init phase**: the 0 to 3 counter in a view's flags that makes `Init` hooks run once and `Checked` hooks on every refresh ([20 §7](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#7-under-the-hood-hook-lists-the-view-refresh-and-the-render-hook-queue))
- <a id="init-process"></a>**init process**: a small PID 1, such as tini behind `docker run --init`, that forwards signals to the application and reaps finished processes ([44 §6](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#6-serving-ssr-apps-in-containers-the-node-server-as-pid-1-graceful-shutdown-health-probes-and-what-nginx-or-a-cdn-still-serves))
- <a id="initial-bundle"></a>**initial bundle**: the files the first view needs before it can run ([31 §5](modules/31-performance.md#5-loading-the-initial-bundle-lazy-routes-defer-and-preloading-as-one-plan))
- <a id="initial-containing-block"></a>**initial containing block**: the viewport-sized rectangle the root element lives in, used when no ancestor provides a containing block ([10 §4](modules/10-css-essentials.md#4-positioning-stacking-contexts-and-z-index)).
- <a id="initializer"></a>**initializer**: a function run during bootstrap whose promise or observable must settle before the root component is created ([12 §6](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#6-bootstrapping-bootstrapapplication-the-platform-and-initializers)). · also in [16 §7](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#7-injectiontoken-multi-providers-environment-providers-and-initializers)
- <a id="injectasync"></a>**injectAsync**: the v22.0 helper that returns a function giving a promise of a lazily loaded, auto-provided service; the loader runs once and each call returns a new promise ([16 §4](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#4-the-injection-context-inject-runininjectioncontext-and-injectasync)).
- <a id="injection-context"></a>**injection context**: a place where `inject()` may be called, such as an initializer run by bootstrap ([12 §6](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#6-bootstrapping-bootstrapapplication-the-platform-and-initializers); owned by module 16). · also in [16 §1](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#1-why-dependency-injection-containers-constructor-injection-and-inject), [20 §2](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#2-constructor-ngoninit-and-inputs-what-exists-when)
- <a id="injection-token"></a>**injection token**: a typed key for a value in the injector ([12 §4](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#4-environments-and-configuration-build-time-versus-run-time); owned by module 16). · also in [35 §6](modules/35-animations.md#6-function-callbacks-and-the-v22-leave-semantics-animationcallbackevent-animationcomplete-and-nested-leaves)
- <a id="injectiontoken"></a>**InjectionToken**: a typed key created with a description, for a value that has no class; a `factory` makes it provide itself in root ([16 §7](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#7-injectiontoken-multi-providers-environment-providers-and-initializers)).
- <a id="injector"></a>**injector**: the object that resolves a token through a provider and caches the result ([16 §1](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#1-why-dependency-injection-containers-constructor-injection-and-inject)).
- <a id="injectorcreate"></a>**Injector.create**: the static method that builds a lighter injector from `Provider | StaticProvider` and an optional parent; its result has `destroy()` since v20.0 ([16 §8](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#8-lifetime-and-dynamic-injectors-destroyref-environmentinjector-injectorcreate-forward-references-and-cycles)).
- <a id="inline-axis"></a>**inline axis**: the axis along which lines of text run; `dir` flips it ([34 §11](modules/34-i18n.md#11-right-to-left-layouts-dir-logical-css-properties-and-bidirectional-text))
- <a id="inline-size-containment"></a>**inline-size containment**: the box's inline size is computed as if it had no content, while content still sets its block size; `container-type: inline-size` applies it, and `size` applies size containment on both axes ([10 §5](modules/10-css-essentials.md#5-responsive-css-media-queries-container-queries-and-fluid-type)).
- <a id="inliner"></a>**inliner**: the build step after compilation that replaces each `$localize` call with its translation and writes one copy per locale ([34 §7](modules/34-i18n.md#7-compile-time-localization-one-build-per-locale-subpath-lang-and-dir))
- <a id="input"></a>**input**: a property a parent binds with `[x]`; data flows down through it ([19 §1](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#1-inputs-outputs-and-two-way-binding-the-parent-and-child-contract))
- <a id="input-delay--processing-duration--presentation-delay"></a>**input delay / processing duration / presentation delay**: the three phases of an interaction's latency that INP measures ([08 §6](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#6-core-web-vitals-lcp-inp-and-cls)).
- <a id="input-modality"></a>**input modality**: the kind of input the user produced last (keyboard, mouse or touch), which the CDK's `InputModalityDetector` remembers ([36 §9](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#9-cdk-accessibility-focustrap-focusmonitor-liveannouncer-and-the-key-managers))
- <a id="input-selector-and-projector"></a>**input selector** and **projector**: the selectors that pick slices for `createSelector`, and the function that turns their results into the answer ([28 §5](modules/28-state-management.md#5-selectors-memoization-composition-and-reading-the-store-as-signals))
- <a id="input-transform"></a>**input transform**: a function applied to a bound value before an input stores it, such as `booleanAttribute` or `numberAttribute` ([17 §7](modules/17-signals.md#7-signal-based-component-apis)).
- <a id="insertion-point-overlay"></a>**insertion point (overlay)**: the element a position strategy names for a popover overlay's host, which goes after it or inside it; without one the host goes into the overlay container ([36 §13](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#13-under-the-hood-from-createoverlayref-to-a-popover-in-the-top-layer-and-from-mattheme-to-a-computed-color))
- <a id="install-script"></a>**install script**: a `preinstall`, `install` or `postinstall` script that npm runs during install; `ignore-scripts` stops it ([33 §11](modules/33-security.md#11-dependency-and-supply-chain-security-lockfiles-npm-audit-install-scripts-and-subresource-integrity))
- <a id="installmode"></a>**installMode**: an asset group's setting for when its files are downloaded: `prefetch` at install, `lazy` on first request ([37 §5](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#5-caching-with-asset-and-data-groups-navigation-requests-and-the-app-shell))
- <a id="instant"></a>**instant**: one moment in time, the same for everyone; stored as UTC, shown in the user's time zone ([34 §10](modules/34-i18n.md#10-dates-times-numbers-and-money-time-zones-currency-codes-and-minor-units)) · also in [41 §9](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#9-dates-time-zones-decimals-and-money-across-the-wire)
- <a id="instantiation"></a>**instantiation**: the first `TestBed.inject` or `createComponent` call, which creates the module's injector and locks its configuration ([29 §4](modules/29-testing-angular.md#4-testbed-the-testing-module-inject-runininjectioncontext-overrides-and-teardown))
- <a id="instruction"></a>**instruction**: a small runtime function (`ɵɵdomElementStart`, `ɵɵtext`, `ɵɵadvance`…) that a template function calls to create or update DOM ([12 §8](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it)). · also in [19 §8](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#8-under-the-hood-the-compiled-definition-projection-slots-and-query-refresh), [35 §12](modules/35-animations.md#12-under-the-hood-from-animateleave-to-a-removed-node)
- <a id="integration-test"></a>**integration test**: a test that runs several real units together (a routed page, its service, its HTTP calls) and fakes only the network ([29 §1](modules/29-testing-angular.md#1-what-to-test-where-unit-integration-component-and-end-to-end-tests-the-pyramid-and-the-trophy))
- <a id="integrity-hash"></a>**integrity hash**: a digest of a file or tarball (`sha512` in a lockfile, `sha384` in an SRI attribute) that changes when one byte changes ([33 §11](modules/33-security.md#11-dependency-and-supply-chain-security-lockfiles-npm-audit-install-scripts-and-subresource-integrity))
- <a id="integrity-marker"></a>**integrity marker**: the `<!--nghm-->` comment the server puts first in `<body>`; the client refuses to hydrate if it is gone ([32 §4](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#4-hydration-reusing-the-servers-dom-mismatches-and-ngskiphydration))
- <a id="intent-transfer"></a>**intent (transfer)**: one confirmed transfer, its payload and its idempotency key; a changed amount is a new intent ([46 §8](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#8-under-the-hood-a-money-transfer-with-idempotency-keys-optimistic-ui-that-never-claims-success-and-the-unknown-outcome))
- <a id="intent-method"></a>**intent method**: a facade method named for what the user wants, such as `load()` or `select(id)` ([38 §3](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#3-facades-and-the-data-access-layer))
- <a id="interaction"></a>**interaction**: in Exercise 44.2, one user action, such as a click, whose requests share one trace ID and one sampling decision ([Exercise 44.2](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#ex44-2))
- <a id="interaction-test"></a>**interaction test**: a story with a `play` function that simulates user actions ([38 §10](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#10-storybook-for-component-development))
- <a id="interceptor"></a>**interceptor**: a function that can read or replace a request and its response as it passes through the `HttpClient` chain ([27 §1](modules/27-http-client.md#1-httpclient-versus-fetch-what-the-framework-adds-over-the-platform)) · also in [27 §4](modules/27-http-client.md#4-interceptors-the-functional-chain-order-and-next-and-class-interceptors-through-withinterceptorsfromdi)
- <a id="interleave"></a>**interleave**: to pass on every value from several sources in the order they arrive, as `merge` does ([22 §7](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#7-combining-streams-combinelatest-withlatestfrom-forkjoin-zip-merge-and-concat))
- <a id="intermediary"></a>**intermediary**: a proxy, cache or gateway between client and origin server; the method tells it what it may do with a message ([09 §1](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#1-http-semantics-methods-status-codes-and-headers)).
- <a id="internal-api"></a>**internal API**: code shipped in the `rxjs` package but not exported from `'rxjs'` or tagged an implementation detail; it may change in the next major ([22 §9](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#9-under-the-hood-subscribe-the-subscriber-chain-and-the-subjects-observer-list))
- <a id="internal-redirect"></a>**internal redirect**: Nginx restarting location matching with a new URI inside the server, without telling the browser ([44 §3](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#3-nginx-for-a-single-page-app-location-matching-the-try_files-fallback-caching-headers-for-hashed-assets-and-gzip-or-brotli))
- <a id="internal-signal-of-a-control"></a>**internal signal (of a control)**: a signal behind the status, pristine and touched getters; reading it in a binding marks the view, and `value` has no such signal ([25 §7](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#7-status-updateon-valuechanges-and-the-unified-events-stream))
- <a id="internationalization-i18n"></a>**internationalization (i18n)**: designing and preparing an app so it can be adapted to different locales: no text, format or direction hard-coded ([34 §1](modules/34-i18n.md#1-i18n-l10n-and-the-locale-language-tags-what-a-locale-decides-and-locale_id))
- <a id="interop-standalone-and-ngmodules"></a>**interop (standalone and NgModules)**: the rules that let standalone components and NgModules use each other: a standalone component imports an NgModule, an NgModule imports a standalone class ([15 §6](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#6-interop-ngmodule-libraries-in-standalone-apps-and-standalone-pieces-in-ngmodule-apps)).
- <a id="interopngcontrol"></a>**InteropNgControl**: the fake `NgControl` that `FormField` provides for accessor interop ([26 §8](modules/26-signal-forms.md#8-custom-controls-formvaluecontrol-formcheckboxcontrol-and-how-formfield-picks-a-control)).
- <a id="interpolation"></a>**interpolation**: `{{ expression }}`, a text binding that converts the value to a string ([13 §2](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#2-interpolation-and-template-expressions-what-is-allowed)).
- <a id="intersectionobserver--resizeobserver"></a>**IntersectionObserver / ResizeObserver**: observers that report, asynchronously, a target's visibility within a root and an element's size, replacing scroll and resize polling ([08 §3](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#3-dom-apis-and-observers)).
- <a id="invalidation"></a>**invalidation**: removing or refreshing a stored entry ([45 §7](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#7-rendering-strategy-and-caching-layers-a-rendering-choice-per-route-and-every-cache-from-the-cdn-to-the-component))
- <a id="inversion-of-control"></a>**inversion of control**: the reversal in which the framework, not your code, drives the program and calls into it ([12 §1](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#1-what-a-framework-does-and-angulars-philosophy)). · also in [16 §1](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#1-why-dependency-injection-containers-constructor-injection-and-inject)
- <a id="isactive"></a>**isActive**: the function `isActive(url, router, matchOptions?)` that returns a `Signal<boolean>` for whether a URL is active ([24 §7](modules/24-routing.md#7-the-navigation-lifecycle-events-cancellation-and-the-transition-pipeline))
- <a id="islooselyequal"></a>**IsLooselyEqual**: the algorithm behind `==`, which coerces operands of different types ([01 §6](modules/01-js-values-types-coercion.md#6-equality)).
- <a id="iso-4217"></a>**ISO 4217**: the standard of currency codes such as `EUR` and `JPY`, with each currency's minor unit digits ([34 §10](modules/34-i18n.md#10-dates-times-numbers-and-money-time-zones-currency-codes-and-minor-units))
- <a id="iso-4217-currency-code"></a>**ISO 4217 currency code**: the three-letter code of a currency, such as `EUR` or `JPY` ([41 §9](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#9-dates-time-zones-decimals-and-money-across-the-wire))
- <a id="iso-8601"></a>**ISO 8601**: the international notation for dates and times; JavaScript's date time string format is a simplification of it ([34 §10](modules/34-i18n.md#10-dates-times-numbers-and-money-time-zones-currency-codes-and-minor-units)) · also in [41 §9](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#9-dates-time-zones-decimals-and-money-across-the-wire)
- <a id="isolate-scope"></a>**isolate scope**: an AngularJS scope that does not inherit prototypally from its parent ([39 §1](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#1-angularjs-scopes-watchers-and-the-digest-cycle))
- <a id="isolated-modules--per-file-transpiler"></a>**isolated modules / per-file transpiler**: a tool (esbuild, `ts.transpileModule`, Node) that turns one file into JavaScript without seeing the others, so it cannot use type information ([06 §1](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#1-what-typescript-is-erased-types-tsc-and-transpile-only-tools)).
- <a id="isolation-bidi"></a>**isolation (bidi)**: keeping a run of text from affecting how its neighbours are ordered, with `<bdi>` or `dir="auto"` ([34 §11](modules/34-i18n.md#11-right-to-left-layouts-dir-logical-css-properties-and-bidirectional-text))
- <a id="isstandalone"></a>**isStandalone**: a function that returns the `standalone` field of a component, directive or pipe definition, `false` for anything else ([15 §2](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#2-standalone-components-and-the-imports-array)).
- <a id="isstrictlyequal"></a>**IsStrictlyEqual**: the algorithm behind `===`: no coercion, `NaN` unequal to itself, `+0` equal to `-0` ([01 §6](modules/01-js-values-types-coercion.md#6-equality)).
- <a id="issuer-iss"></a>**issuer (iss)**: the claim naming the authorization server that issued a token ([42 §5](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#5-spring-security-as-a-resource-server-validating-jwts-and-turning-scopes-into-authorities))
- <a id="iterable"></a>**iterable**: a JavaScript object with a `[Symbol.iterator]()` method (array, `Set`, `Map`, string, generator), which `@for` accepts ([18 §2](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#2-for-track-context-variables-empty-and-what-angular-can-iterate))
- <a id="iterable--iterator"></a>**iterable / iterator**: an object with a `[Symbol.iterator]()` method, versus the object with `next()` that method returns ([03 §8](modules/03-js-objects-prototypes-classes.md#8-iteration-protocols-and-generators)).
- <a id="iteration-count"></a>**iteration count**: how many times a CSS animation runs, a number or `infinite`, set by `animation-iteration-count` ([35 §3](modules/35-animations.md#3-css-keyframe-animations-keyframes-fill-modes-iteration-and-component-scoped-names))
- <a id="iterator-helpers"></a>**iterator helpers**: the lazy ES2025 methods on iterators (`map`, `filter`, `take`, `drop`, `flatMap`, `find`…) ([03 §8](modules/03-js-objects-prototypes-classes.md#8-iteration-protocols-and-generators)).
- <a id="ivy"></a>**Ivy**: the name of Angular's current compiler and runtime design, in which each decorator compiles to a static definition on its class ([12 §8](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it)). · also in [39 §5](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#5-the-version-matrix-part-2-v9-to-v15-from-ivy-to-standalone)

## J

- <a id="jank"></a>**jank**: motion that stutters because the browser produced a frame late ([35 §11](modules/35-animations.md#11-performance-and-reduced-motion-compositor-properties-measuring-jank-and-prefers-reduced-motion))
- <a id="jasmine"></a>**Jasmine**: a test framework with its own `describe`, `it`, `expect` and spies, used with Karma ([29 §3](modules/29-testing-angular.md#3-karma-jasmine-and-jest-the-legacy-and-alternative-runners-and-migrating-to-vitest))
- <a id="jest"></a>**Jest**: a Node test runner with jsdom that Angular projects use through the third-party `jest-preset-angular` ([29 §3](modules/29-testing-angular.md#3-karma-jasmine-and-jest-the-legacy-and-alternative-runners-and-migrating-to-vitest))
- <a id="jit-just-in-time-compilation"></a>**JIT (just-in-time compilation)**: compiling templates and decorators in the browser at run time, which is why `ɵɵsetNgModuleScope` still registers a module's scope when `ngJitMode` allows it ([15 §8](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-for-modules-and-standalone-components-and-the-standalone-injector)).
- <a id="jit-just-in-time-compilation-1"></a>**JIT (just-in-time) compilation**: compiling a component in the browser or test runtime when its definition is first read; needs `@angular/compiler` loaded ([12 §7](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#7-aot-versus-jit-and-template-type-checking)).
- <a id="jit-mode"></a>**JIT mode**: compiling Angular components at run time, where the decorators are really called; standard field decorators throw there ([07 §7](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#7-decorators-tc39-standard-versus-experimentaldecorators-and-what-angular-does-with-them)).
