- <a id="jitter"></a>**jitter**: a random part added to each retry wait so that many clients do not retry in step ([23 §4](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#4-retrying-and-repeating-retry-with-backoff-and-repeat)) · also in [27 §5](modules/27-http-client.md#5-errors-httperrorresponse-an-error-mapping-layer-and-retry-with-backoff-and-jitter), [43 §9](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#9-scaling-out-and-under-the-hood-sticky-sessions-the-broker-relay-backpressure-and-one-message-traced-across-two-instances)
- <a id="jsaction-attribute"></a>**jsaction (attribute)**: the attribute the server writes on an element with a listener, naming the event types to capture, such as `jsaction="click:;"` ([32 §5](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#5-event-replay-keeping-the-clicks-made-before-the-code-arrives))
- <a id="jsaction-library"></a>**JSAction (library)**: the event-delegation library behind the `jsaction` name that Angular's event contract comes from ([32 §5](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#5-event-replay-keeping-the-clicks-made-before-the-code-arrives))
- <a id="jsdom"></a>**jsdom**: a JavaScript implementation of the DOM and HTML Standards for Node.js, used by the labs; it has no accessibility tree or layout ([11](modules/11-accessibility.md)). · also in [13](modules/13-components-and-templates.md), [14](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md), [29 §2](modules/29-testing-angular.md#2-the-runner-vitest-through-angularbuildunit-test-jsdom-and-browser-mode)
- <a id="json-merge-patch"></a>**JSON Merge Patch**: RFC 7386's patch format: a member left out stays unchanged and a `null` member means removal ([41 §2](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#2-dto-contracts-what-crosses-the-wire-absent-versus-null-and-checking-responses-at-the-boundary))
- <a id="json-pointer"></a>**JSON Pointer**: RFC 6901's string syntax for one value inside a JSON document, such as `#/quantity` in its URI-fragment form ([41 §5](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#5-error-contracts-with-problemdetail-rfc-9457-and-mapping-them-in-angular))
- <a id="jsonp"></a>**JSONP**: an endpoint that wraps data in a caller-named callback and is loaded as a `<script>`; a bypass route for host allowlists ([09 §8](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#8-content-security-policy-and-trusted-types)).
- <a id="jsx"></a>**JSX**: a syntax extension of JavaScript that compiles to function calls ([40 §2](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#2-templates-jsx-and-single-file-components-what-the-compiler-knows))
- <a id="jwks-json-web-key-set"></a>**JWKS (JSON Web Key Set)**: the set of public keys an issuer publishes so that resource servers can verify its tokens' signatures ([42 §5](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#5-spring-security-as-a-resource-server-validating-jwts-and-turning-scopes-into-authorities))
- <a id="jwt"></a>**JWT**: JSON Web Token: claims and a signature (or encryption) written as base64url text in dot-separated parts ([42 §1](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#1-sessions-versus-tokens-what-the-server-keeps-what-each-request-carries-revocation-and-scaling))
- <a id="jwt-json-web-token"></a>**JWT (JSON Web Token)**: a token format of claims as base64url text, usually signed; anyone holding a signed JWT can read its claims ([33 §8](modules/33-security.md#8-where-the-token-lives-memory-web-storage-httponly-cookies-and-a-backend-for-frontend))

## K

- <a id="karma"></a>**Karma**: a test runner that launches real browsers and runs Jasmine specs in them; the Angular CLI default before v21 ([29 §3](modules/29-testing-angular.md#3-karma-jasmine-and-jest-the-legacy-and-alternative-runners-and-migrating-to-vitest))
- <a id="keepalive"></a>**keepalive**: a `fetch` option that lets a request outlive the page that started it, with the bodies of all in-flight `keepalive` requests together limited to 64 KiB ([09 §2](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#2-fetch-xhr-and-the-request-lifecycle)).
- <a id="kept-objects"></a>**kept objects**: the per-job list that keeps a `WeakRef` target alive after `new WeakRef` or `deref()` until the current synchronous run ends ([05 §5](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#5-weak-references-weakmap-weakset-weakref-and-finalizationregistry)).
- <a id="kernel"></a>**kernel**: the smallest code whose correctness a case's design depends on, with tests ([46 §1](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#1-from-method-to-case-the-parts-every-case-study-carries-and-where-each-cases-difficulty-lies))
- <a id="key"></a>**key**: a child's identity among its siblings; reconciliation keeps the instance and state of a child whose key and type match ([40 §3](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#3-reacts-model-re-rendering-reconciliation-memoization-and-the-react-compiler))
- <a id="key-id-kid"></a>**key id (kid)**: a token header value naming which key in a JWKS signed it, used during key rollover ([42 §5](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#5-spring-security-as-a-resource-server-validating-jwts-and-turning-scopes-into-authorities))
- <a id="key-manager"></a>**key manager**: a CDK class (`ListKeyManager` and its subclasses) that turns key presses into an active item in a list ([36 §9](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#9-cdk-accessibility-focustrap-focusmonitor-liveannouncer-and-the-key-managers))
- <a id="key-modifier"></a>**key modifier**: `alt`, `control`, `meta` or `shift` written before the key in a key filter such as `(keydown.shift.enter)`; the set must match the pressed modifiers exactly ([13 §5](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#5-event-binding-and-two-way-binding)).
- <a id="key-relay-angular-aria-combobox"></a>**key relay (Angular Aria combobox)**: `ngCombobox` re-dispatching a copy of a navigation key or Enter on the popup's listbox, on the next render ([Exercise 36.2](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#ex36-2))
- <a id="key-remapping"></a>**key remapping**: the `as` clause of a mapped type that renames keys, or drops them by producing `never` ([07 §2](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#2-keyof-indexed-access-mapped-and-template-literal-types)).
- <a id="key-value-differ"></a>**key-value differ**: Angular's helper that compares an object's keys and values with its previous copy; `NgStyle` uses it ([14 §4](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#4-built-in-directives-ngclass-ngstyle-the-outlets-and-what-control-flow-replaced)).
- <a id="keyboard-alternative-drag-and-drop"></a>**keyboard alternative (drag and drop)**: a control, such as a button or a key chord, that makes the same change as a drag, because the CDK's drag-and-drop listens to pointer events only ([Exercise 36.3](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#ex36-3))
- <a id="keyframe"></a>**keyframe**: the style values at one point of an animation's run ([35 §1](modules/35-animations.md#1-motion-in-a-ui-what-to-animate-the-browsers-three-animation-mechanisms-and-where-angular-fits))
- <a id="keyframeeffect"></a>**KeyframeEffect**: the object that holds an animation's keyframes and timing; an `Animation` plays it ([35 §4](modules/35-animations.md#4-the-web-animations-api-elementanimate-the-animation-object-and-getanimations))
- <a id="keyset-cursor"></a>**keyset cursor**: the last row's sort values and id, so the next page is the rows after that row ([46 §4](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#4-a-large-data-grid-server-side-filtering-sorting-and-pagination-the-query-in-the-url-and-keyset-cursors))
- <a id="kill-switch-flag-sense"></a>**kill switch (flag sense)**: turning a flag off at the provider so the feature stops without a deploy; not the service worker's kill switch of module 37 ([44 §8](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#8-feature-flags-with-openfeature-and-canary-releases-providers-evaluation-context-sticky-rollout-kill-switches-and-version-skew))

## L

- <a id="lab-data"></a>**lab data**: performance metrics from one controlled run on one machine ([31 §1](modules/31-performance.md#1-measure-first-the-loop-lab-versus-field-and-what-each-tool-answers))
- <a id="lab-measurement"></a>**lab measurement**: one controlled run on a stated setup, which explains why but not how bad for whom ([31 §1](modules/31-performance.md#1-measure-first-the-loop-lab-versus-field-and-what-each-tool-answers), [47 §2](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#2-four-front-end-stories-a-performance-win-a-migration-a-production-incident-and-a-disagreement-over-architecture))
- <a id="label-in-name"></a>**label in name**: WCAG 2.5.3, the accessible name must contain the visible label text ([11 §6](modules/11-accessibility.md#6-accessible-names-descriptions-and-forms)).
- <a id="labeled-arrow"></a>**labeled arrow**: an arrow that says what flows and in which direction ([45 §2](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#2-communicating-under-time-pressure-a-time-plan-complete-trade-off-statements-and-diagrams-at-three-zoom-levels))
- <a id="landing-step"></a>**landing step**: the first visible step that is not yet valid, where a restored draft opens ([46 §5](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#5-a-multi-step-form-wizard-with-draft-saving-a-step-model-per-step-validation-and-drafts-that-survive-and-migrate))
- <a id="landmark"></a>**landmark**: a region role (banner, main, navigation, complementary, contentinfo, region) that assistive technology can jump to ([11 §2](modules/11-accessibility.md#2-semantic-html-landmarks-and-headings)).
- <a id="lang-attribute"></a>**lang attribute**: the language of a page, set on `<html>` per locale by a localized build ([34 §7](modules/34-i18n.md#7-compile-time-localization-one-build-per-locale-subpath-lang-and-dir))
- <a id="last-event-id"></a>**last event ID**: the value of the latest `id` field, which persists across events and is copied onto each event's `lastEventId` ([43 §2](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#2-server-sent-events-on-the-wire-the-textevent-stream-format-and-how-eventsource-reconnects))
- <a id="last-wins"></a>**last wins**: with several providers for one token in one injector, the last one answers and the others are dropped without an error ([16 §2](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#2-providers-and-tokens-useclass-usevalue-usefactory-and-useexisting)).
- <a id="last-event-id-1"></a>**Last-Event-ID**: the request header an `EventSource` sends on reconnecting when its last event ID is not empty ([43 §2](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#2-server-sent-events-on-the-wire-the-textevent-stream-format-and-how-eventsource-reconnects))
- <a id="last-modified--if-modified-since"></a>**Last-Modified / If-Modified-Since**: the date-based validator and the conditional request that sends it back; implicitly weak, so `ETag` is preferred ([09 §4](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#4-http-caching)).
- <a id="last-write-wins"></a>**last-write-wins**: a write policy that keeps the latest save and silently drops earlier concurrent ones ([45 §8](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#8-under-the-hood-failure-modes-observability-offline-levels-and-consistency-choices))
- <a id="lastsuccessfulnavigation"></a>**lastSuccessfulNavigation**: the `Router` signal holding the `Navigation` of the last navigation that ended with `NavigationEnd`; a property before v21 ([24 §7](modules/24-routing.md#7-the-navigation-lifecycle-events-cancellation-and-the-transition-pipeline))
- <a id="late-value"></a>**late value**: a value a source emits after the `async` pipe has replaced it with another source; the pipe ignores it ([14 Q14.27](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#q14-27)).
- <a id="late-work"></a>**late work**: asynchronous work that finishes after its owner was destroyed ([20 §5](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#5-teardown-ngondestroy-destroyref-and-takeuntildestroyed))
- <a id="latest-value"></a>**latest value**: the last value a source has emitted so far, which `combineLatest` and `withLatestFrom` combine ([22 §7](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#7-combining-streams-combinelatest-withlatestfrom-forkjoin-zip-merge-and-concat))
- <a id="latest-only"></a>**latest-only**: showing only the answer to the most recent request, never an older one arriving late ([46 §2](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#2-autocomplete-and-typeahead-search-a-request-policy-a-prefix-cache-ranking-on-the-server-and-an-accessible-combobox))
- <a id="layer"></a>**layer**: one read-only filesystem change an image keeps, made by one Dockerfile instruction ([44 §2](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#2-a-docker-multi-stage-build-for-angular-build-and-runtime-stages-layer-caching-dockerignore-and-a-non-root-image))
- <a id="layer-cache"></a>**layer cache**: the stored layers Docker reuses while an instruction and its inputs are unchanged; a changed layer invalidates every later one ([44 §2](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#2-a-docker-multi-stage-build-for-angular-build-and-runtime-stages-layer-caching-dockerignore-and-a-non-root-image))
- <a id="layered-jar"></a>**layered jar**: a Spring Boot jar split into layers, such as dependencies and application code, so Docker caches the stable ones ([44 §2](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#2-a-docker-multi-stage-build-for-angular-build-and-runtime-stages-layer-caching-dockerignore-and-a-non-root-image))
- <a id="layout-shell"></a>**layout shell**: the component that draws the frame (heading, outlet) around every feature ([38 §12](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#12-a-complete-production-folder-tree-folder-by-folder))
- <a id="layout-thrashing"></a>**layout thrashing**: forced synchronous layouts repeated in a loop that interleaves reads and writes; fixed by batching reads before writes ([08 §2](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#2-layout-paint-and-composite-what-each-change-costs)). · also in [17 §4](modules/17-signals.md#4-effects)
- <a id="layoutcount"></a>**LayoutCount**: the DevTools Protocol counter of layout passes the browser has run ([35 §11](modules/35-animations.md#11-performance-and-reduced-motion-compositor-properties-measuring-jank-and-prefers-reduced-motion))
- <a id="lazy-observable"></a>**lazy (Observable)**: for a cold Observable, creating it runs nothing and each `subscribe` starts a new run of the producer ([22 §1](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#1-push-collections-observables-promises-iterators-and-laziness))
- <a id="lazy-boundary"></a>**lazy boundary**: a route or block loaded on demand ([45 §4](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#4-component-architecture-data-model-and-data-flow-from-screens-to-a-component-tree-and-a-client-side-model))
- <a id="lazy-chunk"></a>**lazy chunk**: a chunk outside the initial bundle, fetched later through a dynamic `import()` ([31 §5](modules/31-performance.md#5-loading-the-initial-bundle-lazy-routes-defer-and-preloading-as-one-plan))
- <a id="lazy-instantiation"></a>**lazy instantiation**: an injector builds an instance on the first request for its token, not when the injector is created ([16 §8](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#8-lifetime-and-dynamic-injectors-destroyref-environmentinjector-injectorcreate-forward-references-and-cycles)).
- <a id="lazy-loading"></a>**lazy loading**: downloading a feature's code only when a navigation first needs it ([15 §4](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#4-lazy-loading-without-modules-loadcomponent-and-loadchildren-with-route-arrays)).
- <a id="lazy-loaded-module"></a>**lazy-loaded module**: an NgModule the router loads on demand with `loadChildren`, with its own injector ([15 §1](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#1-what-ngmodules-did-compilation-scope-injector-and-lazy-loading-unit)).
- <a id="lcp-element"></a>**LCP element**: the element the browser reports as the page's largest paint ([31 §7](modules/31-performance.md#7-images-ngoptimizedimage-with-priority-fill-loaders-and-placeholders))
- <a id="leading--trailing-edge"></a>**leading / trailing edge**: the start and the end of a timing window ([22 §8](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#8-timing-operators-debouncetime-throttletime-audittime-and-sampletime))
- <a id="leak-subscription"></a>**leak (subscription)**: a subscription, and everything it references, kept alive after its owner is gone ([23 §6](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#6-unsubscription-strategies-in-angular-and-finding-leaks))
- <a id="lease"></a>**lease**: a lock's time limit, renewed by its holder, so a holder that vanished stops blocking others when it runs out ([46 Q46.21](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#q46-21))
- <a id="leave-animation"></a>**leave animation**: an animation that runs before an element is removed; `animate.leave` delays the DOM removal until it ends ([35 §1](modules/35-animations.md#1-motion-in-a-ui-what-to-animate-the-browsers-three-animation-mechanisms-and-where-angular-fits))
- <a id="leave-classes"></a>**leave classes**: the CSS classes `animate.leave` adds before an element is removed; the element leaves the DOM when its longest animation ends ([35 §5](modules/35-animations.md#5-animateenter-and-animateleave-with-css-classes-syntax-bindings-and-when-the-element-leaves))
- <a id="legacy-api"></a>**legacy (API)**: still public and supported, but documented as kept for backwards compatibility, without a deprecation tag; `@HostBinding` and `@HostListener` ([13 §7](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#7-the-host-element-host-metadata-hostbinding-and-hostlistener)).
- <a id="legacy-code"></a>**legacy code**: our working definition: code that still runs in production and that its current team did not write or cannot change safely ([47 §3](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#3-legacy-codebases-and-migrations-without-blame-the-constraints-of-the-time-how-you-learned-the-code-before-changing-it-and-words-that-explain-instead-of-accuse))
- <a id="legacy-id"></a>**legacy ID**: an ID older Angular versions computed for a message; `ng extract-i18n --format legacy-migrate` writes a map from legacy to current IDs ([34 §6](modules/34-i18n.md#6-extraction-and-translation-files-ng-extract-i18n-formats-and-message-ids))
- <a id="lexical-static-scope"></a>**lexical (static) scope**: names resolve by where code is written, not by where it is called ([02 §1](modules/02-js-scope-closures-this.md#1-lexical-scope-and-the-scope-chain)).
- <a id="library"></a>**library**: code you call; it does some work and returns control to you ([12 §1](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#1-what-a-framework-does-and-angulars-philosophy)).
- <a id="library-angular"></a>**library (Angular)**: a package of Angular code for other apps, published partially compiled and finished by the CLI's linker ([15 §6](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#6-interop-ngmodule-libraries-in-standalone-apps-and-standalone-pieces-in-ngmodule-apps)); the linker is owned by [12](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#q12-28).
- <a id="library-type"></a>**library type**: what a project's code does: `feature`, `ui`, `data-access` or `util` ([38 §4](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#4-layers-library-types-and-dependency-rules))
- <a id="libuv"></a>**libuv**: the C library that runs Node's event loop and its I/O ([04 §1](modules/04-js-async-event-loop.md#1-the-event-loop-tasks-microtasks-and-rendering)).
- <a id="license-allow-list"></a>**license allow-list**: the licenses your legal team approved; a rule of the evaluation table ([37 §12](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#12-the-production-ecosystem-and-how-to-evaluate-a-library))
- <a id="lifecycle-hook"></a>**lifecycle hook**: a method such as `ngOnInit` that Angular calls at set moments; it is not an injection context, owner module 20 ([16 §4](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#4-the-injection-context-inject-runininjectioncontext-and-injectasync)). · also in [20 §1](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#1-the-lifecycle-map-phases-and-the-order-of-the-hooks)
- <a id="lift--operate-internal"></a>**lift / operate (internal)**: the internal functions the built-in operators use to wrap a source ([22 §5](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#5-pipeable-operators-and-writing-your-own))
- <a id="lifting-state-up"></a>**lifting state up**: moving a value to the nearest common parent of the components that need it ([19 §2](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#2-sharing-state-beyond-the-tree-services-with-signals-and-di-scoped-state))
- <a id="lighthouse"></a>**Lighthouse**: an automated auditor that runs one controlled page load and reports audits and a score ([31 §1](modules/31-performance.md#1-measure-first-the-loop-lab-versus-field-and-what-each-tool-answers))
- <a id="lighthouse-ci"></a>**Lighthouse CI**: a tool that runs Lighthouse in a pipeline, several times per page, and checks the results against assertions ([31 §2](modules/31-performance.md#2-lab-metrics-in-a-real-browser-performance-observers-devtools-protocol-counters-and-lighthouse-in-ci))
- <a id="linear-stepper"></a>**linear stepper**: a stepper marked `linear`, which refuses to leave a step until that step's control is valid, interacted with and not pending ([36 §8](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#8-the-smaller-primitives-the-stepper-breakpointobserver-and-clipboard))
- <a id="link-preview-unfurl"></a>**link preview (unfurl)**: the card a chat or social app builds from a pasted URL ([32 §1](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#1-why-render-on-the-server-csr-ssr-ssg-and-hybrid-rendering-and-what-each-costs))
- <a id="linkedsignal"></a>**linkedSignal**: writable derived state: a signal you can set that resets from its source whenever the source changes ([17 §5](modules/17-signals.md#5-linkedsignal-writable-derived-state)).
- <a id="linker"></a>**linker**: the application-build step that turns partial `ɵɵngDeclare*` declarations into full definitions (OXC by default in `@angular/build`) ([12 §8](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it)).
- <a id="linter"></a>**linter**: a tool that reports code that is probably wrong or against a convention ([38 §9](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#9-linting-and-formatting-angular-eslint-prettier-import-ordering-and-commit-hooks))
- <a id="linting"></a>**linting**: static checks of source code, here of Angular templates, before they run ([11 §7](modules/11-accessibility.md#7-testing-accessibility)).
- <a id="list-wide-selector"></a>**list-wide selector**: one derivation over a whole collection in a store, recomputed for every item when any item changes ([31 §3](modules/31-performance.md#3-runtime-cost-of-a-change-onpush-zoneless-signals-and-per-check-template-work))
- <a id="literal-widening"></a>**literal widening**: a `let` initialised with `'x'` gets type `string`, a `const` keeps `'x'` ([06 §6](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#6-enums-literal-unions-as-const-and-satisfies)).
- <a id="live-announcer"></a>**live announcer**: the CDK's `LiveAnnouncer`, which writes messages into one shared live region element ([36 §9](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#9-cdk-accessibility-focustrap-focusmonitor-liveannouncer-and-the-key-managers))
- <a id="live-binding"></a>**live binding**: an import that reads the exporter's variable on every access instead of copying its value ([05 §1](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#1-es-modules-static-structure-linking-and-live-bindings)).
- <a id="live-consumer"></a>**live consumer**: an effect or template view, which producers link to and notify; non-live computeds are not linked and validate by polling ([17 §9](modules/17-signals.md#9-under-the-hood-the-reactive-graph)). · also in [21 §9](modules/21-change-detection.md#9-under-the-hood-the-notification-the-dirty-flags-and-the-refresh)
- <a id="live-region"></a>**live region**: an element whose text changes assistive technology reports without the user moving focus ([11 §5](modules/11-accessibility.md#5-focus-management-in-spas-and-live-regions)).
- <a id="live-reload"></a>**live reload**: reloading the whole page after a rebuild ([12 §5](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#5-the-dev-loop-ng-serve-hot-module-replacement-and-angular-devtools)).
- <a id="live-update"></a>**live update**: a change the server pushes to the client ([45 §5](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#5-state-and-api-design-where-each-piece-of-state-lives-and-the-api-the-screens-need))
- <a id="live-coding-loop"></a>**live-coding loop**: our seven steps for a live-coding round: clarify, say the plan, build in increments, narrate, try edges, check time, wrap up ([47 §4](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#4-live-coding-an-angular-task-clarify-say-the-plan-build-in-working-increments-narrate-and-recover-when-stuck))
- <a id="live-coding-round"></a>**live-coding round**: a round in which you build one component live; the round 45 §1 calls machine coding ([47 §4](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#4-live-coding-an-angular-task-clarify-say-the-plan-build-in-working-increments-narrate-and-recover-when-stuck))
- <a id="liveness-probe"></a>**liveness probe**: a check whose failure makes the orchestrator restart the container; it must not depend on rendering or other services ([44 §6](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#6-serving-ssr-apps-in-containers-the-node-server-as-pid-1-graceful-shutdown-health-probes-and-what-nginx-or-a-cdn-still-serves))
- <a id="loadchildren"></a>**loadChildren**: a route property whose function loads a `Routes` array (or, in legacy code, an NgModule) for the route's children ([15 §4](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#4-lazy-loading-without-modules-loadcomponent-and-loadchildren-with-route-arrays)).
- <a id="loadcomponent"></a>**loadComponent**: a route property whose function loads one standalone component, usually with a dynamic `import()` ([15 §4](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#4-lazy-loading-without-modules-loadcomponent-and-loadchildren-with-route-arrays)).
- <a id="loader"></a>**loader**: a route's `loadChildren` or `loadComponent` function, which returns routes or a component, usually through a dynamic `import()`; `loadChildren` runs during recognition, `loadComponent` after the resolvers, and each result is cached on the route ([24 §4](modules/24-routing.md#4-lazy-loading-in-depth-loaders-canmatch-and-route-injectors))
- <a id="loader-translation"></a>**loader (translation)**: the class a runtime library calls to get one language's dictionary, or one scope's ([34 §9](modules/34-i18n.md#9-runtime-translation-libraries-transloco-and-ngx-translate-lazy-scopes-and-the-trade-off-against-built-in-i18n))
- <a id="loading-plan"></a>**loading plan**: the decision, for each piece of code, of when it downloads: initial bundle, lazy route, `@defer` block or early fetch ([31 §5](modules/31-performance.md#5-loading-the-initial-bundle-lazy-routes-defer-and-preloading-as-one-plan)) · also in [45 §6](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#6-performance-and-accessibility-as-design-steps-budgets-a-loading-plan-long-lists-and-keyboard-and-pointer-paths)
- <a id="loadtranslations"></a>**loadTranslations**: the `@angular/localize` function that parses translations into the map and installs `$localize.translate` ([34 §8](modules/34-i18n.md#8-runtime-translation-with-angularlocalize-loadtranslations-before-bootstrap))
- <a id="local-logout"></a>**local logout**: ending only the app's own session and client state, which leaves the provider's session and other devices alone ([42 §8](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#8-logout-everywhere-and-session-timeout-ux))
- <a id="locale"></a>**locale**: a language with an optional region (`en-GB`) whose formatting tables the pipes read; an unregistered language other than `en` is an error ([14 §6](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#6-built-in-pipes-and-locale-data)). · also in [34 §1](modules/34-i18n.md#1-i18n-l10n-and-the-locale-language-tags-what-a-locale-decides-and-locale_id)
- <a id="locale-data"></a>**locale data**: the table of month names, patterns, symbols and plural rule that one locale supplies to a formatter; Angular's copy ships in `@angular/common` ([34 §3](modules/34-i18n.md#3-angulars-locale-data-and-formatting-functions-versus-intl))
- <a id="locale_id"></a>**LOCALE_ID**: the injection token holding the locale the pipes use, `en-US` by default; owner [34](modules/34-i18n.md) ([14 §6](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#6-built-in-pipes-and-locale-data)). · also in [34 §1](modules/34-i18n.md#1-i18n-l10n-and-the-locale-language-tags-what-a-locale-decides-and-locale_id)
- <a id="locality-ivy-locality-principle"></a>**locality (Ivy locality principle)**: a decorator compiles with knowledge of its own class and imports only, not the whole program ([12 §8](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it)).
- <a id="localization-l10n"></a>**localization (l10n)**: building and shipping a version of an app for one locale ([34 §1](modules/34-i18n.md#1-i18n-l10n-and-the-locale-language-tags-what-a-locale-decides-and-locale_id))
- <a id="localized-build"></a>**localized build**: an `ng build` with `localize` on: one compilation, then one translated copy of the app per locale ([34 §7](modules/34-i18n.md#7-compile-time-localization-one-build-per-locale-subpath-lang-and-dir))
- <a id="location"></a>**Location**: the Angular service that writes and reads the app's URL through a `LocationStrategy`; `path()` returns the route without the base href ([24 §10](modules/24-routing.md#10-the-router-and-the-platform-history-api-navigation-api-and-location-strategies))
- <a id="location-nginx"></a>**location (Nginx)**: an Nginx block that configures the requests whose URI it matches ([44 §3](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#3-nginx-for-a-single-page-app-location-matching-the-try_files-fallback-caching-headers-for-hashed-assets-and-gzip-or-brotli))
- <a id="locationstrategy"></a>**LocationStrategy**: the class that decides how a route becomes a browser URL; `PathLocationStrategy` is the default and `HashLocationStrategy` comes from `withHashLocation()` ([24 §10](modules/24-routing.md#10-the-router-and-the-platform-history-api-navigation-api-and-location-strategies))
- <a id="locator"></a>**locator**: what a query searches for: a class, a template reference name or any provider token ([19 §5](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#5-queries-view-and-content-queries-with-signal-queries))
- <a id="locator-harness"></a>**locator (harness)**: a function a harness builds with `locatorFor` to find an element or child harness inside its host ([36 §10](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#10-component-harnesses-for-material-the-cdk-and-your-own-components))
- <a id="locator-playwright"></a>**locator (Playwright)**: a description of how a user finds an element, resolved again on every use and strict by default ([30 §4](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#4-locators-page-objects-and-fixtures))
- <a id="lock"></a>**lock**: a mechanism that refuses other edits while one user holds it ([46 §7](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#7-a-notifications-center-and-an-editing-indicator-delivery-unread-counts-across-tabs-and-presence-with-a-time-to-live))
- <a id="lockfile"></a>**lockfile**: `package-lock.json`: the exact version, tarball URL and integrity hash of every installed package, so an install repeats and a swapped file is refused ([33 §11](modules/33-security.md#11-dependency-and-supply-chain-security-lockfiles-npm-audit-install-scripts-and-subresource-integrity))
- <a id="logical-assignment"></a>**logical assignment**: `||=`, `&&=` and `??=`, which assign only when the corresponding operator would pick the right side ([01 §7](modules/01-js-values-types-coercion.md#7-null-undefined-and-the-nullish-operators)).
- <a id="logical-property"></a>**logical property**: a property defined relative to the writing mode (`margin-inline`, `inline-size`) instead of a physical side ([10 §5](modules/10-css-essentials.md#5-responsive-css-media-queries-container-queries-and-fluid-type)). · also in [34 §11](modules/34-i18n.md#11-right-to-left-layouts-dir-logical-css-properties-and-bidirectional-text)
- <a id="login-csrf"></a>**login CSRF**: a forged sign-in that logs the victim into the attacker's account ([42 §6](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#6-csrf-with-cookiecsrftokenrepository-and-angulars-xsrf-support-csrfspa-in-spring-security-7))
- <a id="login-entry"></a>**login entry**: the one route every signed-out path leads to, here our `/login` route; its button starts the full-page BFF login ([Exercise 42.3](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#ex42-3))
- <a id="logout-token"></a>**logout token**: the signed JWT of back-channel logout; it names a user (`sub`) or a provider session (`sid`) and has an `events` claim ([42 §8](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#8-logout-everywhere-and-session-timeout-ux))
- <a id="lone-surrogate"></a>**lone surrogate**: half of a surrogate pair on its own, which makes a string ill-formed ([01 §3](modules/01-js-values-types-coercion.md#3-strings-and-unicode)).
- <a id="long-animation-frame"></a>**long animation frame**: a rendering update that takes more than 50 ms ([31 §2](modules/31-performance.md#2-lab-metrics-in-a-real-browser-performance-observers-devtools-protocol-counters-and-lighthouse-in-ci)).
- <a id="long-polling"></a>**long polling**: the server holds a request until there is news or a timeout, and the client asks again at once (RFC 6202) ([43 §1](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#1-choosing-a-transport-polling-long-polling-server-sent-events-websockets-and-web-push-when-no-tab-is-open))
- <a id="longest-animation"></a>**longest animation**: the animation of an element with the largest delay plus duration, whose end event Angular waits for before removing classes or the element; infinite animations are skipped ([35 §5](modules/35-animations.md#5-animateenter-and-animateleave-with-css-classes-syntax-bindings-and-when-the-element-leaves))
- <a id="longest-increasing-subsequence"></a>**longest increasing subsequence**: the longest run of children whose old positions already increase; Vue's keyed diff leaves it in place and moves the rest ([40 Exercise 40.1](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#ex40-1))
- <a id="lost-response"></a>**lost response**: a request whose answer never arrived (a timeout, a dropped connection), so the client does not know whether it was applied ([46 §8](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#8-under-the-hood-a-money-transfer-with-idempotency-keys-optimistic-ui-that-never-claims-success-and-the-unknown-outcome))
- <a id="lost-update"></a>**lost update**: one client's write overwriting a newer change it never saw ([41 §5](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#5-error-contracts-with-problemdetail-rfc-9457-and-mapping-them-in-angular))
- <a id="lru"></a>**LRU**: least recently used; a cache that evicts the entry untouched the longest ([05 §5](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#5-weak-references-weakmap-weakset-weakref-and-finalizationregistry)).
- <a id="lts-long-term-support"></a>**LTS (long-term support)**: the 12 months after active support, with only critical fixes and security patches ([39 §3](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#3-how-angular-ships-semantic-versioning-the-release-cadence-support-windows-and-the-deprecation-policy))
- <a id="lview"></a>**LView**: the internal array holding one template instance's live state ([35 §12](modules/35-animations.md#12-under-the-hood-from-animateleave-to-a-removed-node))
- <a id="lview-and-lcontainer"></a>**LView and LContainer**: the runtime arrays that hold one view's nodes and binding values, and the embedded views of one block ([18 §9](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#9-under-the-hood-what-the-blocks-compile-to-how-for-reconciles-and-how-defer-moves-between-states))

## M

- <a id="machine-coding-round"></a>**machine-coding round**: a round in which you build one component live ([45 §1](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#1-the-front-end-system-design-interview-what-it-evaluates-its-formats-and-how-it-differs-from-a-backend-design-round))
- <a id="macrotask"></a>**macrotask**: a task of the event loop, such as a `setTimeout` or `setInterval` callback; every microtask runs before the next one ([23 §9](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#9-schedulers-queue-asap-async-and-animation-frame-observeon-and-subscribeon))
- <a id="main-axis--cross-axis"></a>**main axis / cross axis**: the flex container's direction set by `flex-direction`, and the perpendicular one ([10 §3](modules/10-css-essentials.md#3-flexbox-grid-and-subgrid)).
- <a id="main-thread"></a>**main thread**: the browser thread that runs the page's JavaScript, style, layout and paint ([31 §1](modules/31-performance.md#1-measure-first-the-loop-lab-versus-field-and-what-each-tool-answers))
- <a id="makeenvironmentproviders"></a>**makeEnvironmentProviders**: the function that wraps a list of providers into `EnvironmentProviders` ([16 §7](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#7-injectiontoken-multi-providers-environment-providers-and-initializers)).
- <a id="manual-audit"></a>**manual audit**: a human pass (keyboard, zoom, screen reader) for what rules cannot decide ([11 §7](modules/11-accessibility.md#7-testing-accessibility)).
- <a id="manualcleanup"></a>**manualCleanup**: the render-hook option that keeps the hook alive after its owner is destroyed until `AfterRenderRef.destroy()` is called ([20 §4](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#4-render-hooks-afternextrender-aftereveryrender-and-their-phases))
- <a id="mapped-type"></a>**mapped type**: a type that builds one property per key, `{ [K in Keys]: … }`, optionally adding or removing `readonly` and `?` ([07 §2](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#2-keyof-indexed-access-mapped-and-template-literal-types)).
- <a id="mapper"></a>**mapper**: code that copies an entity's fields into a DTO at the controller edge ([41 §2](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#2-dto-contracts-what-crosses-the-wire-absent-versus-null-and-checking-responses-at-the-boundary))
- <a id="maptocanactivate"></a>**mapToCanActivate**: a helper that turns class guards into functional guards ([24 §5](modules/24-routing.md#5-functional-guards-canmatch-canactivate-candeactivate-and-redirectcommand))
- <a id="marble-diagram"></a>**marble diagram**: a text picture of a stream over virtual time, one character per frame: `-` nothing, a letter a value, `|` complete, `#` error ([22 §7](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#7-combining-streams-combinelatest-withlatestfrom-forkjoin-zip-merge-and-concat))
- <a id="margin-collapsing"></a>**margin collapsing**: adjoining vertical margins of in-flow blocks in one BFC combine into one margin (largest positive plus most negative) ([10 §1](modules/10-css-essentials.md#1-the-box-model-and-formatting-contexts)).
- <a id="mark-and-sweep"></a>**mark-and-sweep**: the collection algorithm that keeps what roots reach and frees the rest, so unreachable cycles are collected ([05 §4](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#4-garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks)).
- <a id="marker-directive"></a>**marker directive**: a directive that exists to be found by a query and to carry options, such as a column's header ([19 §4](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#4-template-driven-apis-ng-template-ngtemplateoutlet-and-typed-context))
- <a id="markforcheck"></a>**markForCheck**: tells Angular a view must be re-checked; owner [21](modules/21-change-detection.md) ([14 §8](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#8-the-async-pipe-under-the-hood)).
- <a id="masked-token"></a>**masked token**: a CSRF token XORed with fresh random bytes and sent together with them, so it differs on every response ([42 §6](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#6-csrf-with-cookiecsrftokenrepository-and-angulars-xsrf-support-csrfspa-in-spring-security-7))
- <a id="masking"></a>**masking**: XOR of a frame's payload with a random key; a client masks every frame it sends, a server none (RFC 6455 §5.1) ([43 §5](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#5-websockets-and-stomp-in-spring-the-handshake-frames-messagemapping-brokers-user-destinations-and-heart-beats))
- <a id="mattheme"></a>**mat.theme**: the Angular Material Sass mixin that turns a palette, a font family and a density into the `--mat-sys-*` system variables at build time ([36 §3](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#3-theming-with-material-3-mattheme-system-variables-and-light-and-dark-with-color-scheme))
- <a id="match-controller"></a>**match (controller)**: the controller method that removes and returns every open request a URL, object or predicate selects ([27 §11](modules/27-http-client.md#11-testing-and-the-backend-seam-providehttpclienttesting-httptestingcontroller-and-what-they-replace))
- <a id="material-design-3-m3"></a>**Material Design 3 (M3)**: the third version of Google's Material Design system, which Angular Material's theming follows ([36 §1](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#1-material-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-styled-components-behavior-primitives-and-headless-patterns))
- <a id="material_animations"></a>**MATERIAL_ANIMATIONS**: the Material injection token `{ animationsDisabled?: boolean }` that switches its motion off; without it Material follows `prefers-reduced-motion` ([36 §2](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#2-material-components-in-a-v22-app-standalone-imports-form-fields-dialogs-direction-and-motion))
- <a id="matformfieldcontrol"></a>**MatFormFieldControl**: the contract a control such as `matInput` or `mat-select` implements to sit in a `mat-form-field`; since 22.2 it accepts Signal Forms state ([36 §2](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#2-material-components-in-a-v22-app-standalone-imports-form-fields-dialogs-direction-and-motion))
- <a id="matrix-parameter"></a>**matrix parameter**: a `;key=value` pair on one URL segment, scoped to that segment ([24 §2](modules/24-routing.md#2-route-parameters-path-matrix-query-and-fragment-inheritance-and-component-input-binding))
- <a id="mattabledatasource"></a>**MatTableDataSource**: Material's `DataSource` that filters, sorts and pages an array in memory for a `MatTable` ([36 §7](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#7-tables-and-long-lists-cdktable-datasource-mattable-and-virtual-scrolling))
- <a id="max_animation_timeout"></a>**MAX_ANIMATION_TIMEOUT**: the injection token giving the longest wait, in milliseconds, before Angular removes an element whose leave callback never completed (default 4000) ([35 §6](modules/35-animations.md#6-function-callbacks-and-the-v22-leave-semantics-animationcallbackevent-animationcomplete-and-nested-leaves))
- <a id="maybeasync"></a>**MaybeAsync**: a value, an Observable or a Promise of it; the router keeps the first value of an Observable or Promise guard ([24 §5](modules/24-routing.md#5-functional-guards-canmatch-canactivate-candeactivate-and-redirectcommand))
- <a id="mdc"></a>**MDC**: the `material-components-web` package that Angular Material's v15 components were re-implemented on ([39 §5](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#5-the-version-matrix-part-2-v9-to-v15-from-ivy-to-standalone))
- <a id="mdc-logging"></a>**MDC (logging)**: the logging framework's per-thread context map, from which Spring Boot builds the `[traceId-spanId]` correlation ID ([44 §10](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#10-under-the-hood-opentelemetry-for-the-web-traceparent-and-one-click-traced-to-a-spring-boot-span-and-log-line))
- <a id="meaning"></a>**meaning**: the part of a message's metadata, before `|`, that separates identical texts used differently; it feeds the computed ID ([34 §4](modules/34-i18n.md#4-marking-text-for-translation-i18n-attributes-metadata-ids-and-localize-in-code))
- <a id="media-query"></a>**media query**: an `@media` condition on the viewport or a user preference (`width`, `hover`, `prefers-color-scheme`) ([10 §5](modules/10-css-essentials.md#5-responsive-css-media-queries-container-queries-and-fluid-type)).
- <a id="memo-react"></a>**memo (React)**: a wrapper that skips a component's re-render when its new props are shallow-equal to the old ones ([40 §3](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#3-reacts-model-re-rendering-reconciliation-memoization-and-the-react-compiler))
- <a id="memo-cache"></a>**memo cache**: the per-instance array (`useMemoCache`, internal) in which compiled React code keeps each memoized block's dependencies and results ([40 §3](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#3-reacts-model-re-rendering-reconciliation-memoization-and-the-react-compiler))
- <a id="memoization"></a>**memoization**: caching a function's results by its arguments ([02 §6](modules/02-js-scope-closures-this.md#6-higher-order-functions-currying-partial-application-debounce-and-throttle)). · also in [14 §7](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#7-pipe-computed-or-method-call-in-a-template), [28 §5](modules/28-state-management.md#5-selectors-memoization-composition-and-reading-the-store-as-signals), [31 §3](modules/31-performance.md#3-runtime-cost-of-a-change-onpush-zoneless-signals-and-per-check-template-work)
- <a id="mergeapplicationconfig"></a>**mergeApplicationConfig**: the core function that merges application configurations from left to right ([32 §2](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#2-adding-angularssr-what-ng-add-generates-the-build-output-and-the-server-entry))
- <a id="message"></a>**message**: one unit of translatable text: the source text, its placeholders and its metadata; an `i18n` attribute or a `$localize` call makes one ([34 §4](modules/34-i18n.md#4-marking-text-for-translation-i18n-attributes-metadata-ids-and-localize-in-code))
- <a id="message-authorization"></a>**message authorization**: rules by message type and destination that allow or deny each inbound STOMP message; the first matching rule decides and no match denies ([43 §7](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#7-authentication-on-the-handshake-cookies-tokens-in-the-connect-frame-origin-checks-and-csrf-in-spring-security))
- <a id="message-passing"></a>**message passing**: sending data between page and worker with `postMessage`, received as `message` events ([31 §9](modules/31-performance.md#9-off-the-main-thread-web-workers-in-angular-and-runoutsideangular-in-zone-apps))
- <a id="messagechannel"></a>**MessageChannel**: a pair of connected ports; a message posted on one arrives as a task on the other ([04 §1](modules/04-js-async-event-loop.md#1-the-event-loop-tasks-microtasks-and-rendering)).
- <a id="meta-framework"></a>**meta-framework**: a framework built on top of a UI library, such as Next.js on React ([40 §1](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#1-library-framework-progressive-framework-what-each-project-decides-for-you))
- <a id="meta-reducer"></a>**meta-reducer**: a function that takes a reducer and returns a wrapped reducer; the runtime checks are meta-reducers ([28 §4](modules/28-state-management.md#4-ngrx-store-actions-reducers-createactiongroup-createfeature-and-runtime-checks))
- <a id="metadata"></a>**metadata**: the `@Component` object, data about the class that the compiler reads to build its definition ([13 §1](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#1-component-anatomy-selector-template-styles-imports-and-the-v22-file-convention)).
- <a id="metafile"></a>**metafile**: the JSON description of a build, listing each output file's inputs, their bytes and what each input imports ([31 §6](modules/31-performance.md#6-bundle-analysis-budgets-and-tree-shaking-pitfalls))
- <a id="method-bivariance"></a>**method bivariance**: parameters of members declared with method syntax are compared in both directions even under `strictFunctionTypes` ([07 §5](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#5-variance-parameter-bivariance-and-strictfunctiontypes)).
- <a id="method-validation"></a>**method validation**: Spring MVC's validation of constraints declared directly on a controller method's parameters, which raises `HandlerMethodValidationException` ([41 §7](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#7-validation-on-both-layers-bean-validation-angular-validators-and-server-errors-on-form-fields))
- <a id="micro-frontend"></a>**micro-frontend**: a separately built and deployed part of the UI, loaded by a host at run time ([38 §8](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#8-micro-frontends-module-federation-native-federation-and-when-they-are-justified))
- <a id="microenterprise"></a>**microenterprise**: in the EAA, an enterprise with fewer than 10 persons and annual turnover or balance sheet total of at most EUR 2 million; exempt for services (Article 4(5)) ([11 §1](modules/11-accessibility.md#1-why-accessibility-and-what-the-standards-require)).
- <a id="microsyntax"></a>**microsyntax**: the mini-language inside `*dir="…"` (`let`, `as`, keys such as `of`) that the compiler expands into `<ng-template>` inputs and `let-` variables ([14 §2](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#2-structural-directives-ng-template-templateref-viewcontainerref-and-the-microsyntax)).
- <a id="microtask"></a>**microtask**: a job run right after the current task, before the next one: promise reactions, `await` continuations, `queueMicrotask` ([04 §1](modules/04-js-async-event-loop.md#1-the-event-loop-tasks-microtasks-and-rendering)). · also in [22 §1](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#1-push-collections-observables-promises-iterators-and-laziness)
- <a id="microtask-checkpoint"></a>**microtask checkpoint**: the step that drains the whole microtask queue, including microtasks queued while draining ([04 §1](modules/04-js-async-event-loop.md#1-the-event-loop-tasks-microtasks-and-rendering)).
- <a id="migration-ng-update"></a>**migration (ng update)**: a versioned schematic that `ng update` runs to rewrite code for a new version ([12 §2](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#2-the-cli-and-the-workspace-angularjson-projects-schematics-and-ng-update)). · also in [39 §7](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#7-updating-in-practice-ng-update-migration-schematics-and-the-update-guide)
- <a id="migration-of-a-draft"></a>**migration (of a draft)**: a function turning answers of one schema version into the next ([46 §5](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#5-a-multi-step-form-wizard-with-draft-saving-a-step-model-per-step-validation-and-drafts-that-survive-and-migrate))
- <a id="migration-pass"></a>**migration pass**: one run of the standalone schematic in one mode, after which the app should build and its tests pass ([15 §7](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#7-migrating-with-ng-generate-angularcorestandalone-three-passes-hybrid-apps-and-common-failures)).
- <a id="migration-path"></a>**migration path**: the mapping from an old API's features to their replacements and the order in which to apply it ([35 §9](modules/35-animations.md#9-migrating-from-angularanimations-to-native-css-and-animateenteranimateleave))
- <a id="migration-program"></a>**migration program**: many migrations run as small changes in a planned order, instead of one pull request ([39 §10](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#10-a-migration-program-for-an-old-angular-codebase-order-dependencies-and-checkpoints))
- <a id="min-content-size"></a>**min-content size**: a box's size with every soft wrap opportunity taken; for text, roughly the longest unbreakable word ([10 §3](modules/10-css-essentials.md#3-flexbox-grid-and-subgrid)).
- <a id="minification"></a>**minification**: rewriting code into fewer characters without changing behavior ([12 §3](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#3-building-the-application-builder-output-layout-and-budgets)). · also in [39 §2](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#2-why-angular-was-rewritten-and-what-carried-over)
- <a id="minification-safe-di-annotation"></a>**minification-safe DI annotation**: AngularJS dependency names listed as strings (`fn.$inject` or an array ending with the function), so renaming parameters does not break injection ([39 §2](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#2-why-angular-was-rewritten-and-what-carried-over))
- <a id="minimum-and-after"></a>**minimum and after**: `after` delays entering the loading state; `minimum` holds a state once entered ([18 §5](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#5-defer-blocks-and-states-placeholder-loading-error-minimum-and-after))
- <a id="minimum-query-length"></a>**minimum query length**: the shortest normalized input that may be looked up or sent ([46 §2](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#2-autocomplete-and-typeahead-search-a-request-policy-a-prefix-cache-ranking-on-the-server-and-an-accessible-combobox))
- <a id="minor-unit"></a>**minor unit**: the smallest unit of a currency, such as the cent; `JPY` and `CLP` have 0 digits, `EUR` and `USD` 2 ([34 §10](modules/34-i18n.md#10-dates-times-numbers-and-money-time-zones-currency-codes-and-minor-units))
- <a id="minor-units"></a>**minor units**: the smallest unit of a currency counted as an integer, such as cents ([34 §10](modules/34-i18n.md#10-dates-times-numbers-and-money-time-zones-currency-codes-and-minor-units); [41 §9](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#9-dates-time-zones-decimals-and-money-across-the-wire)) · also in [Exercise 47.1](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#ex47-1)
- <a id="mirroring"></a>**mirroring**: flipping a picture for right-to-left layouts; only direction-bearing icons need it ([34 §11](modules/34-i18n.md#11-right-to-left-layouts-dir-logical-css-properties-and-bidirectional-text))
- <a id="missing-key-handler"></a>**missing-key handler**: the class that decides what a runtime library shows for a missing key; both libraries' defaults return the key ([34 §9](modules/34-i18n.md#9-runtime-translation-libraries-transloco-and-ngx-translate-lazy-scopes-and-the-trade-off-against-built-in-i18n))
- <a id="mitigation"></a>**mitigation**: an action that stops users' pain before the cause is known, such as restoring the previous release's files ([47 §2](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#2-four-front-end-stories-a-performance-win-a-migration-a-production-incident-and-a-disagreement-over-architecture))
- <a id="mix-up-attack"></a>**mix-up attack**: tricking a client into sending a code to the wrong authorization server; the `iss` parameter exposes it ([42 §2](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#2-oauth-20-and-openid-connect-for-a-single-page-app-authorization-code-with-pkce-and-why-the-implicit-flow-is-gone))
- <a id="mixin"></a>**mixin**: a function that takes a class and returns a subclass with added behavior ([03 §4](modules/03-js-objects-prototypes-classes.md#4-inheritance-composition-and-mixins)).
- <a id="mixin-sass"></a>**mixin (Sass)**: a Sass block of declarations reused with `@include`, optionally taking arguments and a `@content` block ([10 §7](modules/10-css-essentials.md#7-native-nesting-versus-sass)).
- <a id="mixing-rule"></a>**mixing rule**: legacy `@angular/animations` and `animate.enter`/`animate.leave` must not share a component or a content projection; separate components of one app may use either ([35 §9](modules/35-animations.md#9-migrating-from-angularanimations-to-native-css-and-animateenteranimateleave))
- <a id="mock"></a>**mock**: a test double pre-programmed with expectations about the calls it should receive; not the same as `vi.fn` ([29 §7](modules/29-testing-angular.md#7-test-doubles-fakes-stubs-spies-and-mocks-vifn-provider-overrides-and-over-mocking))
- <a id="mock-clock"></a>**mock clock**: a replacement for the timer functions that moves only when the test advances it ([29 §10](modules/29-testing-angular.md#10-async-tests-vitest-fake-timers-versus-fakeasync-and-waitforasync))
- <a id="mockstore"></a>**MockStore**: the test double from `@ngrx/store/testing` whose `overrideSelector` fixes a selector's result ([28 §5](modules/28-state-management.md#5-selectors-memoization-composition-and-reading-the-store-as-signals)) · also in [29 §9](modules/29-testing-angular.md#9-testing-guards-interceptors-forms-effects-resources-and-stores)
- <a id="model"></a>**model**: an input plus an output named `xChange` behind one `[(x)]` ([19 §1](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#1-inputs-outputs-and-two-way-binding-the-parent-and-child-contract))
- <a id="model-input"></a>**model input**: a writable signal input made with `model()`, bound two ways with `[(…)]`; Angular Aria's listbox selection `value` is one ([36 §11](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#11-angular-aria-headless-signal-driven-aria-patterns))
- <a id="model-signal"></a>**model signal**: the writable signal you own that holds a form's data and is the source of truth for `form()` ([26 §1](modules/26-signal-forms.md#1-why-a-third-forms-api-model-first-signals-end-to-end))
- <a id="model-first-form"></a>**model-first form**: a form whose source of truth is a model signal, not a tree of controls ([26 §1](modules/26-signal-forms.md#1-why-a-third-forms-api-model-first-signals-end-to-end))
- <a id="modifier"></a>**modifier**: an `inject()` option (`optional`, `self`, `skipSelf`, `host`) that changes where the walk starts or ends, or whether a miss returns `null` ([16 §5](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#5-the-injector-tree-platform-root-route-and-element-injectors-and-the-modifiers)).
- <a id="modular-monolith"></a>**modular monolith**: one deployable split into libraries with enforced boundaries ([38 §8](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#8-micro-frontends-module-federation-native-federation-and-when-they-are-justified))
- <a id="module-augmentation"></a>**module augmentation**: `declare module './x' { … }` inside a module, merging new members into existing declarations of another module ([06 §8](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#8-declaration-files-types-and-augmentation)).
- <a id="module-constructor"></a>**module constructor**: the constructor of an NgModule class, run when the injector that imports the module is created ([15 §5](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#5-legacy-ngmodule-patterns-declarations-exports-forrootforchild-sharedmodule-and-coremodule)).
- <a id="module-environment"></a>**module environment**: the scope of a module's top-level declarations, where imports are indirect bindings ([05 §1](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#1-es-modules-static-structure-linking-and-live-bindings)).
- <a id="module-federation"></a>**Module Federation**: a webpack (or rspack) runtime that loads remotes and negotiates shared packages ([38 §8](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#8-micro-frontends-module-federation-native-federation-and-when-they-are-justified))
- <a id="module-map"></a>**module map**: the browser's per-Document (or per-worker) table, keyed by URL and module type, that makes each module fetch, parse and evaluate once ([05 §1](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#1-es-modules-static-structure-linking-and-live-bindings)).
- <a id="module-namespace-object"></a>**module namespace object**: the object an `import * as ns` or `import()` gives you, with one read-only property per export ([05 §2](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#2-commonjs-interop-and-dynamic-import)).
- <a id="module-record"></a>**Module Record**: the engine's description of one module (its import and export entries and, after linking, its environment) ([05 §1](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#1-es-modules-static-structure-linking-and-live-bindings)).
- <a id="module-resolution"></a>**module resolution**: how the compiler maps an import specifier to a file (`bundler`, `nodenext`) ([06 §7](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#7-tsconfig-the-strict-family-module-settings-and-typescript-60-defaults)).
- <a id="modulewithproviders"></a>**ModuleWithProviders**: an object `{ ngModule, providers? }` that pairs an NgModule with extra providers; what `forRoot()` and `forChild()` return ([15 §5](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#5-legacy-ngmodule-patterns-declarations-exports-forrootforchild-sharedmodule-and-coremodule)).
- <a id="monkey-patch"></a>**monkey patch**: replacing a function of another object at run time, as zone.js does with the browser's async APIs ([21 §3](modules/21-change-detection.md#3-zonejs-and-ngzone-how-changes-used-to-be-detected-and-why-zone-left-the-default))
- <a id="monorepo"></a>**monorepo**: one repository that holds many projects ([38 §7](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#7-nx-monorepos-the-project-graph-tags-nxenforce-module-boundaries-affected-commands-and-caching))
- <a id="multi-provider"></a>**multi provider**: a provider with `multi: true`; every such provider for one token appends to one array, kept per injector ([16 §7](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#7-injectiontoken-multi-providers-environment-providers-and-initializers)).
- <a id="multi-stage-build"></a>**multi-stage build**: a Dockerfile with several `FROM` lines, where a later stage copies chosen files from an earlier one and leaves the rest behind ([44 §2](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#2-a-docker-multi-stage-build-for-angular-build-and-runtime-stages-layer-caching-dockerignore-and-a-non-root-image))
- <a id="multipartform-data"></a>**multipart/form-data**: an HTTP request body that carries several parts, such as a file and its metadata, each with its own headers ([41 §8](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#8-files-multipart-and-chunked-uploads-progress-and-downloads-with-content-disposition-and-streaming))
- <a id="multiplexing--stream"></a>**multiplexing / stream**: interleaving many independent request-response pairs (streams) on one connection ([09 §3](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#3-http11-http2-and-http3)).
- <a id="must-have-gate"></a>**must-have gate**: a requirement an option passes or fails, removing a failing option before any scoring ([40 §8](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#8-choosing-a-framework-balanced-trade-offs-and-how-to-answer-why-angular))
- <a id="mutation"></a>**mutation**: a server write run by `injectMutation`, which tracks its pending and error state ([28 §11](modules/28-state-management.md#11-alternatives-and-the-decision-framework-ngxs-elf-tanstack-query-and-when-a-store-library-is-justified))
- <a id="mutation-xss"></a>**mutation XSS**: markup that changes when it is parsed again, so a checked string turns dangerous ([33 §3](modules/33-security.md#3-sanitization-by-security-context-html-style-url-and-resource-url))
- <a id="mutationobserver"></a>**MutationObserver**: an observer of DOM changes whose records are batched and delivered in a microtask after the synchronous code ([08 §3](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#3-dom-apis-and-observers)).

## N

- <a id="name-from-content"></a>**name from content**: a role's rule that its name is the text of its children, as for buttons and links ([11 §6](modules/11-accessibility.md#6-accessible-names-descriptions-and-forms)).
- <a id="name-prohibited"></a>**name prohibited**: a role (such as `generic`) on which `aria-label` and `aria-labelledby` must not be used ([11 §3](modules/11-accessibility.md#3-aria-roles-states-properties-and-its-rules)).
- <a id="named-auxiliary-outlet"></a>**named (auxiliary) outlet**: an outlet with a `name`, showing the routes marked `outlet: '<name>'`; its branch is written in parentheses in the URL and navigates on its own ([24 §3](modules/24-routing.md#3-nested-and-named-outlets))
- <a id="named-event"></a>**named event**: an event whose stream block has an `event:` field; only a listener added for that name receives it, not `onmessage` ([43 §4](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#4-the-angular-sse-client-eventsource-or-a-fetch-stream-as-an-observable-and-when-httpclient-fits))
- <a id="named-input"></a>**named input**: a reusable name for a set of task inputs, such as the default `{projectRoot}/**/*` ([38 §7](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#7-nx-monorepos-the-project-graph-tags-nxenforce-module-boundaries-affected-commands-and-caching))
- <a id="nan"></a>**NaN**: the number value for an undefined numeric result; the only value not equal to itself ([01 §2](modules/01-js-values-types-coercion.md#2-numbers)).
- <a id="narrowing"></a>**narrowing**: the checker refining a variable's type after a test, separately on each branch ([06 §3](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#3-narrowing-and-control-flow-analysis)). · also in [13 §3](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#3-safe-navigation-the-non-null-assertion-and-any)
- <a id="native-control"></a>**native control**: an `<input>`, `<select>` or `<textarea>` element bound with `[formField]`, as opposed to a custom control ([26 §3](modules/26-signal-forms.md#3-binding-with-formfield-native-inputs-attributes-classes-and-rendering)).
- <a id="native-federation"></a>**Native Federation**: Module Federation's model on ES modules and an import map, built with esbuild through the Angular application builder ([38 §8](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#8-micro-frontends-module-federation-native-federation-and-when-they-are-justified))
- <a id="navigation-and-transition"></a>**navigation and transition**: one attempt to reach a URL, run by the router as a transition with a navigation id that grows by one per attempt ([24 §7](modules/24-routing.md#7-the-navigation-lifecycle-events-cancellation-and-the-transition-pipeline))
- <a id="navigation-api"></a>**Navigation API**: the `navigation` object whose `navigate` event reports every navigation and lets a page intercept same-origin ones ([08 §7](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#7-history-api-versus-navigation-api)).
- <a id="navigation-error-handler-withnavigationerrorhandler"></a>**navigation error handler (withNavigationErrorHandler)**: a function run in an injection context when a navigation fails; returning a `RedirectCommand` turns the error into a redirect ([24 §6](modules/24-routing.md#6-resolvers-and-route-data-resolvefn-failures-and-router-resources))
- <a id="navigation-object"></a>**Navigation object**: the description of a navigation (`id`, `extractedUrl`, `finalUrl`, `trigger`, `abort`) held by `currentNavigation` while it runs ([24 §7](modules/24-routing.md#7-the-navigation-lifecycle-events-cancellation-and-the-transition-pipeline))
- <a id="navigation-request"></a>**navigation request**: a `GET` with mode `navigate` that accepts HTML and matches `navigationUrls`, answered with the version's `index.html` ([37 §5](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#5-caching-with-asset-and-data-groups-navigation-requests-and-the-app-shell))
- <a id="navigationcancellationcode"></a>**NavigationCancellationCode**: the enum on `NavigationCancel.code`: `Redirect` 0, `SupersededByNewNavigation` 1, `NoDataFromResolver` 2, `GuardRejected` 3, `Aborted` 4 ([24 §7](modules/24-routing.md#7-the-navigation-lifecycle-events-cancellation-and-the-transition-pipeline))
- <a id="navigationerror"></a>**NavigationError**: the router event for a navigation that failed with an error, for example a resolver that threw; the navigation promise rejects ([24 §6](modules/24-routing.md#6-resolvers-and-route-data-resolvefn-failures-and-router-resources))
- <a id="necessaryasof"></a>**necessaryAsOf**: in the Update Guide's data, the version from which a step's change is required ([39 §12](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#12-under-the-hood-how-ng-update-selects-migrations-and-how-the-update-guide-builds-its-checklist))
- <a id="nested-expression"></a>**nested expression**: an ICU expression written inside a branch of another, such as a plural inside a select ([34 §5](modules/34-i18n.md#5-icu-expressions-plural-select-and-nesting))
- <a id="nested-shape"></a>**nested shape**: entities stored inside their parents, as an API returns them ([45 §4](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#4-component-architecture-data-model-and-data-flow-from-screens-to-a-component-tree-and-a-client-side-model))
- <a id="nesting-selector-"></a>**nesting selector (&)**: the selector that stands for the parent rule's selector in a nested rule, in native CSS and in Sass ([10 §7](modules/10-css-essentials.md#7-native-nesting-versus-sass)).
- <a id="network-profile"></a>**network profile**: a named round-trip time and throughput used to estimate or simulate loading ([45 §3](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#3-requirements-first-functional-and-non-functional-requirements-constraints-and-back-of-envelope-numbers))
- <a id="network-layer-mocking"></a>**network-layer mocking**: intercepting the browser's own requests and answering them, so the app runs unchanged against a scripted backend ([30 §5](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#5-api-mocking-at-the-network-layer-and-where-contract-tests-take-over))
- <a id="never"></a>**never**: the type with no values; assignable to everything, nothing is assignable to it ([06 §4](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#4-unions-intersections-and-discriminated-unions)).
- <a id="new-items-buffer"></a>**new-items buffer**: live posts held out of the list until the reader asks to see them ([46 §3](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#3-an-infinite-scroll-feed-cursor-pages-deduplication-new-items-without-a-jump-and-a-bounded-window))
- <a id="ng-add"></a>**ng add**: the CLI command that installs a package and runs its setup schematic ([30 §2](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#2-playwright-and-cypress-architecture-ng-e2e-and-the-end-of-protractor))
- <a id="ng-global-utilities"></a>**ng global utilities**: debugging functions such as `ng.getComponent` published on `window.ng` in development mode ([12 §5](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#5-the-dev-loop-ng-serve-hot-module-replacement-and-angular-devtools)).
- <a id="ng-update-migration"></a>**ng update migration**: a code change the CLI applies to a workspace when updating a package, such as `migrate-karma-to-vitest` ([29 §3](modules/29-testing-angular.md#3-karma-jasmine-and-jest-the-legacy-and-alternative-runners-and-migrating-to-vitest))
- <a id="ng_async_validators"></a>**NG_ASYNC_VALIDATORS**: the multi-provider token on which a directive implementing `AsyncValidator` registers ([25 §6](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#6-async-validators-pending-status-cancellation-and-debouncing))
- <a id="ng_status_classes"></a>**NG_STATUS_CLASSES**: the preset, exported from `@angular/forms/signals/compat`, that maps the seven `ng-` status classes to field state ([26 §3](modules/26-signal-forms.md#3-binding-with-formfield-native-inputs-attributes-classes-and-rendering)).
- <a id="ng_value_accessor"></a>**NG_VALUE_ACCESSOR**: the multi-provider token under which a component or directive registers itself as an accessor ([25 §10](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#10-controlvalueaccessor-and-the-validator-interface-custom-controls-under-the-hood))
- <a id="ng-packagr"></a>**ng-packagr**: the build tool behind `ng build ui-lib` that writes a library in the Angular Package Format ([38 §5](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#5-angular-cli-libraries-ng-generate-library-ng-packagr-and-consuming-the-build))
- <a id="ng-update-field"></a>**ng-update field**: the `package.json` field holding a package's `packageGroup` and `migrations` ([12 §2](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#2-the-cli-and-the-workspace-angularjson-projects-schematics-and-ng-update)).
- <a id="ng02824"></a>**NG02824**: the runtime error code of `FetchBackend` when upload progress is requested ([27 §9](modules/27-http-client.md#9-progress-events-reportuploadprogress-reportdownloadprogress-and-why-upload-progress-needs-xhr))
- <a id="ngc"></a>**ngc**: the Angular compiler's command-line tool; it compiles `@Component` and other decorators ahead of time into static definitions such as `ɵfac` and `ɵcmp` ([07 §7](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#7-decorators-tc39-standard-versus-experimentaldecorators-and-what-angular-does-with-them)). · also in [12 §7](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#7-aot-versus-jit-and-template-type-checking), [13](modules/13-components-and-templates.md), [14](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md), [18 §1](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#1-built-in-blocks-versus-directives-and-the-if-family-with-as), [19 §1](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#1-inputs-outputs-and-two-way-binding-the-parent-and-child-contract)
- <a id="ngcc"></a>**ngcc**: the Angular compatibility compiler, which made View Engine libraries usable in Ivy builds until v16 removed it ([39 §5](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#5-the-version-matrix-part-2-v9-to-v15-from-ivy-to-standalone))
- <a id="ngcontrolstatus"></a>**NgControlStatus**: the directive whose host bindings set the `ng-valid`, `ng-touched`, `ng-pristine` and similar classes from the control's signal-backed state ([25 §7](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#7-status-updateon-valuechanges-and-the-unified-events-stream))
- <a id="ngcspnonce"></a>**ngCspNonce**: the root-element attribute that carries the response's nonce to Angular and to the build's nonce copying ([33 §5](modules/33-security.md#5-content-security-policy-with-angular-nonces-csp_nonce-and-autocsp))
- <a id="ngform"></a>**NgForm**: the directive that attaches to a `<form>` (unless `ngNoForm`) and owns the `FormGroup` that template-driven controls register with ([25 §4](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#4-template-driven-forms-in-depth-ngmodel-ngform-and-asynchronous-registration))
- <a id="ngjitmode"></a>**ngJitMode**: a global that an optimized build sets to `false`; code guarded by it, such as `ɵɵsetNgModuleScope`, runs only when just-in-time compilation is possible ([15 §8](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-for-modules-and-standalone-components-and-the-standalone-injector); [Q12.15](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#q12-15)).
- <a id="ngmodel-and-the-name-attribute"></a>**NgModel and the name attribute**: the `ngModel` directive, which creates a `FormControl` keyed by `name` and registers it with the nearest `NgForm` or `ngModelGroup` in a microtask ([25 §4](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#4-template-driven-forms-in-depth-ngmodel-ngform-and-asynchronous-registration))
- <a id="ngmodelgroup"></a>**ngModelGroup**: a directive that nests the `ngModel` controls inside it in a child group of the form ([25 §4](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#4-template-driven-forms-in-depth-ngmodel-ngform-and-asynchronous-registration))
- <a id="ngmodeloptions"></a>**ngModelOptions**: the options object of one `ngModel`; `standalone: true` keeps the control out of the enclosing form ([25 §4](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#4-template-driven-forms-in-depth-ngmodel-ngform-and-asynchronous-registration))
- <a id="ngmodule"></a>**NgModule**: a class decorated with `@NgModule` that groups declarables and providers; it gave declared components a compilation scope, gave an injector providers, and was the unit the router lazy loaded ([15 §1](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#1-what-ngmodules-did-compilation-scope-injector-and-lazy-loading-unit)).
- <a id="ngmoduleref"></a>**NgModuleRef**: the handle to a running NgModule, returned by `bootstrapModule`; its `instance` is the module and its `injector` the module's injector ([15 §3](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#3-bootstrapping-and-providers-without-modules-bootstrapapplication-provide-functions-and-importprovidersfrom)).
- <a id="ngnocva"></a>**ngNoCva**: an attribute that opts a host out of the built-in `ControlValueAccessor` directives so a custom control binds ([26 §8](modules/26-signal-forms.md#8-custom-controls-formvaluecontrol-formcheckboxcontrol-and-how-formfield-picks-a-control)).
- <a id="ngondestroy"></a>**ngOnDestroy**: the method Angular calls on an instance when its owner (an injector or a component) is destroyed; hooks in general are owned by module 20 ([16 §8](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#8-lifetime-and-dynamic-injectors-destroyref-environmentinjector-injectorcreate-forward-references-and-cycles)).
- <a id="ngprojectas"></a>**ngProjectAs**: an attribute that makes a node match slots by a given selector instead of its own identity; static values only ([19 §3](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#3-content-projection-ng-content-slots-ngprojectas-fallback-content-and-the-lifecycle-trap))
- <a id="ngrx-store"></a>**NgRx Store**: the single state tree, fed by dispatched actions and read through selectors ([28 §4](modules/28-state-management.md#4-ngrx-store-actions-reducers-createactiongroup-createfeature-and-runtime-checks))
- <a id="ngservermode"></a>**ngServerMode**: the global flag Angular checks to know it is rendering on a server ([18 §7](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#7-what-lands-in-a-chunk-testing-deferred-blocks-and-the-ssr-pointer)) · also in [20 §4](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#4-render-hooks-afternextrender-aftereveryrender-and-their-phases)
- <a id="ngskiphydration"></a>**ngSkipHydration**: an attribute on a component host that opts the component out of hydration: the browser clears and re-renders it ([32 §4](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#4-hydration-reusing-the-servers-dom-mismatches-and-ngskiphydration))
- <a id="ngsw-bypass"></a>**ngsw-bypass**: a request header or query parameter that makes the Angular worker leave the request to the browser ([37 §5](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#5-caching-with-asset-and-data-groups-navigation-requests-and-the-app-shell))
- <a id="ngsw-configjson"></a>**ngsw-config.json**: the file you write with the Angular worker's rules (asset groups, data groups, navigation URLs, `appData`); the build reads it and the worker never does ([37 Q37.09](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#q37-09))
- <a id="ngsw-workerjs"></a>**ngsw-worker.js**: the prebuilt worker script the build copies from `@angular/service-worker`; it reads `ngsw.json`, installs versions and serves requests from them ([37 Q37.09](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#q37-09))
- <a id="ngswjson"></a>**ngsw.json**: the manifest the build generates from `ngsw-config.json`, listing every file with its hash plus `appData` ([37 §4](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#4-angularservice-worker-registration-ngsw-configjson-and-the-generated-ngswjson))
- <a id="ngupgrade"></a>**ngUpgrade**: the `@angular/upgrade/static` package that builds a hybrid application ([39 §9](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#9-from-angularjs-to-angular-ngupgrade-hybrids-the-strangler-fig-and-rewrites))
- <a id="ngx_brotli"></a>**ngx_brotli**: Google's third-party pair of Nginx modules (on-the-fly and static) that adds Brotli; nginx open source has none ([44 §3](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#3-nginx-for-a-single-page-app-location-matching-the-try_files-fallback-caching-headers-for-hashed-assets-and-gzip-or-brotli))
- <a id="ngxs-state-class"></a>**NGXS state class**: a class decorated with `@State` whose `@Action` methods change the state through a `StateContext` ([28 §11](modules/28-state-management.md#11-alternatives-and-the-decision-framework-ngxs-elf-tanstack-query-and-when-a-store-library-is-justified))
- <a id="ngzone"></a>**NgZone**: the zone Angular forks from zone.js; it emits `onUnstable`, `onMicrotaskEmpty` and `onStable` and, in a zone-based app, triggers a tick ([21 §3](modules/21-change-detection.md#3-zonejs-and-ngzone-how-changes-used-to-be-detected-and-why-zone-left-the-default))
- <a id="no-op-store"></a>**no-op store**: a storage implementation whose reads return nothing and whose writes do nothing, provided on the server so the same component runs there ([32 Exercise 32.2](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#ex32-2))
- <a id="nodatafromresolver"></a>**NoDataFromResolver**: the cancellation code of a navigation whose resolver completed without a value ([24 §6](modules/24-routing.md#6-resolvers-and-route-data-resolvefn-failures-and-router-resources))
