- <a id="node-injector"></a>**node injector**: the injector tied to a template node, through which `ɵɵpipe` creates a pipe instance; owner [16](modules/16-dependency-injection.md) ([14 §5](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#5-pipes-pure-and-impure-custom-pipes-injection-and-imports)).
- <a id="non-determinism"></a>**non-determinism**: an input a test does not control, such as timing, shared data, the clock or the environment, that makes results differ between runs ([30 §7](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#7-flaky-tests-root-causes-diagnosis-and-fixes))
- <a id="non-functional-requirement"></a>**non-functional requirement**: a statement of how well the product does it, such as speed, accessibility or availability ([45 §1](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#1-the-front-end-system-design-interview-what-it-evaluates-its-formats-and-how-it-differs-from-a-backend-design-round))
- <a id="non-null-assertion-x"></a>**non-null assertion (x!)**: a claim to the type checker that a value is not `null` or `undefined`; erased at compile time, so it changes no run-time behavior ([13 §3](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#3-safe-navigation-the-non-null-assertion-and-any)).
- <a id="nonce"></a>**nonce**: a random, per-response value placed in the CSP and on each trusted `<script>`; only scripts carrying it run ([09 §8](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#8-content-security-policy-and-trusted-types)).
- <a id="nonce-openid-connect"></a>**nonce (OpenID Connect)**: a random value copied into the ID token, tying it to the login ([42 §2](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#2-oauth-20-and-openid-connect-for-a-single-page-app-authorization-code-with-pkce-and-why-the-implicit-flow-is-gone))
- <a id="noop-driver"></a>**noop driver**: the animation driver of `provideNoopAnimations()`, which plays nothing and still reports states and callbacks ([35 §8](modules/35-animations.md#8-legacy-angularanimations-triggers-states-transitions-query-and-stagger))
- <a id="normalization-of-a-query"></a>**normalization (of a query)**: turning the input into one key per meaning: trim, lowercase, collapse spaces ([46 §2](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#2-autocomplete-and-typeahead-search-a-request-policy-a-prefix-cache-ranking-on-the-server-and-an-accessible-combobox))
- <a id="normalization-and-denormalization"></a>**normalization** and **denormalization**: storing a collection as `ids` plus an id-keyed map so one update replaces one entry, and joining them back for display ([28 §10](modules/28-state-management.md#10-optimistic-updates-normalization-and-undo))
- <a id="normalized-shape"></a>**normalized shape**: entities stored once in id-keyed maps and referred to by id ([45 §4](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#4-component-architecture-data-model-and-data-flow-from-screens-to-a-component-tree-and-a-client-side-model))
- <a id="normalized-uri"></a>**normalized URI**: the request URI after decoding `%XX`, resolving `.` and `..`, and merging repeated slashes; what Nginx matches locations against ([44 §3](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#3-nginx-for-a-single-page-app-location-matching-the-try_files-fallback-caching-headers-for-hashed-assets-and-gzip-or-brotli))
- <a id="normalizer"></a>**normalizer**: a function that maps text to a comparable form (here lower-casing); the exercise injects it through `SEARCH_NORMALIZER` ([14 Exercise 14.4](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#ex14-4)).
- <a id="notification"></a>**notification**: one call of an observer's callbacks: a `next` with a value, an `error` or a `complete` ([22 §2](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#2-anatomy-observable-observer-subscription-and-teardown))
- <a id="notification-change-detection"></a>**notification (change detection)**: a call to the scheduler saying some state may need a render, made by a signal read in a template, `setInput`, a listener, `markForCheck`, an attached view or a render hook ([21 §5](modules/21-change-detection.md#5-zoneless-change-detection-the-scheduler-its-triggers-and-pendingtasks))
- <a id="notification-reactivity"></a>**notification (reactivity)**: what a state write does to tell the runtime that work is owed ([40 §9](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#9-under-the-hood-one-state-change-traced-through-angular-react-vue-and-solid))
- <a id="notification-contract"></a>**notification contract**: zero or more `next`, then at most one `error` or `complete`, then nothing; the teardown runs once ([22 §2](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#2-anatomy-observable-observer-subscription-and-teardown))
- <a id="notification-payload"></a>**notification payload**: the JSON a backend sends as a push message, whose `notification` field the Angular worker turns into a shown notification ([37 §7](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#7-push-notifications-and-installability-swpush-notification-clicks-and-the-manifest))
- <a id="notifications-center"></a>**notifications center**: the bell and its list of unread items ([46 §7](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#7-a-notifications-center-and-an-editing-indicator-delivery-unread-counts-across-tabs-and-presence-with-a-time-to-live))
- <a id="notifier"></a>**notifier**: an Observable used only as a signal by operators such as `buffer`, `takeUntil` and `skipUntil`; its values are ignored ([22 §6](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#6-the-operator-catalogue-creation-transformation-filtering-utility-conditional-and-aggregation))
- <a id="notifier-retryrepeat-delay"></a>**notifier (retry/repeat delay)**: the Observable a `delay` option yields; its first value re-subscribes, its complete ends the result without the error, its error ends it with that error ([23 §4](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#4-retrying-and-repeating-retry-with-backoff-and-repeat))
- <a id="npm-ci"></a>**npm ci**: the clean install that uses exactly the lockfile, exits with an error when it disagrees with `package.json` and never rewrites it ([33 §11](modules/33-security.md#11-dependency-and-supply-chain-security-lockfiles-npm-audit-install-scripts-and-subresource-integrity)) · also in [38 §11](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#11-a-ci-pipeline-for-a-front-end-lint-test-build-end-to-end-and-deploy-previews)
- <a id="nullable-control-and-nonnullable"></a>**nullable control and nonNullable**: a control typed `T | null` because `reset()` writes `null`; `nonNullable: true` types it `T` and makes `reset()` restore the creation value ([25 §3](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#3-typed-forms-formbuilder-and-nonnullableformbuilder))
- <a id="nullinjector"></a>**NullInjector**: the top of the injector tree; a lookup that reaches it throws `NG0201`, or returns the fallback or `null` when one is given ([16 §5](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#5-the-injector-tree-platform-root-route-and-element-injectors-and-the-modifiers)).
- <a id="nullish"></a>**nullish**: `null` or `undefined`, the two values `??` and `?.` react to ([01 §7](modules/01-js-values-types-coercion.md#7-null-undefined-and-the-nullish-operators)).
- <a id="numberepsilon"></a>**Number.EPSILON**: the gap between 1 and the next representable double; a relative tolerance for float comparison, not an absolute one ([01 §2](modules/01-js-values-types-coercion.md#2-numbers)).
- <a id="numbering-system"></a>**numbering system**: the set of digits a locale writes numbers with, such as `latn` (0-9) or `arab` (Arabic-Indic); reported by `resolvedOptions()` ([34 §2](modules/34-i18n.md#2-the-platforms-intl-apis-dates-numbers-plurals-lists-collation-and-display-names))
- <a id="nx-workspace"></a>**Nx workspace**: a monorepo with Nx's project graph, tags, affected commands and cache on top ([38 §7](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#7-nx-monorepos-the-project-graph-tags-nxenforce-module-boundaries-affected-commands-and-caching))

## O

- <a id="oauth-20"></a>**OAuth 2.0**: a framework in which an authorization server issues an access token that a client uses at a resource server ([42 §2](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#2-oauth-20-and-openid-connect-for-a-single-page-app-authorization-code-with-pkce-and-why-the-implicit-flow-is-gone))
- <a id="object-identity"></a>**object identity**: two object references are equal only when they point to the same object ([01 §1](modules/01-js-values-types-coercion.md#1-values-and-types)).
- <a id="objectis"></a>**Object.is**: JavaScript's "same value" test; for objects it means the same reference; owner [01 §6](modules/01-js-values-types-coercion.md#6-equality) ([14 §5](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#5-pipes-pure-and-impure-custom-pipes-injection-and-imports)).
- <a id="observability"></a>**observability**: how you would notice a failure mode, through signals such as errors and traces ([45 §1](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#1-the-front-end-system-design-interview-what-it-evaluates-its-formats-and-how-it-differs-from-a-backend-design-round))
- <a id="observable"></a>**Observable**: a push collection of zero to many values; a cold source creates a producer per subscription, while a hot source uses a producer outside them ([22 §1](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#1-push-collections-observables-promises-iterators-and-laziness))
- <a id="observableinput"></a>**ObservableInput**: anything `from` accepts as a source (an Observable, array-like, Promise, iterable, async iterable or `ReadableStream`) ([22 §7](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#7-combining-streams-combinelatest-withlatestfrom-forkjoin-zip-merge-and-concat)) · also in [23 §1](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#1-higher-order-observables-switchmap-mergemap-concatmap-and-exhaustmap)
- <a id="observe"></a>**observe**: the request option that picks what the Observable emits: the body, the `HttpResponse` or every event ([27 §3](modules/27-http-client.md#3-making-requests-typed-responses-observe-responsetype-immutable-params-and-headers-and-httpcontext))
- <a id="observer"></a>**observer**: the consumer's set of callbacks, `next`, `error` and `complete`, each optional ([22 §2](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#2-anatomy-observable-observer-subscription-and-teardown))
- <a id="observer-list"></a>**observer list**: the array of subscribers a `Subject` delivers to; `next` iterates a copy of it ([22 §9](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#9-under-the-hood-subscribe-the-subscriber-chain-and-the-subjects-observer-list))
- <a id="offline-level"></a>**offline level**: how much of the app works without a network: none, cached reads or queued writes ([45 §8](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#8-under-the-hood-failure-modes-observability-offline-levels-and-consistency-choices))
- <a id="offline-state"></a>**offline state**: the browser's report that it has no network, shown as a banner while the app keeps working ([37 §10](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#10-error-ux-expected-and-unexpected-errors-http-failures-offline-state-and-route-errors))
- <a id="offset"></a>**offset**: the distance from UTC written after a time, such as `+02:00`; `Z` means zero ([41 §9](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#9-dates-time-zones-decimals-and-money-across-the-wire))
- <a id="offset-pagination"></a>**offset pagination**: asking for a page by number and size, usually with a total count; it can jump to any page but shifts under inserts ([41 §6](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#6-pagination-sorting-and-filtering-offset-versus-cursor-and-how-spring-data-serializes-a-page))
- <a id="oidc-client-library"></a>**OIDC client library**: a package that runs the OAuth or OpenID Connect flow inside the page, such as `angular-oauth2-oidc` or `angular-auth-oidc-client` ([42 §7](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#7-the-angular-side-an-auth-state-at-startup-guards-as-ux-interceptors-and-angular-oauth2-oidc-versus-angular-auth-oidc-client))
- <a id="on"></a>**on**: the helper that pairs an action creator with a reducer handler inside `createReducer` ([28 §4](modules/28-state-management.md#4-ngrx-store-actions-reducers-createactiongroup-createfeature-and-runtime-checks))
- <a id="one-shot-stream"></a>**one-shot stream**: a body stream that can be consumed only once; a second read throws ([09 §2](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#2-fetch-xhr-and-the-request-lifecycle)).
- <a id="one-way-data-flow"></a>**one-way data flow**: property bindings carry values from component to DOM and event bindings from DOM to component, checked once per tick ([39 §2](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#2-why-angular-was-rewritten-and-what-carried-over); owner [13 §4](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#4-property-attribute-class-and-style-bindings))
- <a id="onerrorresumenextwith"></a>**onErrorResumeNextWith**: an operator that moves on to the next source when the current one errors or completes, so errors disappear ([23 §3](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#3-errors-catcherror-placement-finalize-and-error-versus-complete))
- <a id="oninvalid"></a>**onInvalid**: the `submit()` option called when validation blocks the action, after the interactive fields are touched ([26 §7](modules/26-signal-forms.md#7-submission-submit-formroot-submitting-and-server-errors)).
- <a id="onmicrotaskempty--onstable"></a>**onMicrotaskEmpty / onStable**: `NgZone` events emitted when no entry is open and no microtask is pending, the first inside the zone and the second outside it ([21 §3](modules/21-change-detection.md#3-zonejs-and-ngzone-how-changes-used-to-be-detected-and-why-zone-left-the-default))
- <a id="onpush"></a>**OnPush**: a component is re-checked only when an input, an event or a signal it reads changes; the default since v22.0, owner [21](modules/21-change-detection.md) ([13 §1](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#1-component-anatomy-selector-template-styles-imports-and-the-v22-file-convention)). · also in [14 §7](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#7-pipe-computed-or-method-call-in-a-template), [17 §1](modules/17-signals.md#1-why-signals-exist), [19 §1](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#1-inputs-outputs-and-two-way-binding-the-parent-and-child-contract), [20 §1](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#1-the-lifecycle-map-phases-and-the-order-of-the-hooks), [38 §2](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#2-smart-container-and-presentational-components), [39 §6](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#6-the-version-matrix-part-3-v16-to-v22-from-signals-to-onpush-by-default)
- <a id="onpush-compatible"></a>**OnPush-compatible**: code that announces every change its template shows, through a signal, the `async` pipe, `markForCheck`, an input or an event ([21 §6](modules/21-change-detection.md#6-migrating-a-zone-based-app-to-zoneless))
- <a id="onsameurlnavigation"></a>**onSameUrlNavigation**: the router option that sets what a navigation to the current URL does: `'ignore'` (default, skipped) or `'reload'` ([24 §6](modules/24-routing.md#6-resolvers-and-route-data-resolvefn-failures-and-router-resources))
- <a id="onsuccess--onerror"></a>**onSuccess / onError**: the `validateAsync` options that map a resolved result and a failure to field errors ([26 §6](modules/26-signal-forms.md#6-async-validation-and-debounce-validateasync-validatehttp-pending-and-cancellation)).
- <a id="opaque-token"></a>**opaque token**: a token with no readable claims, only a reference the authorization server can look up ([42 §5](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#5-spring-security-as-a-resource-server-validating-jwts-and-turning-scopes-into-authorities))
- <a id="opcode-i18n"></a>**opcode (i18n)**: a number or string in the list the runtime parses a message into, encoding steps such as "create this text node" or "switch this ICU" ([34 §12](modules/34-i18n.md#12-under-the-hood-from-an-i18n-attribute-to-translated-dom))
- <a id="open-graph-tags"></a>**Open Graph tags**: `<meta property="og:…">` elements in the `<head>` defined by the Open Graph protocol ([32 §1](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#1-why-render-on-the-server-csr-ssr-ssg-and-hybrid-rendering-and-what-each-costs))
- <a id="open-redirect"></a>**open redirect**: a redirect that follows an untrusted URL to another site, so an attacker's link sends the visitor to the attacker's page ([42 §7](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#7-the-angular-side-an-auth-state-at-startup-guards-as-ux-interceptors-and-angular-oauth2-oidc-versus-angular-auth-oidc-client))
- <a id="openapi-description"></a>**OpenAPI description**: a JSON (or YAML) document of an API's paths and schemas; springdoc 3.1.1 writes OpenAPI 3.1.0 by default ([41 §3](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#3-openapi-with-springdoc-and-generated-typescript-clients-openapi-generator-orval-and-ng-openapi-gen))
- <a id="openfeature"></a>**OpenFeature**: a vendor-neutral API and SDKs for evaluating flags; the provider, not OpenFeature, does the evaluation ([44 §8](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#8-feature-flags-with-openfeature-and-canary-releases-providers-evaluation-context-sticky-rollout-kill-switches-and-version-skew))
- <a id="openid-connect"></a>**OpenID Connect**: an identity layer on OAuth 2.0 that adds the ID token ([42 §2](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#2-oauth-20-and-openid-connect-for-a-single-page-app-authorization-code-with-pkce-and-why-the-implicit-flow-is-gone))
- <a id="opening-handshake"></a>**opening handshake**: the HTTP `GET` with `Upgrade: websocket` that turns a connection into a WebSocket (RFC 6455 §1.3) ([43 §5](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#5-websockets-and-stomp-in-spring-the-handshake-frames-messagemapping-brokers-user-destinations-and-heart-beats))
- <a id="operation-id"></a>**operation id**: an id on each queued write so the server applies a resent write once ([45 §8](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#8-under-the-hood-failure-modes-observability-offline-levels-and-consistency-choices))
- <a id="operations-dashboard"></a>**operations dashboard**: a screen of widgets that each show one live metric ([46 §6](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#6-a-real-time-operations-dashboard-aggregation-per-widget-staleness-hidden-tabs-and-screens-left-open-for-days))
- <a id="operator-factory"></a>**operator factory**: a function that takes arguments and returns a pipeable operator, such as `map(fn)` ([22 §5](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#5-pipeable-operators-and-writing-your-own))
- <a id="operatorfunction--monotypeoperatorfunction"></a>**OperatorFunction / MonoTypeOperatorFunction**: the types of an operator, `Observable<T>` to `Observable<R>`, and the same with `R` equal to `T` ([22 §5](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#5-pipeable-operators-and-writing-your-own))
- <a id="operatorsubscriber-internal"></a>**OperatorSubscriber (internal)**: the subscriber each built-in operator adds to the chain; it turns a throw in the operator into an `error` notification ([22 §9](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#9-under-the-hood-subscribe-the-subscriber-chain-and-the-subjects-observer-list))
- <a id="opinionated"></a>**opinionated**: the framework picks one way to do each thing ([12 §1](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#1-what-a-framework-does-and-angulars-philosophy)).
- <a id="opt-out-provider"></a>**opt-out provider**: a feature passed to a provider function to switch a default off, such as `withNoIncrementalHydration()` ([39 §11](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#11-the-v22-upgrade-what-ng-update-changes-and-why-each-migration-keeps-the-old-behavior))
- <a id="optimistic-ui"></a>**optimistic UI**: applying a change on screen before the server answers ([45 §8](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#8-under-the-hood-failure-modes-observability-offline-levels-and-consistency-choices))
- <a id="optimistic-update-pessimistic-update-rollback-and-inverse-operation"></a>**optimistic update**, **pessimistic update**, **rollback** and **inverse operation**: applying a change locally before the server answers, waiting for the answer first, undoing the change when the server fails, and the previous value or call that undoes it ([28 §10](modules/28-state-management.md#10-optimistic-updates-normalization-and-undo))
- <a id="optional-chaining-"></a>**optional chaining (?.)**: property access or call that short-circuits the whole chain to `undefined` when its left side is nullish ([01 §7](modules/01-js-values-types-coercion.md#7-null-undefined-and-the-nullish-operators)). · also in [13 §3](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#3-safe-navigation-the-non-null-assertion-and-any)
- <a id="optional-control-key"></a>**optional control key**: an optional property (`nickname?`) in a `FormGroup`'s type, the only key that `addControl` and `removeControl` accept at compile time ([25 §3](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#3-typed-forms-formbuilder-and-nonnullableformbuilder))
- <a id="origin"></a>**origin**: the scheme, host and port of a URL; two URLs are same-origin only when all three match ([09 §5](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#5-the-same-origin-policy-and-cors)).
- <a id="origin-cascade"></a>**origin (cascade)**: where a style sheet comes from: user agent, user or author ([10 §2](modules/10-css-essentials.md#2-the-cascade-origins-specificity-and-layers)).
- <a id="origin-overlay"></a>**origin (overlay)**: the element a connected position strategy places the pane against ([36 §5](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#5-portals-and-overlays-where-floating-ui-renders-how-it-is-positioned-and-how-it-closes))
- <a id="origin-check"></a>**Origin check**: the server comparing a request's `Origin` header with the app's own and rejecting a foreign one on state changes ([33 §9](modules/33-security.md#9-csrf-in-single-page-apps-when-cookies-make-you-vulnerable-samesite-and-angulars-xsrf-support)) · also in [43 §7](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#7-authentication-on-the-handshake-cookies-tokens-in-the-connect-frame-origin-checks-and-csrf-in-spring-security)
- <a id="origin-map"></a>**origin map**: the server-only `HTTP_TRANSFER_CACHE_ORIGIN_MAP` token that renames the origin the server calls to the origin the browser calls, so the transfer cache sees the same request ([32 §8](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#8-data-across-the-boundary-the-http-transfer-cache-transferstate-and-resource-ids))
- <a id="origin-pattern"></a>**origin pattern**: an allowed-origin entry with a wildcard host, such as `https://*.example.com`, set through `setAllowedOriginPatterns`; the alternative to `"*"` when credentials are allowed ([41 §1](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#1-cors-on-both-sides-springs-corsconfigurationsource-spring-security-preflight-caching-credentials-and-the-dev-proxy))
- <a id="origin-server"></a>**origin server**: the server that owns the requested resource, as opposed to a proxy or cache in front of it ([09 §1](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#1-http-semantics-methods-status-codes-and-headers)).
- <a id="outbox"></a>**outbox**: a client-side queue of writes waiting to be sent ([45 §8](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#8-under-the-hood-failure-modes-observability-offline-levels-and-consistency-choices))
- <a id="outcome"></a>**outcome**: the single verdict for a test after all its attempts: `expected`, `unexpected`, `flaky` or `skipped` ([30 §9](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#9-under-the-hood-how-playwrights-runner-groups-shards-retries-and-isolates-tests))
- <a id="outer--inner-observable"></a>**outer / inner Observable**: the outer Observable emits values; each inner Observable is the one an operator creates and subscribes for one outer value ([23 §1](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#1-higher-order-observables-switchmap-mergemap-concatmap-and-exhaustmap))
- <a id="outlet"></a>**outlet**: a directive that renders something it is given, a template (`NgTemplateOutlet`) or a component class (`NgComponentOutlet`) ([14 §4](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#4-built-in-directives-ngclass-ngstyle-the-outlets-and-what-control-flow-replaced)).
- <a id="outlet-commands"></a>**outlet commands**: the `{ outlets: { name: [...] | null } }` object in a `navigate` array, which changes one outlet's branch and removes it with `null` ([24 §3](modules/24-routing.md#3-nested-and-named-outlets))
- <a id="outlet-tree"></a>**outlet tree**: the tree of outlets and their rendered components, which mirrors the route tree ([24 §3](modules/24-routing.md#3-nested-and-named-outlets))
- <a id="output"></a>**output**: an event a child announces and the parent handles with `(x)`; not a DOM event and it does not bubble ([19 §1](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#1-inputs-outputs-and-two-way-binding-the-parent-and-child-contract)) · also in [38 §2](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#2-smart-container-and-presentational-components)
- <a id="output-hashing"></a>**output hashing**: a content hash in each output file name, so only changed files get new URLs ([12 §3](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#3-building-the-application-builder-output-layout-and-budgets)).
- <a id="outputemitterref"></a>**OutputEmitterRef**: the object `output()` returns; a test observes it with `subscribe` ([29 §6](modules/29-testing-angular.md#6-component-tests-dom-queries-componentfixture-signal-inputs-with-setinput-and-bindings-outputs-and-onpush))
- <a id="outputmode"></a>**outputMode**: the application builder option choosing a server build or a static one (`'server'` or `'static'`) ([32 §2](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#2-adding-angularssr-what-ng-add-generates-the-build-output-and-the-server-entry))
- <a id="over-mocking"></a>**over-mocking**: replacing so much, or an invented contract, that a test passes while the real code is broken or the test pins how the code works ([29 §7](modules/29-testing-angular.md#7-test-doubles-fakes-stubs-spies-and-mocks-vifn-provider-overrides-and-over-mocking))
- <a id="overflow-strategy"></a>**overflow strategy**: what Spring does when a send buffer passes its limit: `TERMINATE` (close with 4500, the default) or `DROP` (discard the oldest messages) ([43 §9](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#9-scaling-out-and-under-the-hood-sticky-sessions-the-broker-relay-backpressure-and-one-message-traced-across-two-instances))
- <a id="overlay"></a>**overlay**: an outlet the CDK creates outside your component tree, with a host element, a pane and a position strategy; `createOverlayRef` makes one ([36 §5](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#5-portals-and-overlays-where-floating-ui-renders-how-it-is-positioned-and-how-it-closes))
- <a id="overlay-container"></a>**overlay container**: the `div.cdk-overlay-container` the CDK appends to `body`, where an overlay's host goes by default ([36 §5](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#5-portals-and-overlays-where-floating-ui-renders-how-it-is-positioned-and-how-it-closes))
- <a id="overlay-pane-class"></a>**overlay pane class**: the class a `panelClass` option puts on an overlay's pane element, where an override for a dialog or menu can sit because they render outside the subtree that opened them ([36 §4](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#4-customizing-the-theme-system-and-component-token-overrides-and-theming-your-own-components))
- <a id="override"></a>**override**: a test-only replacement of a provider or of metadata, applied before the module is instantiated ([29 §4](modules/29-testing-angular.md#4-testbed-the-testing-module-inject-runininjectioncontext-overrides-and-teardown))
- <a id="override-mixin"></a>**override mixin**: a Sass mixin that writes custom properties under the selector that includes it; component ones reject a misspelled token name at build time ([36 §4](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#4-customizing-the-theme-system-and-component-token-overrides-and-theming-your-own-components))
- <a id="owasp-top-10"></a>**OWASP Top 10**: OWASP's list of web-application risk categories; the guide uses the 2021 edition ([33 §1](modules/33-security.md#1-threat-model-of-an-angular-single-page-app-what-the-attacker-controls-what-runs-where-and-where-each-defense-lives))
- <a id="owner-of-a-subscription"></a>**owner (of a subscription)**: the object whose lifetime a subscription serves, and whose destruction should end it ([23 §6](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#6-unsubscription-strategies-in-angular-and-finding-leaks))
- <a id="oxc"></a>**OXC**: a set of JavaScript tools written in Rust; `@angular/build` uses it to run the linker by default ([12 §3](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#3-building-the-application-builder-output-layout-and-budgets)).

## P

- <a id="package-group"></a>**package group**: the packages released and updated together, listed in a package's `ng-update.packageGroup` field ([39 §7](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#7-updating-in-practice-ng-update-migration-schematics-and-the-update-guide); owner [12 §1](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#1-what-a-framework-does-and-angulars-philosophy))
- <a id="page-object"></a>**page object**: a class that wraps one page's locators and actions so tests never hold selectors ([30 §4](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#4-locators-page-objects-and-fixtures))
- <a id="page-view-id"></a>**page-view id**: an identifier for one document load, stamped on every event of that page view ([44 §9](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#9-frontend-error-tracking-and-real-user-monitoring-releases-source-maps-beacons-and-sampling))
- <a id="pagedmodel"></a>**PagedModel**: Spring Data's DTO for a `Page`, rendering `content` and a `page` metadata object, instead of the domain type `PageImpl` ([41 §6](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#6-pagination-sorting-and-filtering-offset-versus-cursor-and-how-spring-data-serializes-a-page))
- <a id="palette-material"></a>**palette (Material)**: the Sass map passed as a theme's color, made of tonal palettes, such as `mat.$azure-palette` ([36 §3](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#3-theming-with-material-3-mattheme-system-variables-and-light-and-dark-with-color-scheme))
- <a id="pane"></a>**pane**: the `div.cdk-overlay-pane` inside an overlay's host that carries the overlay's content ([36 §5](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#5-portals-and-overlays-where-floating-ui-renders-how-it-is-positioned-and-how-it-closes))
- <a id="parallelindex"></a>**parallelIndex**: a Playwright worker's slot from 0 to workers − 1, unique among workers running at the same time ([30 §8](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#8-ci-strategy-for-end-to-end-tests-workers-sharding-retries-policy-and-test-data))
- <a id="parameter-inheritance"></a>**parameter inheritance**: what a child route receives of its parents' params and data, set by `paramsInheritanceStrategy` (`'always'` since v22, `'emptyOnly'` before) ([24 §2](modules/24-routing.md#2-route-parameters-path-matrix-query-and-fragment-inheritance-and-component-input-binding))
- <a id="parammap"></a>**paramMap**: the route's params as a `ParamMap`, read with `get` ([24 §2](modules/24-routing.md#2-route-parameters-path-matrix-query-and-fragment-inheritance-and-component-input-binding))
- <a id="parent-id"></a>**parent ID**: the 16-hex-digit identifier of the calling span, sent as the third field of `traceparent` ([44 §10](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#10-under-the-hood-opentelemetry-for-the-web-traceparent-and-one-click-traced-to-a-spring-boot-span-and-log-line))
- <a id="parse-httpresource-option"></a>**parse (httpResource option)**: the function that turns the raw response into the resource's value; a throw puts the resource in `'error'` ([27 §10](modules/27-http-client.md#10-httpresource-signal-driven-requests-and-when-to-prefer-it-over-httpclient))
- <a id="parse--safeparse"></a>**parse / safeParse**: validate and return the data or throw a `ZodError`, versus return a `{ success, data | error }` result object ([07 §8](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#8-runtime-validation-with-zod-4-schema-first-types)).
- <a id="part"></a>**part**: one section of a multipart body, with its own headers such as `Content-Disposition` ([41 §8](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#8-files-multipart-and-chunked-uploads-progress-and-downloads-with-content-disposition-and-streaming))
- <a id="partial-application"></a>**partial application**: presetting some arguments of a function once, getting a function of the rest ([02 §6](modules/02-js-scope-closures-this.md#6-higher-order-functions-currying-partial-application-debounce-and-throttle)).
- <a id="partial-compilation"></a>**partial compilation**: the `compilationMode: "partial"` output for npm libraries, `ɵɵngDeclare*` declarations with a `minVersion` ([12 §8](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it)). · also in [39 §5](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#5-the-version-matrix-part-2-v9-to-v15-from-ivy-to-standalone)
- <a id="partialmatchroutesnapshot"></a>**PartialMatchRouteSnapshot**: the partial route snapshot (params, query params, URL and more) that `canMatch` and a redirect function receive ([24 §5](modules/24-routing.md#5-functional-guards-canmatch-canactivate-candeactivate-and-redirectcommand))
- <a id="pass-through"></a>**pass-through**: `$localize` without a translation map: it strips the metadata and returns the source text ([34 §8](modules/34-i18n.md#8-runtime-translation-with-angularlocalize-loadtranslations-before-bootstrap))
- <a id="passive-listener"></a>**passive listener**: a listener that promises not to call `preventDefault()`, so scrolling need not wait for it ([08 §4](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#4-events-propagation-default-actions-passive-listeners-and-delegation)).
- <a id="patch-flag"></a>**patch flag**: a number Vue's compiler attaches to a node to say which parts can change ([40 §2](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#2-templates-jsx-and-single-file-components-what-the-compiler-knows))
- <a id="patchstate"></a>**patchState**: the function that merges a partial state into a store or `signalState` ([28 §8](modules/28-state-management.md#8-componentstore-and-signalstore-signalstate-signalstore-withstate-withcomputed-withmethods-and-protected-state))
- <a id="path-filter"></a>**path filter**: a rule that runs a pipeline only when a changed file lies under a watched folder ([44 §7](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#7-cicd-for-both-layers-one-image-promoted-through-stages-a-monorepo-or-separate-repositories-and-versioning-the-contract))
- <a id="path-match-prefix-full"></a>**path match (prefix, full)**: `'prefix'` (default) matches when the route's path is a prefix of the remaining segments; `'full'` only when it consumes all of them ([24 §1](modules/24-routing.md#1-router-configuration-providerouter-route-matching-redirects-and-the-wildcard))
- <a id="path-parameter"></a>**path parameter**: a `:name` segment of a route path, captured from the matching URL segment ([24 §2](modules/24-routing.md#2-route-parameters-path-matrix-query-and-fragment-inheritance-and-component-input-binding))
- <a id="paths-mapping"></a>**paths mapping**: a `tsconfig` entry that maps an import name to a location, such as `ui-lib` to `./dist/ui-lib` ([38 §5](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#5-angular-cli-libraries-ng-generate-library-ng-packagr-and-consuming-the-build))
- <a id="pattern-angular-aria"></a>**pattern (Angular Aria)**: one APG widget that Angular Aria implements as a set of directives: listbox, tabs, menu and menubar, toolbar, accordion, tree, grid or combobox ([36 §11](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#11-angular-aria-headless-signal-driven-aria-patterns))
- <a id="payload"></a>**payload**: the attacker's input, such as markup or script text, that an attack tries to get a page to run ([33 §2](modules/33-security.md#2-xss-and-angulars-template-model-escaping-by-default-trusted-templates-and-the-paths-around-them))
- <a id="peer-dependency"></a>**peer dependency**: a package a library requires but the consumer installs, so one shared copy is used rather than a bundled one ([12 §1](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#1-what-a-framework-does-and-angulars-philosophy)). · also in [36 §1](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#1-material-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-styled-components-behavior-primitives-and-headless-patterns), [38 §5](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#5-angular-cli-libraries-ng-generate-library-ng-packagr-and-consuming-the-build), [39 §7](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#7-updating-in-practice-ng-update-migration-schematics-and-the-update-guide)
- <a id="peer-dependency-library-evaluation"></a>**peer dependency (library evaluation)**: a package a library expects the app to install, within its `peerDependencies` range; the first rule of the evaluation table ([37 §12](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#12-the-production-ecosystem-and-how-to-evaluate-a-library))
- <a id="pending"></a>**PENDING**: the control status while an async validator runs; neither `VALID` nor `INVALID` ([25 §6](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#6-async-validators-pending-status-cancellation-and-debouncing))
- <a id="pending-money"></a>**pending (money)**: what the screen shows for a transfer with no final answer: an immediate acknowledgement, never "sent" or a new balance ([46 §8](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#8-under-the-hood-a-money-transfer-with-idempotency-keys-optimistic-ui-that-never-claims-success-and-the-unknown-outcome))
- <a id="pending-1"></a>**pending()**: true while an async rule loads; `valid()` and `invalid()` are both false then ([26 §6](modules/26-signal-forms.md#6-async-validation-and-debounce-validateasync-validatehttp-pending-and-cancellation)).
- <a id="pendingtasks"></a>**PendingTasks**: Angular's counter of unfinished work that tests and the server wait for ([18 §5](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#5-defer-blocks-and-states-placeholder-loading-error-minimum-and-after)) · also in [21 §5](modules/21-change-detection.md#5-zoneless-change-detection-the-scheduler-its-triggers-and-pendingtasks)
- <a id="per-check-work"></a>**per-check work**: whatever a view's template expressions and hooks compute each time Angular checks that view ([31 §3](modules/31-performance.md#3-runtime-cost-of-a-change-onpush-zoneless-signals-and-per-check-template-work))
- <a id="per-host-connection-limit"></a>**per-host connection limit**: the number of connections a browser opens to one server, 6 or 8 under HTTP/1.1, which each open stream or held request uses one of (RFC 6202 §5.1) ([43 §1](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#1-choosing-a-transport-polling-long-polling-server-sent-events-websockets-and-web-push-when-no-tab-is-open))
- <a id="per-instance-state"></a>**per-instance state**: data owned by one component instance, given by listing its class in the component's `providers` ([16 §6](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#6-providers-versus-viewproviders-and-per-instance-state)).
- <a id="per-iteration-binding"></a>**per-iteration binding**: the fresh copy of a `for (let …)` loop variable that each iteration gets ([02 §3](modules/02-js-scope-closures-this.md#3-closures)).
- <a id="per-page-view-sampling"></a>**per-page-view sampling**: drawing once, when the page view starts, whether it reports, so a sampled page view sends every event and any other none ([44 §9](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#9-frontend-error-tracking-and-real-user-monitoring-releases-source-maps-beacons-and-sampling))
- <a id="percentage-rollout"></a>**percentage rollout**: giving a share of users one variant and the rest another ([44 §8](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#8-feature-flags-with-openfeature-and-canary-releases-providers-evaluation-context-sticky-rollout-kill-switches-and-version-skew))
- <a id="percentile-nearest-rank"></a>**percentile (nearest-rank)**: the value at rank ceil(p × n) of the sorted window; other tools interpolate and differ ([46 §6](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#6-a-real-time-operations-dashboard-aggregation-per-widget-staleness-hidden-tabs-and-screens-left-open-for-days))
- <a id="performance-budget"></a>**performance budget**: a size limit the build enforces so a regression fails before it ships ([31 §1](modules/31-performance.md#1-measure-first-the-loop-lab-versus-field-and-what-each-tool-answers)) · also in [45 §6](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#6-performance-and-accessibility-as-design-steps-budgets-a-loading-plan-long-lists-and-keyboard-and-pointer-paths)
- <a id="performance-entry"></a>**performance entry**: an object the browser records for one performance event, with a name, a type and a time ([31 §2](modules/31-performance.md#2-lab-metrics-in-a-real-browser-performance-observers-devtools-protocol-counters-and-lighthouse-in-ci))
- <a id="performance-loop"></a>**performance loop**: pick a user-visible symptom, measure it, change one thing, and measure again with the same instrument ([31 §1](modules/31-performance.md#1-measure-first-the-loop-lab-versus-field-and-what-each-tool-answers))
- <a id="performance-panel"></a>**Performance panel**: the Chrome DevTools panel that records what the main thread did during a run ([31 §1](modules/31-performance.md#1-measure-first-the-loop-lab-versus-field-and-what-each-tool-answers))
- <a id="performanceobserver"></a>**PerformanceObserver**: the browser API that subscribes to performance entries of one type ([31 §2](modules/31-performance.md#2-lab-metrics-in-a-real-browser-performance-observers-devtools-protocol-counters-and-lighthouse-in-ci))
- <a id="pessimistic-ui"></a>**pessimistic UI**: changing the screen only after the server confirms ([45 §8](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#8-under-the-hood-failure-modes-observability-offline-levels-and-consistency-choices))
- <a id="phase-of-a-render-hook"></a>**phase (of a render hook)**: one of `earlyRead`, `write`, `mixedReadWrite` and `read`; each runs across all components before the next starts ([20 §4](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#4-render-hooks-afternextrender-aftereveryrender-and-their-phases))
- <a id="physical-property"></a>**physical property**: a CSS property that names a side, such as `margin-left`, and does not follow the writing direction ([34 §11](modules/34-i18n.md#11-right-to-left-layouts-dir-logical-css-properties-and-bidirectional-text))
- <a id="pid-1"></a>**PID 1**: a container's first process; Linux ignores signals with the default action for it, so it stops only if it handles them ([44 §6](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#6-serving-ssr-apps-in-containers-the-node-server-as-pid-1-graceful-shutdown-health-probes-and-what-nginx-or-a-cdn-still-serves))
- <a id="pii"></a>**PII**: personally identifiable information, anything that points at one person, such as an e-mail address ([37 §11](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#11-logging-and-error-tracking-a-reporter-port-context-privacy-source-maps-and-sampling))
- <a id="pill"></a>**pill**: the "N new posts" button that shows the buffer ([46 §3](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#3-an-infinite-scroll-feed-cursor-pages-deduplication-new-items-without-a-jump-and-a-bounded-window))
- <a id="pipe"></a>**pipe**: a function the template calls with `value | name: arg`, with a cache of one result per place it is used ([14 §5](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#5-pipes-pure-and-impure-custom-pipes-injection-and-imports)).
- <a id="pipe-rxjs"></a>**pipe (RxJS)**: applies operators left to right; the method `source.pipe(...)` applies them to a source, the standalone `pipe(...)` composes them into one operator ([22 §5](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#5-pipeable-operators-and-writing-your-own))
- <a id="pipeable-operator"></a>**pipeable operator**: a function from an Observable to an Observable that leaves its input unchanged; subscribing to the result subscribes to the input ([22 §5](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#5-pipeable-operators-and-writing-your-own))
- <a id="pipeline-stage"></a>**pipeline stage**: one check in the CI run, such as lint or build ([38 §11](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#11-a-ci-pipeline-for-a-front-end-lint-test-build-end-to-end-and-deploy-previews))
- <a id="pixel-diff"></a>**pixel diff**: the count (or ratio) of pixels that differ between a screenshot and its baseline ([30 §6](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#6-visual-regression-and-accessibility-checks-in-end-to-end-tests))
- <a id="pkce"></a>**PKCE**: Proof Key for Code Exchange: binds an authorization code to the client that started the flow ([42 §2](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#2-oauth-20-and-openid-connect-for-a-single-page-app-authorization-code-with-pkce-and-why-the-implicit-flow-is-gone))
- <a id="placeholder"></a>**placeholder**: a named slot in a message for a part that is not text, such as an interpolation or an element; translators move it but do not change it ([34 §4](modules/34-i18n.md#4-marking-text-for-translation-i18n-attributes-metadata-ids-and-localize-in-code))
- <a id="placeholder-image"></a>**placeholder (image)**: a small image or data URL shown until the real image loads ([31 §7](modules/31-performance.md#7-images-ngoptimizedimage-with-priority-fill-loaders-and-placeholders))
- <a id="placeholder-loading-and-error-blocks"></a>**placeholder, loading and error blocks**: the optional `@placeholder`, `@loading` and `@error` sub-blocks of a `@defer`, one per state besides the content ([18 §5](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#5-defer-blocks-and-states-placeholder-loading-error-minimum-and-after))
- <a id="plain-date"></a>**plain date**: a calendar day with no time, such as a birthday; not an instant ([34 §10](modules/34-i18n.md#10-dates-times-numbers-and-money-time-zones-currency-codes-and-minor-units))
- <a id="platform"></a>**platform**: the page-wide Angular layer that `platformBrowser()` returns and on which applications are bootstrapped ([15 §3](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#3-bootstrapping-and-providers-without-modules-bootstrapapplication-provide-functions-and-importprovidersfrom); owned by [12 §6](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#6-bootstrapping-bootstrapapplication-the-platform-and-initializers)).
- <a id="platform-angular"></a>**platform (Angular)**: the page-wide layer and injector shared by every Angular app on the page ([12 §6](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#6-bootstrapping-bootstrapapplication-the-platform-and-initializers)).
- <a id="platform-accessibility-api"></a>**platform accessibility API**: the operating-system interface through which a browser hands the accessibility tree to assistive technology ([11 §2](modules/11-accessibility.md#2-semantic-html-landmarks-and-headings)).
- <a id="platform-id"></a>**platform ID**: the value of the `PLATFORM_ID` token, `'browser'` or `'server'`, that tells code which environment it runs in ([32 §7](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#7-platform-safe-code-browser-apis-afternextrender-document-request-and-response_init))
- <a id="platform-injector"></a>**platform injector**: the singleton injector above the root that the typings say is "shared by all applications on the page"; `providedIn: 'platform'` registers there ([16 §3](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#3-providedin-service-scopes-and-tree-shaking)).
- <a id="platform-specific-provider"></a>**platform-specific provider**: a provider that supplies a different class or value in the browser and on the server, by a factory on `PLATFORM_ID` or by a provider in `app.config.server.ts` ([32 §7](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#7-platform-safe-code-browser-apis-afternextrender-document-request-and-response_init))
- <a id="platformlocation"></a>**PlatformLocation**: the layer under the strategies that reaches the browser's History API ([24 §10](modules/24-routing.md#10-the-router-and-the-platform-history-api-navigation-api-and-location-strategies)) · also in [29 §4](modules/29-testing-angular.md#4-testbed-the-testing-module-inject-runininjectioncontext-overrides-and-teardown)
- <a id="play-state"></a>**play state**: whether a CSS animation is `running` or `paused`, set by `animation-play-state` ([35 §3](modules/35-animations.md#3-css-keyframe-animations-keyframes-fill-modes-iteration-and-component-scoped-names))
- <a id="playbackrate"></a>**playbackRate**: the speed multiplier of an `Animation`; 1 is normal, 2 twice as fast, 0 stopped ([35 §4](modules/35-animations.md#4-the-web-animations-api-elementanimate-the-animation-object-and-getanimations))
- <a id="playstate"></a>**playState**: an `Animation`'s state: `idle`, `running`, `paused` or `finished` ([35 §4](modules/35-animations.md#4-the-web-animations-api-elementanimate-the-animation-object-and-getanimations))
- <a id="playwright"></a>**Playwright**: a browser automation library and test runner whose test code runs in Node and drives browsers out of process ([30 §2](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#2-playwright-and-cypress-architecture-ng-e2e-and-the-end-of-protractor))
- <a id="plural-category"></a>**plural category**: the locale's label for a number (`one`, `few`, `other`…), which `i18nPlural` tries after an exact key such as `=0` ([14 §6](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#6-built-in-pipes-and-locale-data)). · also in [34 §2](modules/34-i18n.md#2-the-platforms-intl-apis-dates-numbers-plurals-lists-collation-and-display-names)
- <a id="plural-expression"></a>**plural expression**: an ICU expression that picks a branch by number: an exact match, then the locale's plural category, then `other` ([34 §5](modules/34-i18n.md#5-icu-expressions-plural-select-and-nesting))
- <a id="politeness-aria-live"></a>**politeness (aria-live)**: `off`, `polite` or `assertive`: whether and how urgently a live-region change is announced ([11 §5](modules/11-accessibility.md#5-focus-management-in-spas-and-live-regions)).
- <a id="polling"></a>**polling**: asking again for the same data on a schedule, here one request per tick or one request a fixed delay after each response ([23 §8](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#8-angular-patterns-typeahead-polling-cache-then-network-and-racing-requests)) · also in [43 §1](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#1-choosing-a-transport-polling-long-polling-server-sent-events-websockets-and-web-push-when-no-tab-is-open)
- <a id="polyfill"></a>**polyfill**: code that adds a missing standard API at run time; syntax cannot be polyfilled, only compiled ([05 §8](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#8-modern-features-by-edition-es2015-to-es2026)). · also in [34 §4](modules/34-i18n.md#4-marking-text-for-translation-i18n-attributes-metadata-ids-and-localize-in-code)
- <a id="polyfills-build"></a>**polyfills (build)**: the entries a build loads before the application, where zone.js was typically listed ([21 §3](modules/21-change-detection.md#3-zonejs-and-ngzone-how-changes-used-to-be-detected-and-why-zone-left-the-default))
- <a id="polyrepo"></a>**polyrepo**: a setup in which the front end and the back end live in separate repositories and share the contract by publishing it ([44 §7](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#7-cicd-for-both-layers-one-image-promoted-through-stages-a-monorepo-or-separate-repositories-and-versioning-the-contract))
- <a id="popover-manual"></a>**popover (manual)**: an element with `popover="manual"`, which only code shows and hides: it is not light-dismissed, and a shown one is in the top layer ([36 §5](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#5-portals-and-overlays-where-floating-ui-renders-how-it-is-positioned-and-how-it-closes))
- <a id="popstate"></a>**popstate**: the event fired when the active history entry changes by traversal or by fragment navigation (then followed by `hashchange`); `pushState` and `replaceState` do not fire it ([08 §7](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#7-history-api-versus-navigation-api)).
- <a id="port"></a>**port**: the interface or `InjectionToken` that names what a unit needs from a collaborator, so a double can implement it ([29 §7](modules/29-testing-angular.md#7-test-doubles-fakes-stubs-spies-and-mocks-vifn-provider-overrides-and-over-mocking)) · also in [38 §3](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#3-facades-and-the-data-access-layer)
- <a id="portable-domain-core"></a>**portable domain core**: framework-free application state and rules that each framework reads through an adapter ([40 §8](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#8-choosing-a-framework-balanced-trade-offs-and-how-to-answer-why-angular))
- <a id="portal"></a>**portal**: content a component, template or DOM node renders somewhere other than where it is written; the CDK has `ComponentPortal`, `TemplatePortal` and `DomPortal` ([36 §5](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#5-portals-and-overlays-where-floating-ui-renders-how-it-is-positioned-and-how-it-closes))
- <a id="portal-outlet"></a>**portal outlet**: the place a portal renders into, such as the `CdkPortalOutlet` directive or the pane of an overlay ([36 §5](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#5-portals-and-overlays-where-floating-ui-renders-how-it-is-positioned-and-how-it-closes))
- <a id="position-strategy"></a>**position strategy**: the object that places an overlay's pane: global (against the viewport) or flexible connected (against an origin element) ([36 §5](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#5-portals-and-overlays-where-floating-ui-renders-how-it-is-positioned-and-how-it-closes))
- <a id="positioned-element"></a>**positioned element**: an element whose `position` is anything but `static` ([10 §4](modules/10-css-essentials.md#4-positioning-stacking-contexts-and-z-index)).
- <a id="possiblein"></a>**possibleIn**: in the Update Guide's data, the first version in which a step's change can be made ([39 §12](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#12-under-the-hood-how-ng-update-selects-migrations-and-how-the-update-guide-builds-its-checklist))
- <a id="post-order"></a>**post-order**: a node visited after its children; the view hooks run this way, so a child's `ngAfterViewInit` precedes its parent's ([20 §1](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#1-the-lifecycle-map-phases-and-the-order-of-the-hooks))
- <a id="post-order-evaluation"></a>**post-order evaluation**: a module's body runs after the bodies of all its dependencies ([05 §1](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#1-es-modules-static-structure-linking-and-live-bindings)).
- <a id="postcss"></a>**PostCSS**: a tool that transforms CSS with plugins; Tailwind's Angular setup uses its plugin ([13 §8](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#8-styling-strategies-component-styles-global-styles-css-variables-and-tailwind)).
- <a id="pour"></a>**POUR**: WCAG's four principles, perceivable, operable, understandable, robust ([11 §1](modules/11-accessibility.md#1-why-accessibility-and-what-the-standards-require)).
- <a id="practice-of-a-team"></a>**practice (of a team)**: how a team routinely updates, tests and ships its front end ([47 §6](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#6-questions-to-ask-about-the-front-end-practice-versions-and-updates-testing-the-design-system-and-accessibility-performance-contracts-and-releases-and-how-to-read-the-answers))
- <a id="pre-commit-hook"></a>**pre-commit hook**: a script Git runs before it records a commit ([38 §9](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#9-linting-and-formatting-angular-eslint-prettier-import-ordering-and-commit-hooks))
- <a id="pre-order"></a>**pre-order**: a node visited before its children; `ngOnChanges`, `ngOnInit` and `ngDoCheck` run this way ([20 §1](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#1-the-lifecycle-map-phases-and-the-order-of-the-hooks))
- <a id="prebuilt-theme"></a>**prebuilt theme**: one of the eight compiled CSS theme files in `@angular/material/prebuilt-themes`, each fixed to light or dark ([36 §3](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#3-theming-with-material-3-mattheme-system-variables-and-light-and-dark-with-color-scheme))
- <a id="prebundling"></a>**prebundling**: Vite processing third-party dependencies once at the first `ng serve` ([12 §5](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#5-the-dev-loop-ng-serve-hot-module-replacement-and-angular-devtools)).
- <a id="precache"></a>**precache**: storing resources in a cache during the worker's `install` event, before they are requested ([09 §9](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#9-service-workers-and-the-pwa-manifest)).
- <a id="precompressed-file"></a>**precompressed file**: a compressed copy, such as `main.js.gz`, written before deployment and sent instead of the original ([44 §3](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#3-nginx-for-a-single-page-app-location-matching-the-try_files-fallback-caching-headers-for-hashed-assets-and-gzip-or-brotli))
- <a id="precondition"></a>**precondition**: a request header condition the server tests before performing the method (RFC 9110 §13) ([45 §8](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#8-under-the-hood-failure-modes-observability-offline-levels-and-consistency-choices))
- <a id="preconnect"></a>**preconnect**: a `<link rel="preconnect">` that opens the connection to another origin early ([31 §7](modules/31-performance.md#7-images-ngoptimizedimage-with-priority-fill-loaders-and-placeholders))
- <a id="predicate-location"></a>**predicate location**: a `location $variable` block (mainline 1.31.5) that matches when the variable is non-empty and not "0", checked after regex locations ([44 §3](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#3-nginx-for-a-single-page-app-location-matching-the-try_files-fallback-caching-headers-for-hashed-assets-and-gzip-or-brotli))
- <a id="prefers-reduced-motion"></a>**prefers-reduced-motion**: the media query that reports whether the user asked the device for less motion ([35 §11](modules/35-animations.md#11-performance-and-reduced-motion-compositor-properties-measuring-jank-and-prefers-reduced-motion))
- <a id="prefetch"></a>**prefetch**: a second trigger, `prefetch on …` or `prefetch when …`, that fetches a `@defer` block's code without rendering it ([18 §6](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#6-triggers-and-prefetching))
- <a id="prefetching-defer"></a>**prefetching (@defer)**: fetching a `@defer` block's chunk on a trigger that fires before the one that renders it ([31 §5](modules/31-performance.md#5-loading-the-initial-bundle-lazy-routes-defer-and-preloading-as-one-plan))
- <a id="prefix-selector-prefix"></a>**prefix (selector prefix)**: a short project-wide start for selectors, such as the CLI default `app-`; `ng` is reserved for Angular ([13 §1](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#1-component-anatomy-selector-template-styles-imports-and-the-v22-file-convention)).
- <a id="prefix-cache"></a>**prefix cache**: a cache keyed by the normalized query whose complete entries can answer longer queries ([46 §2](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#2-autocomplete-and-typeahead-search-a-request-policy-a-prefix-cache-ranking-on-the-server-and-an-accessible-combobox))
- <a id="prefix-location"></a>**prefix location**: a `location` given as a plain string, matching URIs that start with it; the longest match is remembered ([44 §3](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#3-nginx-for-a-single-page-app-location-matching-the-try_files-fallback-caching-headers-for-hashed-assets-and-gzip-or-brotli))
- <a id="preflight"></a>**preflight**: the `OPTIONS` request a browser sends before an unusual cross-origin request, asking whether the real one is allowed ([09 §5](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#5-the-same-origin-policy-and-cors)).
- <a id="preload-scanner"></a>**preload scanner**: a secondary HTML parser that finds and fetches resources while the main parser is blocked ([08 §1](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#1-the-critical-rendering-path-from-bytes-to-pixels)).
- <a id="preloadallmodules"></a>**PreloadAllModules**: the built-in preloading strategy that loads every lazy route after a `NavigationEnd`, started by the router's bootstrap listener ([24 §8](modules/24-routing.md#8-preloading-strategies-and-route-reuse))
- <a id="preloading"></a>**preloading**: loading a lazy route's configuration or chunk in the background before the user navigates to it ([24 §8](modules/24-routing.md#8-preloading-strategies-and-route-reuse))
- <a id="preloading-routes"></a>**preloading (routes)**: fetching lazy route chunks early, on purpose, after the first navigation ([31 §5](modules/31-performance.md#5-loading-the-initial-bundle-lazy-routes-defer-and-preloading-as-one-plan))
- <a id="preloading-strategy"></a>**preloading strategy**: a class with `preload(route, load)`, called once per lazy route, that returns `load()` to load now or `of(null)` to skip; `NoPreloading` is the default and `PreloadAllModules` loads every lazy route ([24 §8](modules/24-routing.md#8-preloading-strategies-and-route-reuse))
- <a id="preprocessor"></a>**preprocessor**: a tool such as Sass that compiles an extended stylesheet language into plain CSS at build time ([10 §7](modules/10-css-essentials.md#7-native-nesting-versus-sass)).
- <a id="prerelease-version"></a>**prerelease (version)**: a build before a release, such as `22.0.0-next.1`, ordered below `22.0.0` ([39 §12](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#12-under-the-hood-how-ng-update-selects-migrations-and-how-the-update-guide-builds-its-checklist))
- <a id="prerender-fallback"></a>**prerender fallback**: what a parameterized `Prerender` route does for a value it did not build: `Server` (default), `Client` or `None` ([32 §3](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#3-server-routes-and-rendermode-per-route-rendering-getprerenderparams-fallbacks-headers-and-status))
- <a id="prerendering"></a>**prerendering**: building HTML at build time ([45 §7](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#7-rendering-strategy-and-caching-layers-a-rendering-choice-per-route-and-every-cache-from-the-cdn-to-the-component))
- <a id="presence"></a>**presence**: which users are connected on which instance; each instance knows only its own until the user registry broadcast shares it ([Q43.19](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#q43-19)) · also in [46 §7](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#7-a-notifications-center-and-an-editing-indicator-delivery-unread-counts-across-tabs-and-presence-with-a-time-to-live)
- <a id="presentational-component"></a>**presentational component**: a component that gets data through inputs, sends events through outputs and injects no data services ([38 §2](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#2-smart-container-and-presentational-components)) · also in [45 §4](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#4-component-architecture-data-model-and-data-flow-from-screens-to-a-component-tree-and-a-client-side-model)
- <a id="presentational-role"></a>**presentational role**: `presentation` or `none`, which removes an element's own semantics while keeping its content exposed ([11 §3](modules/11-accessibility.md#3-aria-roles-states-properties-and-its-rules)).
- <a id="primary-outlet"></a>**primary outlet**: the unnamed outlet, which shows the routes without an `outlet` property ([24 §3](modules/24-routing.md#3-nested-and-named-outlets))
- <a id="primitive"></a>**primitive**: an immutable value with no properties of its own (string, number, bigint, boolean, undefined, symbol, null); compared by value ([01 §1](modules/01-js-values-types-coercion.md#1-values-and-types)).
- <a id="pristinedirty-and-toucheduntouched"></a>**pristine/dirty and touched/untouched**: two flags: dirty means the user changed the value in the view, touched means the field was blurred; a programmatic `setValue` sets neither ([25 §7](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#7-status-updateon-valuechanges-and-the-unified-events-stream))
- <a id="private-api-ɵ-ɵɵ"></a>**private API (ɵ, ɵɵ)**: Angular symbols prefixed `ɵ`, `ɵɵ` or `_`, which may change in any release ([12 §8](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it)).
- <a id="private-element"></a>**private element**: a `#name` field or method, stored outside the property table and reachable only from inside the class body ([03 §3](modules/03-js-objects-prototypes-classes.md#3-classes-the-sugar-and-what-is-not-sugar)).
- <a id="problem-detail"></a>**problem detail**: RFC 9457's JSON shape for an HTTP error, sent as `application/problem+json`, with the members `type`, `title`, `status`, `detail` and `instance` ([41 §5](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#5-error-contracts-with-problemdetail-rfc-9457-and-mapping-them-in-angular))
- <a id="problem-type-uri"></a>**problem type URI**: the `type` member of a problem detail, the identifier clients key on; when absent it is `about:blank`, meaning nothing beyond the HTTP status ([41 §5](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#5-error-contracts-with-problemdetail-rfc-9457-and-mapping-them-in-angular))
- <a id="processnexttick"></a>**process.nextTick**: Node's queue that runs before promise microtasks, as soon as the current operation completes ([04 §2](modules/04-js-async-event-loop.md#2-nodes-event-loop-and-why-it-matters-for-ssr)).
- <a id="processor"></a>**processor**: a linter hook that hands code embedded in a file, such as an inline template, to other rules ([38 §9](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#9-linting-and-formatting-angular-eslint-prettier-import-ordering-and-commit-hooks))
- <a id="producer--consumer"></a>**producer / consumer**: a node that is read (a signal, a computed) versus a node that reads (a computed, an effect, a template view) ([17 §1](modules/17-signals.md#1-why-signals-exist), [§9](modules/17-signals.md#9-under-the-hood-the-reactive-graph)).
- <a id="producer--consumer-rxjs"></a>**producer / consumer (RxJS)**: the producer is the function that creates an Observable's values, the consumer is the code that receives them ([22 §1](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#1-push-collections-observables-promises-iterators-and-laziness))
- <a id="production-build"></a>**production build**: the optimized, hash-named files `ng build` writes with the production configuration, the artifact that ships ([30 §1](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#1-what-belongs-at-the-end-to-end-level-journeys-cost-and-a-strategy-across-levels))
- <a id="profiler"></a>**profiler**: a tool that records when the framework's change detection events start and end, such as the Angular DevTools Profiler tab or the Chrome performance track ([21 §8](modules/21-change-detection.md#8-debugging-the-profiler-providechecknochangesconfig-and-stability))
- <a id="progress-resource-signal"></a>**progress (resource signal)**: the signal of the latest download progress event, set when the request asks for progress ([27 §10](modules/27-http-client.md#10-httpresource-signal-driven-requests-and-when-to-prefer-it-over-httpclient))
- <a id="progress-event"></a>**progress event**: a request event that reports how many bytes of the request or response body have moved; `observe: 'events'` exposes it ([27 §9](modules/27-http-client.md#9-progress-events-reportuploadprogress-reportdownloadprogress-and-why-upload-progress-needs-xhr))
- <a id="progressive-framework"></a>**progressive framework**: a framework that can be dropped into a page as a library and grows by adding official pieces ([40 §1](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#1-library-framework-progressive-framework-what-each-project-decides-for-you))
- <a id="project-angular-workspace"></a>**project (Angular workspace)**: one application or library in a workspace ([12 §2](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#2-the-cli-and-the-workspace-angularjson-projects-schematics-and-ng-update)).
- <a id="project-alias"></a>**project alias**: the import name a project must be imported through, so a relative path into it counts as a violation ([Exercise 38.3](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#ex38-3))
- <a id="project-graph"></a>**project graph**: the map of projects and the imports between them that Nx builds from code and configuration ([38 §7](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#7-nx-monorepos-the-project-graph-tags-nxenforce-module-boundaries-affected-commands-and-caching))
- <a id="project-story"></a>**project story**: our term for a design or migration you took part in, told in STAR with your part as the Action ([47 §7](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#7-under-the-hood-how-one-story-is-drilled-into-numbers-you-can-defend-and-telling-a-design-or-a-migration-as-your-own-project))
- <a id="projectablenodes"></a>**projectableNodes**: the creation option that gives one array of DOM nodes per slot of a dynamic component ([19 §7](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#7-dynamic-components-viewcontainerrefcreatecomponent-createcomponent-and-bindings))
- <a id="promise"></a>**promise**: an object representing a future value, with a state (pending, fulfilled or rejected) that settles once ([04 §3](modules/04-js-async-event-loop.md#3-promises-from-callbacks-to-a-state-machine)).
- <a id="promise-combinator"></a>**promise combinator**: `Promise.all`, `allSettled`, `race` or `any`, which combine several promises into one ([04 §4](modules/04-js-async-event-loop.md#4-combinators-and-helpers)).
- <a id="promisewithresolvers"></a>**Promise.withResolvers**: returns a promise together with its `resolve` and `reject` functions ([04 §4](modules/04-js-async-event-loop.md#4-combinators-and-helpers)).
- <a id="promotion"></a>**promotion**: deploying the image digest that passed one stage to the next stage, without rebuilding it ([44 §7](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#7-cicd-for-both-layers-one-image-promoted-through-stages-a-monorepo-or-separate-repositories-and-versioning-the-contract))
- <a id="prompt"></a>**prompt**: the question an interviewer asks that a story answers ([47 §1](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#1-the-behavioral-round-what-a-story-is-evidence-of-star-with-the-weight-on-action-and-an-explicit-result-and-a-story-bank))
- <a id="propagation"></a>**propagation**: sending the trace context to the next hop in a header so its spans join the same trace ([44 §10](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#10-under-the-hood-opentelemetry-for-the-web-traceparent-and-one-click-traced-to-a-spring-boot-span-and-log-line))
- <a id="property-descriptor"></a>**property descriptor**: the record behind a property: a value or getter/setter plus the `writable`, `enumerable` and `configurable` flags ([03 §1](modules/03-js-objects-prototypes-classes.md#1-properties-and-descriptors)).
- <a id="props-and-emptyprops"></a>**props** and **emptyProps**: NgRx helpers that declare an action's payload type, or no payload ([28 §4](modules/28-state-management.md#4-ngrx-store-actions-reducers-createactiongroup-createfeature-and-runtime-checks))
- <a id="protected-state-and-unprotected"></a>**protected state** and **unprotected**: the type-level rule that only a store's own methods may call `patchState`, switched off by `protectedState: false`, and the `@ngrx/signals/testing` helper that lets a test write ([28 §8](modules/28-state-management.md#8-componentstore-and-signalstore-signalstate-signalstore-withstate-withcomputed-withmethods-and-protected-state))
- <a id="protocol-relative-url"></a>**protocol-relative URL**: a URL starting `//host/path`, which keeps the page's scheme and names another host ([33 §7](modules/33-security.md#7-server-rendered-pages-escaping-in-the-server-html-the-state-script-and-ssrf))
- <a id="prototype"></a>**prototype**: the object another object delegates failed property lookups to ([03 §2](modules/03-js-objects-prototypes-classes.md#2-the-prototype-chain)).
- <a id="prototype-chain"></a>**prototype chain**: the sequence of prototypes a property read walks until it finds the key or reaches `null` ([03 §2](modules/03-js-objects-prototypes-classes.md#2-the-prototype-chain)).
- <a id="prototype-pollution"></a>**prototype pollution**: writing to `Object.prototype` through a `__proto__` key in untrusted input, which affects every object ([03, Q03.07](modules/03-js-objects-prototypes-classes.md#q03-07)).
- <a id="protractor"></a>**Protractor**: the Angular team's former end-to-end framework, built on WebDriverJS; its CLI builder was removed in v19.0 ([30 §2](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#2-playwright-and-cypress-architecture-ng-e2e-and-the-end-of-protractor))
- <a id="provenance"></a>**provenance**: a signed statement of where and how a package was built, which `npm audit signatures` verifies ([33 §11](modules/33-security.md#11-dependency-and-supply-chain-security-lockfiles-npm-audit-install-scripts-and-subresource-integrity))
- <a id="provide-function"></a>**provide* function**: a function such as `provideRouter` that returns the `EnvironmentProviders` one feature needs, replacing a provider-carrying NgModule ([15 §3](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#3-bootstrapping-and-providers-without-modules-bootstrapapplication-provide-functions-and-importprovidersfrom)). · also in [16 §9](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#9-di-patterns-testing-and-the-spring-container)
- <a id="provideinject-vue"></a>**provide/inject (Vue)**: functions that store a value under a key in a component and read it from the nearest ancestor that stored one ([40 §5](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#5-dependency-injection-and-shared-state-injectors-context-and-provideinject))
- <a id="providedin"></a>**providedIn**: the `@Injectable` option that registers the class's recipe on the class itself, in the root or platform injector, instead of in a `providers` array ([16 §3](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#3-providedin-service-scopes-and-tree-shaking)).
- <a id="provideenvironmentinitializer"></a>**provideEnvironmentInitializer**: the v19.0 function that runs a function once, in an injection context, when an environment injector is created; it replaces the deprecated `ENVIRONMENT_INITIALIZER` token ([16 §7](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#7-injectiontoken-multi-providers-environment-providers-and-initializers)).
- <a id="providehttpclient"></a>**provideHttpClient**: the function that registers `HttpClient`, its interceptor handler, a backend and the XSRF interceptor, then the providers of the features passed to it ([27 §2](modules/27-http-client.md#2-setting-up-httpclient-providehttpclient-its-features-and-the-fetch-backend-default))
- <a id="providehttpclienttesting"></a>**provideHttpClientTesting**: the provider function that swaps `HttpBackend` for the in-memory test backend and provides `HttpTestingController`; it goes after `provideHttpClient` ([27 §11](modules/27-http-client.md#11-testing-and-the-backend-seam-providehttpclienttesting-httptestingcontroller-and-what-they-replace))
- <a id="provider"></a>**provider**: the recipe that tells an injector how to produce the value for a token ([16 §1](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#1-why-dependency-injection-containers-constructor-injection-and-inject)).
- <a id="provider-openfeature"></a>**provider (OpenFeature)**: the vendor's or your own code that resolves a flag value from the evaluation context ([44 §8](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#8-feature-flags-with-openfeature-and-canary-releases-providers-evaluation-context-sticky-rollout-kill-switches-and-version-skew))
- <a id="provider-react"></a>**provider (React)**: the element `<SomeContext value={…}>` that hands one value to the components below it that read the context; it builds nothing ([40 §5](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#5-dependency-injection-and-shared-state-injectors-context-and-provideinject))
- <a id="provider-delegate"></a>**provider delegate**: an internal hook (`delegate`) through which `run` redirects the timer providers to the test scheduler ([23 §10](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#10-testing-with-testscheduler-marble-syntax-and-virtual-time-under-the-hood))
- <a id="provider-factory"></a>**provider factory**: the function behind `useFactory`, a `@Service` `factory` or an `InjectionToken` `factory`; it runs in an injection context ([16 §4](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#4-the-injection-context-inject-runininjectioncontext-and-injectasync)).
- <a id="provider-scope"></a>**provider scope**: the injector where a class is registered; a class is one instance per injector, not per application ([19 §2](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#2-sharing-state-beyond-the-tree-services-with-signals-and-di-scoped-state))
- <a id="provider-verification"></a>**provider verification**: the backend replaying a consumer's recorded contract interactions in its own build (owner of consumer-driven contracts: [30 §5](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#5-api-mocking-at-the-network-layer-and-where-contract-tests-take-over); [41 §3](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#3-openapi-with-springdoc-and-generated-typescript-clients-openapi-generator-orval-and-ng-openapi-gen))
- <a id="providers"></a>**providers**: the component metadata array of providers visible to its view and its projected content ([16 §6](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#6-providers-versus-viewproviders-and-per-instance-state)).
- <a id="providesignalformsconfig"></a>**provideSignalFormsConfig**: the provider function that sets app-wide Signal Forms options, such as the `classes` map ([26 §3](modules/26-signal-forms.md#3-binding-with-formfield-native-inputs-attributes-classes-and-rendering)).
- <a id="providestore-and-providestate"></a>**provideStore** and **provideState**: standalone provider functions that register the root store and a feature's reducer ([28 §4](modules/28-state-management.md#4-ngrx-store-actions-reducers-createactiongroup-createfeature-and-runtime-checks))
- <a id="proxy"></a>**Proxy**: an object that intercepts fundamental operations (get, set, has, delete…) on a target through handler traps ([03 §5](modules/03-js-objects-prototypes-classes.md#5-proxy-and-reflect)).
- <a id="proxy-buffering"></a>**proxy buffering**: Nginx collecting a proxied response in buffers before sending it to the client; `proxy_buffering off` sends data as it arrives ([44 §4](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#4-nginx-as-the-reverse-proxy-proxy_pass-forwarded-headers-into-spring-boot-and-sse-or-websocket-connections-through-the-proxy))
- <a id="proxy-headers"></a>**proxy headers**: `Forwarded` and `X-Forwarded-*`, which a load balancer adds to pass on the client's original host and scheme; ignored unless `trustProxyHeaders` keeps them ([32 §9](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#9-deploying-ssr-node-and-other-runtimes-static-output-caching-rendered-pages-and-allowed-hosts))
- <a id="proxy_read_timeout"></a>**proxy_read_timeout**: the Nginx timeout (60 seconds by default) between two successive reads from the proxied server; a longer silence closes the connection ([44 §4](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#4-nginx-as-the-reverse-proxy-proxy_pass-forwarded-headers-into-spring-boot-and-sse-or-websocket-connections-through-the-proxy))
- <a id="proxyzone"></a>**ProxyZone**: the zone.js zone that records pending work for a test; `waitForAsync` needs it and the Vitest patch provides it ([29 §10](modules/29-testing-angular.md#10-async-tests-vitest-fake-timers-versus-fakeasync-and-waitforasync))
- <a id="pseudo-class--pseudo-element"></a>**pseudo-class / pseudo-element**: a selector for state not in the document tree (`:hover`) / for an element not directly in it (`::before`) ([10 §2](modules/10-css-essentials.md#2-the-cascade-origins-specificity-and-layers)).
- <a id="pseudo-element-tree"></a>**pseudo-element tree**: the generated elements `::view-transition`, `::view-transition-group()`, `::view-transition-image-pair()`, `::view-transition-old()` and `::view-transition-new()` that exist only during a view transition ([35 §10](modules/35-animations.md#10-view-transitions-with-the-router-withviewtransitions-view-transition-name-and-onviewtransitioncreated))
