- <a id="public-api-surface"></a>**public API surface**: everything a consumer can import and rely on; for a library, what its entry files export ([38 §6](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#6-public-api-surfaces-public-apits-exports-secondary-entry-points-and-versioning))
- <a id="public-api-surface-library"></a>**public API surface (library)**: the inputs, outputs, tokens and exported functions a library's callers depend on ([36 §12](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#12-a-design-system-on-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-or-a-wrapper-around-material))
- <a id="public-client"></a>**public client**: a client that cannot keep a secret, such as a browser app ([42 §2](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#2-oauth-20-and-openid-connect-for-a-single-page-app-authorization-code-with-pkce-and-why-the-implicit-flow-is-gone))
- <a id="pure-function"></a>**pure function**: a function with no side effects whose result depends only on its inputs ([28 §4](modules/28-state-management.md#4-ngrx-store-actions-reducers-createactiongroup-createfeature-and-runtime-checks))
- <a id="pure-pipe"></a>**pure pipe**: a pipe that calls `transform` again only when an argument differs by `Object.is` from the last call; the default ([14 §5](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#5-pipes-pure-and-impure-custom-pipes-injection-and-imports)).
- <a id="push--pull"></a>**push / pull**: push: the producer decides when a value arrives; pull: the consumer asks for it ([22 §1](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#1-push-collections-observables-promises-iterators-and-laziness))
- <a id="push-service"></a>**push service**: the browser vendor's server that delivers push messages to a browser ([37 §7](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#7-push-notifications-and-installability-swpush-notification-clicks-and-the-manifest)) · also in [43 §1](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#1-choosing-a-transport-polling-long-polling-server-sent-events-websockets-and-web-push-when-no-tab-is-open)
- <a id="push-dirty-pull-value"></a>**push-dirty, pull-value**: a write pushes only a dirty flag through the graph; values are recomputed when read, and only where a producer's version changed ([17 §9](modules/17-signals.md#9-under-the-hood-the-reactive-graph)).

## Q

- <a id="quarantine"></a>**quarantine**: moving a flaky test to a separate suite so healthy tests keep giving clear feedback, until it is fixed ([30 §7](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#7-flaky-tests-root-causes-diagnosis-and-fixes))
- <a id="query"></a>**query**: a lookup Angular keeps up to date for the elements, components or directives a component declares or projects ([19 §5](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#5-queries-view-and-content-queries-with-signal-queries))
- <a id="query-animation"></a>**query (animation)**: a function that finds descendants of the trigger's element, such as `':enter'`, to animate inside one transition ([35 §8](modules/35-animations.md#8-legacy-angularanimations-triggers-states-transitions-query-and-stagger))
- <a id="query--command-flattening-choice"></a>**query / command (flattening choice)**: a query is a read whose old answer is worthless once a new one is asked (`switchMap`); a command is a write that must not run twice at once (`exhaustMap`) ([23 §2](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#2-choosing-a-flattening-operator-races-lost-writes-cancellation-and-recursion))
- <a id="query-key"></a>**query key**: the array that names a TanStack Query cache entry ([28 §11](modules/28-state-management.md#11-alternatives-and-the-decision-framework-ngxs-elf-tanstack-query-and-when-a-store-library-is-justified))
- <a id="query-parameter"></a>**query parameter**: a `key=value` pair after `?`, shared by the whole URL ([24 §2](modules/24-routing.md#2-route-parameters-path-matrix-query-and-fragment-inheritance-and-component-input-binding))
- <a id="querylist"></a>**QueryList**: the live collection a plural decorator query gives, with `changes`, `first`, `last`, `length`, `get` and `toArray` ([19 §6](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#6-decorator-based-inputs-outputs-and-queries-and-the-migration-schematics))
- <a id="queue-scheduler"></a>**queue scheduler**: the scheduler `State` observes actions on, which runs a task at once unless another is running and then queues it ([28 §12](modules/28-state-management.md#12-under-the-hood-how-ngrx-store-reduces-actions-and-how-signalstore-keeps-state-in-signals))
- <a id="quic"></a>**QUIC**: the UDP-based transport under HTTP/3, with streams, loss recovery per stream and an integrated TLS 1.3 handshake ([09 §3](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#3-http11-http2-and-http3)).
- <a id="quirk"></a>**quirk**: behavior that looks wrong but that something may depend on ([47 §3](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#3-legacy-codebases-and-migrations-without-blame-the-constraints-of-the-time-how-you-learned-the-code-before-changing-it-and-words-that-explain-instead-of-accuse))
- <a id="quota"></a>**quota**: the cap on the data an origin may store in IndexedDB, Cache Storage and similar; exceeding it throws `QuotaExceededError` ([09 §7](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#7-browser-storage-what-each-is-safe-for)).

## R

- <a id="race-condition"></a>**race condition**: a result that depends on which async job finishes first ([23 §2](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#2-choosing-a-flattening-operator-races-lost-writes-cancellation-and-recursion))
- <a id="radiogroup"></a>**radiogroup**: the ARIA role for a set of mutually exclusive `role="radio"` elements, one checked at a time, which an ARIA rating can use in place of native radio inputs ([Exercise 36.4](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#ex36-4))
- <a id="re-entrancy"></a>**re-entrancy**: a `next` called from inside a subscriber while a delivery is running; it finishes before the outer delivery goes on ([22 §4](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#4-subjects-subject-behaviorsubject-replaysubject-and-asyncsubject))
- <a id="re-export"></a>**re-export**: listing in an NgModule's `exports` a module it imports, so importers also get that module's exports ([15 §5](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#5-legacy-ngmodule-patterns-declarations-exports-forrootforchild-sharedmodule-and-coremodule)).
- <a id="re-render"></a>**re-render**: React calling a component function again, and by default every component it returns ([40 §3](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#3-reacts-model-re-rendering-reconciliation-memoization-and-the-react-compiler))
- <a id="re-subscription"></a>**re-subscription**: subscribing to the same source again, as if for the first time; a cold source runs again, so a retried HTTP call is a new request ([23 §4](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#4-retrying-and-repeating-retry-with-backoff-and-repeat))
- <a id="react-compiler"></a>**React Compiler**: the Babel plugin `babel-plugin-react-compiler` that inserts the equivalent of manual memoization at build time ([40 §3](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#3-reacts-model-re-rendering-reconciliation-memoization-and-the-react-compiler))
- <a id="reactive-context"></a>**reactive context**: code Angular runs while tracking dependencies (a `computed`, an `effect`, a template), where signal reads are recorded ([17 §1](modules/17-signals.md#1-why-signals-exist)).
- <a id="reactive-form"></a>**reactive form**: a form whose model is created in the component class and bound with `ReactiveFormsModule` directives such as `[formControl]` and `formControlName` ([25 §1](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#1-two-forms-apis-template-driven-and-reactive-and-the-control-directive-and-accessor-triangle))
- <a id="reactive-proxy-vue"></a>**reactive Proxy (Vue)**: Vue's deep wrapper that tracks property access (owner [03 §5](modules/03-js-objects-prototypes-classes.md#5-proxy-and-reflect); [40 §4](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#4-signals-beyond-angular-vue-refs-solid-svelte-runes-preact-and-qwik))
- <a id="read-query-option"></a>**read (query option)**: the option that picks which value to take from the matched element, such as `ElementRef`, `TemplateRef` or `Injector` ([19 §5](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#5-queries-view-and-content-queries-with-signal-queries))
- <a id="read-receipt"></a>**read receipt**: a message saying a notification was read, which may arrive before the notification ([46 §7](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#7-a-notifications-center-and-an-editing-indicator-delivery-unread-counts-across-tabs-and-presence-with-a-time-to-live))
- <a id="read-your-writes"></a>**read-your-writes**: the tab that wrote shows its own change at once ([45 §8](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#8-under-the-hood-failure-modes-observability-offline-levels-and-consistency-choices))
- <a id="readiness-probe"></a>**readiness probe**: a check whose failure takes the instance out of the load balancer without restarting it ([44 §6](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#6-serving-ssr-apps-in-containers-the-node-server-as-pid-1-graceful-shutdown-health-probes-and-what-nginx-or-a-cdn-still-serves))
- <a id="readme"></a>**README**: our suggested file for the reviewer: how to run and test, what is implemented, decisions and trade-offs, what is next, time spent ([47 §5](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#5-take-home-assignments-scope-and-time-box-what-the-repository-must-show-the-readme-and-the-review-call))
- <a id="readystate"></a>**readyState**: an `EventSource`'s state, `CONNECTING`, `OPEN` or `CLOSED`; after `error` it tells a retry (`CONNECTING`) from a failure (`CLOSED`) ([43 §2](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#2-server-sent-events-on-the-wire-the-textevent-stream-format-and-how-eventsource-reconnects))
- <a id="real-user-monitoring-rum"></a>**real user monitoring (RUM)**: a script in the page that sends each visit's metrics to your own server ([31 §1](modules/31-performance.md#1-measure-first-the-loop-lab-versus-field-and-what-each-tool-answers))
- <a id="real-user-monitoring"></a>**real-user monitoring**: field data collected from real visitors' browsers ([44 §9](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#9-frontend-error-tracking-and-real-user-monitoring-releases-source-maps-beacons-and-sampling), [47 §6](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#6-questions-to-ask-about-the-front-end-practice-versions-and-updates-testing-the-design-system-and-accessibility-performance-contracts-and-releases-and-how-to-read-the-answers))
- <a id="realm"></a>**realm**: a separate global environment with its own built-ins (an iframe, a worker, a `vm` context); objects from two realms fail `instanceof` against each other's constructors ([01 §1](modules/01-js-values-types-coercion.md#1-values-and-types)).
- <a id="recognition"></a>**recognition**: the step where the router matches the URL against the route table, evaluating `canMatch` guards and loading `loadChildren` routes ([24 §4](modules/24-routing.md#4-lazy-loading-in-depth-loaders-canmatch-and-route-injectors))
- <a id="recommended-migration"></a>**recommended migration**: an optional migration flagged `recommended`, pre-selected in `ng update`'s prompt ([39 §12](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#12-under-the-hood-how-ng-update-selects-migrations-and-how-the-update-guide-builds-its-checklist))
- <a id="reconcile"></a>**reconcile**: the runtime function that compares a `@for` collection with the live views by key and keeps, moves, creates or destroys views ([18 §9](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#9-under-the-hood-what-the-blocks-compile-to-how-for-reconciles-and-how-defer-moves-between-states))
- <a id="reconciliation"></a>**reconciliation**: pairing the new element tree with the old, keeping a child with the same type and key and replacing any other ([40 §3](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#3-reacts-model-re-rendering-reconciliation-memoization-and-the-react-compiler))
- <a id="reconciling"></a>**RECONCILING**: in `@openfeature/web-sdk` 1.10.0, the provider state while flags for a new evaluation context are still loading, entered when the provider's `onContextChange` returns a promise ([Exercise 44.3](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#ex44-3))
- <a id="reconnect-storm"></a>**reconnect storm**: many clients reconnecting at the same moment, for example after a deploy closes every socket on an instance ([43 §9](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#9-scaling-out-and-under-the-hood-sticky-sessions-the-broker-relay-backpressure-and-one-message-traced-across-two-instances))
- <a id="reconnection-time"></a>**reconnection time**: how long an `EventSource` waits before reconnecting, in milliseconds, set by a `retry` field of ASCII digits only ([43 §2](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#2-server-sent-events-on-the-wire-the-textevent-stream-format-and-how-eventsource-reconnects))
- <a id="recorder"></a>**recorder**: the exercise's shared array into which each constructor pushes its name, so a test can assert creation order ([14 Exercise 14.3](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#ex14-3)).
- <a id="redirect-relative-absolute-function"></a>**redirect (relative, absolute, function)**: a route's `redirectTo` rewrites the URL and matching continues; absolute when it starts with `/` or returns a `UrlTree`; a function receives a `PartialMatchRouteSnapshot` and runs in an injection context ([24 §1](modules/24-routing.md#1-router-configuration-providerouter-route-matching-redirects-and-the-wildcard))
- <a id="redirect-uri"></a>**redirect URI**: the callback address where the IdP sends the user back ([42 §2](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#2-oauth-20-and-openid-connect-for-a-single-page-app-authorization-code-with-pkce-and-why-the-implicit-flow-is-gone))
- <a id="redirectcommand"></a>**RedirectCommand**: a `UrlTree` plus navigation options, returned or thrown by a guard to cancel and redirect ([24 §5](modules/24-routing.md#5-functional-guards-canmatch-canactivate-candeactivate-and-redirectcommand))
- <a id="reducer"></a>**reducer**: a pure function `(state, action) => nextState` that changes state only by returning a new object ([28 §4](modules/28-state-management.md#4-ngrx-store-actions-reducers-createactiongroup-createfeature-and-runtime-checks)) · also in [47 Exercise 47.2](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#ex47-2)
- <a id="redux-devtools-time-travel-maxage-and-logonly"></a>**Redux DevTools**, **time travel**, **maxAge** and **logOnly**: the browser extension that `@ngrx/store-devtools` connects to, jumping to an earlier recorded state, the cap on recorded history, and the option that restricts the extension's features ([28 §7](modules/28-state-management.md#7-ngrx-entity-router-store-and-store-devtools))
- <a id="redux-pattern"></a>**Redux pattern**: state changes in one direction, only when an action is dispatched and a reducer computes the next state ([28 §4](modules/28-state-management.md#4-ngrx-store-actions-reducers-createactiongroup-createfeature-and-runtime-checks))
- <a id="ref-vue"></a>**ref (Vue)**: Vue's signal-like container, read and written through `.value` ([40 §4](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#4-signals-beyond-angular-vue-refs-solid-svelte-runes-preact-and-qwik))
- <a id="reference-count-share"></a>**reference count (share)**: the number of subscribers `share` is serving; at zero it resets and unsubscribes from the source ([22 §3](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#3-cold-and-hot-unicast-and-multicast))
- <a id="referentially-transparent-operator"></a>**referentially transparent (operator)**: one operator value can be applied to many Observables without changing state in the operator ([22 §5](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#5-pipeable-operators-and-writing-your-own))
- <a id="reflect"></a>**Reflect**: the namespace of functions that perform each fundamental operation's default behavior, used to forward from traps ([03 §5](modules/03-js-objects-prototypes-classes.md#5-proxy-and-reflect)).
- <a id="reflected-xss"></a>**reflected XSS**: cross-site scripting where a response echoes part of the request that carried the payload ([33 §2](modules/33-security.md#2-xss-and-angulars-template-model-escaping-by-default-trusted-templates-and-the-paths-around-them))
- <a id="refresh-of-a-view"></a>**refresh (of a view)**: a view's template running during the walk ([21 §4](modules/21-change-detection.md#4-the-tree-walk-global-and-targeted-refreshes))
- <a id="refresh-loop"></a>**refresh loop**: a failing refresh that triggers another refresh, so the calls never end ([27 §7](modules/27-http-client.md#7-authentication-attaching-tokens-refreshing-on-401-with-a-request-queue-and-avoiding-refresh-loops))
- <a id="refresh-token"></a>**refresh token**: a longer-lived credential exchanged for a new bearer token when the current one expires ([27 §7](modules/27-http-client.md#7-authentication-attaching-tokens-refreshing-on-401-with-a-request-queue-and-avoiding-refresh-loops))
- <a id="refresh-token-rotation"></a>**refresh token rotation**: the authorization server issuing a new refresh token on every use and invalidating the previous one ([42 §4](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#4-refresh-tokens-rotation-reuse-detection-silent-renew-and-refreshing-across-tabs))
- <a id="refreshview--checkalways--haschildviewstorefresh"></a>**RefreshView / CheckAlways / HasChildViewsToRefresh**: internal view flags: refresh this view now, refresh whenever the walk is global, and pass through this view to reach a flagged descendant ([21 §4](modules/21-change-detection.md#4-the-tree-walk-global-and-targeted-refreshes))
- <a id="regex-location"></a>**regex location**: a `location ~` (case-sensitive) or `location ~*` (case-insensitive) block, matched by a regular expression ([44 §3](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#3-nginx-for-a-single-page-app-location-matching-the-try_files-fallback-caching-headers-for-hashed-assets-and-gzip-or-brotli))
- <a id="registered-property"></a>**registered property**: a custom property declared with `@property`, with a type, `inherits` and an initial value ([10 §6](modules/10-css-essentials.md#6-custom-properties-theming-and-color-scheme)).
- <a id="registered-symbol"></a>**registered symbol**: a symbol created by `Symbol.for(key)`, recreatable from its string and therefore not usable as a weak key ([05 §5](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#5-weak-references-weakmap-weakset-weakref-and-finalizationregistry)).
- <a id="registerlocaledata"></a>**registerLocaleData**: stores an imported locale array so Angular's formatting functions can find it; it downloads nothing ([34 §3](modules/34-i18n.md#3-angulars-locale-data-and-formatting-functions-versus-intl))
- <a id="registration-service-worker"></a>**registration (service worker)**: the browser's record that a worker script serves a scope ([37 §4](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#4-angularservice-worker-registration-ngsw-configjson-and-the-generated-ngswjson))
- <a id="relative-import"></a>**relative import**: an import that names a file by its position, such as `../../orders/x` ([38 §4](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#4-layers-library-types-and-dependency-rules))
- <a id="relative-luminance-and-contrast-ratio"></a>**relative luminance and contrast ratio**: the 0 to 1 brightness of a colour, and `(L1 + 0.05) / (L2 + 0.05)` over two of them ([11 §1](modules/11-accessibility.md#1-why-accessibility-and-what-the-standards-require)).
- <a id="release-identifier"></a>**release identifier**: one string that names a build, used by the build, the tracker and every report so maps and events match ([44 §9](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#9-frontend-error-tracking-and-real-user-monitoring-releases-source-maps-beacons-and-sampling))
- <a id="release-manifest"></a>**release manifest**: in Exercise 44.1, the `releases.json` object listing each kept release with its hashed keys, written before `index.html` so a later publish knows which old chunks it may delete ([Exercise 44.1](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#ex44-1))
- <a id="release-age-delay"></a>**release-age delay**: an npm setting (`min-release-age`, `before`) that installs only versions published long enough ago ([33 §11](modules/33-security.md#11-dependency-and-supply-chain-security-lockfiles-npm-audit-install-scripts-and-subresource-integrity))
- <a id="reloadvalidation"></a>**reloadValidation()**: reloads every async rule of a field and its descendants ([26 §6](modules/26-signal-forms.md#6-async-validation-and-debounce-validateasync-validatehttp-pending-and-cancellation)).
- <a id="remote"></a>**remote**: a separately deployed build that a host loads ([38 §8](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#8-micro-frontends-module-federation-native-federation-and-when-they-are-justified))
- <a id="remote-cache"></a>**remote cache**: a computation cache shared between machines ([38 §7](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#7-nx-monorepos-the-project-graph-tags-nxenforce-module-boundaries-affected-commands-and-caching))
- <a id="render-callback"></a>**render callback**: a function registered with `afterNextRender` or `afterEveryRender` that Angular calls after it has finished rendering the whole application ([20 §4](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#4-render-hooks-afternextrender-aftereveryrender-and-their-phases))
- <a id="render-effect"></a>**render effect**: a computation that writes to the DOM and re-runs when what it read changes ([40 §4](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#4-signals-beyond-angular-vue-refs-solid-svelte-runes-preact-and-qwik))
- <a id="render-function"></a>**render function**: a function that returns a description of the UI ([40 §2](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#2-templates-jsx-and-single-file-components-what-the-compiler-knows))
- <a id="render-mode"></a>**render mode**: the per-route choice of where and when the HTML is built, a `RenderMode` value (`Client`, `Server`, `Prerender`) in the server routes ([32 §1](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#1-why-render-on-the-server-csr-ssr-ssg-and-hybrid-rendering-and-what-each-costs))
- <a id="render-prop"></a>**render prop**: a function a component calls to draw part of itself, the React idiom nearest to passing a template ([19 §4](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#4-template-driven-apis-ng-template-ngtemplateoutlet-and-typed-context))
- <a id="render-tree"></a>**render tree**: the visible DOM nodes with their computed styles; `display: none` nodes are left out ([08 §1](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#1-the-critical-rendering-path-from-bytes-to-pixels)).
- <a id="render-blocking--parser-blocking"></a>**render-blocking / parser-blocking**: a resource that delays the first paint (CSS by default), versus one that stops DOM construction (a classic synchronous script) ([08 §1](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#1-the-critical-rendering-path-from-bytes-to-pixels)).
- <a id="rendered-range"></a>**rendered range**: the pair of item indexes, start and end, that currently have a row in the DOM ([31 §4](modules/31-performance.md#4-long-lists-track-virtual-scrolling-with-the-cdk-and-the-alternatives))
- <a id="renderer2"></a>**Renderer2**: Angular's abstract class for writing to elements; per its typings it gives no extra security over direct DOM access ([14 §1](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#1-directives-and-attribute-directives-host-inputs-elementref-and-renderer2)). · also in [39 §4](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#4-the-version-matrix-part-1-v2-to-v8)
- <a id="rendering-opportunity"></a>**rendering opportunity**: a moment the browser may update the screen; only then do `requestAnimationFrame` callbacks, style, layout and paint run ([04 §1](modules/04-js-async-event-loop.md#1-the-event-loop-tasks-microtasks-and-rendering)).
- <a id="rendering-strategy"></a>**rendering strategy**: where and when a route's HTML is built ([45 §7](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#7-rendering-strategy-and-caching-layers-a-rendering-choice-per-route-and-every-cache-from-the-cdn-to-the-component))
- <a id="reorder-move"></a>**reorder (move)**: a change of a row's position in an `@for` that keeps its element; with a stable `track` it starts no leave animation ([35 §7](modules/35-animations.md#7-animating-lists-for-enter-leave-reorder-and-stagger))
- <a id="replaced-element"></a>**replaced element**: an element whose content CSS does not render, such as an image ([10 §1](modules/10-css-essentials.md#1-the-box-model-and-formatting-contexts)).
- <a id="replay"></a>**replay**: sending the queued writes, in order, after reconnecting ([45 §8](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#8-under-the-hood-failure-modes-observability-offline-levels-and-consistency-choices))
- <a id="replay-idempotency"></a>**replay (idempotency)**: answering a completed retry with the stored first response; our server's choice and Stripe API v1's documented behavior, not the draft's requirement ([46 §8](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#8-under-the-hood-a-money-transfer-with-idempotency-keys-optimistic-ui-that-never-claims-success-and-the-unknown-outcome))
- <a id="replay-buffer"></a>**replay buffer**: the values a `ReplaySubject` (and so `shareReplay`) keeps for late subscribers, up to `bufferSize` and `windowTime` ([23 §5](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#5-multicasting-in-depth-share-configuration-sharereplay-and-connect))
- <a id="replaysubject"></a>**ReplaySubject**: a `Subject` that stores the last n values (unbounded by default) and replays them to every new subscriber ([22 §4](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#4-subjects-subject-behaviorsubject-replaysubject-and-asyncsubject))
- <a id="report-only-mode"></a>**report-only mode**: a policy sent as `Content-Security-Policy-Report-Only`, which reports violations without blocking anything ([33 §5](modules/33-security.md#5-content-security-policy-with-angular-nonces-csp_nonce-and-autocsp))
- <a id="report-only-rollout"></a>**report-only rollout**: sending the Trusted Types directives as `Content-Security-Policy-Report-Only` first, so each report finds a sink before enforcement breaks it ([33 §6](modules/33-security.md#6-trusted-types-with-angular-the-frameworks-policies-enforcement-and-rollout))
- <a id="reportdownloadprogress"></a>**reportDownloadProgress**: the request option that asks for `DownloadProgress` events; both backends support it; added in v22.0 ([27 §9](modules/27-http-client.md#9-progress-events-reportuploadprogress-reportdownloadprogress-and-why-upload-progress-needs-xhr))
- <a id="reporter-port"></a>**reporter port**: an injection token with one method, `send`, that the `ErrorHandler` calls so no vendor code sits in the handler ([37 §11](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#11-logging-and-error-tracking-a-reporter-port-context-privacy-source-maps-and-sampling), token in [16 §7](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#7-injectiontoken-multi-providers-environment-providers-and-initializers))
- <a id="reportuploadprogress"></a>**reportUploadProgress**: the request option that asks for `UploadProgress` events; the fetch backend throws `NG02824` when it is set, and it needs `withXhr()`; added in v22.0 ([27 §9](modules/27-http-client.md#9-progress-events-reportuploadprogress-reportdownloadprogress-and-why-upload-progress-needs-xhr))
- <a id="request"></a>**REQUEST**: the `@angular/core` token holding the Fetch `Request` of the page being rendered on the server, `null` in the browser and in prerendered pages ([32 §7](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#7-platform-safe-code-browser-apis-afternextrender-document-request-and-response_init))
- <a id="request-handler"></a>**request handler**: the exported function that wraps the Express app for callers such as the Angular CLI's dev server and build ([32 §2](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#2-adding-angularssr-what-ng-add-generates-the-build-output-and-the-server-entry))
- <a id="request-queue"></a>**request queue**: the requests waiting on a refresh and retried when it ends ([27 §7](modules/27-http-client.md#7-authentication-attaching-tokens-refreshing-on-401-with-a-request-queue-and-avoiding-refresh-loops))
- <a id="request-waterfall"></a>**request waterfall**: requests that start only after earlier ones finish ([45 §4](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#4-component-architecture-data-model-and-data-flow-from-screens-to-a-component-tree-and-a-client-side-model))
- <a id="request_context"></a>**REQUEST_CONTEXT**: the `@angular/core` token holding the `unknown` value the server passes as the second argument of `handle` ([32 §7](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#7-platform-safe-code-browser-apis-afternextrender-document-request-and-response_init))
- <a id="requestidlecallback"></a>**requestIdleCallback**: the browser call that runs work when the page is idle; the `idle` trigger uses it, with a `setTimeout` fallback ([18 §6](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#6-triggers-and-prefetching))
- <a id="require-trusted-types-for"></a>**require-trusted-types-for**: the CSP directive (`'script'`) that makes every sink throw on a plain string ([33 §6](modules/33-security.md#6-trusted-types-with-angular-the-frameworks-policies-enforcement-and-rollout))
- <a id="required-input"></a>**required input**: an input declared with `input.required()`; it has no default and throws `NG0950` if read before it is bound ([20 §2](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#2-constructor-ngoninit-and-inputs-what-exists-when))
- <a id="required-variable"></a>**required variable**: an environment variable whose absence stops the start instead of producing a broken file ([44 §5](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#5-runtime-configuration-without-rebuilding-configjson-environment-substitution-at-container-start-and-provideappinitializer))
- <a id="required-versus-optional-migration"></a>**required versus optional migration**: a required migration runs unasked during `ng update`; an optional one is offered in a prompt, pre-selected only when `recommended` ([39 §7](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#7-updating-in-practice-ng-update-migration-schematics-and-the-update-guide))
- <a id="reset-share"></a>**reset (share)**: forgetting the shared run (Subject and source subscription) so the next subscriber starts a new one; decided separately at zero subscribers, on complete and on error ([23 §5](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#5-multicasting-in-depth-share-configuration-sharereplay-and-connect))
- <a id="resetonsuccess"></a>**resetOnSuccess**: the `retry` option that zeroes the retry count whenever the source emits ([23 §4](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#4-retrying-and-repeating-retry-with-backoff-and-repeat))
- <a id="resolution"></a>**resolution**: the walk a lookup makes: up the element injectors, then the environment injectors, until a provider answers or the `NullInjector` ends it ([16 §5](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#5-the-injector-tree-platform-root-route-and-element-injectors-and-the-modifiers)).
- <a id="resolution-reason"></a>**resolution reason**: the field that says why an evaluation returned its value, such as `SPLIT`, `DISABLED` or `ERROR` ([44 §8](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#8-feature-flags-with-openfeature-and-canary-releases-providers-evaluation-context-sticky-rollout-kill-switches-and-version-skew))
- <a id="resolved-data"></a>**resolved data**: the first value of each resolver, merged into the route's `data` under its `resolve` key ([24 §6](modules/24-routing.md#6-resolvers-and-route-data-resolvefn-failures-and-router-resources))
- <a id="resolved-options"></a>**resolved options**: what `resolvedOptions()` returns: the locale, numbering system and options a formatter actually uses after fallback ([34 §2](modules/34-i18n.md#2-the-platforms-intl-apis-dates-numbers-plurals-lists-collation-and-display-names))
- <a id="resolvefn"></a>**ResolveFn<T>**: the function type of a resolver, `(route, state) => T | RedirectCommand` as a value, Promise or Observable, run in the route's injection context ([24 §6](modules/24-routing.md#6-resolvers-and-route-data-resolvefn-failures-and-router-resources))
- <a id="resolver"></a>**resolver**: a function whose result becomes route data before activation ([24 §2](modules/24-routing.md#2-route-parameters-path-matrix-query-and-fragment-inheritance-and-component-input-binding))
- <a id="resource"></a>**resource**: a signal-shaped view of an async read, with `params`, a `loader`, and `value`, `status`, `error` and `isLoading` signals ([17 §6](modules/17-signals.md#6-resources-async-data-as-signals)).
- <a id="resource-rest"></a>**resource (REST)**: a thing the API names by URL ([45 §5](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#5-state-and-api-design-where-each-piece-of-state-lives-and-the-api-the-screens-need))
- <a id="resource-server"></a>**resource server**: the API that accepts access tokens ([42 §2](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#2-oauth-20-and-openid-connect-for-a-single-page-app-authorization-code-with-pkce-and-why-the-implicit-flow-is-gone))
- <a id="resource-url"></a>**resource URL**: a URL the browser loads and runs as code, such as an iframe `src` ([33 §3](modules/33-security.md#3-sanitization-by-security-context-html-style-url-and-resource-url))
- <a id="resourcestatus"></a>**ResourceStatus**: `'idle' | 'loading' | 'reloading' | 'resolved' | 'error' | 'local'` ([17 §6](modules/17-signals.md#6-resources-async-data-as-signals)).
- <a id="response-head"></a>**response head**: the status line and headers of a response, which `fetch` resolves on before the body has arrived ([09 §2](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#2-fetch-xhr-and-the-request-lifecycle)).
- <a id="response_init"></a>**RESPONSE_INIT**: the `@angular/core` token holding the mutable `ResponseInit` (status, headers) of the page being rendered on the server, `null` in the browser and in prerendered pages ([32 §7](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#7-platform-safe-code-browser-apis-afternextrender-document-request-and-response_init))
- <a id="responseentityexceptionhandler"></a>**ResponseEntityExceptionHandler**: Spring MVC's base class for a `@ControllerAdvice` that renders Spring MVC's exceptions as problem details ([41 §5](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#5-error-contracts-with-problemdetail-rfc-9457-and-mapping-them-in-angular))
- <a id="responsetype"></a>**responseType**: the request option that picks how the body is parsed: `json`, `text`, `blob` or `arraybuffer` ([27 §3](modules/27-http-client.md#3-making-requests-typed-responses-observe-responsetype-immutable-params-and-headers-and-httpcontext))
- <a id="resubscription"></a>**resubscription**: resending on every open what the server must know, such as a subscribe request per topic, because a new socket remembers nothing ([43 §6](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#6-the-angular-websocket-client-rxjs-websocket-stomprx-stomp-and-reconnecting-without-losing-subscriptions))
- <a id="result-selector"></a>**result selector**: the deprecated second function argument of `switchMap`, `mergeMap`, `concatMap` and `exhaustMap` that combined outer and inner values; replaced by an inner `map` ([23 §1](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#1-higher-order-observables-switchmap-mergemap-concatmap-and-exhaustmap))
- <a id="result-sentence"></a>**result sentence**: our term for a story's last sentence, which states the outcome, with a number and its baseline where one exists, even when the outcome is negative ([47 §1](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#1-the-behavioral-round-what-a-story-is-evidence-of-star-with-the-weight-on-action-and-an-explicit-result-and-a-story-bank))
- <a id="resumability-qwik"></a>**resumability (Qwik)**: pausing execution on the server and resuming it on the client without replaying the application logic ([40 §4](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#4-signals-beyond-angular-vue-refs-solid-svelte-runes-preact-and-qwik))
- <a id="resumable-upload"></a>**resumable upload**: a chunked upload whose server reports what it holds, so a failure resends only the rest ([41 §8](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#8-files-multipart-and-chunked-uploads-progress-and-downloads-with-content-disposition-and-streaming))
- <a id="resume"></a>**resume**: opening a saved draft on a later visit or another device ([46 §5](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#5-a-multi-step-form-wizard-with-draft-saving-a-step-model-per-step-validation-and-drafts-that-survive-and-migrate))
- <a id="resync"></a>**resync**: reloading a snapshot instead of replaying what a throttled client missed ([46 §6](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#6-a-real-time-operations-dashboard-aggregation-per-widget-staleness-hidden-tabs-and-screens-left-open-for-days))
- <a id="retainer"></a>**retainer**: an object on the path from a GC root that keeps another object alive by pointing at it ([31 §8](modules/31-performance.md#8-memory-leaks-heap-snapshots-detached-nodes-and-the-devtools-workflow))
- <a id="retainer-path"></a>**retainer path**: the chain of retainers from a GC root to a retained object ([31 §8](modules/31-performance.md#8-memory-leaks-heap-snapshots-detached-nodes-and-the-devtools-workflow))
- <a id="retargeting--composed-event"></a>**retargeting / composed event**: a composed event crosses the shadow boundary and outside listeners see the host as its target ([08 §5](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#5-web-components-custom-elements-shadow-dom-and-templates)).
- <a id="retries-policy"></a>**retries policy**: a team's rule for how many retries CI allows and what a pass on retry triggers ([30 §8](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#8-ci-strategy-for-end-to-end-tests-workers-sharding-retries-policy-and-test-data))
- <a id="retry-ability"></a>**retry-ability**: Cypress's rule that queries and assertions are retried until a timeout while other commands run once ([30 §3](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#3-auto-waiting-actionability-checks-web-first-assertions-and-cypress-retry-ability))
- <a id="retryable-error"></a>**retryable error**: a failure a later attempt may not meet (a 503), unlike one that fails the same way every time (a 400) ([23 §4](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#4-retrying-and-repeating-retry-with-backoff-and-repeat)) · also in [27 §5](modules/27-http-client.md#5-errors-httperrorresponse-an-error-mapping-layer-and-retry-with-backoff-and-jitter)
- <a id="return-url"></a>**return URL**: the path a visitor asked for before being sent to sign in, carried so the app can take them back after ([42 §7](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#7-the-angular-side-an-auth-state-at-startup-guards-as-ux-interceptors-and-angular-oauth2-oidc-versus-angular-auth-oidc-client))
- <a id="reuse-detection"></a>**reuse detection**: the authorization server remembering used refresh tokens, so that one presented again revokes the family's active token ([42 §4](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#4-refresh-tokens-rotation-reuse-detection-silent-renew-and-refreshing-across-tabs))
- <a id="reverse-mapping"></a>**reverse mapping**: the numeric-enum object's extra entries from value to name (`Direction[0] === 'Up'`) ([06 §6](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#6-enums-literal-unions-as-const-and-satisfies)).
- <a id="reverse-proxy"></a>**reverse proxy**: a server in front of several apps that forwards each request to one of them by path ([39 §9](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#9-from-angularjs-to-angular-ngupgrade-hybrids-the-strangler-fig-and-rewrites)) · also in [44](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md), [44 §1](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#1-where-the-api-calls-go-direct-calls-a-same-origin-reverse-proxy-an-api-gateway-or-a-backend-for-frontend)
- <a id="reversing-transition"></a>**reversing (transition)**: running a transition back from the value it has reached when the property changes again mid-way; the running transition is cancelled and a new one starts ([35 §2](modules/35-animations.md#2-css-transitions-triggers-interruption-events-and-entry-with-starting-style))
- <a id="review-call"></a>**review call**: the follow-up conversation after a take-home, to walk through a decision and change the code live ([47 §5](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#5-take-home-assignments-scope-and-time-box-what-the-repository-must-show-the-readme-and-the-review-call))
- <a id="revocation"></a>**revocation**: ending a credential before it expires ([42 §1](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#1-sessions-versus-tokens-what-the-server-keeps-what-each-request-carries-revocation-and-scaling))
- <a id="rewrite-angularjs-to-angular"></a>**rewrite (AngularJS to Angular)**: a new framework with no in-place upgrade path from the old one ([39 §2](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#2-why-angular-was-rewritten-and-what-carried-over))
- <a id="ring-buffer"></a>**ring buffer**: a fixed-size store that overwrites its oldest point, so memory stays bounded ([46 §6](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#6-a-real-time-operations-dashboard-aggregation-per-widget-staleness-hidden-tabs-and-screens-left-open-for-days))
- <a id="risk"></a>**risk**: one way the app can break, which a strategy assigns to a test level ([30 §1](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#1-what-belongs-at-the-end-to-end-level-journeys-cost-and-a-strategy-across-levels))
- <a id="risk-based-test-selection"></a>**risk-based test selection**: assigning each risk to the cheapest test level that can catch it ([30 §1](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#1-what-belongs-at-the-end-to-end-level-journeys-cost-and-a-strategy-across-levels))
- <a id="role"></a>**role**: what kind of thing an accessibility-tree node is, such as button, link or heading ([11 §2](modules/11-accessibility.md#2-semantic-html-landmarks-and-headings)).
- <a id="role-query"></a>**role query**: a Testing Library query such as `getByRole('button', { name })` that finds a node by role and accessible name ([29 §11](modules/29-testing-angular.md#11-component-harnesses-and-angular-testing-library))
- <a id="rollback"></a>**rollback**: returning users to the previous release ([44 §7](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#7-cicd-for-both-layers-one-image-promoted-through-stages-a-monorepo-or-separate-repositories-and-versioning-the-contract), [47 §6](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#6-questions-to-ask-about-the-front-end-practice-versions-and-updates-testing-the-design-system-and-accessibility-performance-contracts-and-releases-and-how-to-read-the-answers))
- <a id="rolldown"></a>**rolldown**: the bundler of the optional second chunk-optimization pass ([12 §3](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#3-building-the-application-builder-output-layout-and-budgets)).
- <a id="root-cause"></a>**root cause**: why an incident happened, found after mitigation ([47 §2](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#2-four-front-end-stories-a-performance-win-a-migration-a-production-incident-and-a-disagreement-over-architecture))
- <a id="root-module"></a>**root module**: the NgModule an app bootstraps; its providers go to the root injector ([15 §1](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#1-what-ngmodules-did-compilation-scope-injector-and-lazy-loading-unit)).
- <a id="root-view"></a>**root view**: the view that hosts a root component, where each walk starts ([21 §4](modules/21-change-detection.md#4-the-tree-walk-global-and-targeted-refreshes))
- <a id="rootdir"></a>**rootDir**: the directory whose layout the output mirrors under `outDir`; default `.` (the tsconfig folder) since 6.0 ([06 §7](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#7-tsconfig-the-strict-family-module-settings-and-typescript-60-defaults)).
- <a id="round-trip"></a>**round trip**: one request and its response; RTT is its duration ([45 §3](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#3-requirements-first-functional-and-non-functional-requirements-constraints-and-back-of-envelope-numbers))
- <a id="route"></a>**route**: an entry of the route table, `{ path, component | children | loadChildren | redirectTo, … }` ([24 §1](modules/24-routing.md#1-router-configuration-providerouter-route-matching-redirects-and-the-wildcard))
- <a id="route-handler"></a>**route handler**: a function registered with `page.route` that receives a `Route` and answers, continues, falls back or aborts a request ([30 §5](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#5-api-mocking-at-the-network-layer-and-where-contract-tests-take-over))
- <a id="route-header"></a>**route header**: a `headers` entry of a server route, sent with that route's response ([32 §3](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#3-server-routes-and-rendermode-per-route-rendering-getprerenderparams-fallbacks-headers-and-status))
- <a id="route-injector"></a>**route injector**: the environment injector a route creates when it lists `providers`; siblings get separate ones and a revisit reuses it, routing owned by module 24 ([16 §5](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#5-the-injector-tree-platform-root-route-and-element-injectors-and-the-modifiers)). · also in [24 §4](modules/24-routing.md#4-lazy-loading-in-depth-loaders-canmatch-and-route-injectors)
- <a id="route-providers"></a>**route providers**: providers listed on a route, from which the router creates that route's environment injector once ([15 §4](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#4-lazy-loading-without-modules-loadcomponent-and-loadchildren-with-route-arrays)).
- <a id="route-reuse-strategy"></a>**route reuse strategy**: the `RouteReuseStrategy` that decides whether a route keeps its component (`shouldReuseRoute`) and whether a leaving route is detached and stored and a returning one reattached ([24 §8](modules/24-routing.md#8-preloading-strategies-and-route-reuse))
- <a id="route-table-routes"></a>**route table (Routes)**: the array of routes given to `provideRouter`, matched in array order ([24 §1](modules/24-routing.md#1-router-configuration-providerouter-route-matching-redirects-and-the-wildcard))
- <a id="route-tree"></a>**route tree**: the server routes stored segment by segment (`:id` as `*`), which the server app matches a URL against to find its render mode ([32 §10](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#10-under-the-hood-a-request-through-the-engine-the-serialized-page-and-the-clients-hydration-pass))
- <a id="routeconfigloadstart--routeconfigloadend"></a>**RouteConfigLoadStart / RouteConfigLoadEnd**: the router events emitted around the first load of a lazy route's loader ([24 §4](modules/24-routing.md#4-lazy-loading-in-depth-loaders-canmatch-and-route-injectors))
- <a id="router-event"></a>**router event**: an object emitted on `Router.events` at each step of a navigation, from `NavigationStart` to a terminal event ([24 §7](modules/24-routing.md#7-the-navigation-lifecycle-events-cancellation-and-the-transition-pipeline))
- <a id="router-feature"></a>**router feature**: an optional `with…` function passed to `provideRouter` after the table, such as `withDebugTracing()` ([24 §1](modules/24-routing.md#1-router-configuration-providerouter-route-matching-redirects-and-the-wildcard))
- <a id="router-outlet"></a>**router outlet**: the `<router-outlet />` placeholder where the router inserts the component of a route; routed content follows it as a sibling ([24 §3](modules/24-routing.md#3-nested-and-named-outlets))
- <a id="router-resource"></a>**router resource**: a `resource` returned by a route's `resources` function, enabled by `withRouterResources()`; blocking unless wrapped in `nonBlocking()` ([24 §6](modules/24-routing.md#6-resolvers-and-route-data-resolvefn-failures-and-router-resources))
- <a id="router-state"></a>**router state**: the tree of activated routes for the current URL ([24 §3](modules/24-routing.md#3-nested-and-named-outlets))
- <a id="router-store-and-router-state-serializer"></a>**Router Store** and **router state serializer**: `@ngrx/router-store` mirrors navigation into the store as actions and state, and the serializer reduces Angular's router snapshot to a plain object ([28 §7](modules/28-state-management.md#7-ngrx-entity-router-store-and-store-devtools))
- <a id="routeroutletdata"></a>**routerOutletData**: an input of `RouterOutlet` whose value a routed component reads as a signal through `ROUTER_OUTLET_DATA` ([24 §3](modules/24-routing.md#3-nested-and-named-outlets))
- <a id="routertestingharness"></a>**RouterTestingHarness**: the router's test helper; it renders a root `router-outlet`, navigates with `navigateByUrl` and waits for the navigation and its render ([29 §9](modules/29-testing-angular.md#9-testing-guards-interceptors-forms-effects-resources-and-stores))
- <a id="routes"></a>**Routes**: the router's array of route entries (paths, components, loaders, providers) ([15 §4](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#4-lazy-loading-without-modules-loadcomponent-and-loadchildren-with-route-arrays); owned by [module 24](modules/24-routing.md)).
- <a id="roving-tabindex"></a>**roving tabindex**: a composite-widget technique where exactly one item has `tabindex="0"` and arrow keys move that stop ([11 §4](modules/11-accessibility.md#4-keyboard-focus-order-roving-tabindex-and-focus-traps)). · also in [37 Exercise 37.2](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#ex37-2)
- <a id="row"></a>**row**: one embedded view that a `@for` creates for one item ([18 §2](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#2-for-track-context-variables-empty-and-what-angular-can-iterate))
- <a id="row-definition"></a>**row definition**: a `cdkRowDef` that lists the columns a data row of a CDK table shows ([36 §7](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#7-tables-and-long-lists-cdktable-datasource-mattable-and-virtual-scrolling))
- <a id="row-recycling"></a>**row recycling**: reusing a detached row view, kept in a template cache, for the next item that enters the range ([31 §4](modules/31-performance.md#4-long-lists-track-virtual-scrolling-with-the-cdk-and-the-alternatives))
- <a id="rp-initiated-logout"></a>**RP-initiated logout**: the app (the relying party) sending the browser to the identity provider's logout URL so the provider ends its session too ([42 §8](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#8-logout-everywhere-and-session-timeout-ux))
- <a id="rule"></a>**rule**: one named check of a linter, set to off, warn or error ([38 §9](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#9-linting-and-formatting-angular-eslint-prettier-import-ordering-and-commit-hooks))
- <a id="rule-engine"></a>**rule engine**: a program such as axe-core that checks the rendered DOM against a list of accessibility rules ([11 §7](modules/11-accessibility.md#7-testing-accessibility)).
- <a id="rum-real-user-monitoring"></a>**RUM (real user monitoring)**: collecting performance data from your own real users, for example with the `web-vitals` library ([08 §6](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#6-core-web-vitals-lcp-inp-and-cls)).
- <a id="rum-collector"></a>**RUM collector**: the endpoint that receives real-user-monitoring events ([44 §9](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#9-frontend-error-tracking-and-real-user-monitoring-releases-source-maps-beacons-and-sampling))
- <a id="rune-svelte"></a>**rune (Svelte)**: a Svelte 5 keyword such as `$state` ([40 §4](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#4-signals-beyond-angular-vue-refs-solid-svelte-runes-preact-and-qwik))
- <a id="rung"></a>**rung**: one question of the follow-up ladder, asking for more specific evidence than the one above ([47 §7](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#7-under-the-hood-how-one-story-is-drilled-into-numbers-you-can-defend-and-telling-a-design-or-a-migration-as-your-own-project))
- <a id="runguardsandresolvers"></a>**runGuardsAndResolvers**: the route property that sets when guards and resolvers run again for a route that stays active; the default `'paramsChange'` ignores query changes ([24 §6](modules/24-routing.md#6-resolvers-and-route-data-resolvefn-failures-and-router-resources))
- <a id="runininjectioncontext"></a>**runInInjectionContext**: the free function `runInInjectionContext(injector, fn)` that makes `injector` current while `fn` runs synchronously; there is no `Injector` method of that name in the 22.2.1 typings ([16 §4](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#4-the-injection-context-inject-runininjectioncontext-and-injectasync)).
- <a id="running-example"></a>**running example**: the team task board that carries every step of the method ([45 §1](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#1-the-front-end-system-design-interview-what-it-evaluates-its-formats-and-how-it-differs-from-a-backend-design-round))
- <a id="runtime-angular"></a>**runtime (Angular)**: the `@angular/core` code that creates components and updates the DOM in the browser ([12 §1](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#1-what-a-framework-does-and-angulars-philosophy)).
- <a id="runtime-checks"></a>**runtime checks**: guards around every reducer, on by default only in development for immutability ([28 §4](modules/28-state-management.md#4-ngrx-store-actions-reducers-createactiongroup-createfeature-and-runtime-checks))
- <a id="runtime-configuration"></a>**runtime configuration**: settings the app reads after it starts, not compiled into the bundle ([12 §4](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#4-environments-and-configuration-build-time-versus-run-time)).
- <a id="runtime-stage"></a>**runtime stage**: the last stage, whose result is the image that runs ([44 §2](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#2-a-docker-multi-stage-build-for-angular-build-and-runtime-stages-layer-caching-dockerignore-and-a-non-root-image))
- <a id="runtime-translation"></a>**runtime translation**: keeping `$localize` live in the browser, with the translations loaded before the app starts ([34 §8](modules/34-i18n.md#8-runtime-translation-with-angularlocalize-loadtranslations-before-bootstrap))
- <a id="runtimequeryobjects"></a>**Runtime.queryObjects**: the CDP method that returns the live objects with a given prototype ([31 §8](modules/31-performance.md#8-memory-leaks-heap-snapshots-detached-nodes-and-the-devtools-workflow))
- <a id="rxjsoperators-subpath"></a>**rxjs/operators subpath**: the older import site for operators; the docs call it deprecated without a version, and the 7.2.0 CHANGELOG says it "will be deprecated soon" ([22 §5](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#5-pipeable-operators-and-writing-your-own))
- <a id="rxmethod-and-signalmethod"></a>**rxMethod** and **signalMethod**: functions that turn an RxJS pipeline (or a plain function) into a store method callable with a value, a signal or, for `rxMethod`, an Observable ([28 §9](modules/28-state-management.md#9-signalstore-in-depth-rxmethod-withentities-custom-features-and-the-events-plugin))
- <a id="rxresource-with-stream"></a>**rxResource with stream**: a resource whose loader returns an Observable; each value replaces the resource's value, and completing with no value is an error (NG0991) ([43 §8](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#8-from-streams-to-signals-one-shared-connection-tosignal-and-rxresource-bursts-and-connection-state))

## S

- <a id="s256"></a>**S256**: the PKCE method that hashes the verifier with SHA-256 and base64url-encodes it ([42 §2](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#2-oauth-20-and-openid-connect-for-a-single-page-app-authorization-code-with-pkce-and-why-the-implicit-flow-is-gone))
- <a id="safe-method"></a>**safe method**: an HTTP method whose semantics are essentially read-only (`GET`, `HEAD`, `OPTIONS`, `TRACE`) ([09 §1](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#1-http-semantics-methods-status-codes-and-headers)).
- <a id="safe-value"></a>**safe value**: the wrapper object (`SafeHtml`, `SafeStyle`, `SafeScript`, `SafeUrl`, `SafeResourceUrl`) a bypass returns; a binding accepts it only in its own context ([33 §4](modules/33-security.md#4-domsanitizer-and-bypasssecuritytrust-when-trusting-a-value-is-dangerous-and-the-safe-patterns))
- <a id="safe_mode"></a>**SAFE_MODE**: the Angular worker's state after its own initialization fails, serving traffic from the network ([37 §6](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#6-updates-versions-swupdate-prompting-a-reload-unrecoverable-states-and-the-kill-switch))
- <a id="safelisted-header"></a>**safelisted header**: a request header a script may set on a cross-origin request without forcing a preflight (`Accept`, `Accept-Language`, `Content-Language`, `Content-Type` with restricted values, single-range `Range`) ([09 §5](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#5-the-same-origin-policy-and-cors)).
- <a id="safesubscriber-internal"></a>**SafeSubscriber (internal)**: the subscriber `subscribe` wraps around the consumer's observer ([22 §9](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#9-under-the-hood-subscribe-the-subscriber-chain-and-the-subjects-observer-list))
- <a id="safety-worker"></a>**safety worker**: `safety-worker.js`, a worker that unregisters itself and deletes the Angular worker's caches, served as a kill switch ([37 §6](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#6-updates-versions-swupdate-prompting-a-reload-unrecoverable-states-and-the-kill-switch))
- <a id="same-origin-deployment"></a>**same-origin deployment**: serving the app and its API from one scheme, host and port, so the browser sends no cross-origin request ([44 §1](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#1-where-the-api-calls-go-direct-calls-a-same-origin-reverse-proxy-an-api-gateway-or-a-backend-for-frontend))
- <a id="samevalue"></a>**SameValue**: the equality behind `Object.is`: `NaN` equals `NaN`, `+0` differs from `-0` ([01 §6](modules/01-js-values-types-coercion.md#6-equality)).
- <a id="samevaluezero"></a>**SameValueZero**: the equality used by `Map`, `Set` and `includes`: like SameValue, but `+0` equals `-0` ([01 §6](modules/01-js-values-types-coercion.md#6-equality)).
- <a id="sample"></a>**sample**: emit the latest value at each tick of a clock, only if a new one arrived ([22 §8](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#8-timing-operators-debouncetime-throttletime-audittime-and-sampletime))
- <a id="sample-tree"></a>**sample tree**: the lab's `sample-app/` folder, the smallest tree holding every folder kind of the module ([38 §12](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#12-a-complete-production-folder-tree-folder-by-folder))
- <a id="sampled-flag"></a>**sampled flag**: trace-flags bit 1 (`01`), meaning the caller may have recorded trace data; a recommendation, not a rule ([44 §10](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#10-under-the-hood-opentelemetry-for-the-web-traceparent-and-one-click-traced-to-a-spring-boot-span-and-log-line))
- <a id="sampling"></a>**sampling**: keeping only a fraction of reports to limit volume ([37 §11](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#11-logging-and-error-tracking-a-reporter-port-context-privacy-source-maps-and-sampling))
- <a id="sanitization"></a>**sanitization**: cleaning a value so it is safe for one security context while keeping it usable; escaping, by contrast, turns it into text ([33 §3](modules/33-security.md#3-sanitization-by-security-context-html-style-url-and-resource-url))
- <a id="sanitizer-function-reference"></a>**sanitizer function reference**: the `ɵɵsanitize*` function (internal) the compiler passes as a binding instruction's last argument, called each time the bound value changes ([33 §12](modules/33-security.md#12-under-the-hood-from-a-template-binding-to-a-sanitized-dom-write))
- <a id="sass-module-use"></a>**Sass module (@use)**: a stylesheet loaded with `@use` that exposes its mixins, functions and variables under a namespace ([10 §7](modules/10-css-essentials.md#7-native-nesting-versus-sass)).
- <a id="satisfies"></a>**satisfies**: checks an expression against a type while keeping the expression's own inferred type ([06 §6](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#6-enums-literal-unions-as-const-and-satisfies)).
- <a id="saved-request"></a>**saved request**: a request Spring Security keeps in the session to return to after login ([42 §9](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#9-under-the-hood-one-bff-login-and-the-first-api-call-through-spring-securitys-filters))
- <a id="scaninternals-internal"></a>**scanInternals (internal)**: the shared function behind `scan` and `reduce` ([22 §6](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#6-the-operator-catalogue-creation-transformation-filtering-utility-conditional-and-aggregation))
- <a id="scavenger--mark-compact"></a>**Scavenger / Mark-Compact**: V8's young-generation copying collector and its whole-heap collector ([05 §4](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#4-garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks)).
- <a id="scheduler"></a>**scheduler**: the object that decides when RxJS work runs; timing operators default to `asyncScheduler`, which uses `setInterval` (module 23) ([22 §8](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#8-timing-operators-debouncetime-throttletime-audittime-and-sampletime)) · also in [23 §9](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#9-schedulers-queue-asap-async-and-animation-frame-observeon-and-subscribeon)
- <a id="scheduler-change-detection"></a>**scheduler (change detection)**: the service that sets dirty flags on notifications and schedules one tick, racing `setTimeout` against `requestAnimationFrame` ([21 §5](modules/21-change-detection.md#5-zoneless-change-detection-the-scheduler-its-triggers-and-pendingtasks))
- <a id="scheduling"></a>**scheduling**: the runtime's decision of when the follow-up work runs: a tick, a microtask, a scheduler task or synchronously ([40 §9](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#9-under-the-hood-one-state-change-traced-through-angular-react-vue-and-solid))
- <a id="schema-angular-template"></a>**schema (Angular template)**: a setting that tells the template compiler which unknown element or property names to accept instead of reporting an error ([37 Q37.05](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#q37-05))
- <a id="schema-and-rule"></a>**schema and rule**: a rule is a validation or state declaration on a path of the model; a schema groups rules, declared once per model ([26 §1](modules/26-signal-forms.md#1-why-a-third-forms-api-model-first-signals-end-to-end))
- <a id="schema-version"></a>**schema version**: a number saved with a draft naming the form's shape when it was written ([46 §5](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#5-a-multi-step-form-wizard-with-draft-saving-a-step-model-per-step-validation-and-drafts-that-survive-and-migrate))
- <a id="schema-first-type"></a>**schema-first type**: a static type derived from a run-time validation schema (`z.infer<typeof Schema>`), so the check and the type cannot drift ([07 §8](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#8-runtime-validation-with-zod-4-schema-first-types)).
- <a id="schema-and-apply"></a>**schema() and apply**: `schema()` names a reusable, non-reactive function that attaches rules, and `apply(path, schema)` mounts it on a sub-path ([26 §4](modules/26-signal-forms.md#4-schema-rules-and-validation-built-in-rules-validate-and-reusable-schemas)).
- <a id="schemapath"></a>**SchemaPath**: the handle a schema function receives for a place in the model; rules attach to it and it holds no value ([26 §4](modules/26-signal-forms.md#4-schema-rules-and-validation-built-in-rules-validate-and-reusable-schemas)).
- <a id="schemas-component"></a>**schemas (component)**: standalone-only metadata declaring which unknown elements and properties a template may contain ([15 §2](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#2-standalone-components-and-the-imports-array)).
- <a id="schematic"></a>**schematic**: a code generator or rewriter that edits a virtual file tree ([12 §2](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#2-the-cli-and-the-workspace-angularjson-projects-schematics-and-ng-update)). · also in [18 §8](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#8-migrating-with-ng-generate-angularcorecontrol-flow), [29 §3](modules/29-testing-angular.md#3-karma-jasmine-and-jest-the-legacy-and-alternative-runners-and-migrating-to-vitest)
- <a id="scope"></a>**scope**: who owns a project's code, such as `orders`, `catalog` or `shared` ([38 §4](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#4-layers-library-types-and-dependency-rules))
- <a id="scope-angularjs"></a>**scope (AngularJS)**: a plain object that holds the model for a part of the page ([39 §1](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#1-angularjs-scopes-watchers-and-the-digest-cycle))
- <a id="scope-oauth"></a>**scope (OAuth)**: a permission granted to the client, carried in a token's `scope` or `scp` claim ([42 §5](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#5-spring-security-as-a-resource-server-validating-jwts-and-turning-scopes-into-authorities))
- <a id="scope-service-worker"></a>**scope (service worker)**: the URLs a registered worker may control ([37 §4](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#4-angularservice-worker-registration-ngsw-configjson-and-the-generated-ngswjson))
- <a id="scope-template"></a>**scope (template)**: the region of a template where a name can be read; each block opens a nested scope that reads outer names, never the reverse ([13 §6](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#6-template-reference-variables-and-let)).
- <a id="scope-transloco"></a>**scope (Transloco)**: a named slice of translations, requested as `<scope>/<lang>` when a component that provides it renders, and merged under its alias ([34 §9](modules/34-i18n.md#9-runtime-translation-libraries-transloco-and-ngx-translate-lazy-scopes-and-the-trade-off-against-built-in-i18n))
- <a id="scope-chain"></a>**scope chain**: the links from an environment record to its outer environments, walked outward during lookup ([02 §1](modules/02-js-scope-closures-this.md#1-lexical-scope-and-the-scope-chain)).
- <a id="scope_-prefix"></a>**SCOPE_ prefix**: the prefix Spring Security's default converter puts before each scope to make an authority ([42 §5](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#5-spring-security-as-a-resource-server-validating-jwts-and-turning-scopes-into-authorities))
- <a id="scoped-proxy"></a>**scoped proxy**: Spring's stand-in injected into a longer-lived bean that fetches the real shorter-lived bean from its scope; Spring term, documented only ([16 §9](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#9-di-patterns-testing-and-the-spring-container)).
- <a id="screen-reader"></a>**screen reader**: assistive technology that speaks or brailles the accessibility tree, such as NVDA, VoiceOver, TalkBack or Narrator ([11 §7](modules/11-accessibility.md#7-testing-accessibility)).
- <a id="scroll-container"></a>**scroll container**: a box with `overflow` `hidden`, `scroll` or `auto`; its flex-item automatic minimum size is zero ([10 §3](modules/10-css-essentials.md#3-flexbox-grid-and-subgrid)).
- <a id="scroll-event"></a>**Scroll event**: the router event emitted after `NavigationEnd` carrying the restored position (back or forward only) and the fragment anchor ([24 §9](modules/24-routing.md#9-view-transitions-scroll-restoration-and-titles))
- <a id="scroll-position"></a>**scroll position**: Spring Data's `ScrollPosition`, the place in a query result after which the next scroll step starts (the position is exclusive) ([41 §6](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#6-pagination-sorting-and-filtering-offset-versus-cursor-and-how-spring-data-serializes-a-page))
- <a id="scroll-strategy"></a>**scroll strategy**: the object that decides what scrolling does while an overlay is open: reposition, block, close or nothing ([36 §5](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#5-portals-and-overlays-where-floating-ui-renders-how-it-is-positioned-and-how-it-closes))
- <a id="seam"></a>**seam**: the place where one implementation can be swapped for another; in Angular, dependency injection ([29 §7](modules/29-testing-angular.md#7-test-doubles-fakes-stubs-spies-and-mocks-vifn-provider-overrides-and-over-mocking))
- <a id="searchresult"></a>**SearchResult**: the exercise's result row (`id`, `title`) whose hand-written guard is passed to `httpResource` as `parse`, so a wrong shape puts the resource in the error state ([27 Exercise 27.6](modules/27-http-client.md#ex27-6))
- <a id="sec-fetch-site"></a>**Sec-Fetch-Site**: the Fetch Metadata header whose value is `same-origin`, `same-site`, `none` or `cross-site` ([33 §9](modules/33-security.md#9-csrf-in-single-page-apps-when-cookies-make-you-vulnerable-samesite-and-angulars-xsrf-support))
- <a id="secondary-entry-point"></a>**secondary entry point**: a library sub-folder with its own `ng-package.json`, bundle and subpath ([38 §6](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#6-public-api-surfaces-public-apits-exports-secondary-entry-points-and-versioning))
- <a id="secure-context"></a>**secure context**: a window or worker that meets minimum authentication and confidentiality standards, which the async Clipboard API requires ([36 §8](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#8-the-smaller-primitives-the-stepper-breakpointobserver-and-clipboard))
- <a id="security-context"></a>**security context**: the kind of place a bound value lands (HTML, style, URL, resource URL), which decides the rule Angular applies ([33 §3](modules/33-security.md#3-sanitization-by-security-context-html-style-url-and-resource-url))
- <a id="security-schema"></a>**security schema**: the compiler's table (`SECURITY_SCHEMA`, internal) from an element and property to a security context, looked up once per binding at compile time ([33 §12](modules/33-security.md#12-under-the-hood-from-a-template-binding-to-a-sanitized-dom-write))
- <a id="seed"></a>**seed**: the known data a reset writes before a test ([30 §8](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#8-ci-strategy-for-end-to-end-tests-workers-sharding-retries-policy-and-test-data))
- <a id="segment-group"></a>**segment group**: a node of a `UrlTree` holding the segments of one outlet (the primary group for ordinary URLs) ([24 §2](modules/24-routing.md#2-route-parameters-path-matrix-query-and-fragment-inheritance-and-component-input-binding))
- <a id="select"></a>**select**: the selector (element, attribute or class) a node must match to enter an `<ng-content>` slot ([19 §3](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#3-content-projection-ng-content-slots-ngprojectas-fallback-content-and-the-lifecycle-trap))
- <a id="select-store"></a>**select (store)**: a method that turns a state-picking function into a stream built from `map` and `distinctUntilChanged` ([28 §3](modules/28-state-management.md#3-the-service-with-behaviorsubject-store-the-legacy-but-common-pattern-and-its-migration))
- <a id="select-expression"></a>**select expression**: an ICU expression that picks a branch by the value of a string, else `other` ([34 §5](modules/34-i18n.md#5-icu-expressions-plural-select-and-nesting))
- <a id="selection-of-change-detection"></a>**selection (of change detection)**: which views a notification refreshes, decided by the strategy and the dirty marks ([21 §1](modules/21-change-detection.md#1-the-synchronization-problem-dirty-checking-and-fine-grained-reactivity))
- <a id="selection-mode"></a>**selection mode**: the Angular Aria input that makes moving select (`follow`, the default) or leaves selection to Space and Enter (`explicit`) ([36 §11](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#11-angular-aria-headless-signal-driven-aria-patterns))
- <a id="selector"></a>**selector**: the restricted CSS pattern (element, attribute or class, `:not()`, comma lists) that picks the elements a component is bound to ([13 §1](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#1-component-anatomy-selector-template-styles-imports-and-the-v22-file-convention)).
- <a id="selector-ngrx-store"></a>**selector (NgRx store)**: a memoized pure function from the state tree to a value, built with `createSelector` or `createFeatureSelector` ([28 §5](modules/28-state-management.md#5-selectors-memoization-composition-and-reading-the-store-as-signals))
- <a id="selector-signal-store"></a>**selector (signal store)**: a `computed` over a store's state that components read; it re-runs after every write to the state it reads, and its dependants stop when its result is equal ([28 §2](modules/28-state-management.md#2-the-service-with-signals-store-state-shape-derived-selectors-methods-and-async-loads))
- <a id="selector-factory"></a>**selector factory**: a function such as `(id) => createSelector(...)` that returns one selector (one memo) per argument; it replaces selectors with props ([28 §5](modules/28-state-management.md#5-selectors-memoization-composition-and-reading-the-store-as-signals))
- <a id="selectsignal"></a>**selectSignal**: `Store.selectSignal(selector)`, a `computed` over the store's state signal ([28 §5](modules/28-state-management.md#5-selectors-memoization-composition-and-reading-the-store-as-signals))
- <a id="self-contained-token"></a>**self-contained token**: a token that carries its own claims and a signature, so any instance can check it without a lookup, such as a JWT ([42 §1](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#1-sessions-versus-tokens-what-the-server-keeps-what-each-request-carries-revocation-and-scaling))
- <a id="semantic-rule"></a>**semantic rule**: a validation rule that needs server data, such as uniqueness or stock, so only the server can decide it ([41 §7](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#7-validation-on-both-layers-bean-validation-angular-validators-and-server-errors-on-form-fields))
- <a id="semantic-versioning"></a>**semantic versioning**: major.minor.patch numbers where a breaking change needs a new major ([38 §6](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#6-public-api-surfaces-public-apits-exports-secondary-entry-points-and-versioning)) · also in [39 §3](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#3-how-angular-ships-semantic-versioning-the-release-cadence-support-windows-and-the-deprecation-policy)
- <a id="semver-semantic-versioning"></a>**SemVer (semantic versioning)**: a version-numbering contract in which breaking changes come only in major versions ([12 Q12.25](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#q12-25)).
- <a id="semver-range"></a>**semver range**: a condition on version numbers, such as `>21.2.3 <=22.2.1`, that a version satisfies or not ([39 §12](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#12-under-the-hood-how-ng-update-selects-migrations-and-how-the-update-guide-builds-its-checklist))
- <a id="send-buffer"></a>**send buffer**: the per-session queue where Spring keeps outgoing messages while another send to that client is in progress ([43 §9](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#9-scaling-out-and-under-the-hood-sticky-sessions-the-broker-relay-backpressure-and-one-message-traced-across-two-instances))
- <a id="send-time-limit"></a>**send time limit**: how long one send to a client may take (10 s by default) before Spring closes the session ([43 §9](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#9-scaling-out-and-under-the-hood-sticky-sessions-the-broker-relay-backpressure-and-one-message-traced-across-two-instances))
- <a id="sender-constrained-token"></a>**sender-constrained token**: a token bound to a key the client holds, so a copy without the key is useless ([42 §4](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#4-refresh-tokens-rotation-reuse-detection-silent-renew-and-refreshing-across-tabs))
- <a id="sensitivity-analysis"></a>**sensitivity analysis**: checking which plausible weight change would flip the winner of a decision ([40 §8](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#8-choosing-a-framework-balanced-trade-offs-and-how-to-answer-why-angular))
- <a id="sentinel"></a>**sentinel**: an empty element after the last item whose entering the viewport triggers the next load ([46 §3](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#3-an-infinite-scroll-feed-cursor-pages-deduplication-new-items-without-a-jump-and-a-bounded-window))
- <a id="sequence-number"></a>**sequence number**: the server's counter on each delta, which lets the client ignore what the snapshot holds and notice what is missing ([43 §8](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#8-from-streams-to-signals-one-shared-connection-tosignal-and-rxresource-bursts-and-connection-state))
- <a id="serialization-ssr"></a>**serialization (SSR)**: the last server step, writing into the HTML what hydration needs: the integrity marker, `ngh` annotations, the event replay call, `ng-server-context` and the state script ([32 §10](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#10-under-the-hood-a-request-through-the-engine-the-serialized-page-and-the-clients-hydration-pass))
- <a id="server-app"></a>**server app**: the internal `@angular/ssr` object (`AngularServerApp`) that holds one build's server routes, HTML templates and bootstrap function and answers a request by serving, sending the shell or rendering ([32 §10](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#10-under-the-hood-a-request-through-the-engine-the-serialized-page-and-the-clients-hydration-pass))
- <a id="server-cache"></a>**server cache**: a copy of data the server owns, fetched, kept for a while and invalidated; not client state ([28 §1](modules/28-state-management.md#1-kinds-of-state-server-cache-ui-form-url-and-session-state-and-where-each-belongs))
- <a id="server-config"></a>**server config**: `app.config.server.ts`, which merges server-only providers into the shared application config ([32 §2](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#2-adding-angularssr-what-ng-add-generates-the-build-output-and-the-server-entry))
- <a id="server-context-attribute"></a>**server context attribute**: `ng-server-context` on the root element, `ssg` for a prerendered page and `ssr` for a server-rendered one; a client-rendered shell has none ([32 §10](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#10-under-the-hood-a-request-through-the-engine-the-serialized-page-and-the-clients-hydration-pass))
- <a id="server-entry-point"></a>**server entry point**: the file the server bundle's application starts from, `main.server.ts` in a generated project ([32 §2](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#2-adding-angularssr-what-ng-add-generates-the-build-output-and-the-server-entry))
- <a id="server-error-on-a-field"></a>**server error on a field**: a validation error the server returns for one field, shown under that field's control ([26 §7](modules/26-signal-forms.md#7-submission-submit-formroot-submitting-and-server-errors); [41 §7](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#7-validation-on-both-layers-bean-validation-angular-validators-and-server-errors-on-form-fields))
- <a id="server-platform"></a>**server platform**: the platform Angular creates to render on Node: it provides the `'server'` platform ID, a DOM emulation and the server-side services ([32 §7](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#7-platform-safe-code-browser-apis-afternextrender-document-request-and-response_init))
- <a id="server-push"></a>**Server Push**: an HTTP/2 feature letting the server send resources the client has not requested; Chrome disabled it by default in 106 ([09 §3](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#3-http11-http2-and-http3)).
- <a id="server-route"></a>**server route**: an entry of the `ServerRoute[]` given to `withRoutes` that names a router path and the `renderMode` that path renders with ([32 §3](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#3-server-routes-and-rendermode-per-route-rendering-getprerenderparams-fallbacks-headers-and-status))
- <a id="server-session"></a>**server session**: a login the server keeps as a record, with the browser holding only the session id ([42 §1](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#1-sessions-versus-tokens-what-the-server-keeps-what-each-request-carries-revocation-and-scaling))
- <a id="server-stamp"></a>**server stamp**: a value the server makes once per render and the browser reuses instead of making its own, carried by `TransferState` or a `resource` with an `id` ([32 Exercise 32.3](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#ex32-3))
- <a id="server-cache-library"></a>**server-cache library**: a library that fetches, caches by key and refreshes server data, such as TanStack Query ([28 §11](modules/28-state-management.md#11-alternatives-and-the-decision-framework-ngxs-elf-tanstack-query-and-when-a-store-library-is-justified))
