- <a id="server-sent-events-sse"></a>**Server-Sent Events (SSE)**: one HTTP response the server keeps open and writes text events into, one way ([43 §1](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#1-choosing-a-transport-polling-long-polling-server-sent-events-websockets-and-web-push-when-no-tab-is-open))
- <a id="server-side-filtering"></a>**server-side filtering**: sending the filters, sort and page size to the server, which returns one page, because the browser holds only that page ([46 §4](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#4-a-large-data-grid-server-side-filtering-sorting-and-pagination-the-query-in-the-url-and-keyset-cursors))
- <a id="serversentevent"></a>**ServerSentEvent**: WebFlux's type for one event, with builder methods for `id`, `event`, `retry`, `comment` and `data` ([43 §3](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#3-server-sent-events-in-spring-sseemitter-in-spring-mvc-and-a-flux-of-serversentevent-in-webflux))
- <a id="service"></a>**service**: a class Angular injects into components and other classes ([19 §2](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#2-sharing-state-beyond-the-tree-services-with-signals-and-di-scoped-state))
- <a id="service-worker"></a>**service worker**: a script that runs between page and network with its own lifecycle and no DOM access, able to answer requests from a cache ([09 §9](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#9-service-workers-and-the-pwa-manifest)). · also in [37 §4](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#4-angularservice-worker-registration-ngsw-configjson-and-the-generated-ngswjson)
- <a id="service-worker-scope"></a>**service worker scope**: the URLs a worker controls, by default its script's directory ([09 §9](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#9-service-workers-and-the-pwa-manifest)).
- <a id="service-with-signals-store"></a>**service-with-signals store**: a service with private state in a signal, `computed` selectors and methods as the only writers ([38 §3](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#3-facades-and-the-data-access-layer))
- <a id="session-cookie"></a>**session cookie**: a cookie holding an id the server maps to a signed-in user ([33 §8](modules/33-security.md#8-where-the-token-lives-memory-web-storage-httponly-cookies-and-a-backend-for-frontend)) · also in [42 §3](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#3-where-the-oauth-client-runs-a-browser-only-client-a-token-mediating-backend-or-a-backend-for-frontend-with-spring)
- <a id="session-fixation"></a>**session fixation**: an attack that plants a session id before the victim signs in ([42 §9](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#9-under-the-hood-one-bff-login-and-the-first-api-call-through-spring-securitys-filters))
- <a id="session-id"></a>**session id**: the key to a server session, held by the browser in a cookie and sent with every request ([42 §1](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#1-sessions-versus-tokens-what-the-server-keeps-what-each-request-carries-revocation-and-scaling))
- <a id="session-state"></a>**session state**: user, tokens and preferences for this tab or device, held in a root service and optionally mirrored to storage ([28 §1](modules/28-state-management.md#1-kinds-of-state-server-cache-ui-form-url-and-session-state-and-where-each-belongs))
- <a id="session-window-cls"></a>**session window (CLS)**: a run of layout shifts less than 1 s apart, at most 5 s long; CLS reports the largest window ([08 §6](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#6-core-web-vitals-lcp-inp-and-cls)).
- <a id="setinput"></a>**setInput**: `ComponentRef.setInput`, which sets a component input from code and marks the component's view ([21 §2](modules/21-change-detection.md#2-onpush-and-eager-strategies-and-what-marks-a-view-dirty)) · also in [29 §6](modules/29-testing-angular.md#6-component-tests-dom-queries-componentfixture-signal-inputs-with-setinput-and-bindings-outputs-and-onpush)
- <a id="settled--resolved"></a>**settled / resolved**: a settled promise is fulfilled or rejected; a resolved promise has its fate fixed, possibly by following another promise that is still pending ([04 §3](modules/04-js-async-event-loop.md#3-promises-from-callbacks-to-a-state-machine)).
- <a id="shadow-dom"></a>**Shadow DOM**: the browser's encapsulated DOM subtree, which the native `<slot>` belongs to ([19 §3](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#3-content-projection-ng-content-slots-ngprojectas-fallback-content-and-the-lifecycle-trap))
- <a id="shadow-root--light-dom"></a>**shadow root / light DOM**: the private subtree attached to an element, with its own style scope, versus the element's ordinary children ([08 §5](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#5-web-components-custom-elements-shadow-dom-and-templates)).
- <a id="shadowcss"></a>**ShadowCss**: the private compiler class that rewrites a component's CSS for emulated encapsulation ([13 §9](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#9-view-encapsulation-emulated-shadowdom-and-none-and-what-the-compiler-does-to-your-css)).
- <a id="shadowing"></a>**shadowing**: an own property hiding a property of the same name further up the chain ([03 §2](modules/03-js-objects-prototypes-classes.md#2-the-prototype-chain)).
- <a id="shallow-copy--deep-copy"></a>**shallow copy / deep copy**: copying one level of properties and sharing nested objects, versus copying every level ([03 §6](modules/03-js-objects-prototypes-classes.md#6-copying-shallow-deep-and-structured)).
- <a id="shallow-equality"></a>**shallow equality**: same keys, each value equal by `Object.is` ([40 §3](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#3-reacts-model-re-rendering-reconciliation-memoization-and-the-react-compiler))
- <a id="shallow-test"></a>**shallow test**: a component test that replaces children with stubs, for example through `TestBed.overrideComponent` ([29 §1](modules/29-testing-angular.md#1-what-to-test-where-unit-integration-component-and-end-to-end-tests-the-pyramid-and-the-trophy))
- <a id="shape-hidden-class"></a>**shape (hidden class)**: the engine's internal record of which properties an object has, used to optimize property access; changing an object's prototype invalidates it ([03 §2](modules/03-js-objects-prototypes-classes.md#2-the-prototype-chain)).
- <a id="shard"></a>**shard**: the slice of a test suite that one CI machine runs, chosen with `--shard=current/total` ([30 §8](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#8-ci-strategy-for-end-to-end-tests-workers-sharding-retries-policy-and-test-data))
- <a id="shareable-state"></a>**shareable state**: state a shared link or the back button must restore ([45 §5](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#5-state-and-api-design-where-each-piece-of-state-lives-and-the-api-the-screens-need))
- <a id="shared-folder"></a>**shared (folder)**: code with no feature knowledge, such as ui primitives, pipes and utilities ([38 §1](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#1-folder-structures-that-scale-by-feature-domain-slices-and-core-shared-and-feature-boundaries))
- <a id="shared-cache"></a>**shared cache**: a cache between the server and many users (a CDN or reverse proxy) that can give one stored response to everyone ([32 §9](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#9-deploying-ssr-node-and-other-runtimes-static-output-caching-rendered-pages-and-allowed-hosts)) · also in [45 §7](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#7-rendering-strategy-and-caching-layers-a-rendering-choice-per-route-and-every-cache-from-the-cdn-to-the-component)
- <a id="shared-cache--cdn"></a>**shared cache / CDN**: a cache that serves many users (a proxy or a content delivery network), unlike a browser's private cache ([09 §4](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#4-http-caching)).
- <a id="shared-dependency"></a>**shared dependency**: a package host and remotes agree to load once instead of bundling each ([38 §8](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#8-micro-frontends-module-federation-native-federation-and-when-they-are-justified))
- <a id="shared-element"></a>**shared element**: elements on the old and new pages with the same `view-transition-name`, which morph into each other ([35 §10](modules/35-animations.md#10-view-transitions-with-the-router-withviewtransitions-view-transition-name-and-onviewtransitioncreated))
- <a id="sharedmodule"></a>**SharedModule**: the habit of one NgModule that declares and re-exports the declarables every feature needs; giving it providers was the anti-pattern ([15 §5](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#5-legacy-ngmodule-patterns-declarations-exports-forrootforchild-sharedmodule-and-coremodule)).
- <a id="shell-form"></a>**shell form**: a Dockerfile `CMD` or `ENTRYPOINT` written as a plain string, run under `/bin/sh -c`, which does not pass signals on ([44 §6](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#6-serving-ssr-apps-in-containers-the-node-server-as-pid-1-graceful-shutdown-health-probes-and-what-nginx-or-a-cdn-still-serves))
- <a id="short-circuit-interceptor"></a>**short-circuit (interceptor)**: returning a response from an interceptor without calling `next`, so the backend is never reached ([27 §4](modules/27-http-client.md#4-interceptors-the-functional-chain-order-and-next-and-class-interceptors-through-withinterceptorsfromdi))
- <a id="side-effect"></a>**side effect**: a change to anything outside the expression being evaluated, such as assignment or `++`; template expressions may not have one ([13 §2](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#2-interpolation-and-template-expressions-what-is-allowed)).
- <a id="side-effect-module"></a>**side effect (module)**: a top-level statement that does something when its file loads, such as writing to a global ([31 §6](modules/31-performance.md#6-bundle-analysis-budgets-and-tree-shaking-pitfalls))
- <a id="side-effect-import"></a>**side-effect import**: `import './file.css'`, an import with no bindings; checked in 6.0 by `noUncheckedSideEffectImports` ([06 §7](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#7-tsconfig-the-strict-family-module-settings-and-typescript-60-defaults)).
- <a id="sideeffects-library-evaluation"></a>**sideEffects (library evaluation)**: a `package.json` hint that a package's files are "pure" and "safe to prune if unused" (webpack); `@angular/elements` and `@angular/service-worker` set it to `false` ([37 §12](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#12-the-production-ecosystem-and-how-to-evaluate-a-library))
- <a id="sideeffects-field"></a>**sideEffects field**: a `package.json` hint telling bundlers which files can be dropped when nothing imported from them is used ([05 §2](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#2-commonjs-interop-and-dynamic-import)).
- <a id="sigkill"></a>**SIGKILL**: the signal that ends a process at once; it cannot be caught, so nothing drains ([44 §6](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#6-serving-ssr-apps-in-containers-the-node-server-as-pid-1-graceful-shutdown-health-probes-and-what-nginx-or-a-cdn-still-serves))
- <a id="signal"></a>**signal**: a getter function with a reactive node attached, which records who reads it and notifies them when its value changes ([17 §1](modules/17-signals.md#1-why-signals-exist)). · also in [39 §6](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#6-the-version-matrix-part-3-v16-to-v22-from-signals-to-onpush-by-default), [17 §1](modules/17-signals.md#1-why-signals-exist)
- <a id="signal-process"></a>**signal (process)**: a notice the kernel delivers to a process, such as SIGTERM or SIGKILL; a process may handle, ignore or take the default action ([44 §6](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#6-serving-ssr-apps-in-containers-the-node-server-as-pid-1-graceful-shutdown-health-probes-and-what-nginx-or-a-cdn-still-serves))
- <a id="signal-telemetry"></a>**signal (telemetry)**: the data that reports a failure, such as an error report, a trace or a count ([45 §8](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#8-under-the-hood-failure-modes-observability-offline-levels-and-consistency-choices))
- <a id="signal-forms"></a>**Signal Forms**: the third Angular forms API (`@angular/forms/signals`, stable since 22.0): a form built from a writable model signal with `form()` ([26 §1](modules/26-signal-forms.md#1-why-a-third-forms-api-model-first-signals-end-to-end)) · also in [39 §6](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#6-the-version-matrix-part-3-v16-to-v22-from-signals-to-onpush-by-default)
- <a id="signal-input"></a>**signal input**: an input declared with `input()` that the component reads as a signal ([38 §2](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#2-smart-container-and-presentational-components))
- <a id="signal-input--model-input"></a>**signal input / model input**: a read-only `InputSignal` Angular writes from the parent's binding, versus a writable `ModelSignal` that also creates an `xChange` output for `[(x)]` ([17 §7](modules/17-signals.md#7-signal-based-component-apis)).
- <a id="signal-query"></a>**signal query**: `viewChild`, `viewChildren`, `contentChild` or `contentChildren`, which return signals that update as the view changes ([17 §7](modules/17-signals.md#7-signal-based-component-apis)).
- <a id="signalformcontrol"></a>**SignalFormControl**: an `AbstractControl` created from a raw value and a schema, backed by a field tree (`fieldTree`, `sourceValue`), so a `FormGroup` can contain it ([26 §9](modules/26-signal-forms.md#9-interop-and-migration-compatform-signalformcontrol-and-what-changed-from-v21-to-v22)).
- <a id="signalstate"></a>**signalState**: the state container without a class or features, a plain object of signals ([28 §8](modules/28-state-management.md#8-componentstore-and-signalstore-signalstate-signalstore-withstate-withcomputed-withmethods-and-protected-state))
- <a id="signalstore-and-store-feature"></a>**SignalStore** and **store feature**: the signal-based store of `@ngrx/signals`, an injectable class that `signalStore(...)` assembles from functions (`withState`, `withComputed`, `withMethods`, `withHooks`, `withProps`) that each add members ([28 §8](modules/28-state-management.md#8-componentstore-and-signalstore-signalstate-signalstore-withstate-withcomputed-withmethods-and-protected-state))
- <a id="signature"></a>**signature**: proof that the holder of the issuer's key wrote a token's header and payload; a changed byte fails the check ([42 §1](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#1-sessions-versus-tokens-what-the-server-keeps-what-each-request-carries-revocation-and-scaling))
- <a id="sigterm"></a>**SIGTERM**: the signal that asks a process to stop; a server can catch it and shut down gracefully ([44 §6](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#6-serving-ssr-apps-in-containers-the-node-server-as-pid-1-graceful-shutdown-health-probes-and-what-nginx-or-a-cdn-still-serves))
- <a id="silence-debouncetime"></a>**silence (debounceTime)**: a stretch of `d` with no new value, after which `debounceTime(d)` emits ([22 §8](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#8-timing-operators-debouncetime-throttletime-audittime-and-sampletime))
- <a id="silent-renew"></a>**silent renew**: rerunning the authorization request in a hidden iframe on the identity provider's session cookie to get new tokens without user interaction ([42 §4](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#4-refresh-tokens-rotation-reuse-detection-silent-renew-and-refreshing-across-tabs))
- <a id="silent-update"></a>**silent update**: a state change that no notification announces, so no view is scheduled to refresh ([21 §6](modules/21-change-detection.md#6-migrating-a-zone-based-app-to-zoneless))
- <a id="simple-broker"></a>**simple broker**: Spring's in-memory STOMP broker: it stores subscriptions and broadcasts, with no acks or receipts and no second instance ([43 §5](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#5-websockets-and-stomp-in-spring-the-handshake-frames-messagemapping-brokers-user-destinations-and-heart-beats))
- <a id="simplechange"></a>**SimpleChange**: the record of one input change: `previousValue`, `currentValue` and `firstChange` ([20 §2](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#2-constructor-ngoninit-and-inputs-what-exists-when))
- <a id="simplechanges"></a>**SimpleChanges**: the object `ngOnChanges` receives, keyed by input property name, one `SimpleChange` per changed input ([20 §2](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#2-constructor-ngoninit-and-inputs-what-exists-when))
- <a id="simulated-throttling"></a>**simulated throttling**: computing a load's timing from a recorded trace instead of slowing the real network ([45 §3](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#3-requirements-first-functional-and-non-functional-requirements-constraints-and-back-of-envelope-numbers))
- <a id="single-flight"></a>**single flight**: keeping one refresh in flight so concurrent callers wait on it instead of starting their own ([27 §7](modules/27-http-client.md#7-authentication-attaching-tokens-refreshing-on-401-with-a-request-queue-and-avoiding-refresh-loops))
- <a id="single-source-of-truth"></a>**single source of truth**: the one state signal of a feature store from which every selector derives, so no value is stored twice ([28 §2](modules/28-state-management.md#2-the-service-with-signals-store-state-shape-derived-selectors-methods-and-async-loads))
- <a id="single-file-component-sfc"></a>**single-file component (SFC)**: a `.vue` or `.svelte` file that keeps template, script and style together ([40 §2](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#2-templates-jsx-and-single-file-components-what-the-compiler-knows))
- <a id="single-page-application-spa"></a>**single-page application (SPA)**: an app that swaps content inside one document on navigation instead of loading a new page ([11 §5](modules/11-accessibility.md#5-focus-management-in-spas-and-live-regions)).
- <a id="single-pointer-alternative"></a>**single-pointer alternative**: a way to operate a drag with clicks or taps instead ([45 §6](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#6-performance-and-accessibility-as-design-steps-budgets-a-loading-plan-long-lists-and-keyboard-and-pointer-paths))
- <a id="singleton"></a>**singleton**: one instance shared by every consumer of an injector, because the injector caches it ([16 §1](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#1-why-dependency-injection-containers-constructor-injection-and-inject)). · also in [38 §8](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#8-micro-frontends-module-federation-native-federation-and-when-they-are-justified)
- <a id="sink"></a>**sink**: a DOM API that turns a string into markup or code (`innerHTML`, `eval`, `script.src`) ([09 §8](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#8-content-security-policy-and-trusted-types)).
- <a id="site"></a>**site**: the registrable domain (a public suffix plus one label); looser than an origin, and the unit cookies' `SameSite` uses ([09 §5](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#5-the-same-origin-policy-and-cors)).
- <a id="sizes"></a>**sizes**: the HTML attribute telling the browser the width the image will render at, so it can choose from a width-descriptor `srcset` ([31 §7](modules/31-performance.md#7-images-ngoptimizedimage-with-priority-fill-loaders-and-placeholders))
- <a id="skeleton"></a>**skeleton**: a placeholder that reserves a loading area's size ([45 §6](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#6-performance-and-accessibility-as-design-steps-budgets-a-loading-plan-long-lists-and-keyboard-and-pointer-paths))
- <a id="skip-link"></a>**skip link**: a link at the top of the page that moves focus past repeated content to the main content ([11 §4](modules/11-accessibility.md#4-keyboard-focus-order-roving-tabindex-and-focus-traps)).
- <a id="skip-predicate"></a>**skip predicate**: the function a key manager calls to decide whether an item is passed over, by default one that skips disabled items ([36 §9](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#9-cdk-accessibility-focustrap-focusmonitor-liveannouncer-and-the-key-managers))
- <a id="skip_auth"></a>**SKIP_AUTH**: an `HttpContextToken` that marks the refresh request so the auth interceptor leaves it alone ([27 §7](modules/27-http-client.md#7-authentication-attaching-tokens-refreshing-on-401-with-a-request-queue-and-avoiding-refresh-loops))
- <a id="sloppy-mode"></a>**sloppy mode**: non-strict code, the default for classic scripts; failed writes are silently ignored there ([01 §1](modules/01-js-values-types-coercion.md#1-values-and-types)).
- <a id="slot"></a>**slot**: a placeholder in a shadow tree that displays light DOM children, by name or by default ([08 §5](modules/08-browser-rendering-dom-events.md#5-web-components-custom-elements-shadow-dom-and-templates)).
- <a id="slot-content-projection"></a>**slot (content projection)**: one `<ng-content>` placeholder of a component ([19 §3](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#3-content-projection-ng-content-slots-ngprojectas-fallback-content-and-the-lifecycle-trap))
- <a id="slot-pipe"></a>**slot (pipe)**: the cell a view keeps for one pipe expression, holding its last arguments and result ([14 Q14.17](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#q14-17)).
- <a id="slot-view-storage"></a>**slot (view storage)**: a numbered position in a view's storage that holds a node or a binding's last value ([12 §8](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it)).
- <a id="smoke-test"></a>**smoke test**: a test that only checks that the built app starts and its main page loads ([30 §1](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#1-what-belongs-at-the-end-to-end-level-journeys-cost-and-a-strategy-across-levels))
- <a id="snapshot"></a>**snapshot**: the static image of the page (or of a named element) the browser takes before and after the update ([35 §10](modules/35-animations.md#10-view-transitions-with-the-router-withviewtransitions-view-transition-name-and-onviewtransitioncreated))
- <a id="snapshot-state"></a>**snapshot (state)**: the whole current state, loaded over HTTP, which later deltas are applied to ([43 §8](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#8-from-streams-to-signals-one-shared-connection-tosignal-and-rxresource-bursts-and-connection-state))
- <a id="sockjs"></a>**SockJS**: a fallback transport for networks or browsers without WebSocket, enabled in Spring with `withSockJS()`; legacy today ([43 §5](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#5-websockets-and-stomp-in-spring-the-handshake-frames-messagemapping-brokers-user-destinations-and-heart-beats))
- <a id="soft-redirect"></a>**soft redirect**: a redirect written as an HTML page with `<meta http-equiv="refresh">`, used where there is no response to carry a `Location` header ([32 §3](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#3-server-routes-and-rendermode-per-route-rendering-getprerenderparams-fallbacks-headers-and-status))
- <a id="soft-disabled"></a>**soft-disabled**: Angular Aria's default for disabled items: they stay focusable but cannot be selected or activated ([36 §11](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#11-angular-aria-headless-signal-driven-aria-patterns))
- <a id="source-file"></a>**source file**: the file extraction writes, `messages.xlf` by default, with one entry per message in the source locale ([34 §6](modules/34-i18n.md#6-extraction-and-translation-files-ng-extract-i18n-formats-and-message-ids))
- <a id="source-locale"></a>**source locale**: the locale an app's templates and code are written in; `en-US` unless `i18n.sourceLocale` sets another ([34 §1](modules/34-i18n.md#1-i18n-l10n-and-the-locale-language-tags-what-a-locale-decides-and-locale_id))
- <a id="source-map"></a>**source map**: a file mapping positions in minified output back to source files ([31 §6](modules/31-performance.md#6-bundle-analysis-budgets-and-tree-shaking-pitfalls))
- <a id="source-of-truth"></a>**source of truth**: the one place whose value wins when copies of the same data disagree ([28 §1](modules/28-state-management.md#1-kinds-of-state-server-cache-ui-form-url-and-session-state-and-where-each-belongs)) · also in [45 §5](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#5-state-and-api-design-where-each-piece-of-state-lives-and-the-api-the-screens-need)
- <a id="source-map-upload"></a>**source-map upload**: sending the build's source maps to the error tracker under a release identifier ([44 §9](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#9-frontend-error-tracking-and-real-user-monitoring-releases-source-maps-beacons-and-sampling))
- <a id="spa-fallback"></a>**SPA fallback**: a server rule that answers app routes with `index.html` so the client router can handle deep links ([30 §1](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#1-what-belongs-at-the-end-to-end-level-journeys-cost-and-a-strategy-across-levels)) · also in [44 §3](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#3-nginx-for-a-single-page-app-location-matching-the-try_files-fallback-caching-headers-for-hashed-assets-and-gzip-or-brotli)
- <a id="spacer"></a>**spacer**: an empty element sized to the whole list so the scrollbar behaves as if every row existed ([31 §4](modules/31-performance.md#4-long-lists-track-virtual-scrolling-with-the-cdk-and-the-alternatives))
- <a id="span"></a>**span**: one timed operation in a trace, with its own span ID and the span ID of its caller ([44 §10](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#10-under-the-hood-opentelemetry-for-the-web-traceparent-and-one-click-traced-to-a-spring-boot-span-and-log-line))
- <a id="specificity"></a>**specificity**: the A-B-C weight (ids, classes/attributes/pseudo-classes, types/pseudo-elements) of a selector, compared column by column ([10 §2](modules/10-css-essentials.md#2-the-cascade-origins-specificity-and-layers)). · also in [13 §9](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#9-view-encapsulation-emulated-shadowdom-and-none-and-what-the-compiler-does-to-your-css)
- <a id="spike-library-evaluation"></a>**spike (library evaluation)**: a throwaway branch that adds a candidate library to your real build to measure it ([37 §12](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#12-the-production-ecosystem-and-how-to-evaluate-a-library))
- <a id="spring-session"></a>**Spring Session**: the Spring library that keeps HTTP sessions in a shared store such as Redis or a database table, so every instance reads them ([42 §1](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#1-sessions-versus-tokens-what-the-server-keeps-what-each-request-carries-revocation-and-scaling))
- <a id="springdoc"></a>**springdoc**: springdoc-openapi, the library that reads a Spring app's controllers and DTOs and publishes its OpenAPI description at `/v3/api-docs` ([41 §3](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#3-openapi-with-springdoc-and-generated-typescript-clients-openapi-generator-orval-and-ng-openapi-gen))
- <a id="spy"></a>**spy**: a stub that also records how it was called; `vi.fn()` and `vi.spyOn` create spies ([29 §7](modules/29-testing-angular.md#7-test-doubles-fakes-stubs-spies-and-mocks-vifn-provider-overrides-and-over-mocking))
- <a id="spy-object"></a>**spy object**: a stand-in whose methods are all spies; Jasmine builds one with `createSpyObj`, the migration writes an object of `vi.fn()` calls ([29 §3](modules/29-testing-angular.md#3-karma-jasmine-and-jest-the-legacy-and-alternative-runners-and-migrating-to-vitest))
- <a id="srcset"></a>**srcset**: the HTML attribute listing candidate image files for the browser to choose from ([31 §7](modules/31-performance.md#7-images-ngoptimizedimage-with-priority-fill-loaders-and-placeholders))
- <a id="sseemitter"></a>**SseEmitter**: Spring MVC's return value for streaming events: the controller returns it, and any thread later writes events into the open response ([43 §3](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#3-server-sent-events-in-spring-sseemitter-in-spring-mvc-and-a-flux-of-serversentevent-in-webflux))
- <a id="ssg-static-site-generation-prerendering"></a>**SSG (static site generation, prerendering)**: the HTML is rendered once at build time and served as a file ([31 §10](modules/31-performance.md#10-rendering-strategy-as-a-performance-decision-csr-ssr-and-ssg)) · also in [32 §1](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#1-why-render-on-the-server-csr-ssr-ssg-and-hybrid-rendering-and-what-each-costs)
- <a id="ssr-server-side-rendering"></a>**SSR (server-side rendering)**: the server renders the HTML for each request ([31 §10](modules/31-performance.md#10-rendering-strategy-as-a-performance-decision-csr-ssr-and-ssg)) · also in [32 §1](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#1-why-render-on-the-server-csr-ssr-ssg-and-hybrid-rendering-and-what-each-costs)
- <a id="ssrf-server-side-request-forgery"></a>**SSRF (server-side request forgery)**: an attack that makes a server send requests to an address the attacker chooses ([32 §9](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#9-deploying-ssr-node-and-other-runtimes-static-output-caching-rendered-pages-and-allowed-hosts), [33](modules/33-security.md)) · also in [33 §1](modules/33-security.md#1-threat-model-of-an-angular-single-page-app-what-the-attacker-controls-what-runs-where-and-where-each-defense-lives)
- <a id="stability--pending-task"></a>**stability / pending task**: the app is stable when no pending task is open; a pending task is a unit in the app's counter of unfinished work, added by `PendingTasks` and by the scheduler itself ([21 §5](modules/21-change-detection.md#5-zoneless-change-detection-the-scheduler-its-triggers-and-pendingtasks))
- <a id="stable"></a>**stable**: no pending task is left, so scheduled work has finished; `await fixture.whenStable()` waits for it ([29 §5](modules/29-testing-angular.md#5-zoneless-fixtures-whenstable-testbedtick-detectchanges-and-automatic-change-detection))
- <a id="stable-sort"></a>**stable sort**: a sort whose order is the same on every request, so pages and cursors do not repeat or skip items ([41 §6](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#6-pagination-sorting-and-filtering-offset-versus-cursor-and-how-spring-data-serializes-a-page))
- <a id="stackcontextmanager"></a>**StackContextManager**: the browser SDK's default context manager; the context is active only during a synchronous call, so it is lost after `await` ([44 §10](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#10-under-the-hood-opentelemetry-for-the-web-traceparent-and-one-click-traced-to-a-spring-boot-span-and-log-line))
- <a id="stacking-context"></a>**stacking context**: a self-contained layer painted as one unit in its parent context; `z-index` only orders siblings inside the same context ([10 §4](modules/10-css-essentials.md#4-positioning-stacking-contexts-and-z-index)).
- <a id="stage-27"></a>**Stage 2.7**: the TC39 stage "approved in principle and undergoing validation", between Stage 2 and Stage 3 ([07 §7](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#7-decorators-tc39-standard-versus-experimentaldecorators-and-what-angular-does-with-them)).
- <a id="staged-files"></a>**staged files**: the files added with `git add` for the next commit ([38 §9](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#9-linting-and-formatting-angular-eslint-prettier-import-ordering-and-commit-hooks))
- <a id="stagger"></a>**stagger**: delaying each item of a list a little more than the one before, to make a cascade; in CSS, an `animation-delay` computed from the item's index ([35 §7](modules/35-animations.md#7-animating-lists-for-enter-leave-reorder-and-stagger))
- <a id="stagger-legacy"></a>**stagger (legacy)**: a function that starts each queried element's animation a fixed step after the previous one; the engine builds it into longer keyframe timelines, not a `delay` ([35 §8](modules/35-animations.md#8-legacy-angularanimations-triggers-states-transitions-query-and-stagger))
- <a id="stale-view"></a>**stale (view)**: a view that shows an older value than the state it reads ([20 §6](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#6-expressionchangedafterithasbeencheckederror-cause-and-fixes))
- <a id="stale-data"></a>**stale data**: a replayed value from a run nobody was watching, delivered to a late subscriber as if current ([23 §5](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#5-multicasting-in-depth-share-configuration-sharereplay-and-connect))
- <a id="stale-limit"></a>**stale limit**: the silence after which a widget is marked stale while its value stays visible ([46 §6](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#6-a-real-time-operations-dashboard-aggregation-per-widget-staleness-hidden-tabs-and-screens-left-open-for-days))
- <a id="stale-response"></a>**stale response**: a response that arrives after a newer request has replaced the query it answered ([28 §2](modules/28-state-management.md#2-the-service-with-signals-store-state-shape-derived-selectors-methods-and-async-loads))
- <a id="stale-while-revalidate"></a>**stale-while-revalidate**: serving the stored value while a fresh one loads, and replacing it when it arrives ([23 §8](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#8-angular-patterns-typeahead-polling-cache-then-network-and-racing-requests)) · also in [28 §11](modules/28-state-management.md#11-alternatives-and-the-decision-framework-ngxs-elf-tanstack-query-and-when-a-store-library-is-justified)
- <a id="staleness"></a>**staleness**: how old an answer may be ([45 §7](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#7-rendering-strategy-and-caching-layers-a-rendering-choice-per-route-and-every-cache-from-the-cdn-to-the-component))
- <a id="standalone-component"></a>**standalone component**: a component that lists its own template dependencies in `imports` and needs no NgModule; the default since v19.0 ([15 §2](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#2-standalone-components-and-the-imports-array)). · also in [39 §10](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#10-a-migration-program-for-an-old-angular-codebase-order-dependencies-and-checkpoints)
- <a id="standalone-injector"></a>**standalone injector**: the environment injector Angular creates, once per component definition, for a standalone component created as a root view whose imports carry providers; a child of the current environment injector ([15 §6](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#6-interop-ngmodule-libraries-in-standalone-apps-and-standalone-pieces-in-ngmodule-apps)).
- <a id="standalone-migration-schematic"></a>**standalone migration (schematic)**: `ng generate @angular/core:standalone`, the schematic that converts an NgModule app in three passes (`convert-to-standalone`, `prune-ng-modules`, `standalone-bootstrap`) ([15 §7](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#7-migrating-with-ng-generate-angularcorestandalone-three-passes-hybrid-apps-and-common-failures)).
- <a id="standalone-false"></a>**standalone: false**: the opt-out that lets a component, directive or pipe be declared in an NgModule ([15 §2](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#2-standalone-components-and-the-imports-array)).
- <a id="standaloneservice"></a>**StandaloneService**: the internal service, one per environment injector, that creates and caches standalone injectors and destroys them with its injector ([15 §8](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-for-modules-and-standalone-components-and-the-standalone-injector)).
- <a id="standard-decorator--legacy-decorator"></a>**standard decorator / legacy decorator**: a TC39-proposal decorator called with `(value, context)` (TypeScript 5.0+, no parameter decorators), versus the older semantics enabled by `experimentalDecorators` ([07 §7](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#7-decorators-tc39-standard-versus-experimentaldecorators-and-what-angular-does-with-them)).
- <a id="standard-schema"></a>**Standard Schema**: the shared `~standard` object interface that validation libraries implement; `validateStandardSchema` turns its issues into field errors ([26 §4](modules/26-signal-forms.md#4-schema-rules-and-validation-built-in-rules-validate-and-reusable-schemas)).
- <a id="star"></a>**STAR**: situation, task, action, result; the shape of a story, with two sentences of situation and task, the action as the substance and the result last ([47 §1](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#1-the-behavioral-round-what-a-story-is-evidence-of-star-with-the-weight-on-action-and-an-explicit-result-and-a-story-bank))
- <a id="start-threshold"></a>**start threshold**: how far the pointer must move, as horizontal plus vertical distance (5 px by default), before the CDK turns a press into a drag ([36 §6](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#6-drag-and-drop-sorting-transferring-between-lists-and-the-keyboard-gap))
- <a id="starvation"></a>**starvation**: an endless chain of microtasks or a long synchronous block that keeps timers, I/O and rendering from ever running ([04 §8](modules/04-js-async-event-loop.md#8-unhandled-rejections-queuemicrotask-and-starvation)).
- <a id="starvation-polling"></a>**starvation (polling)**: no result ever arrives because every new trigger cancels the request the one before started; `switchMap` over a request slower than the tick period ([23 §8](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#8-angular-patterns-typeahead-polling-cache-then-network-and-racing-requests))
- <a id="state"></a>**state**: the data a screen depends on and that changes while the app runs ([28 §1](modules/28-state-management.md#1-kinds-of-state-server-cache-ui-form-url-and-session-state-and-where-each-belongs))
- <a id="state-oauth"></a>**state (OAuth)**: a random request value returned unchanged, tying the callback to the request ([42 §2](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#2-oauth-20-and-openid-connect-for-a-single-page-app-authorization-code-with-pkce-and-why-the-implicit-flow-is-gone))
- <a id="state-versus-event"></a>**state (versus event)**: a value that exists now and is read synchronously, which is what a signal holds ([23 §7](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#7-rxjs-and-signals-decision-rules-tosignal-toobservable-and-rxresource))
- <a id="state--property-aria"></a>**state / property (ARIA)**: an `aria-*` attribute for something that changes often (`aria-expanded`) or a relationship that rarely does (`aria-labelledby`) ([11 §3](modules/11-accessibility.md#3-aria-roles-states-properties-and-its-rules)).
- <a id="state-key"></a>**state key**: the string a value is stored under in `TransferState` and the state script; `makeStateKey` returns the string it is given ([32 §8](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#8-data-across-the-boundary-the-http-transfer-cache-transferstate-and-resource-ids))
- <a id="state-placement"></a>**state placement**: choosing the home of each piece of state ([45 §5](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#5-state-and-api-design-where-each-piece-of-state-lives-and-the-api-the-screens-need))
- <a id="state-script"></a>**state script**: the `<script id="ng-state" type="application/json">` element in the server HTML that holds the transferred JSON ([32 §8](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#8-data-across-the-boundary-the-http-transfer-cache-transferstate-and-resource-ids))
- <a id="state-transfer"></a>**state transfer**: handing a value from the server render to the browser by writing it into the page as JSON, which the browser reads during hydration ([32 §8](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#8-data-across-the-boundary-the-http-transfer-cache-transferstate-and-resource-ids))
- <a id="state_source-and-state-watcher"></a>**STATE_SOURCE** and **state watcher**: SignalStore's internal symbol holding one `WritableSignal` per top-level key, and a `watchState` callback that receives the whole state after each `patchState` ([28 §12](modules/28-state-management.md#12-under-the-hood-how-ngrx-store-reduces-actions-and-how-signalstore-keeps-state-in-signals))
- <a id="static-analysis"></a>**static analysis**: checking code without running it, such as the type checker and the linter ([29 §1](modules/29-testing-angular.md#1-what-to-test-where-unit-integration-component-and-end-to-end-tests-the-pyramid-and-the-trophy))
- <a id="static-hoisting"></a>**static hoisting**: the compiler marking a constant element so the renderer reuses it ([40 §2](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#2-templates-jsx-and-single-file-components-what-the-compiler-knows))
- <a id="static-output"></a>**static output**: the build from `outputMode: 'static'`, plain files with no server entry ([32 §9](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#9-deploying-ssr-node-and-other-runtimes-static-output-caching-rendered-pages-and-allowed-hosts))
- <a id="static-query"></a>**static query**: a decorator query option that resolves the result early, for an element outside any embedded view ([19 §6](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#6-decorator-based-inputs-outputs-and-queries-and-the-migration-schematics))
- <a id="static-context-paradigm"></a>**static-context paradigm**: client-side evaluation for one user, with the context set globally and asynchronously and evaluations synchronous afterwards ([44 §8](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#8-feature-flags-with-openfeature-and-canary-releases-providers-evaluation-context-sticky-rollout-kill-switches-and-version-skew))
- <a id="status-valid-invalid-pending-disabled"></a>**status (VALID, INVALID, PENDING, DISABLED)**: the one of four strings every control carries; the booleans `valid`, `invalid`, `pending` and `disabled` each match one of them ([25 §7](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#7-status-updateon-valuechanges-and-the-unified-events-stream))
- <a id="status-0"></a>**status 0**: the `status` of an `HttpErrorResponse` when no HTTP response was read: a network failure, a timeout or an aborted call ([27 §5](modules/27-http-client.md#5-errors-httperrorresponse-an-error-mapping-layer-and-retry-with-backoff-and-jitter))
- <a id="status-classes"></a>**status classes**: the `ng-valid`, `ng-dirty` style classes that reactive and template-driven forms add on their own and Signal Forms adds only when configured ([26 §3](modules/26-signal-forms.md#3-binding-with-formfield-native-inputs-attributes-classes-and-rendering)).
- <a id="status-message"></a>**status message**: WCAG 4.1.3's term for a change of status the user should learn of without focus moving ([11 §5](modules/11-accessibility.md#5-focus-management-in-spas-and-live-regions)).
- <a id="status-read"></a>**status read**: our lookup of a transfer by its idempotency key, used to settle an unknown outcome; the draft defines none ([46 §8](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#8-under-the-hood-a-money-transfer-with-idempotency-keys-optimistic-ui-that-never-claims-success-and-the-unknown-outcome))
- <a id="status-union"></a>**status union**: a union of literal strings (`idle`, `loading`, `loaded`, `error`) naming the phase of an async load ([28 §2](modules/28-state-management.md#2-the-service-with-signals-store-state-shape-derived-selectors-methods-and-async-loads))
- <a id="statuscode-resource-signal"></a>**statusCode (resource signal)**: the signal of the latest HTTP status of the resource's response, including an error response ([27 §10](modules/27-http-client.md#10-httpresource-signal-driven-requests-and-when-to-prefer-it-over-httpclient))
- <a id="step-control"></a>**step control**: the form control (`stepControl`) a stepper step checks for validity before a linear stepper lets the user leave it ([36 §8](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#8-the-smaller-primitives-the-stepper-breakpointobserver-and-clipboard))
- <a id="step-model"></a>**step model**: the data saying which steps exist, which are shown and which are valid ([46 §5](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#5-a-multi-step-form-wizard-with-draft-saving-a-step-model-per-step-validation-and-drafts-that-survive-and-migrate))
- <a id="sticky-assignment"></a>**sticky assignment**: a user keeps the same variant (or release) on every evaluation because it depends only on stable inputs ([44 §8](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#8-feature-flags-with-openfeature-and-canary-releases-providers-evaluation-context-sticky-rollout-kill-switches-and-version-skew))
- <a id="sticky-column"></a>**sticky column**: a column definition marked `sticky`, which stays in view when the table scrolls sideways ([36 §7](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#7-tables-and-long-lists-cdktable-datasource-mattable-and-virtual-scrolling))
- <a id="sticky-session"></a>**sticky session**: load-balancer routing that sends one client to the same instance every time, so that instance's memory holds the client's session ([42 §1](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#1-sessions-versus-tokens-what-the-server-keeps-what-each-request-carries-revocation-and-scaling)) · also in [43 §9](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#9-scaling-out-and-under-the-hood-sticky-sessions-the-broker-relay-backpressure-and-one-message-traced-across-two-instances)
- <a id="stomp"></a>**STOMP**: Simple Text Oriented Messaging Protocol; frames of a command, headers, a blank line, a body and a NULL octet, carried here inside WebSocket messages (STOMP 1.2) ([43 §5](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#5-websockets-and-stomp-in-spring-the-handshake-frames-messagemapping-brokers-user-destinations-and-heart-beats))
- <a id="stop-signal"></a>**stop signal**: the signal `docker stop` sends first, SIGTERM unless `STOPSIGNAL` or `--stop-signal` sets another ([44 §6](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#6-serving-ssr-apps-in-containers-the-node-server-as-pid-1-graceful-shutdown-health-probes-and-what-nginx-or-a-cdn-still-serves))
- <a id="stopimmediatepropagation"></a>**stopImmediatePropagation**: the DOM event method that stops the remaining listeners on the same element and the event's trip to ancestors ([14 Exercise 14.1](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#ex14-1)).
- <a id="store"></a>**store**: a container for some of the kinds of state, not a kind of state itself ([28 §1](modules/28-state-management.md#1-kinds-of-state-server-cache-ui-form-url-and-session-state-and-where-each-belongs))
- <a id="stored-xss"></a>**stored XSS**: cross-site scripting where the server saves the payload and serves it to other users ([33 §2](modules/33-security.md#2-xss-and-angulars-template-model-escaping-by-default-trusted-templates-and-the-paths-around-them))
- <a id="story"></a>**story**: one component rendered in one state, outside the app ([38 §10](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#10-storybook-for-component-development))
- <a id="story-bank"></a>**story bank**: our term for six to eight prepared stories, each tagged with the prompts it answers ([47 §1](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#1-the-behavioral-round-what-a-story-is-evidence-of-star-with-the-weight-on-action-and-an-explicit-result-and-a-story-bank))
- <a id="story-skeleton"></a>**story skeleton**: our term for the ordered beats one story type needs; section 2 holds the four (performance win, migration, incident, disagreement) ([47 §2](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#2-four-front-end-stories-a-performance-win-a-migration-a-production-incident-and-a-disagreement-over-architecture))
- <a id="strangler-fig"></a>**strangler fig**: a migration that grows a new system around the old one and moves traffic to it route by route until the old one can be removed ([39 §9](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#9-from-angularjs-to-angular-ngupgrade-hybrids-the-strangler-fig-and-rewrites))
- <a id="strategy"></a>**strategy**: one interface with interchangeable implementations behind a token; the injector picks one ([16 §9](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#9-di-patterns-testing-and-the-spring-container)).
- <a id="strategy-angular-elements"></a>**strategy (Angular Elements)**: the object behind an Angular element that creates, updates and destroys its component through `connect`, `disconnect`, `setInputValue`, `getInputValue` and `events` ([37 §13](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#13-under-the-hood-createcustomelements-strategy-and-the-service-workers-driver))
- <a id="strategy-change-detection"></a>**strategy (change detection)**: a component setting that says when its view is checked: `OnPush` only when marked, `Eager` whenever the walk reaches it ([21 §2](modules/21-change-detection.md#2-onpush-and-eager-strategies-and-what-marks-a-view-dirty))
- <a id="strategy-pattern"></a>**strategy (pattern)**: one interface with interchangeable algorithms; `AsyncPipe` picks a promise or subscribable strategy ([14 §8](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#8-the-async-pipe-under-the-hood)).
- <a id="strategy-factory-angular-elements"></a>**strategy factory (Angular Elements)**: the object that builds one strategy per element; replaceable through `createCustomElement`'s `strategyFactory` option ([37 §13](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#13-under-the-hood-createcustomelements-strategy-and-the-service-workers-driver))
- <a id="streaming-resource"></a>**streaming resource**: `resource({ stream })`, whose loader returns a signal of `{ value }` or `{ error }` items that can change over time ([43 §8](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#8-from-streams-to-signals-one-shared-connection-tosignal-and-rxresource-bursts-and-connection-state))
- <a id="streaming-response"></a>**streaming response**: a response whose body the server writes while it is produced, such as Spring's `StreamingResponseBody` ([41 §8](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#8-files-multipart-and-chunked-uploads-progress-and-downloads-with-content-disposition-and-streaming))
- <a id="strict-csp"></a>**strict CSP**: a Content Security Policy that trusts scripts by nonce or hash plus `'strict-dynamic'`, never by host ([33 §5](modules/33-security.md#5-content-security-policy-with-angular-nonces-csp_nonce-and-autocsp))
- <a id="strict-family"></a>**strict family**: the eight checks `strict` turns on in TypeScript 6.0 (`noImplicitAny`, `strictNullChecks`, …), each switchable on its own ([06 §7](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#7-tsconfig-the-strict-family-module-settings-and-typescript-60-defaults)).
- <a id="strict-mode"></a>**strict mode**: the language mode that turns silent failures into errors and leaves `this` undefined in plain calls; always on in modules and class bodies ([02 §5](modules/02-js-scope-closures-this.md#5-strict-mode)).
- <a id="strict-mode-locator"></a>**strict mode (locator)**: the rule that an action on a locator matching several elements throws instead of picking one ([30 §4](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#4-locators-page-objects-and-fixtures))
- <a id="strictstandalone"></a>**strictStandalone**: an `angularCompilerOptions` flag that rejects every `standalone: false` (`NG2023`) ([15 §2](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#2-standalone-components-and-the-imports-array)).
- <a id="stricttemplates"></a>**strictTemplates**: Angular's strictest template type-checking mode; it turns on `strictContextGenerics`, among other flags ([07 §9](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#9-typing-patterns-in-angular-code)). · also in [12 §7](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#7-aot-versus-jit-and-template-type-checking), [19 §1](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#1-inputs-outputs-and-two-way-binding-the-parent-and-child-contract)
- <a id="string-based-lazy-loading"></a>**string-based lazy loading**: a `loadChildren` string such as `path/to/module#ModuleName` that the build resolves ([39 §4](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#4-the-version-matrix-part-1-v2-to-v8))
- <a id="strip--strict--loose-object"></a>**strip / strict / loose object**: Zod's three unknown-key modes: drop unknown keys (the `z.object` default), reject them, or keep them ([07 §8](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#8-runtime-validation-with-zod-4-schema-first-types)).
- <a id="strong-focus-indicator"></a>**strong focus indicator**: an outline drawn on the focused element, switched on once with `mat.strong-focus-indicators()` ([36 §4](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#4-customizing-the-theme-system-and-component-token-overrides-and-theming-your-own-components))
- <a id="structural-directive"></a>**structural directive**: a directive on an `<ng-template>` that decides when, and how often, the template is stamped out; `*` is shorthand that wraps an element in the `<ng-template>` ([14 §2](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#2-structural-directives-ng-template-templateref-viewcontainerref-and-the-microsyntax)).
- <a id="structural-styles-cdk-overlay"></a>**structural styles (CDK overlay)**: the CDK's own CSS that places the overlay container, host and pane, with no look, loaded by `createOverlayRef` itself ([36 §13](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#13-under-the-hood-from-createoverlayref-to-a-popover-in-the-top-layer-and-from-mattheme-to-a-computed-color))
- <a id="structural-type"></a>**structural type**: a type matched by the members an object has, here `Subscribable<T>` (an object with `subscribe({ next, error })`), so the model needs no RxJS ([14 Exercise 14.5](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#ex14-5); concept owner [06 §2](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#2-structural-typing-assignability-and-readonly)).
- <a id="structural-typing"></a>**structural typing**: compatibility decided by members (shape), not by declared names ([06 §2](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#2-structural-typing-assignability-and-readonly)).
- <a id="structured-clone"></a>**structured clone**: the HTML algorithm behind `structuredClone` and `postMessage`, which copies cycles, `Date`, `Map` and `Set` but not functions, symbols or prototypes ([03 §6](modules/03-js-objects-prototypes-classes.md#6-copying-shallow-deep-and-structured)). · also in [09 §7](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#7-browser-storage-what-each-is-safe-for), [31 §9](modules/31-performance.md#9-off-the-main-thread-web-workers-in-angular-and-runoutsideangular-in-zone-apps)
- <a id="structured-field-string"></a>**structured-field String**: an RFC 9651 String, written in double quotes; the Idempotency-Key value must be one ([46 §8](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#8-under-the-hood-a-money-transfer-with-idempotency-keys-optimistic-ui-that-never-claims-success-and-the-unknown-outcome))
- <a id="stub"></a>**stub**: a test double that gives canned answers to calls made during the test ([29 §7](modules/29-testing-angular.md#7-test-doubles-fakes-stubs-spies-and-mocks-vifn-provider-overrides-and-over-mocking))
- <a id="stub-server"></a>**stub server**: a small fake backend served over HTTP that tests seed and reset ([30 §5](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#5-api-mocking-at-the-network-layer-and-where-contract-tests-take-over))
- <a id="subgrid"></a>**subgrid**: a nested grid whose rows or columns use the parent grid's tracks (`grid-template-columns: subgrid`) ([10 §3](modules/10-css-essentials.md#3-flexbox-grid-and-subgrid)).
- <a id="subject"></a>**Subject**: an Observable that is also an observer, so values can be pushed into it ([22 §3](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#3-cold-and-hot-unicast-and-multicast))
- <a id="subjectobserved"></a>**Subject.observed**: `true` while a Subject has at least one subscriber; a test can assert it to prove a subscription ended ([23 §6](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#6-unsubscription-strategies-in-angular-and-finding-leaks))
- <a id="submission-action"></a>**submission action**: the async function `submit()` runs on a valid form; it returns nothing for success or errors that name their target `fieldTree` ([26 §7](modules/26-signal-forms.md#7-submission-submit-formroot-submitting-and-server-errors)).
- <a id="submission-error"></a>**submission error**: an error returned by a submission action and attached to its target field; it clears when the field's value changes ([26 §7](modules/26-signal-forms.md#7-submission-submit-formroot-submitting-and-server-errors)).
- <a id="submit"></a>**submit()**: the function exported by `@angular/forms/signals` that marks fields touched, checks validity, runs an async action while `submitting()` is true and attaches the errors the action returns ([26 §7](modules/26-signal-forms.md#7-submission-submit-formroot-submitting-and-server-errors)).
- <a id="submitted-and-ng-submitted"></a>**submitted and ng-submitted**: the flag on `NgForm` and `FormGroupDirective` set by a submit, valid or not, and the class the `<form>` gets from it ([25 §8](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#8-error-display-strategies-and-accessible-errors))
- <a id="submitting"></a>**submitting()**: the field state signal that is `true` while a submission action runs ([26 §7](modules/26-signal-forms.md#7-submission-submit-formroot-submitting-and-server-errors)).
- <a id="subpath"></a>**subPath**: a locale's URL segment in `angular.json`'s `i18n` block; its output folder name and base `href`, the locale code by default ([34 §7](modules/34-i18n.md#7-compile-time-localization-one-build-per-locale-subpath-lang-and-dir))
- <a id="subpath-exports"></a>**subpath (exports)**: a package-name suffix such as `ui-lib/testing` that an `exports` entry maps to a file ([38 §6](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#6-public-api-surfaces-public-apits-exports-secondary-entry-points-and-versioning))
- <a id="subresource-integrity-sri"></a>**Subresource Integrity (SRI)**: the browser compares a fetched script or stylesheet with the hash in its `integrity` attribute and refuses a mismatch; Angular's `subresourceIntegrity` build option writes the attributes and an import map for lazy chunks ([33 §11](modules/33-security.md#11-dependency-and-supply-chain-security-lockfiles-npm-audit-install-scripts-and-subresource-integrity))
- <a id="subscribable"></a>**subscribable**: any object with a `subscribe` method, such as an RxJS `Observable`; a cold one starts its work for each subscriber ([14 §8](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#8-the-async-pipe-under-the-hood)).
- <a id="subscriber"></a>**subscriber**: the internal class that wraps an observer and adds the guarantees RxJS gives: it stops after `complete` or `error` and closes its subscription ([22 §2](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#2-anatomy-observable-observer-subscription-and-teardown))
- <a id="subscription"></a>**subscription**: the live connection between a source and the code that listens to it; to dispose it is to close it; owner [22](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md) ([14 §8](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#8-the-async-pipe-under-the-hood)). · also in [22 §1](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#1-push-collections-observables-promises-iterators-and-laziness)
- <a id="subscription-push"></a>**subscription (push)**: the push service's record for one worker, an endpoint URL plus keys, which your backend stores and sends to ([37 §7](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#7-push-notifications-and-installability-swpush-notification-clicks-and-the-manifest))
- <a id="subscription-marble"></a>**subscription marble**: a marble string with `^` for subscribe and `!` for unsubscribe, asserted with `expectSubscriptions` ([23 §10](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#10-testing-with-testscheduler-marble-syntax-and-virtual-time-under-the-hood))
- <a id="subtag"></a>**subtag**: one part of a language tag: the language (required), an optional script and an optional region ([34 §1](modules/34-i18n.md#1-i18n-l10n-and-the-locale-language-tags-what-a-locale-decides-and-locale_id))
- <a id="success-criterion-sc"></a>**success criterion (SC)**: one testable WCAG requirement, at level A, AA or AAA ([11 §1](modules/11-accessibility.md#1-why-accessibility-and-what-the-standards-require)).
- <a id="sunset"></a>**sunset**: the `Sunset` response header (RFC 8594) saying when a resource is expected to stop responding ([41 §4](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#4-evolving-the-contract-compatible-changes-api-versioning-in-spring-framework-7-and-deprecation-headers))
- <a id="superdomain"></a>**superdomain**: a shared parent domain; Cypress binds each test to one ([30 §2](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#2-playwright-and-cypress-architecture-ng-e2e-and-the-end-of-protractor))
- <a id="superseded-navigation"></a>**superseded navigation**: a navigation cancelled with code 1 because a newer one started ([24 §7](modules/24-routing.md#7-the-navigation-lifecycle-events-cancellation-and-the-transition-pipeline))
- <a id="supply-chain"></a>**supply chain**: the packages and tools a build is made of, and the risk that one of them is malicious ([33 §1](modules/33-security.md#1-threat-model-of-an-angular-single-page-app-what-the-attacker-controls-what-runs-where-and-where-each-defense-lives))
- <a id="support-window"></a>**support window**: how long the old contract must keep working, bounded by how long old tabs and cached bundles may still run ([44 §7](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#7-cicd-for-both-layers-one-image-promoted-through-stages-a-monorepo-or-separate-repositories-and-versioning-the-contract))
- <a id="support-window-angular-version"></a>**support window (Angular version)**: how long a major Angular version receives fixes ([39 §3](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#3-how-angular-ships-semantic-versioning-the-release-cadence-support-windows-and-the-deprecation-policy), [47 §6](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#6-questions-to-ask-about-the-front-end-practice-versions-and-updates-testing-the-design-system-and-accessibility-performance-contracts-and-releases-and-how-to-read-the-answers))
- <a id="suppressederror"></a>**SuppressedError**: the error `using` produces when a disposer throws while another error is in flight; it holds both (`error`, `suppressed`) ([05 §7](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#7-explicit-resource-management-using-and-disposablestack)).
- <a id="surrogate-pair"></a>**surrogate pair**: two code units that together encode one code point above U+FFFF, such as most emoji ([01 §3](modules/01-js-values-types-coercion.md#3-strings-and-unicode)).
- <a id="symbol"></a>**symbol**: a unique primitive used as a property key that cannot collide with string keys ([01 §4](modules/01-js-values-types-coercion.md#4-symbols)).
- <a id="symbolobservable"></a>**Symbol.observable**: the interop key RxJS reads on a foreign object to adopt it as an Observable (`'@@observable'` where the symbol is missing) ([22 Exercise 22.1](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#ex22-1))
- <a id="syntactic-rule"></a>**syntactic rule**: a validation rule decidable from the value alone, such as required, length or pattern, which a client can mirror ([41 §7](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#7-validation-on-both-layers-bean-validation-angular-validators-and-server-errors-on-form-fields))
- <a id="synthetic-module"></a>**synthetic module**: a module whose exports the host creates directly instead of from source text, such as a JSON module ([05 §3](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#3-import-maps-importmeta-and-import-attributes)).
- <a id="system-connection"></a>**system connection**: the relay's one shared TCP connection to the broker, used for messages from application code (no session id) ([43 §9](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#9-scaling-out-and-under-the-hood-sticky-sessions-the-broker-relay-backpressure-and-one-message-traced-across-two-instances))
- <a id="system-context"></a>**system context**: the diagram level showing the user, our app and the systems around it ([45 §2](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#2-communicating-under-time-pressure-a-time-plan-complete-trade-off-statements-and-diagrams-at-three-zoom-levels))
- <a id="system-design-round"></a>**system design round**: an open question about a whole product, answered by talking and drawing rather than by finishing code ([45 §1](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#1-the-front-end-system-design-interview-what-it-evaluates-its-formats-and-how-it-differs-from-a-backend-design-round))
- <a id="system-override"></a>**system override**: `mat.theme-overrides((primary: …))` inside a selector, which redefines `--mat-sys-*` variables for that subtree ([36 §4](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#4-customizing-the-theme-system-and-component-token-overrides-and-theming-your-own-components))
- <a id="system-variable-system-token"></a>**system variable (system token)**: a `--mat-sys-*` CSS custom property, such as `--mat-sys-primary`, that `mat.theme` declares and every Material component reads ([36 §3](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#3-theming-with-material-3-mattheme-system-variables-and-light-and-dark-with-color-scheme))

## T

- <a id="tab-channel"></a>**tab channel**: our `TabChannel` interface over `BroadcastChannel`, with `post` and `onMessage`, so tests can fake the other tabs ([Exercise 42.3](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#ex42-3))
- <a id="tab-order--tab-stop"></a>**tab order / tab stop**: the sequence Tab and Shift+Tab follow through focusable elements, and one position in it ([11 §4](modules/11-accessibility.md#4-keyboard-focus-order-roving-tabindex-and-focus-traps)).
- <a id="tag"></a>**tag**: a string on a project's configuration that dependency constraints select by ([38 §7](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#7-nx-monorepos-the-project-graph-tags-nxenforce-module-boundaries-affected-commands-and-caching))
- <a id="tag-nx"></a>**tag (Nx)**: a label on a project, such as `type:ui`, that boundary rules match ([38 §4](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#4-layers-library-types-and-dependency-rules))
- <a id="tagged-template"></a>**tagged template**: a template literal preceded by a function (`` tag`text` ``) that receives its text parts ([13 §2](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#2-interpolation-and-template-expressions-what-is-allowed)).
- <a id="tagged-template-literal"></a>**tagged template literal**: a template literal written after a function name, which receives the fixed strings and the values separately; `$localize` is one ([34 §4](modules/34-i18n.md#4-marking-text-for-translation-i18n-attributes-metadata-ids-and-localize-in-code))
- <a id="tail-recursive-conditional-type"></a>**tail-recursive conditional type**: a conditional type whose recursive call is its last step, evaluated without intermediate instantiations since TypeScript 4.5 ([07 §3](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#3-conditional-types-distributivity-infer-and-recursive-types)).
- <a id="tailwind-css"></a>**Tailwind CSS**: a build-time utility-class CSS framework; in Angular its stylesheet is a global one ([13 §8](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#8-styling-strategies-component-styles-global-styles-css-variables-and-tailwind)).
- <a id="take-home"></a>**take-home**: a scoped task done alone and reviewed later, usually in a call ([47 §5](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#5-take-home-assignments-scope-and-time-box-what-the-repository-must-show-the-readme-and-the-review-call))
- <a id="tapresponse-and-mapresponse"></a>**tapResponse** and **mapResponse**: `@ngrx/operators` functions that handle a response with `{ next, error }` callbacks and keep the outer stream alive ([28 §6](modules/28-state-management.md#6-effects-functional-effects-flattening-error-handling-and-dispatch-false))
- <a id="target"></a>**target**: a named job of a project (`build`, `serve`, `test`) with a builder, options and configurations ([12 §2](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#2-the-cli-and-the-workspace-angularjson-projects-schematics-and-ng-update)).
- <a id="target-i18n"></a>**target (i18n)**: the translated text a translator adds to a trans-unit; the build inlines it for that locale ([34 §6](modules/34-i18n.md#6-extraction-and-translation-files-ng-extract-i18n-formats-and-message-ids))
- <a id="target--lib-typescript"></a>**target / lib (TypeScript)**: the syntax level `tsc` emits, versus the API declarations it type-checks against ([05 §8](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#8-modern-features-by-edition-es2015-to-es2026)).
- <a id="target-size"></a>**target size**: the clickable area of a control ([45 §6](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#6-performance-and-accessibility-as-design-steps-budgets-a-loading-plan-long-lists-and-keyboard-and-pointer-paths))
- <a id="targeting-key"></a>**targeting key**: the string that identifies the subject of an evaluation, usually the user ([44 §8](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#8-feature-flags-with-openfeature-and-canary-releases-providers-evaluation-context-sticky-rollout-kill-switches-and-version-skew))
- <a id="task-macrotask"></a>**task (macrotask)**: a unit of work from a task queue, such as a timer callback, an event or an I/O callback ([04 §1](modules/04-js-async-event-loop.md#1-the-event-loop-tasks-microtasks-and-rendering)).
- <a id="task-hash"></a>**task hash**: Nx's digest of a task's declared inputs and command, used as its cache key ([38 §13](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#13-under-the-hood-ng-packagrs-build-graph-and-how-nx-computes-affected-projects-and-task-hashes))
- <a id="task-input"></a>**task input**: a file or value whose change makes a task's hash change ([38 §7](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#7-nx-monorepos-the-project-graph-tags-nxenforce-module-boundaries-affected-commands-and-caching))
- <a id="task-output"></a>**task output**: a file a task produces, stored and restored on a cache hit ([38 §7](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#7-nx-monorepos-the-project-graph-tags-nxenforce-module-boundaries-affected-commands-and-caching))
- <a id="task-tracking-zonejs"></a>**task tracking (zone.js)**: a zone.js plugin that records scheduled macrotasks, which `provideStabilityDebugging` logs when the app does not stabilize ([21 §8](modules/21-change-detection.md#8-debugging-the-profiler-providechecknochangesconfig-and-stability))
- <a id="tc39-stages"></a>**TC39 stages**: the maturity levels of a proposal (0, 1, 2, 2.7, 3, 4); Stage 4 means finished, with two compatible implementations ([05 §8](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#8-modern-features-by-edition-es2015-to-es2026)).
- <a id="teardown"></a>**teardown**: what runs when a component is destroyed: its `ngOnDestroy`, its `DestroyRef` callbacks and its effect cleanups ([20 §5](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#5-teardown-ngondestroy-destroyref-and-takeuntildestroyed))
- <a id="teardown-rxjs"></a>**teardown (RxJS)**: the cleanup function a producer may return, run when its subscription ends ([22 §1](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#1-push-collections-observables-promises-iterators-and-laziness)) · also in [23 §1](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#1-higher-order-observables-switchmap-mergemap-concatmap-and-exhaustmap)
- <a id="teardown-testbed"></a>**teardown (TestBed)**: destroying the testing module and its fixtures after each test ([29 §4](modules/29-testing-angular.md#4-testbed-the-testing-module-inject-runininjectioncontext-overrides-and-teardown))
- <a id="template"></a>**template**: the HTML a component renders inside its host element ([13 §1](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#1-component-anatomy-selector-template-styles-imports-and-the-v22-file-convention)). · also in [40 §2](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#2-templates-jsx-and-single-file-components-what-the-compiler-knows)
- <a id="template-context"></a>**template context**: the object given to `createEmbeddedView`; `let-name="key"` reads a key and a bare `let` reads `$implicit` ([14 §2](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#2-structural-directives-ng-template-templateref-viewcontainerref-and-the-microsyntax)).
- <a id="template-expression"></a>**template expression**: the restricted JavaScript inside `{{ }}` or a binding's quotes: reads, calls of component members and operators, no side effects, declarations or globals ([13 §2](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#2-interpolation-and-template-expressions-what-is-allowed)).
- <a id="template-fragment"></a>**template fragment**: markup inside `<ng-template>` that Angular creates only when something renders it ([19 §4](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#4-template-driven-apis-ng-template-ngtemplateoutlet-and-typed-context))
- <a id="template-function"></a>**template function**: the generated function a component's template compiles to, run with a create flag and an update flag ([12 §8](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it)).
- <a id="template-injection"></a>**template injection**: attacker text becoming part of a template, so the template compiler runs it as code ([33 §2](modules/33-security.md#2-xss-and-angulars-template-model-escaping-by-default-trusted-templates-and-the-paths-around-them))
- <a id="template-literal-type"></a>**template literal type**: a string literal type built with template syntax, `` `${A}:${B}` ``, expanding over unions ([07 §2](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#2-keyof-indexed-access-mapped-and-template-literal-types)).
- <a id="template-reference-variable"></a>**template reference variable**: `#name` on an element, a name for a DOM element, a component or directive instance or a `TemplateRef`, readable in the same view and in nested ones ([13 §6](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#6-template-reference-variables-and-let)).
- <a id="template-type-checker"></a>**template type checker**: the part of the compiler that type-checks template expressions; a directive's static guards feed it; owner [12 §7](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#7-aot-versus-jit-and-template-type-checking) ([14 §2](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#2-structural-directives-ng-template-templateref-viewcontainerref-and-the-microsyntax)).
- <a id="template-type-checking"></a>**template type checking**: generating TypeScript code from template expressions so TypeScript reports template errors at build time ([12 §7](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#7-aot-versus-jit-and-template-type-checking)).
- <a id="template-validator-directive"></a>**template validator directive**: a validator attached by an attribute selector (`required`, `minlength`, `email`, `min` on a number input) instead of passed as a function ([25 §4](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#4-template-driven-forms-in-depth-ngmodel-ngform-and-asynchronous-registration))
- <a id="template-driven-form"></a>**template-driven form**: a form whose model is created by `FormsModule` directives such as `ngModel` and `NgForm` as the template renders ([25 §1](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#1-two-forms-apis-template-driven-and-reactive-and-the-control-directive-and-accessor-triangle))
- <a id="templateportal"></a>**TemplatePortal**: a portal made of a `TemplateRef` and its context, whose view an outlet creates with `createEmbeddedView` ([36 Q36.09](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#q36-09))
- <a id="templateref"></a>**TemplateRef**: a handle on the content of an `<ng-template>`; a reference on that element holds one ([13 §6](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#6-template-reference-variables-and-let)). · also in [14 §2](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#2-structural-directives-ng-template-templateref-viewcontainerref-and-the-microsyntax)
- <a id="temporal-dead-zone"></a>**temporal dead zone**: the stretch of a scope before a `let` or `const` declaration runs, where reading the name throws a `ReferenceError` ([22 §2](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#2-anatomy-observable-observer-subscription-and-teardown))
- <a id="temporal-dead-zone-tdz"></a>**temporal dead zone (TDZ)**: the time between entering a scope and running a `let`, `const` or `class` declaration, during which any access throws `ReferenceError` ([02 §2](modules/02-js-scope-closures-this.md#2-hoisting-and-the-temporal-dead-zone)).
- <a id="tenant"></a>**tenant**: one isolated slice of the server's data, selected per worker or per test ([30 §8](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#8-ci-strategy-for-end-to-end-tests-workers-sharding-retries-policy-and-test-data))
- <a id="terminal-event"></a>**terminal event**: the one event that ends a navigation: `NavigationEnd`, `NavigationCancel`, `NavigationError` or `NavigationSkipped` ([24 §7](modules/24-routing.md#7-the-navigation-lifecycle-events-cancellation-and-the-transition-pipeline))
- <a id="terminal-notification"></a>**terminal notification**: `error` or `complete`; after one, a stream sends nothing more ([23 §3](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#3-errors-catcherror-placement-finalize-and-error-versus-complete))
- <a id="test-applicationref"></a>**test ApplicationRef**: the testing module's application reference, which also tracks the views of test fixtures ([29 §12](modules/29-testing-angular.md#12-under-the-hood-how-the-builder-boots-testbed-and-how-a-fixture-decides-it-is-stable))
- <a id="test-backend"></a>**test backend**: the in-memory `HttpBackend` of `provideHttpClientTesting`; it holds each request open until the test answers it and simulates no `timeout` or abort ([27 §11](modules/27-http-client.md#11-testing-and-the-backend-seam-providehttpclienttesting-httptestingcontroller-and-what-they-replace))
- <a id="test-data-management"></a>**test data management**: making every test create or reset the data it reads, so tests do not depend on each other ([30 §8](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#8-ci-strategy-for-end-to-end-tests-workers-sharding-retries-policy-and-test-data))
- <a id="test-double"></a>**test double**: a stand-in for a collaborator in a test (stub, fake, spy, mock) ([29 §1](modules/29-testing-angular.md#1-what-to-test-where-unit-integration-component-and-end-to-end-tests-the-pyramid-and-the-trophy))
- <a id="test-environment"></a>**test environment**: the base module, platform and options that `initTestEnvironment` sets once and every testing module extends ([29 §12](modules/29-testing-angular.md#12-under-the-hood-how-the-builder-boots-testbed-and-how-a-fixture-decides-it-is-stable))
- <a id="test-group"></a>**test group**: the tests one worker runs in order, a whole file by default and a single test with `fullyParallel`; sharding never splits one ([30 §9](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#9-under-the-hood-how-playwrights-runner-groups-shards-retries-and-isolates-tests))
- <a id="test-id"></a>**test id**: an attribute (`data-testid` by default) that `getByTestId` queries; not something the user perceives ([30 §4](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#4-locators-page-objects-and-fixtures))
- <a id="test-level"></a>**test level**: how much real code a test runs and what it fakes; unit, component, integration and end-to-end are the usual levels ([29 §1](modules/29-testing-angular.md#1-what-to-test-where-unit-integration-component-and-end-to-end-tests-the-pyramid-and-the-trophy))
- <a id="test-runner"></a>**test runner**: the tool that finds `describe` and `it`, runs them and reports; here Vitest ([29 §2](modules/29-testing-angular.md#2-the-runner-vitest-through-angularbuildunit-test-jsdom-and-browser-mode)) · also in [40 §6](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#6-routing-forms-data-fetching-tooling-and-testing-first-party-or-ecosystem)
- <a id="test-surface-harness"></a>**test surface (harness)**: the set of async methods a component harness exposes, which say what a user sees or does and return values rather than elements ([36 Q36.20](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#q36-20))
- <a id="testbed"></a>**TestBed**: Angular's test harness, which creates a component in a test ([13 §9](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#9-view-encapsulation-emulated-shadowdom-and-none-and-what-the-compiler-does-to-your-css)). · also in [14 §8](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#8-the-async-pipe-under-the-hood), [15 §6](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#6-interop-ngmodule-libraries-in-standalone-apps-and-standalone-pieces-in-ngmodule-apps), [16 §9](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#9-di-patterns-testing-and-the-spring-container)
- <a id="testbedcreatedirective"></a>**TestBed.createDirective**: renders a directive on a host element of a chosen tag and returns a `DirectiveFixture`; added in Angular 22.2 ([29 §8](modules/29-testing-angular.md#8-testing-services-pipes-and-directives-including-testbedcreatedirective))
