- <a id="testelement"></a>**TestElement**: the harness's async handle on one DOM node; each action awaits fixture stability ([29 §11](modules/29-testing-angular.md#11-component-harnesses-and-angular-testing-library))
- <a id="testing-backend"></a>**testing backend**: the `HttpClient` backend that `provideHttpClientTesting()` installs, whose requests an `HttpTestingController` inspects and flushes instead of reaching the network ([29 §9](modules/29-testing-angular.md#9-testing-guards-interceptors-forms-effects-resources-and-stores))
- <a id="testing-module"></a>**testing module**: the Angular module `TestBed` builds for one test from its configured providers and imports; it is rebuilt before each test ([29 §4](modules/29-testing-angular.md#4-testbed-the-testing-module-inject-runininjectioncontext-overrides-and-teardown))
- <a id="testing-pyramid"></a>**testing pyramid**: the test-mix shape credited to Mike Cohn: many low-level unit tests, fewer service tests, few UI tests ([29 §1](modules/29-testing-angular.md#1-what-to-test-where-unit-integration-component-and-end-to-end-tests-the-pyramid-and-the-trophy))
- <a id="testing-trophy"></a>**testing trophy**: Kent C. Dodds's test-mix shape: static analysis at the base, then unit, most effort in integration, few end-to-end ([29 §1](modules/29-testing-angular.md#1-what-to-test-where-unit-integration-component-and-end-to-end-tests-the-pyramid-and-the-trophy))
- <a id="testrequest"></a>**TestRequest**: one open request in the test backend, answered with `flush`, `error` or `event` ([27 §11](modules/27-http-client.md#11-testing-and-the-backend-seam-providehttpclienttesting-httptestingcontroller-and-what-they-replace))
- <a id="textdecoderstream"></a>**TextDecoderStream**: a platform stream that turns chunks of bytes into chunks of text, keeping a character split across chunks whole ([43 §4](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#4-the-angular-sse-client-eventsource-or-a-fetch-stream-as-an-observable-and-when-httpclient-fits))
- <a id="the-method"></a>**the method**: this module's eight steps: requirements, components, data and flow, state, API, performance, accessibility, failure modes ([45 §1](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#1-the-front-end-system-design-interview-what-it-evaluates-its-formats-and-how-it-differs-from-a-backend-design-round))
- <a id="theme-choice"></a>**theme choice**: a user setting with three values, system, light or dark, where "system" defers to the operating system's `prefers-color-scheme` ([Exercise 36.1](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#ex36-1))
- <a id="theme-type"></a>**theme type**: the `mat.theme` color setting `color-scheme` (the default: `light-dark()` values), `light` or `dark` (one set of values) ([36 §3](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#3-theming-with-material-3-mattheme-system-variables-and-light-and-dark-with-color-scheme))
- <a id="thenable"></a>**thenable**: any object with a `then` method, which promises adopt when resolved with it ([04 §3](modules/04-js-async-event-loop.md#3-promises-from-callbacks-to-a-state-machine)).
- <a id="third-party-cookie"></a>**third-party cookie**: a cookie belonging to a site other than the one in the address bar ([09 §6](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#6-cookies)).
- <a id="threat-model"></a>**threat model**: a list of what you protect, who can attack it, from where, and which control stops each attack ([33 §1](modules/33-security.md#1-threat-model-of-an-angular-single-page-app-what-the-attacker-controls-what-runs-where-and-where-each-defense-lives))
- <a id="threshold"></a>**threshold**: how different two pixels' colours may be before they count as different, from 0 (strict) to 1 (lax); Playwright defaults to 0.2 ([30 §6](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#6-visual-regression-and-accessibility-checks-in-end-to-end-tests))
- <a id="throttle"></a>**throttle**: run at most once per interval while calls continue ([02 §6](modules/02-js-scope-closures-this.md#6-higher-order-functions-currying-partial-application-debounce-and-throttle)). · also in [22 §8](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#8-timing-operators-debouncetime-throttletime-audittime-and-sampletime)
- <a id="throughput"></a>**throughput**: bytes moved per unit of time ([45 §3](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#3-requirements-first-functional-and-non-functional-requirements-constraints-and-back-of-envelope-numbers))
- <a id="tick"></a>**tick**: the `ApplicationRef` method that runs one change-detection pass ([19 §7](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#7-dynamic-components-viewcontainerrefcreatecomponent-createcomponent-and-bindings)) · also in [21 §3](modules/21-change-detection.md#3-zonejs-and-ngzone-how-changes-used-to-be-detected-and-why-zone-left-the-default), [29 §5](modules/29-testing-angular.md#5-zoneless-fixtures-whenstable-testbedtick-detectchanges-and-automatic-change-detection)
- <a id="tiebreaker"></a>**tiebreaker**: a unique last sort key, such as `id`, that orders rows whose other sort keys are equal ([41 §6](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#6-pagination-sorting-and-filtering-offset-versus-cursor-and-how-spring-data-serializes-a-page)) · also in [46 §4](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#4-a-large-data-grid-server-side-filtering-sorting-and-pagination-the-query-in-the-url-and-keyset-cursors)
- <a id="time-box"></a>**time box**: the agreed time you stop at ([47 §5](modules/47-behavioral-and-interview-craft.md#5-take-home-assignments-scope-and-time-box-what-the-repository-must-show-the-readme-and-the-review-call))
- <a id="time-plan"></a>**time plan**: how the slot's minutes are split across the method's steps ([45 §2](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#2-communicating-under-time-pressure-a-time-plan-complete-trade-off-statements-and-diagrams-at-three-zoom-levels))
- <a id="time-progression"></a>**time progression**: the `10ms` form of a marble diagram, which moves the virtual clock forward without an event ([23 §10](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#10-testing-with-testscheduler-marble-syntax-and-virtual-time-under-the-hood))
- <a id="time-provider-rxjs"></a>**time provider (RxJS)**: an internal object wrapping a platform timer or clock (`intervalProvider`, `timeoutProvider`, `dateTimestampProvider`…) that the schedulers call; not an Angular DI provider ([23 §9](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#9-schedulers-queue-asap-async-and-animation-frame-observeon-and-subscribeon))
- <a id="time-to-first-byte-ttfb"></a>**time to first byte (TTFB)**: the time between starting to navigate to a page and the first byte of its response arriving ([32 §1](modules/32-ssr-ssg-hydration.md#1-why-render-on-the-server-csr-ssr-ssg-and-hybrid-rendering-and-what-each-costs))
- <a id="time-to-live-ttl"></a>**time to live (TTL)**: how long a cache entry may be served before it counts as expired ([27 §6](modules/27-http-client.md#6-cancellation-and-caching-unsubscribing-aborts-cache-interceptors-sharereplay-and-the-ssr-transfer-cache))
- <a id="time-zone-iana-name"></a>**time zone (IANA name)**: a region's clock rule named like `America/New_York`; `Intl.DateTimeFormat`'s `timeZone` option takes it, and without it the runtime's zone decides ([34 §2](modules/34-i18n.md#2-the-platforms-intl-apis-dates-numbers-plurals-lists-collation-and-display-names))
- <a id="time-to-live"></a>**time-to-live**: how long an entry survives without a heartbeat before it expires ([46 §7](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#7-a-notifications-center-and-an-editing-indicator-delivery-unread-counts-across-tabs-and-presence-with-a-time-to-live))
- <a id="timeline-document"></a>**timeline (document)**: the clock that advances every animation of a page ([35 §1](modules/35-animations.md#1-motion-in-a-ui-what-to-animate-the-browsers-three-animation-mechanisms-and-where-angular-fits))
- <a id="timeouterror"></a>**TimeoutError**: the error an action or assertion throws when its wait ends before the condition holds ([30 §3](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#3-auto-waiting-actionability-checks-web-first-assertions-and-cypress-retry-ability))
- <a id="title-service"></a>**Title service**: the platform-browser service whose `setTitle` and `getTitle` write and read `document.title` ([24 §9](modules/24-routing.md#9-view-transitions-scroll-restoration-and-titles))
- <a id="titlestrategy"></a>**TitleStrategy**: the class that turns the router state into the document title; the default sets the deepest primary route's `title` and leaves the old title when none exists ([24 §9](modules/24-routing.md#9-view-transitions-scroll-restoration-and-titles))
- <a id="tls"></a>**TLS**: Transport Layer Security, the encryption layer under HTTPS and under HTTP/2 and QUIC ([09 §3](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#3-http11-http2-and-http3)).
- <a id="tls-termination"></a>**TLS termination**: ending the HTTPS connection at the proxy, so the server behind it receives plain HTTP and must be told the original scheme ([44 §4](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#4-nginx-as-the-reverse-proxy-proxy_pass-forwarded-headers-into-spring-boot-and-sse-or-websocket-connections-through-the-proxy))
- <a id="tnode"></a>**TNode**: the static, per-template description of a template node, as opposed to the node of one rendered view ([19 §8](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#8-under-the-hood-the-compiled-definition-projection-slots-and-query-refresh))
- <a id="token"></a>**token**: the key a consumer asks an injector for: a class, an abstract class or an `InjectionToken`; typing one is [07 Q07.18](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#q07-18) ([16 §1](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#1-why-dependency-injection-containers-constructor-injection-and-inject)).
- <a id="token-family"></a>**token family**: the chain of refresh tokens issued from one login through rotation ([42 §4](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#4-refresh-tokens-rotation-reuse-detection-silent-renew-and-refreshing-across-tabs))
- <a id="token-introspection"></a>**token introspection**: a resource server asking the authorization server whether a token is active and what it grants (RFC 7662) ([42 §5](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#5-spring-security-as-a-resource-server-validating-jwts-and-turning-scopes-into-authorities))
- <a id="token-layering"></a>**token layering**: exposing your own custom properties whose fallbacks are another system's variables, such as `--mat-sys-*` ([36 §12](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#12-a-design-system-on-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-or-a-wrapper-around-material))
- <a id="token-name"></a>**token name**: the key of an override map, a component token without its `--mat-<component>-` prefix, such as `filled-container-color` ([36 §4](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#4-customizing-the-theme-system-and-component-token-overrides-and-theming-your-own-components))
- <a id="token-relay"></a>**token relay**: a gateway or BFF adding the signed-in user's access token to the call it forwards ([42 §3](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#3-where-the-oauth-client-runs-a-browser-only-client-a-token-mediating-backend-or-a-backend-for-frontend-with-spring))
- <a id="token-revocation"></a>**token revocation**: asking the authorization server to invalidate one token (RFC 7009) ([42 §8](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#8-logout-everywhere-and-session-timeout-ux))
- <a id="token-mediating-backend"></a>**token-mediating backend**: a server that runs the OAuth flow as a confidential client and hands the page the access token ([42 §3](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#3-where-the-oauth-client-runs-a-browser-only-client-a-token-mediating-backend-or-a-backend-for-frontend-with-spring))
- <a id="tokenizer"></a>**tokenizer**: the first step of a template compiler, which splits the source into tags and text runs before anything is compiled ([12 Exercise 12.4](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#ex12-4)).
- <a id="tolerant-reader"></a>**tolerant reader**: a reader that ignores fields it does not know; Jackson 3 does by default ([41 §2](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#2-dto-contracts-what-crosses-the-wire-absent-versus-null-and-checking-responses-at-the-boundary))
- <a id="tonal-palette"></a>**tonal palette**: one hue at tones 0 (black) to 100 (white); Material's light theme takes `primary` from tone 40, the dark theme from tone 80 ([36 §3](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#3-theming-with-material-3-mattheme-system-variables-and-light-and-dark-with-color-scheme))
- <a id="top-layer"></a>**top layer**: the browser-managed layer above everything else that holds fullscreen elements, modal `<dialog>`s and popovers ([10 §4](modules/10-css-essentials.md#4-positioning-stacking-contexts-and-z-index)). · also in [36 §5](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#5-portals-and-overlays-where-floating-ui-renders-how-it-is-positioned-and-how-it-closes)
- <a id="top-down-migration"></a>**top-down migration**: moving to Signal Forms by keeping existing `FormControl` instances inside a signal model with `compatForm` ([26 §9](modules/26-signal-forms.md#9-interop-and-migration-compatform-signalformcontrol-and-what-changed-from-v21-to-v22)).
- <a id="top-level-await"></a>**top-level await**: `await` at the top level of an ES module, which delays the module's evaluation and that of its importers ([04 §5](modules/04-js-async-event-loop.md#5-async-and-await)).
- <a id="topological-order"></a>**topological order**: an order in which every node comes after everything it depends on ([38 §13](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#13-under-the-hood-ng-packagrs-build-graph-and-how-nx-computes-affected-projects-and-task-hashes))
- <a id="toprimitive"></a>**ToPrimitive**: the abstract operation that turns an object into a primitive, using a hint and `Symbol.toPrimitive`, `valueOf` and `toString` ([01 §5](modules/01-js-values-types-coercion.md#5-coercion)).
- <a id="tosignal--toobservable"></a>**toSignal / toObservable**: subscribe to an Observable and expose its latest value as a signal, versus emit a signal's value (once per flush) as an Observable ([17 §8](modules/17-signals.md#8-interop-with-rxjs)).
- <a id="touch-output"></a>**touch output**: the output a custom control emits on blur to mark its field touched ([26 §8](modules/26-signal-forms.md#8-custom-controls-formvaluecontrol-formcheckboxcontrol-and-how-formfield-picks-a-control)).
- <a id="trace"></a>**trace**: a recording of each action of a test that the Trace Viewer lets you step through ([30 §7](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#7-flaky-tests-root-causes-diagnosis-and-fixes))
- <a id="trace-flags"></a>**trace flags**: the last two hex digits of `traceparent`; only the sampled bit is defined in version 00 ([44 §10](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#10-under-the-hood-opentelemetry-for-the-web-traceparent-and-one-click-traced-to-a-spring-boot-span-and-log-line))
- <a id="trace-id"></a>**trace ID**: the 32-hex-digit identifier that every span of one trace carries; all zeroes is invalid (W3C Trace Context §3.2.2.3) ([44 §10](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#10-under-the-hood-opentelemetry-for-the-web-traceparent-and-one-click-traced-to-a-spring-boot-span-and-log-line))
- <a id="trace-table"></a>**trace table**: one change followed through several runtimes in the columns notify, schedule, re-run, compare and write ([40 §9](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#9-under-the-hood-one-state-change-traced-through-angular-react-vue-and-solid))
- <a id="trace-table-request-path"></a>**trace table (request path)**: a list of one request's hops, each with what it reads and what it can return ([41 §10](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#10-under-the-hood-one-failed-request-through-springs-filters-and-handlers-and-angulars-backend)) · also in [42 §9](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#9-under-the-hood-one-bff-login-and-the-first-api-call-through-spring-securitys-filters)
- <a id="traceparent"></a>**traceparent**: the W3C Trace Context header `00-<trace-id>-<parent-id>-<trace-flags>` that carries a trace across hops ([44 §10](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#10-under-the-hood-opentelemetry-for-the-web-traceparent-and-one-click-traced-to-a-spring-boot-span-and-log-line))
- <a id="tracestate"></a>**tracestate**: the W3C Trace Context companion header for vendor-specific trace data ([44 §10](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#10-under-the-hood-opentelemetry-for-the-web-traceparent-and-one-click-traced-to-a-spring-boot-span-and-log-line))
- <a id="track"></a>**track**: a grid column or grid row ([10 §3](modules/10-css-essentials.md#3-flexbox-grid-and-subgrid)).
- <a id="track-key"></a>**track (key)**: the expression in `@for` that computes each item's key; Angular matches old and new rows by it ([18 §2](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#2-for-track-context-variables-empty-and-what-angular-can-iterate))
- <a id="track-migration"></a>**track (migration)**: an independent sequence of migrations with its own pull requests ([39 §10](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#10-a-migration-program-for-an-old-angular-codebase-order-dependencies-and-checkpoints))
- <a id="trackby-table"></a>**trackBy (table)**: the function that gives a table row an identity across emissions, so equal identities keep the row view ([36 §7](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#7-tables-and-long-lists-cdktable-datasource-mattable-and-virtual-scrolling))
- <a id="trade-off-statement"></a>**trade-off statement**: a decision stated with its axis, options, deciding condition, accepted cost and revisit trigger ([45 §2](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#2-communicating-under-time-pressure-a-time-plan-complete-trade-off-statements-and-diagrams-at-three-zoom-levels))
- <a id="trampoline"></a>**trampoline**: running work scheduled from inside running work after the current work ends, instead of nesting it, which is what `queueScheduler` does ([23 §9](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#9-schedulers-queue-asap-async-and-animation-frame-observeon-and-subscribeon))
- <a id="trans-unit"></a>**trans-unit**: one message in an XLIFF 1.2 file: its `id`, `<source>` text, notes and source location, plus a `<target>` in a translation file ([34 §6](modules/34-i18n.md#6-extraction-and-translation-files-ng-extract-i18n-formats-and-message-ids))
- <a id="transfer-cache"></a>**transfer cache**: the server's `HttpClient` responses serialized into the HTML so the browser reuses them during hydration instead of requesting again ([27 §6](modules/27-http-client.md#6-cancellation-and-caching-unsubscribing-aborts-cache-interceptors-sharereplay-and-the-ssr-transfer-cache))
- <a id="transferable"></a>**transferable**: an object that owns a resource that can be moved to another context, so it is available in only one context at a time ([31 §9](modules/31-performance.md#9-off-the-main-thread-web-workers-in-angular-and-runoutsideangular-in-zone-apps))
- <a id="transferstate"></a>**TransferState**: the mechanism that serializes data fetched during server-side rendering into the page so the browser does not fetch it again ([17 §6](modules/17-signals.md#6-resources-async-data-as-signals)).
- <a id="transform-input-option"></a>**transform (input option)**: a function that converts the bound value before it is stored ([19 §1](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#1-inputs-outputs-and-two-way-binding-the-parent-and-child-contract))
- <a id="transformation--filtering--utility--conditional--aggregation-operator-families"></a>**transformation / filtering / utility / conditional / aggregation (operator families)**: operators grouped by purpose: change values or shape; drop or stop; inspect or run side effects; ask about emptiness or membership; collapse to one value ([22 §6](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#6-the-operator-catalogue-creation-transformation-filtering-utility-conditional-and-aggregation))
- <a id="transformedvalue"></a>**transformedValue**: a function returning a writable raw signal synchronized with a model through `parse` and `format`, with `parseErrors()` ([26 §8](modules/26-signal-forms.md#8-custom-controls-formvaluecontrol-formcheckboxcontrol-and-how-formfield-picks-a-control)).
- <a id="transient-user-activation"></a>**transient user activation**: the window state that a user has recently interacted with it directly, which the browser may require before it copies or pastes ([36 §8](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#8-the-smaller-primitives-the-stepper-breakpointobserver-and-clipboard))
- <a id="transition-delay"></a>**transition delay**: the wait between a value change and the start of its transition, set by `transition-delay` ([35 §2](modules/35-animations.md#2-css-transitions-triggers-interruption-events-and-entry-with-starting-style))
- <a id="transition-expression"></a>**transition expression**: the string that says which state change a `transition()` handles, such as `'open <=> closed'` or `':enter'` ([35 §8](modules/35-animations.md#8-legacy-angularanimations-triggers-states-transitions-query-and-stagger))
- <a id="transition-behavior"></a>**transition-behavior**: the property whose `allow-discrete` value lets discrete properties transition ([35 §2](modules/35-animations.md#2-css-transitions-triggers-interruption-events-and-entry-with-starting-style))
- <a id="transitive-dependency"></a>**transitive dependency**: a package your dependencies need, which you did not choose and a lockfile pins ([33 §11](modules/33-security.md#11-dependency-and-supply-chain-security-lockfiles-npm-audit-install-scripts-and-subresource-integrity)) · also in [40 §7](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#7-ecosystem-size-release-policies-and-breaking-changes)
- <a id="translation-key"></a>**translation key**: a stable identifier such as `'cart.title'` that a runtime library looks up in the active language's dictionary, instead of the source text ([34 §9](modules/34-i18n.md#9-runtime-translation-libraries-transloco-and-ngx-translate-lazy-scopes-and-the-trade-off-against-built-in-i18n))
- <a id="translation-map"></a>**translation map**: the table from message ID to translated text that `loadTranslations` fills ([34 §8](modules/34-i18n.md#8-runtime-translation-with-angularlocalize-loadtranslations-before-bootstrap))
- <a id="trap"></a>**trap**: a handler method of a Proxy that intercepts one internal method ([03 §5](modules/03-js-objects-prototypes-classes.md#5-proxy-and-reflect)).
- <a id="tree-shaking"></a>**tree shaking**: a bundler's removal of exports and modules nothing uses, which needs static `import`/`export` and side-effect information ([05 §2](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#2-commonjs-interop-and-dynamic-import)).
- <a id="tree-shakeable-provider"></a>**tree-shakeable provider**: a provider the bundler drops when nothing uses it ([39 §4](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#4-the-version-matrix-part-1-v2-to-v8))
- <a id="tree-shaking-1"></a>**tree-shaking**: the build dropping code that nothing reaches; for a root service, the docs promise it when the code never uses it, and one scratch build kept unused classes reached through an index ([16 §3](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#3-providedin-service-scopes-and-tree-shaking)). · also in [31 §6](modules/31-performance.md#6-bundle-analysis-budgets-and-tree-shaking-pitfalls)
- <a id="trigger"></a>**trigger**: the event or condition that starts a `@defer` load or render (`on idle`, `on viewport`, `on interaction`, `on hover`, `on immediate`, `on timer`, `when`) ([18 §6](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#6-triggers-and-prefetching))
- <a id="trigger-legacy"></a>**trigger (legacy)**: a named state machine declared in a component's `animations` array with `trigger()` and bound to an element as `[@name]="expr"` ([35 §8](modules/35-animations.md#8-legacy-angularanimations-triggers-states-transitions-query-and-stagger))
- <a id="trigger-of-change-detection"></a>**trigger (of change detection)**: what tells Angular to look at the views: a zone notification, a signal write, `markForCheck` or an event ([21 §1](modules/21-change-detection.md#1-the-synchronization-problem-dirty-checking-and-fine-grained-reactivity))
- <a id="trust-boundary"></a>**trust boundary**: where data passes from a less trusted side to a more trusted one; in a single-page app, the line between the browser and the API ([33 §1](modules/33-security.md#1-threat-model-of-an-angular-single-page-app-what-the-attacker-controls-what-runs-where-and-where-each-defense-lives)) · also in [33 §1](modules/33-security.md#1-threat-model-of-an-angular-single-page-app-what-the-attacker-controls-what-runs-where-and-where-each-defense-lives)
- <a id="trusted-types"></a>**Trusted Types**: a browser feature that makes sinks accept only values produced by a developer-written policy, enforced with `require-trusted-types-for` ([09 §8](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#8-content-security-policy-and-trusted-types)).
- <a id="trusted-types-policy"></a>**Trusted Types policy**: a named object from `trustedTypes.createPolicy(name, rules)` whose rules turn a string into a typed value a sink accepts; Angular's are `angular` and `angular#unsafe-bypass` ([33 §6](modules/33-security.md#6-trusted-types-with-angular-the-frameworks-policies-enforcement-and-rollout))
- <a id="trusted-types-allowlist"></a>**trusted-types allowlist**: the CSP directive naming the policies a page may create; creating any other throws ([33 §6](modules/33-security.md#6-trusted-types-with-angular-the-frameworks-policies-enforcement-and-rollout))
- <a id="trustedhtml"></a>**TrustedHTML**: the typed value an HTML sink such as `innerHTML` accepts when Trusted Types are enforced ([33 §6](modules/33-security.md#6-trusted-types-with-angular-the-frameworks-policies-enforcement-and-rollout))
- <a id="truthy--falsy"></a>**truthy / falsy**: whether ToBoolean turns a value into `true` or `false`; the falsy values are `false`, `0`, `-0`, `0n`, `''`, `null`, `undefined` and `NaN` ([01 §5](modules/01-js-values-types-coercion.md#5-coercion)).
- <a id="try_files"></a>**try_files**: an Nginx directive that uses the first listed file that exists, else a status (`=404`) or an internal redirect ([44 §3](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#3-nginx-for-a-single-page-app-location-matching-the-try_files-fallback-caching-headers-for-hashed-assets-and-gzip-or-brotli))
- <a id="ttl-digest"></a>**TTL (digest)**: the digest's loop limit, 10 in AngularJS 1.8.3 ([39 §1](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#1-angularjs-scopes-watchers-and-the-digest-cycle))
- <a id="ttl-header"></a>**TTL header**: the push message's lifetime in seconds, which a push service requires and may shorten (RFC 8030 §5.2) ([43 §1](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#1-choosing-a-transport-polling-long-polling-server-sent-events-websockets-and-web-push-when-no-tab-is-open))
- <a id="two-way-binding"></a>**two-way binding**: `[(x)]="y"`, syntax for the property binding `[x]` plus the listener `(xChange)` that assigns the emitted value back to `y` ([13 §5](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#5-event-binding-and-two-way-binding)). · also in [39 §1](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#1-angularjs-scopes-watchers-and-the-digest-cycle)
- <a id="type-alias"></a>**type alias**: a name for an existing type; it never creates a distinct type ([06 §2](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#2-structural-typing-assignability-and-readonly)).
- <a id="type-assertion"></a>**type assertion**: `value as T`, a claim the compiler accepts without checking the value ([06 §6](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#6-enums-literal-unions-as-const-and-satisfies)).
- <a id="type-assertion-function"></a>**type assertion function**: a function whose return type `x is T` tells TypeScript that `x` is a `T` afterwards; the form of `ngTemplateContextGuard` and `ngTemplateGuard_input` ([14 §2](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#2-structural-directives-ng-template-templateref-viewcontainerref-and-the-microsyntax)).
- <a id="type-checker"></a>**type checker**: the part of TypeScript that analyzes the whole program and reports diagnostics; it emits nothing by itself ([06 §1](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#1-what-typescript-is-erased-types-tsc-and-transpile-only-tools)).
- <a id="type-erasure"></a>**type erasure**: removing every type annotation, interface and type-only import when TypeScript is turned into JavaScript, so nothing about types exists at run time ([06 §1](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#1-what-typescript-is-erased-types-tsc-and-transpile-only-tools)).
- <a id="type-parameter"></a>**type parameter**: a placeholder in a generic signature (`<T>`) that each use fills with a type argument, written or inferred ([07 §1](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#1-generics-constraints-defaults-inference-and-const-type-parameters)).
- <a id="type-predicate"></a>**type predicate**: a return type `value is T` that narrows the caller's argument when the function returns `true`; unchecked ([06 §3](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#3-narrowing-and-control-flow-analysis)).
- <a id="type-stripping"></a>**type stripping**: Node's way of running `.ts`: types are replaced with whitespace, nothing is checked, and syntax that needs generated code is rejected ([06 §1](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#1-what-typescript-is-erased-types-tsc-and-transpile-only-tools)).
- <a id="type-ahead"></a>**type-ahead**: choosing a list item by typing the first letters of its label, which a key manager matches after a short pause ([36 §9](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#9-cdk-accessibility-focustrap-focusmonitor-liveannouncer-and-the-key-managers))
- <a id="type-check-block-tcb"></a>**type-check block (TCB)**: the TypeScript the Angular compiler generates from a template so that TypeScript can check it ([18 §3](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#3-switch-and-exhaustive-checking-with-default-never); owner [12 §7](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#7-aot-versus-jit-and-template-type-checking))
- <a id="typeahead"></a>**typeahead**: a search box that queries as the user types, which needs a rate limit, a change filter, cancellation of the older query and a per-query recovery ([23 §8](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#8-angular-patterns-typeahead-polling-cache-then-network-and-racing-requests)) · also in [46 §2](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#2-autocomplete-and-typeahead-search-a-request-policy-a-prefix-cache-ranking-on-the-server-and-an-accessible-combobox)
- <a id="typed-forms"></a>**typed forms**: the generic form model classes (`FormControl<T>`, `FormGroup<{…}>`) that type `value`, `controls` and `get` from the controls they hold ([25 §3](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#3-typed-forms-formbuilder-and-nonnullableformbuilder))
- <a id="typed-reactive-forms"></a>**typed reactive forms**: reactive forms whose controls carry a value type (`FormControl<string | null>`), strict by default since Angular 14; `nonNullable` removes the `null` ([07 §9](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#9-typing-patterns-in-angular-code)).

## U

- <a id="ui-library"></a>**UI library**: code that renders components into a root you give it and leaves routing, data, forms, build and tests to you ([40 §1](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#1-library-framework-progressive-framework-what-each-project-decides-for-you))
- <a id="ui-state"></a>**UI state**: state local to one component or feature, such as open panels and selection ([28 §1](modules/28-state-management.md#1-kinds-of-state-server-cache-ui-form-url-and-session-state-and-where-each-belongs))
- <a id="undo-stack-redo-stack-and-history-bound"></a>**undo stack**, **redo stack** and **history bound**: the `past` and `future` stacks around the `present`, and the cap on how many past values are kept ([28 §10](modules/28-state-management.md#10-optimistic-updates-normalization-and-undo))
- <a id="unexpected-error"></a>**unexpected error**: a failure nobody on the screen can fix, sent to the `ErrorHandler` with a generic message ([37 §10](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#10-error-ux-expected-and-unexpected-errors-http-failures-offline-state-and-route-errors))
- <a id="unhandled-rejection"></a>**unhandled rejection**: a rejected promise with no handler after a microtask checkpoint; browsers report it, Node crashes by default ([04 §8](modules/04-js-async-event-loop.md#8-unhandled-rejections-queuemicrotask-and-starvation)). · also in [04 §8](modules/04-js-async-event-loop.md#8-unhandled-rejections-queuemicrotask-and-starvation)
- <a id="unicast--multicast"></a>**unicast / multicast**: unicast: one consumer observes a producer; multicast: many consumers observe one producer ([22 §3](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#3-cold-and-hot-unicast-and-multicast))
- <a id="unidirectional-data-flow"></a>**unidirectional data flow**: data goes down through inputs and events go up through outputs ([45 §4](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#4-component-architecture-data-model-and-data-flow-from-screens-to-a-component-tree-and-a-client-side-model))
- <a id="unit-of-re-execution"></a>**unit of re-execution**: the code that runs again after a write: a view, a component subtree, a component or one expression ([40 §9](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#9-under-the-hood-one-state-change-traced-through-angular-react-vue-and-solid))
- <a id="unit-test"></a>**unit test**: a test that runs one function or class with its collaborators replaced by test doubles ([29 §1](modules/29-testing-angular.md#1-what-to-test-where-unit-integration-component-and-end-to-end-tests-the-pyramid-and-the-trophy))
- <a id="unknown"></a>**unknown**: the safe top type: every value is assignable to it, and nothing can be done with it before narrowing ([06 §5](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#5-any-unknown-never-void-and-object-versus-)).
- <a id="unknown-outcome"></a>**unknown outcome**: the state after a lost response; not a failure, settled by a retry with the same key or a status read ([46 §8](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#8-under-the-hood-a-money-transfer-with-idempotency-keys-optimistic-ui-that-never-claims-success-and-the-unknown-outcome))
- <a id="unprivileged-image"></a>**unprivileged image**: the `nginx-unprivileged` image, which runs Nginx as a non-root user (UID 101) on port 8080 ([44 §2](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#2-a-docker-multi-stage-build-for-angular-build-and-runtime-stages-layer-caching-dockerignore-and-a-non-root-image))
- <a id="unread-set"></a>**unread set**: the ids of delivered notifications that have no read receipt; the count is derived from it ([46 §7](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#7-a-notifications-center-and-an-editing-indicator-delivery-unread-counts-across-tabs-and-presence-with-a-time-to-live))
- <a id="unrecoverable-state"></a>**unrecoverable state**: a tab whose app version the worker can no longer serve, so only a reload helps ([37 §6](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#6-updates-versions-swupdate-prompting-a-reload-unrecoverable-states-and-the-kill-switch))
- <a id="unregister-function"></a>**unregister function**: the function `DestroyRef.onDestroy` returns; calling it removes the callback ([20 §5](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#5-teardown-ngondestroy-destroyref-and-takeuntildestroyed))
- <a id="unsafe-prefix"></a>**unsafe: prefix**: what Angular prepends to a URL it rejects ([33 §3](modules/33-security.md#3-sanitization-by-security-context-html-style-url-and-resource-url))
- <a id="unsubscribable"></a>**Unsubscribable**: any object with an `unsubscribe()` method; one of the forms a producer's teardown may take ([22 §2](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#2-anatomy-observable-observer-subscription-and-teardown))
- <a id="unsubscription-strategy"></a>**unsubscription strategy**: the choice of who ends a subscription: the stream (it completes) or the owner (`takeUntilDestroyed`, the `async` pipe, `toSignal`) ([23 §6](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#6-unsubscription-strategies-in-angular-and-finding-leaks))
- <a id="untracked"></a>**untracked**: runs a function without recording its signal reads as dependencies ([17 §3](modules/17-signals.md#3-computed-signals)).
- <a id="untracked-read"></a>**untracked read**: a signal read that registers no dependency; lifecycle hooks run this way ([20 §3](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#3-which-hooks-still-matter-with-signals))
- <a id="untypedformgroup"></a>**UntypedFormGroup**: an alias of `FormGroup<any>` (and its siblings `UntypedFormControl`, `UntypedFormArray`, `UntypedFormBuilder`) that opts out of the typed checks; not marked deprecated in the typings ([25 §3](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#3-typed-forms-formbuilder-and-nonnullableformbuilder))
- <a id="update-banner"></a>**update banner**: a notice that a new app version is ready, with a button that reloads the page into it ([37 Exercise 37.4](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#ex37-4))
- <a id="update-guide"></a>**Update Guide**: the update.angular.dev page that turns a from-version, a to-version and an app level into a before, during and after checklist ([39 §7](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#7-updating-in-practice-ng-update-migration-schematics-and-the-update-guide))
- <a id="update-pass-i18n"></a>**update pass (i18n)**: a later run of a view's template, which applies only the update opcodes whose bindings changed ([34 §12](modules/34-i18n.md#12-under-the-hood-from-an-i18n-attribute-to-translated-dom))
- <a id="updatemode"></a>**updateMode**: an asset group's setting for changed files when a new version arrives: `prefetch` at once, `lazy` on next request ([37 §5](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#5-caching-with-asset-and-data-groups-navigation-requests-and-the-app-shell))
- <a id="updateon"></a>**updateOn**: the option (`'change'`, `'blur'` or `'submit'`) that chooses which view event writes the value to the model; it gates only view-to-model ([25 §7](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#7-status-updateon-valuechanges-and-the-unified-events-stream))
- <a id="upgrade-custom-element"></a>**upgrade (custom element)**: the browser turning each existing or later-created tag of a defined name into an instance of its class ([37 §1](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#1-angular-elements-turning-a-component-into-a-custom-element))
- <a id="upgrade-ngupgrade"></a>**upgrade (ngUpgrade)**: wrap an AngularJS component or service so Angular can use it ([39 §9](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#9-from-angularjs-to-angular-ngupgrade-hybrids-the-strangler-fig-and-rewrites))
- <a id="upgrade-coupling"></a>**upgrade coupling**: how much of a library's public API surface, or of the code built on it, must change when the library underneath releases ([36 §12](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#12-a-design-system-on-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-or-a-wrapper-around-material))
- <a id="uploadstate"></a>**UploadState**: the exercise's union of `progress` (a rounded-down percent), `done` (the server id) and `failed` (the error) that an upload Observable emits instead of raw events ([27 Exercise 27.5](modules/27-http-client.md#ex27-5))
- <a id="upstream"></a>**upstream**: the server a reverse proxy forwards requests to, here Spring Boot ([44 §4](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#4-nginx-as-the-reverse-proxy-proxy_pass-forwarded-headers-into-spring-boot-and-sse-or-websocket-connections-through-the-proxy))
- <a id="url-segment"></a>**URL segment**: one part of the URL path between slashes, the unit the router consumes while matching ([24 §1](modules/24-routing.md#1-router-configuration-providerouter-route-matching-redirects-and-the-wildcard))
- <a id="url-state"></a>**URL state**: state that must survive a reload and be shareable, kept in route and query parameters ([28 §1](modules/28-state-management.md#1-kinds-of-state-server-cache-ui-form-url-and-session-state-and-where-each-belongs))
- <a id="urltree"></a>**UrlTree**: the parsed form of a URL: segment groups, segments with matrix parameters, one query and one fragment ([24 §2](modules/24-routing.md#2-route-parameters-path-matrix-query-and-fragment-inheritance-and-component-input-binding)) · also in [29 §9](modules/29-testing-angular.md#9-testing-guards-interceptors-forms-effects-resources-and-stores)
- <a id="useclass"></a>**useClass**: the provider recipe that constructs a class for a token; two tokens on one class give two instances ([16 §2](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#2-providers-and-tokens-useclass-usevalue-usefactory-and-useexisting)).
- <a id="useexisting"></a>**useExisting**: the provider recipe that makes an alias, answering with the instance of another token ([16 §2](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#2-providers-and-tokens-useclass-usevalue-usefactory-and-useexisting)).
- <a id="usefactory"></a>**useFactory**: the provider recipe that calls a factory function in an injection context, once per injector ([16 §2](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#2-providers-and-tokens-useclass-usevalue-usefactory-and-useexisting)).
- <a id="usepopover"></a>**usePopover**: the overlay config option that makes the host a `popover="manual"` element in the top layer; `true` by default, and off where `showPopover` does not exist ([36 Q36.10](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#q36-10))
- <a id="user-destination"></a>**user destination**: a `/user/…` destination that Spring resolves to one destination per session of a user ([43 §5](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#5-websockets-and-stomp-in-spring-the-handshake-frames-messagemapping-brokers-user-destinations-and-heart-beats))
- <a id="user-journey"></a>**user journey**: a path through the app that delivers one outcome, such as adding a todo and finding it after a reload ([30 §1](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#1-what-belongs-at-the-end-to-end-level-journeys-cost-and-a-strategy-across-levels))
- <a id="user-registry-broadcast"></a>**user registry broadcast**: a destination on which each instance shares its user registry every 10 s, so another instance can resolve a user's sessions ([43 §9](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#9-scaling-out-and-under-the-hood-sticky-sessions-the-broker-relay-backpressure-and-one-message-traced-across-two-instances))
- <a id="userinfo-endpoint"></a>**userinfo endpoint**: the OpenID Connect endpoint that returns claims about the signed-in user when called with an access token; it is an OAuth 2.0 protected resource ([Q42.06](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#q42-06))
- <a id="usevalue"></a>**useValue**: the provider recipe that hands over one given value, stored by reference ([16 §2](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#2-providers-and-tokens-useclass-usevalue-usefactory-and-useexisting)).
- <a id="utc"></a>**UTC**: the zero offset from which time zone offsets and instants are measured ([34 §10](modules/34-i18n.md#10-dates-times-numbers-and-money-time-zones-currency-codes-and-minor-units))
- <a id="utf-16-code-unit"></a>**UTF-16 code unit**: the 16-bit unit a JavaScript string is made of; `length` and indexing count these ([01 §3](modules/01-js-values-types-coercion.md#3-strings-and-unicode)).
- <a id="utility-type"></a>**utility type**: a generic type alias shipped in TypeScript's `lib` files (`Partial`, `Pick`, `Omit`, `ReturnType`, `Awaited`, …), written as an ordinary mapped or conditional type ([07 §4](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#4-utility-types-built-in-and-by-hand)).

## V

- <a id="validate"></a>**validate**: the rule function that takes a path and a function returning an error, a list of errors or nothing, and reruns when the signals it reads change ([26 §4](modules/26-signal-forms.md#4-schema-rules-and-validation-built-in-rules-validate-and-reusable-schemas)).
- <a id="validateasync"></a>**validateAsync**: attaches a `resource` to a field; `params`, `factory`, `onSuccess` and `onError` (plus optional `debounce` and `when`) describe it ([26 §6](modules/26-signal-forms.md#6-async-validation-and-debounce-validateasync-validatehttp-pending-and-cancellation)).
- <a id="validatehttp"></a>**validateHttp**: `validateAsync` with an `httpResource` as the factory; `request` returns a URL or a request object ([26 §6](modules/26-signal-forms.md#6-async-validation-and-debounce-validateasync-validatehttp-pending-and-cancellation)).
- <a id="validatetree"></a>**validateTree**: a rule that returns errors carrying a target `fieldTree`, which decides which field shows each error ([26 §5](modules/26-signal-forms.md#5-cross-field-and-conditional-logic-valueof-validatetree-applywhen-disabled-hidden-and-readonly)).
- <a id="validationerror"></a>**ValidationError**: the object a rule reports, with a `kind`, an optional `message` and rule parameters such as `min` ([26 §4](modules/26-signal-forms.md#4-schema-rules-and-validation-built-in-rules-validate-and-reusable-schemas)).
- <a id="validationresult"></a>**ValidationResult**: what a `validate` function returns: `null`, `undefined` or `void` for no error, or one error or a list ([26 §4](modules/26-signal-forms.md#4-schema-rules-and-validation-built-in-rules-validate-and-reusable-schemas)).
- <a id="validator"></a>**validator**: a token naming the version of a stored response (`ETag`, `Last-Modified`) that a conditional request sends back to the origin ([09 §4](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#4-http-caching)).
- <a id="validator-and-ng_validators"></a>**Validator and NG_VALIDATORS**: the interface whose `validate(control)` returns an error map or `null`, and the multi-provider token that registers it on a control ([25 §10](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#10-controlvalueaccessor-and-the-validator-interface-custom-controls-under-the-hood))
- <a id="validator-and-validatorfn"></a>**validator and ValidatorFn**: a function `(control) => ValidationErrors | null` that every control runs on each validation; `null` means valid ([25 §5](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#5-validators-built-in-custom-and-cross-field))
- <a id="validator-factory"></a>**validator factory**: a function that returns a new validator closed over its arguments, such as `Validators.minLength(3)`, so each call returns a different function ([25 §5](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#5-validators-built-in-custom-and-cross-field))
- <a id="validity-rule"></a>**validity rule**: a function of the answers that says whether a step's answers are acceptable ([46 §5](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#5-a-multi-step-form-wizard-with-draft-saving-a-step-model-per-step-validation-and-drafts-that-survive-and-migrate))
- <a id="value-accessor-controlvalueaccessor"></a>**value accessor (ControlValueAccessor)**: the adapter between a form control and a DOM element or custom component ([25 §1](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#1-two-forms-apis-template-driven-and-reactive-and-the-control-directive-and-accessor-triangle))
- <a id="valuechanges-and-statuschanges"></a>**valueChanges and statusChanges**: the Observables of a control that emit its new value and its new status after each change, and not on subscribe ([25 §7](modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#7-status-updateon-valuechanges-and-the-unified-events-stream))
- <a id="valueof"></a>**valueOf**: the context function that reads another field's value inside a rule, so the rule reruns when it changes ([26 §5](modules/26-signal-forms.md#5-cross-field-and-conditional-logic-valueof-validatetree-applywhen-disabled-hidden-and-readonly)).
- <a id="vapid"></a>**VAPID**: Voluntary Application Server Identification, a signed ES256 JWT in an `Authorization: vapid` header that tells the push service who sends (RFC 8292) ([43 §1](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#1-choosing-a-transport-polling-long-polling-server-sent-events-websockets-and-web-push-when-no-tab-is-open))
- <a id="vapid-key"></a>**VAPID key**: the P-256 public key passed when subscribing, which the push service uses to authenticate your application server ([37 §7](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#7-push-notifications-and-installability-swpush-notification-clicks-and-the-manifest))
- <a id="variable-filter"></a>**variable filter**: `NGINX_ENVSUBST_FILTER`, a pattern that limits which defined environment variables the nginx image's template step lists for `envsubst` ([44 §5](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#5-runtime-configuration-without-rebuilding-configjson-environment-substitution-at-container-start-and-provideappinitializer))
- <a id="variance-annotation"></a>**variance annotation**: `in`, `out` or `in out` on a type parameter, checked by the compiler ([07 §5](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#5-variance-parameter-bivariance-and-strictfunctiontypes)).
- <a id="variant"></a>**variant**: a named value a flag can resolve to ([44 §8](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#8-feature-flags-with-openfeature-and-canary-releases-providers-evaluation-context-sticky-rollout-kill-switches-and-version-skew))
- <a id="vary"></a>**Vary**: the response header that tells caches which request headers the response depends on ([34 §7](modules/34-i18n.md#7-compile-time-localization-one-build-per-locale-subpath-lang-and-dir))
- <a id="vary-accept-encoding"></a>**Vary: Accept-Encoding**: a response header telling caches to keep a copy per `Accept-Encoding` value, owned by [09 §4](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#4-http-caching) ([44 §3](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#3-nginx-for-a-single-page-app-location-matching-the-try_files-fallback-caching-headers-for-hashed-assets-and-gzip-or-brotli))
- <a id="verbatimmodulesyntax"></a>**verbatimModuleSyntax**: the rule that imports and exports are emitted exactly as written, so type-only ones must say `type` ([06 §7](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#7-tsconfig-the-strict-family-module-settings-and-typescript-60-defaults)).
- <a id="verify"></a>**verify**: the controller method that throws when requests are still open; `ignoreCancelled` skips cancelled ones ([27 §11](modules/27-http-client.md#11-testing-and-the-backend-seam-providehttpclienttesting-httptestingcontroller-and-what-they-replace))
- <a id="version--epoch"></a>**version / epoch**: a node's change counter, versus the global counter of writes used as a fast "nothing changed anywhere" check ([17 §9](modules/17-signals.md#9-under-the-hood-the-reactive-graph)).
- <a id="version-hash-service-worker"></a>**version hash (service worker)**: the SHA-1 of an `ngsw.json`, which names one app version inside the worker ([37 §6](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#6-updates-versions-swupdate-prompting-a-reload-unrecoverable-states-and-the-kill-switch))
- <a id="version-map-angular-service-worker"></a>**version map (Angular service worker)**: the driver's internal map from each tab's client ID to the version hash serving it ([37 §13](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#13-under-the-hood-createcustomelements-strategy-and-the-service-workers-driver))
- <a id="version-skew"></a>**version skew**: one tab holding files from two releases, such as `index.html` from one and a chunk from another ([44 §8](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#8-feature-flags-with-openfeature-and-canary-releases-providers-evaluation-context-sticky-rollout-kill-switches-and-version-skew)) · also in [44 §8](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#8-feature-flags-with-openfeature-and-canary-releases-providers-evaluation-context-sticky-rollout-kill-switches-and-version-skew)
- <a id="version_ready"></a>**VERSION_READY**: the `SwUpdate.versionUpdates` event saying a newer version is cached while the tab still runs an older one ([37 §6](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#6-updates-versions-swupdate-prompting-a-reload-unrecoverable-states-and-the-kill-switch))
- <a id="vestibular-motion-disorder"></a>**vestibular motion disorder**: a balance-system condition in which on-screen motion can cause discomfort, as MDN describes it ([35 §11](modules/35-animations.md#11-performance-and-reduced-motion-compositor-properties-measuring-jank-and-prefers-reduced-motion))
- <a id="vifn"></a>**vi.fn**: Vitest's factory for a spy that can also be given canned answers; records calls in `mock.calls` ([29 §7](modules/29-testing-angular.md#7-test-doubles-fakes-stubs-spies-and-mocks-vifn-provider-overrides-and-over-mocking))
- <a id="vispyon"></a>**vi.spyOn**: Vitest's way to spy on an existing method, keeping the original implementation until overridden; undone by `mockRestore` or `vi.restoreAllMocks` ([29 §7](modules/29-testing-angular.md#7-test-doubles-fakes-stubs-spies-and-mocks-vifn-provider-overrides-and-over-mocking))
- <a id="view"></a>**view**: one rendered instance of a template, with its own nodes and binding slots ([12 §8](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it)). · also in [13 §1](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#1-component-anatomy-selector-template-styles-imports-and-the-v22-file-convention), [16 §6](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#6-providers-versus-viewproviders-and-per-instance-state), [20 §7](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#7-under-the-hood-hook-lists-the-view-refresh-and-the-render-hook-queue)
- <a id="view-check"></a>**view check**: Angular re-evaluating a template's bindings; owner [21](modules/21-change-detection.md) ([14 §7](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#7-pipe-computed-or-method-call-in-a-template)).
- <a id="view-container"></a>**view container**: a place in a template that holds views; `ViewContainerRef` is its handle ([19 §7](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#7-dynamic-components-viewcontainerrefcreatecomponent-createcomponent-and-bindings))
- <a id="view-encapsulation"></a>**view encapsulation**: how a component's CSS is kept from affecting other elements: Emulated (compiler rewrite), ShadowDom (browser shadow root) or None ([13 §9](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#9-view-encapsulation-emulated-shadowdom-and-none-and-what-the-compiler-does-to-your-css)).
- <a id="view-engine"></a>**View Engine**: the compiler and runtime Angular used before Ivy became the default in v9 ([39 §5](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#5-the-version-matrix-part-2-v9-to-v15-from-ivy-to-standalone); owner [12 §8](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it))
- <a id="view-flag--dirty-flag"></a>**view flag / dirty flag**: a view flag is an internal bit on a view (`Dirty`, `RefreshView`, `HasChildViewsToRefresh`) saying it needs a refresh; a dirty flag is an internal bit on the `ApplicationRef` (`ViewTreeGlobal`, `ViewTreeTraversal`, `ViewTreeCheck`, `AfterRender`, `RootEffects`) saying a pass is owed ([21 §9](modules/21-change-detection.md#9-under-the-hood-the-notification-the-dirty-flags-and-the-refresh))
- <a id="view-model"></a>**view model**: the shape the screens use, mapped from a DTO ([38 §3](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#3-facades-and-the-data-access-layer))
- <a id="view-query"></a>**view query**: a query over the elements of the component's own template; never into another component's template ([19 §5](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#5-queries-view-and-content-queries-with-signal-queries))
- <a id="view-reference"></a>**view reference**: a handle on one view; `ChangeDetectorRef` is its public type and `ViewRef` the class behind it, with the verbs `markForCheck`, `detectChanges`, `detach` and `reattach` ([21 §7](modules/21-change-detection.md#7-changedetectorref-markforcheck-detectchanges-detach-and-reattach))
- <a id="view-slot"></a>**view slot**: a cell in a view's storage reserved for one binding, where a pipe usage keeps its last arguments and result; owner [12 §8](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it) ([14 §5](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#5-pipes-pure-and-impure-custom-pipes-injection-and-imports)).
- <a id="view-transition"></a>**view transition**: the browser animating from the old state of the page to the new one, started by `document.startViewTransition(update)` ([35 §10](modules/35-animations.md#10-view-transitions-with-the-router-withviewtransitions-view-transition-name-and-onviewtransitioncreated))
- <a id="view-transition-type"></a>**view transition type**: a label on one view transition that CSS can match to pick an animation ([35 §10](modules/35-animations.md#10-view-transitions-with-the-router-withviewtransitions-view-transition-name-and-onviewtransitioncreated))
- <a id="view-transitions-api-startviewtransition"></a>**View Transitions API (startViewTransition)**: the browser API that animates between the old and new DOM; the router calls it with `withViewTransitions` ([24 §9](modules/24-routing.md#9-view-transitions-scroll-restoration-and-titles))
- <a id="view-transition-name"></a>**view-transition-name**: the CSS property that gives an element its own group in the transition; the page itself is the group `root` ([35 §10](modules/35-animations.md#10-view-transitions-with-the-router-withviewtransitions-view-transition-name-and-onviewtransitioncreated))
- <a id="viewcontainerref"></a>**ViewContainerRef**: the place on the page, anchored by a comment node, where embedded views are created; their root elements become its siblings ([14 §2](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#2-structural-directives-ng-template-templateref-viewcontainerref-and-the-microsyntax)). · also in [35 §6](modules/35-animations.md#6-function-callbacks-and-the-v22-leave-semantics-animationcallbackevent-animationcomplete-and-nested-leaves)
- <a id="viewencapsulationshadowdom"></a>**ViewEncapsulation.ShadowDom**: component encapsulation that renders the template into a shadow root, so its styles stay inside it ([37 §3](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#3-elements-at-run-time-lifecycle-change-detection-without-zonejs-styles-and-several-apps-on-one-page))
- <a id="viewport"></a>**viewport**: the visible area of the page in which it is laid out; `vw` is a percentage of its width ([10 §5](modules/10-css-essentials.md#5-responsive-css-media-queries-container-queries-and-fluid-type)).
- <a id="viewportscroller"></a>**ViewportScroller**: the Angular service wrapping the browser's scroll calls (`scrollToPosition`, `scrollToAnchor`, `setHistoryScrollRestoration`) ([24 §9](modules/24-routing.md#9-view-transitions-scroll-restoration-and-titles))
- <a id="viewproviders"></a>**viewProviders**: the component metadata array of providers visible only to the component's own view, not to projected content ([16 §6](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#6-providers-versus-viewproviders-and-per-instance-state)).
- <a id="viewtransition"></a>**ViewTransition**: the object `startViewTransition` returns, with `ready`, `finished` and `skipTransition()` ([35 §10](modules/35-animations.md#10-view-transitions-with-the-router-withviewtransitions-view-transition-name-and-onviewtransitioncreated))
- <a id="violation--incomplete-review-item"></a>**violation / incomplete (review item)**: a rule result that failed, versus one the engine could neither pass nor fail and leaves for a person ([11 §7](modules/11-accessibility.md#7-testing-accessibility)).
- <a id="violation-report"></a>**violation report**: the report a browser sends (to `report-to`) when a CSP rule blocks, or in report-only mode would block, something ([09 §8](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#8-content-security-policy-and-trusted-types)). · also in [33 §5](modules/33-security.md#5-content-security-policy-with-angular-nonces-csp_nonce-and-autocsp)
- <a id="virtual-dom"></a>**virtual DOM**: a tree of plain objects that render functions return; the runtime diffs each new tree against the old and writes only the differences to the DOM ([40 Exercise 40.1](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#ex40-1))
- <a id="virtual-file"></a>**virtual file**: a module that exists only inside the build, such as the unit-test builder's init files ([29 §2](modules/29-testing-angular.md#2-the-runner-vitest-through-angularbuildunit-test-jsdom-and-browser-mode))
- <a id="virtual-scrolling"></a>**virtual scrolling**: keeping only the rows in and near the viewport in the DOM, also called windowing ([31 §4](modules/31-performance.md#4-long-lists-track-virtual-scrolling-with-the-cdk-and-the-alternatives))
- <a id="virtual-time"></a>**virtual time**: the clock of `TestScheduler.run`, advanced by the test instead of the wall clock ([23 §1](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#1-higher-order-observables-switchmap-mergemap-concatmap-and-exhaustmap)) · also in [23 §10](modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#10-testing-with-testscheduler-marble-syntax-and-virtual-time-under-the-hood)
- <a id="visibility-rule"></a>**visibility rule**: a function of the earlier answers that says whether a step is shown ([46 §5](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#5-a-multi-step-form-wizard-with-draft-saving-a-step-model-per-step-validation-and-drafts-that-survive-and-migrate))
- <a id="visibilitychange"></a>**visibilitychange**: the event that fires when a tab becomes hidden or visible; MDN calls it the most reliable moment to send last data ([44 §9](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#9-frontend-error-tracking-and-real-user-monitoring-releases-source-maps-beacons-and-sampling))
- <a id="visual-regression-test"></a>**visual regression test**: a test that compares a screenshot with a baseline image and fails when the pixel diff passes a tolerance ([30 §6](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#6-visual-regression-and-accessibility-checks-in-end-to-end-tests)) · also in [38 §10](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#10-storybook-for-component-development)
- <a id="vite"></a>**Vite**: the development server behind `ng serve`, not the production bundler ([12 §3](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#3-building-the-application-builder-output-layout-and-budgets)).
- <a id="vitest"></a>**Vitest**: the test runner the labs use; it transforms files with a per-file tool, so it does not type-check ([06 header](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md)). · also in [13](modules/13-components-and-templates.md)
- <a id="vnode"></a>**vnode**: one node of a virtual DOM (an element, a text or a component) that keeps the DOM node it was mounted with ([40 Exercise 40.1](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#ex40-1))
- <a id="void-return-type"></a>**void (return type)**: "the caller ignores the result"; a function type returning `void` may still be implemented by one that returns a value ([06 §5](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#5-any-unknown-never-void-and-object-versus-)).
- <a id="void-state"></a>**void state**: the state of an element that is not in the DOM; `:enter` is `void => *` and `:leave` is `* => void` ([35 §8](modules/35-animations.md#8-legacy-angularanimations-triggers-states-transitions-query-and-stagger))

## W

- <a id="wai-aria-pattern-apg"></a>**WAI-ARIA pattern (APG)**: the W3C ARIA Authoring Practices Guide's description of a widget's keyboard behavior, roles and states, such as the listbox or tabs pattern ([36 §1](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#1-material-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-styled-components-behavior-primitives-and-headless-patterns))
- <a id="watch-mode"></a>**watch mode**: a run that repeats the affected specs on every file change ([29 §2](modules/29-testing-angular.md#2-the-runner-vitest-through-angularbuildunit-test-jsdom-and-browser-mode))
- <a id="watchdog-timer"></a>**watchdog (timer)**: a timer that every received frame restarts; if it fires, the connection has been silent too long and is treated as dead ([Exercise 43.2](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#ex43-2))
- <a id="watcher-angularjs"></a>**watcher (AngularJS)**: a function that reads a value, plus a listener that runs when the value differs from the last one seen ([39 §1](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#1-angularjs-scopes-watchers-and-the-digest-cycle))
- <a id="wave-task-order"></a>**wave (task order)**: the tasks whose last dependency finished in the previous wave, so they could run in parallel ([Exercise 38.4](modules/38-architecture-and-production-structure.md#ex38-4))
- <a id="wcag"></a>**WCAG**: the W3C's Web Content Accessibility Guidelines, a layered standard of principles, guidelines and testable success criteria ([11 §1](modules/11-accessibility.md#1-why-accessibility-and-what-the-standards-require)).
- <a id="wcag-tag"></a>**WCAG tag**: an axe label that selects the rules tied to one WCAG level, such as `wcag2aa` ([30 §6](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#6-visual-regression-and-accessibility-checks-in-end-to-end-tests))
- <a id="weak-reference"></a>**weak reference**: a reference the collector ignores when deciding reachability ([05 §5](modules/05-js-modules-memory-modern-features.md#5-weak-references-weakmap-weakset-weakref-and-finalizationregistry)).
- <a id="weak-type"></a>**weak type**: an object type whose properties are all optional; a value sharing none of them is rejected ([06 §2](modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#2-structural-typing-assignability-and-readonly)).
- <a id="web-animations-api"></a>**Web Animations API**: the platform API through which script creates and controls animations (`element.animate()`, `Animation`, `getAnimations()`) ([35 §1](modules/35-animations.md#1-motion-in-a-ui-what-to-animate-the-browsers-three-animation-mechanisms-and-where-angular-fits))
- <a id="web-app-manifest"></a>**web app manifest**: a JSON file (`name`, `icons`, `start_url`, `display`) that tells the browser how to install a site as an app ([09 §9](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#9-service-workers-and-the-pwa-manifest)). · also in [09 §9](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#9-service-workers-and-the-pwa-manifest)
- <a id="web-locks-api"></a>**Web Locks API**: a browser API for named locks that only one script of an origin can hold at a time, across its tabs and workers ([42 §4](modules/42-fullstack-authentication.md#4-refresh-tokens-rotation-reuse-detection-silent-renew-and-refreshing-across-tabs))
- <a id="web-push"></a>**Web Push**: delivering a message to a browser with no tab open, through the browser vendor's push service (RFC 8030) ([43 §1](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#1-choosing-a-transport-polling-long-polling-server-sent-events-websockets-and-web-push-when-no-tab-is-open))
- <a id="web-storage"></a>**Web Storage**: `localStorage` and `sessionStorage`, a small synchronous string key-value store per origin ([09 §7](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#7-browser-storage-what-each-is-safe-for)).
- <a id="web-worker"></a>**Web Worker**: a script that runs on its own thread and talks to the page by messages ([04 §1](modules/04-js-async-event-loop.md#1-the-event-loop-tasks-microtasks-and-rendering)). · also in [31 §9](modules/31-performance.md#9-off-the-main-thread-web-workers-in-angular-and-runoutsideangular-in-zone-apps)
- <a id="web-first-assertion"></a>**web-first assertion**: an `expect(locator)` matcher that re-fetches the element and retries until it passes or the `expect` timeout (5 s) ends ([30 §3](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#3-auto-waiting-actionability-checks-web-first-assertions-and-cypress-retry-ability))
- <a id="webdriver"></a>**WebDriver**: the W3C remote control interface for introspecting and controlling browsers ([30 §2](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#2-playwright-and-cypress-architecture-ng-e2e-and-the-end-of-protractor))
- <a id="webflux"></a>**WebFlux**: Spring's reactive, non-blocking web stack, where a controller can return a `Flux` of events ([43 §3](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#3-server-sent-events-in-spring-sseemitter-in-spring-mvc-and-a-flux-of-serversentevent-in-webflux))
- <a id="websocket"></a>**WebSocket**: one HTTP request upgraded into a two-way channel of frames, with no reconnection or message format built in (RFC 6455) ([43 §1](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#1-choosing-a-transport-polling-long-polling-server-sent-events-websockets-and-web-push-when-no-tab-is-open))
- <a id="websocket--websocketsubject"></a>**webSocket / WebSocketSubject**: RxJS's Subject over one browser socket: `next` sends, subscribers receive, a clean close completes it and any other close or an `error` event errors it ([43 §6](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#6-the-angular-websocket-client-rxjs-websocket-stomprx-stomp-and-reconnecting-without-losing-subscriptions))
- <a id="websocketsubject"></a>**WebSocketSubject**: the `Subject` from `rxjs/webSocket` that sends to and receives from a WebSocket ([22 §4](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#4-subjects-subject-behaviorsubject-replaysubject-and-asyncsubject))
- <a id="weighted-criterion"></a>**weighted criterion**: a property every remaining option is scored on, with a weight the team sets before scoring ([40 §8](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#8-choosing-a-framework-balanced-trade-offs-and-how-to-answer-why-angular))
- <a id="weighted-sum"></a>**weighted sum**: a score per candidate made by multiplying each criterion's score by its weight and adding the products; shown divided by the weight total ([40 Exercise 40.2](modules/40-framework-comparisons.md#ex40-2))
- <a id="well-known-symbol"></a>**well-known symbol**: a symbol the language itself looks up, such as `Symbol.iterator`, `Symbol.toPrimitive` or `Symbol.toStringTag` ([01 §4](modules/01-js-values-types-coercion.md#4-symbols)).
- <a id="widget"></a>**widget**: a small view of one metric on a dashboard ([46 §6](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#6-a-real-time-operations-dashboard-aggregation-per-widget-staleness-hidden-tabs-and-screens-left-open-for-days))
- <a id="widget--composite-widget"></a>**widget / composite widget**: an interactive control, and one that contains items such as a menu or tab list ([11 §3](modules/11-accessibility.md#3-aria-roles-states-properties-and-its-rules)).
- <a id="width-descriptor"></a>**width descriptor**: a `srcset` suffix such as `640w`, the file's width in pixels ([31 §7](modules/31-performance.md#7-images-ngoptimizedimage-with-priority-fill-loaders-and-placeholders))
- <a id="wildcard-height"></a>**wildcard height**: the legacy `height: '*'` style, which animates to the element's natural content height; in CSS, a `grid-template-rows` row or `calc-size()` ([35 §9](modules/35-animations.md#9-migrating-from-angularanimations-to-native-css-and-animateenteranimateleave))
- <a id="wildcard-route"></a>**wildcard route**: the route with path `'**'`, which matches any remaining URL and keeps it ([24 §1](modules/24-routing.md#1-router-configuration-providerouter-route-matching-redirects-and-the-wildcard))
- <a id="wildcard-slot"></a>**wildcard slot**: an `<ng-content>` without `select`, which takes the nodes no other slot claimed ([19 §3](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#3-content-projection-ng-content-slots-ngprojectas-fallback-content-and-the-lifecycle-trap))
- <a id="wildcard-state-"></a>**wildcard state (*)**: matches any state in a transition expression ([35 §8](modules/35-animations.md#8-legacy-angularanimations-triggers-states-transitions-query-and-stagger))
- <a id="window"></a>**Window**: Spring Data's result of one scroll step: a chunk of items from which a scroll position for the next step is taken ([41 §6](modules/41-fullstack-api-contracts.md#6-pagination-sorting-and-filtering-offset-versus-cursor-and-how-spring-data-serializes-a-page))
- <a id="window-error-event"></a>**window error event**: the browser's report of an uncaught exception, which `provideBrowserGlobalErrorListeners()` forwards to the `ErrorHandler` ([37 §8](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#8-errorhandler-and-global-listeners-what-reaches-the-handler-and-what-does-not))
- <a id="windowing"></a>**windowing**: rendering only the rows that are visible ([45 §6](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#6-performance-and-accessibility-as-design-steps-budgets-a-loading-plan-long-lists-and-keyboard-and-pointer-paths))
- <a id="withcredentials"></a>**withCredentials**: the only option of `new EventSource(url, { withCredentials })`; it sets the credentials mode to `include` so cross-origin requests carry cookies, and no header can be set ([43 §4](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#4-the-angular-sse-client-eventsource-or-a-fetch-stream-as-an-observable-and-when-httpclient-fits))
- <a id="withentities-entitymap-and-entity-updater"></a>**withEntities**, **entityMap** and **entity updater**: the store feature that adds `ids`, `entityMap` and a computed `entities`, the lookup of entities by id, and a pure function such as `addEntity` passed to `patchState` ([28 §9](modules/28-state-management.md#9-signalstore-in-depth-rxmethod-withentities-custom-features-and-the-events-plugin))
- <a id="withinterceptors"></a>**withInterceptors**: the HTTP feature that lists functional interceptors; the first listed runs first on the way out ([27 §4](modules/27-http-client.md#4-interceptors-the-functional-chain-order-and-next-and-class-interceptors-through-withinterceptorsfromdi))
- <a id="withinterceptorsfromdi"></a>**withInterceptorsFromDi**: the HTTP feature that runs all `HTTP_INTERCEPTORS` class interceptors as one block at its position in the chain ([27 §4](modules/27-http-client.md#4-interceptors-the-functional-chain-order-and-next-and-class-interceptors-through-withinterceptorsfromdi))
- <a id="withlatestfrom"></a>**withLatestFrom**: an operator that emits only when its source emits, paired with the latest values of the other inputs ([22 §7](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#7-combining-streams-combinelatest-withlatestfrom-forkjoin-zip-merge-and-concat))
- <a id="withnoxsrfprotection"></a>**withNoXsrfProtection**: the HTTP feature that turns the built-in XSRF interceptor off ([27 §8](modules/27-http-client.md#8-xsrf-protection-how-httpclient-reads-the-cookie-and-sets-the-header-and-when-it-does-not))
- <a id="withreducer-witheventhandlers-and-injectdispatch"></a>**withReducer**, **withEventHandlers** and **injectDispatch**: the features that turn events into state changes and into side effects, and the function that returns dispatching functions ([28 §9](modules/28-state-management.md#9-signalstore-in-depth-rxmethod-withentities-custom-features-and-the-events-plugin))
- <a id="withrequestsmadeviaparent"></a>**withRequestsMadeViaParent**: the feature that sends a child injector's requests through its parent's `HttpClient` after the child's own interceptors ([27 §2](modules/27-http-client.md#2-setting-up-httpclient-providehttpclient-its-features-and-the-fetch-backend-default))
- <a id="withxhr"></a>**withXhr**: the feature that makes `HttpClient` use `XMLHttpRequest` through `HttpXhrBackend`, added in v22.0 ([27 §2](modules/27-http-client.md#2-setting-up-httpclient-providehttpclient-its-features-and-the-fetch-backend-default))
- <a id="withxsrfconfiguration"></a>**withXsrfConfiguration**: the HTTP feature that renames the XSRF cookie and header ([27 §8](modules/27-http-client.md#8-xsrf-protection-how-httpclient-reads-the-cookie-and-sets-the-header-and-when-it-does-not))
- <a id="wizard"></a>**wizard**: a form split into steps shown one at a time ([46 §5](modules/46-system-design-case-studies.md#5-a-multi-step-form-wizard-with-draft-saving-a-step-model-per-step-validation-and-drafts-that-survive-and-migrate))
- <a id="worker"></a>**worker**: a thread Vitest runs spec files in; the unit-test builder's init file sets up `TestBed` once in it ([29 §12](modules/29-testing-angular.md#12-under-the-hood-how-the-builder-boots-testbed-and-how-a-fixture-decides-it-is-stable))
- <a id="worker-process"></a>**worker process**: an OS process the test runner starts to run test files; each starts its own browser ([30 §2](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#2-playwright-and-cypress-architecture-ng-e2e-and-the-end-of-protractor))
- <a id="worker-scoped-fixture"></a>**worker-scoped fixture**: a fixture declared with `{ scope: 'worker' }`, set up once per worker process and torn down when the worker ends ([30 §4](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#4-locators-page-objects-and-fixtures))
- <a id="workspace-angular"></a>**workspace (Angular)**: the folder whose `angular.json` maps projects to targets ([12 §2](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#2-the-cli-and-the-workspace-angularjson-projects-schematics-and-ng-update)).
- <a id="wrapper-component"></a>**wrapper component**: your component that renders another library's component behind your own inputs, such as a design-system button around `matButton` ([36 §12](modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#12-a-design-system-on-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-or-a-wrapper-around-material))
- <a id="wrapper-object"></a>**wrapper object**: the temporary `String`, `Number`, `Boolean`, `Symbol` or `BigInt` object a primitive borrows its methods from; `new Boolean(false)` is a truthy object ([01 §1](modules/01-js-values-types-coercion.md#1-values-and-types)).
- <a id="writable-signal"></a>**writable signal**: a signal with `set`, `update` and `asReadonly`, whose writes notify only when the new value is not equal (`Object.is` by default) ([17 §2](modules/17-signals.md#2-writable-signals)).
- <a id="writing-direction"></a>**writing direction**: the way a line of text runs, `ltr` or `rtl` ([34 §11](modules/34-i18n.md#11-right-to-left-layouts-dir-logical-css-properties-and-bidirectional-text))
- <a id="writing-mode"></a>**writing mode**: the direction in which lines of text flow (horizontal or vertical, left-to-right or right-to-left) ([10 §5](modules/10-css-essentials.md#5-responsive-css-media-queries-container-queries-and-fluid-type)).

## X

- <a id="xhr-xmlhttprequest"></a>**XHR (XMLHttpRequest)**: the older, event-based request object, which reports upload and download progress ([09 §2](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#2-fetch-xhr-and-the-request-lifecycle)).
- <a id="xliff"></a>**XLIFF**: the XML Localization Interchange File Format, the default format of Angular's translation files (version 1.2 by default) ([34 §6](modules/34-i18n.md#6-extraction-and-translation-files-ng-extract-i18n-formats-and-message-ids))
- <a id="xsrf-cross-site-request-forgery-also-csrf"></a>**XSRF (cross-site request forgery, also CSRF)**: an attack in which another site makes the victim's browser send a state-changing request with the victim's cookies; depth in 33 ([27 §8](modules/27-http-client.md#8-xsrf-protection-how-httpclient-reads-the-cookie-and-sets-the-header-and-when-it-does-not))
- <a id="xss"></a>**XSS**: cross-site scripting, attacker-controlled text executing as script in your page; owner module 33 ([09 §8](modules/09-web-networking-storage-security.md#8-content-security-policy-and-trusted-types)).
- <a id="xss-cross-site-scripting"></a>**XSS (cross-site scripting)**: an attack that runs an attacker's script in your page; owner [33](modules/33-security.md) ([14 §1](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#1-directives-and-attribute-directives-host-inputs-elementref-and-renderer2)).
- <a id="xssi-cross-site-script-inclusion"></a>**XSSI (cross-site script inclusion)**: another site loading your JSON API in a `<script>` tag; servers prefix JSON with `)]}',` and `HttpClient` strips it ([33 §9](modules/33-security.md#9-csrf-in-single-page-apps-when-cookies-make-you-vulnerable-samesite-and-angulars-xsrf-support))

## Z

- <a id="zip"></a>**zip**: pairs the n-th values of its sources, buffering the faster ones ([22 §7](modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#7-combining-streams-combinelatest-withlatestfrom-forkjoin-zip-merge-and-concat))
- <a id="zone--zonejs"></a>**zone / zone.js**: an execution context that sees its tasks start and end, and the library that patches the browser's async APIs so callbacks run in one ([21 §3](modules/21-change-detection.md#3-zonejs-and-ngzone-how-changes-used-to-be-detected-and-why-zone-left-the-default))
