- <a id="zonejs"></a>**zone.js**: the library that patches the browser's async APIs so Angular learns *that* something may have changed; not used by zoneless apps ([17 §1](modules/17-signals.md#1-why-signals-exist)). · also in [39 §2](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#2-why-angular-was-rewritten-and-what-carried-over)
- <a id="zonejs--zone-based--zoneless"></a>**zone.js / zone-based / zoneless**: zone.js (ZoneJS in the CHANGELOG) patches the browser's async APIs so Angular learns something may have changed; a zone-based app loads it, a zoneless one does not; owner [17 §1](modules/17-signals.md#1-why-signals-exist) ([14 §8](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#8-the-async-pipe-under-the-hood)).
- <a id="zonecontextmanager"></a>**ZoneContextManager**: OpenTelemetry's zone.js-based context manager (`@opentelemetry/context-zone`), which follows async work but needs zone.js ([44 §10](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#10-under-the-hood-opentelemetry-for-the-web-traceparent-and-one-click-traced-to-a-spring-boot-span-and-log-line))
- <a id="zoneless"></a>**zoneless**: change detection without zone.js; `bootstrapModule` builds its root injector with the zoneless providers first; owner [module 21](modules/21-change-detection.md) ([15 §3](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#3-bootstrapping-and-providers-without-modules-bootstrapapplication-provide-functions-and-importprovidersfrom)). · also in [17 §1](modules/17-signals.md#1-why-signals-exist), [20 §1](modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#1-the-lifecycle-map-phases-and-the-order-of-the-hooks), [39 §6](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#6-the-version-matrix-part-3-v16-to-v22-from-signals-to-onpush-by-default)
- <a id="zoom-level"></a>**zoom level**: one of the three diagram levels used here, after C4: system context, containers, components ([45 §2](modules/45-frontend-system-design-method.md#2-communicating-under-time-pressure-a-time-plan-complete-trade-off-statements-and-diagrams-at-three-zoom-levels))

## Symbols

- <a id="101-switching-protocols"></a>**101 Switching Protocols**: the server's answer that accepts the upgrade; from then on the connection speaks WebSocket ([43 §5](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#5-websockets-and-stomp-in-spring-the-handshake-frames-messagemapping-brokers-user-destinations-and-heart-beats))
- <a id="angularformssignalscompat"></a>**@angular/forms/signals/compat**: the entry point with the adapters between Signal Forms and reactive forms (`compatForm`, `SignalFormControl`) ([26 §1](modules/26-signal-forms.md#1-why-a-third-forms-api-model-first-signals-end-to-end))
- <a id="apply"></a>**$apply**: runs your function, then a digest ([39 §1](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#1-angularjs-scopes-watchers-and-the-digest-cycle))
- <a id="term"></a>**<bdi>**: the HTML element that isolates its text from the surrounding text's direction, for text of unknown direction ([34 §11](modules/34-i18n.md#11-right-to-left-layouts-dir-logical-css-properties-and-bidirectional-text))
- <a id="--best-effort-mode"></a>**--best-effort-mode**: a schematic option that migrates even what it cannot prove safe, so the build may break ([19 §6](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#6-decorator-based-inputs-outputs-and-queries-and-the-migration-schematics))
- <a id="default-never"></a>**@default never**: a body-less last block that makes the type checker assign the switched value to `never`, so a missing case fails the build ([18 §3](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#3-switch-and-exhaustive-checking-with-default-never))
- <a id="deprecated-jsdoc-tag"></a>**@deprecated JSDoc tag**: a comment tag on a declaration that editors read to flag uses of it ([39 §3](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#3-how-angular-ships-semantic-versioning-the-release-cadence-support-windows-and-the-deprecation-policy))
- <a id="docker-entrypointd"></a>**/docker-entrypoint.d**: the folder whose executable `*.sh` files the nginx image's `docker-entrypoint.sh` runs, in `sort -V` order, before it starts Nginx ([44 §5](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#5-runtime-configuration-without-rebuilding-configjson-environment-substitution-at-container-start-and-provideappinitializer))
- <a id="dockerignore"></a>**.dockerignore**: a file listing paths removed from the build context before it is sent to the builder ([44 §2](modules/44-fullstack-delivery-and-operations.md#2-a-docker-multi-stage-build-for-angular-build-and-runtime-stages-layer-caching-dockerignore-and-a-non-root-image))
- <a id="empty"></a>**@empty**: the block of a `@for` that renders when the collection has no items ([18 §2](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#2-for-track-context-variables-empty-and-what-angular-can-iterate))
- <a id="enablewebsocketsecurity"></a>**@EnableWebSocketSecurity**: Spring Security's annotation (`@since 5.8`) that secures the inbound channel with a security-context, a CSRF and an authorization interceptor ([43 §7](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#7-authentication-on-the-handshake-cookies-tokens-in-the-connect-frame-origin-checks-and-csrf-in-spring-security))
- <a id="error"></a>**$error**: the implicit variable holding the caught error inside an `@error` block ([37 §9](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#9-error-boundaries-boundary-error-reset-onviewerror-and-onerror))
- <a id="event"></a>**$event**: the name for the native event, or the emitted value of an output, inside an event binding's statement ([13 §5](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#5-event-binding-and-two-way-binding)).
- <a id="implicit"></a>**$implicit**: the context key that a bare `let item` reads ([14 §2](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#2-structural-directives-ng-template-templateref-viewcontainerref-and-the-microsyntax)).
- <a id="keyframes"></a>**@keyframes**: the at-rule that declares a named sequence of keyframes an `animation` can run ([35 §3](modules/35-animations.md#3-css-keyframe-animations-keyframes-fill-modes-iteration-and-component-scoped-names))
- <a id="let"></a>**@let**: `@let name = expression;`, a read-only template variable that Angular keeps current; [Stable since v19.0] ([13 §6](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#6-template-reference-variables-and-let)).
- <a id="localize"></a>**$localize**: the global tag function from `@angular/localize` that translated text compiles to; its `locale` property holds the build's locale ([34 §1](modules/34-i18n.md#1-i18n-l10n-and-the-locale-language-tags-what-a-locale-decides-and-locale_id))
- <a id="messagemapping"></a>**@MessageMapping**: Spring's annotation for a method that handles messages whose destination matches its pattern, as `@RequestMapping` does for paths ([43 §5](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#5-websockets-and-stomp-in-spring-the-handshake-frames-messagemapping-brokers-user-destinations-and-heart-beats))
- <a id="-1"></a>**<ng-container>**: a grouping element that renders nothing and leaves a comment node; the wrapper for a second structural directive ([14 §2](modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#2-structural-directives-ng-template-templateref-viewcontainerref-and-the-microsyntax)).
- <a id="ng-deep"></a>**::ng-deep**: Angular's custom pseudo-class that stops the selector rewrite from its position on; discouraged by the Angular team ([13 §9](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#9-view-encapsulation-emulated-shadowdom-and-none-and-what-the-compiler-does-to-your-css)).
- <a id="_ngcontent--_nghost-attributes"></a>**_ngcontent / _nghost attributes**: the generated attributes of emulated encapsulation, `_ngcontent-<id>` on template elements and `_nghost-<id>` on the host, sharing one suffix per component type ([13 §9](modules/13-components-and-templates.md#9-view-encapsulation-emulated-shadowdom-and-none-and-what-the-compiler-does-to-your-css)).
- <a id="ɵcmp"></a>**ɵcmp**: the static definition the compiler writes on a component class; internal, never to be used directly ([19 §8](modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#8-under-the-hood-the-compiled-definition-projection-slots-and-query-refresh); [12](modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#q12-24))
- <a id="ɵcmp--ɵfac"></a>**ɵcmp / ɵfac**: the static component definition and factory that the Angular compiler emits in place of `@Component` ([07 §7](modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#7-decorators-tc39-standard-versus-experimentaldecorators-and-what-angular-does-with-them)).
- <a id="ɵinj"></a>**ɵinj**: the injector definition the compiler writes onto an NgModule class: its `providers` and `imports` (internal API) ([15 §8](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-for-modules-and-standalone-components-and-the-standalone-injector)).
- <a id="ɵmod"></a>**ɵmod**: the module definition the compiler writes onto an NgModule class; after AOT it holds only the type (internal API) ([15 §8](modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-for-modules-and-standalone-components-and-the-standalone-injector)).
- <a id="ɵɵ-instruction"></a>**ɵɵ instruction**: a function the compiler emits calls to, such as `ɵɵconditional`; internal, never called from application code ([18 §9](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#9-under-the-hood-what-the-blocks-compile-to-how-for-reconciles-and-how-defer-moves-between-states))
- <a id="--path-control-flow-schematic"></a>**--path (control flow schematic)**: the directory under the project root the schematic migrates, default `./` ([18 §8](modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#8-migrating-with-ng-generate-angularcorecontrol-flow))
- <a id="--repeat-each"></a>**--repeat-each**: a Playwright CLI flag that runs every test N times, to make an intermittent failure appear on demand ([30 §7](modules/30-e2e-and-testing-strategy.md#7-flaky-tests-root-causes-diagnosis-and-fixes))
- <a id="reset"></a>**$reset**: the implicit function in an `@error` block that clears the boundary's error and renders its content again ([37 §9](modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#9-error-boundaries-boundary-error-reset-onviewerror-and-onerror))
- <a id="rootscope"></a>**$rootScope**: the one scope every other AngularJS scope descends from ([39 §1](modules/39-version-history-and-migrations.md#1-angularjs-scopes-watchers-and-the-digest-cycle))
- <a id="sendtouser"></a>**@SendToUser**: sends a message method's return value to the user's sessions (or only the sending one with `broadcast = false`) instead of every subscriber ([43 §5](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#5-websockets-and-stomp-in-spring-the-handshake-frames-messagemapping-brokers-user-destinations-and-heart-beats))
- <a id="service-1"></a>**@Service**: the v22 decorator the docs call a shorthand for `@Injectable({ providedIn: 'root' })`; it rejects constructor parameters, owner [16 §3](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#3-providedin-service-scopes-and-tree-shaking) ([16 §1](modules/16-dependency-injection.md#1-why-dependency-injection-containers-constructor-injection-and-inject)).
- <a id="starting-style"></a>**@starting-style**: the at-rule that gives an element the "before" values for its first style update, so an inserted element can transition ([35 §2](modules/35-animations.md#2-css-transitions-triggers-interruption-events-and-entry-with-starting-style))
- <a id="stomprx-stomp"></a>**@stomp/rx-stomp**: an RxJS wrapper over stompjs whose `watch` re-subscribes after each reconnect and whose `publish` queues while offline; read, not run ([43 §6](modules/43-fullstack-realtime.md#6-the-angular-websocket-client-rxjs-websocket-stomprx-stomp-and-reconnecting-without-losing-subscriptions))
- <a id="view-transition-old--view-transition-new"></a>**::view-transition-old() / ::view-transition-new()**: the CSS pseudo-elements for the snapshot of the old view and the live new view, styled to customize the animation (MDN; [24 §9](modules/24-routing.md#9-view-transitions-scroll-restoration-and-titles))
